26: const natural = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
27: 
28: export function naturalOrder(a: string, b: string): number {
29:   const tokens = (s: string) =>
30:     s
31:       .normalize('NFC')
32:       .toLowerCase()
33:       .replace(/\b(chapter|ch)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'ch ')
34:       .replace(/\b(volume|vol)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'vol ')
35:       .match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?|[^\d]+/g) || [];
36:   const category = (s: string) =>
37:     /^(prologue|opening)\b/i.test(s) ? -1 : /^(extra|bonus|special|epilogue)\b/i.test(s) ? 1 : 0;
38:   const categoryDiff = category(a) - category(b);
39:   if (categoryDiff) return categoryDiff;
40:   const aa = tokens(a),
41:     bb = tokens(b);
42:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(aa.length, bb.length); i++) {
43:     const x = aa[i],
44:       y = bb[i],
45:       nx = Number(x),
46:       ny = Number(y);
47:     const both = x.trim() !== '' && y.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(nx) && Number.isFinite(ny);
48:     const result = both ? nx - ny : natural.compare(x, y);
49:     if (result) return result;
50:   }
51:   return aa.length - bb.length || natural.compare(a, b);
52: }

54: export async function naturalSortPaths(paths: string[]): Promise<string[]> {
55:   if (desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths) {
56:     try {
57:       return await desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths(paths);
58:     } catch {
59:       // fallback
60:     }
61:   }
62:   return [...paths].sort(naturalOrder);
63: }

105: export class DesktopHandle {
106:   readonly kind = 'file';
107:   constructor(
108:     readonly name: string,
109:     readonly absolutePath: string,
110:     readonly size: number,
111:     readonly modified: number,
112:     readonly width?: number | null,
113:     readonly height?: number | null,
114:   ) { }
115: 
116:   get absPath(): string {
117:     return this.absolutePath;
118:   }
119: 
120:   get lastModified(): number {
121:     return this.modified;
122:   }
123: 
124:   async getFile(): Promise<File> {
125:     if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) throw new Error('Desktop API not available.');
126:     const buffer = await desktopAPI.readFile(this.absolutePath);
127:     if (!buffer) {
128:       throw new Error(`Failed to read file from desktop path: ${this.absolutePath}`);
129:     }
130:     return new File([buffer], this.name, {
131:       lastModified: this.modified,
132:     });
133:   }
134: }

151: export async function fromDesktopFolder(
152:   folderPath: string,
153:   options: ScanOptions = {},
154: ): Promise<Source> {
155:   if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) throw new Error('Desktop API not available.');
156:   void desktopAPI.recordOpenedRoot?.(folderPath);
157:   const folderName = folderPath.split(/[\\/]/).at(-1) ?? folderPath;
158:   const source: Source = { title: folderName, localPath: folderPath, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
159: 
160:   if (desktopAPI.discoverLibrary) {
161:     try {
162:       const discovery = await withinTime(
163:         desktopAPI.discoverLibrary(folderPath, {
164:           signal: options.signal,
165:           onProgress: (prog) => {
166:             if (prog.total > 0) {
167:               desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
168:             }
169:           },
170:         }),
171:         limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
172:         'Discovering library',
173:         options.signal,
174:       );
175: 
176:       if (discovery && discovery.titles.length > 0) {
177:         source.discoveredTitles = discovery.titles;
178:         let discovered = 0;
179:         for (const title of discovery.titles) {
180:           for (const ch of title.chapters) {
181:             for (const p of ch.pages) {
182:               alive(options.signal);
183:               const relPath =
184:                 discovery.titles.length > 1 ? `${title.title}/${p.relativePath}` : p.relativePath;
185:               assertPathLimits(relPath);
186:               assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
187:               assertAtMost(
188:                 source.files.size + 1,
189:                 limits.pagesPerTitle,
190:                 'Image candidates per title',
191:               );
192:               source.files.set(
193:                 relPath,
194:                 new DesktopHandle(
195:                   p.name,
196:                   p.absolutePath,
197:                   p.size,
198:                   p.modified,
199:                   p.width,
200:                   p.height,
201:                 ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
202:               );
203:             }
204:           }
205:         }
206:         return source;
207:       }
208:     } catch (e) {
209:       if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') {
210:         throw e;
211:       }
212:       console.warn('Native discoverLibrary failed, falling back to listFolder:', e);
213:     }
214:   }
215: 
216:   const entries: FolderEntry[] = await withinTime(
217:     desktopAPI.listFolder(folderPath, {
218:       signal: options.signal,
219:       onProgress: (prog) => {
220:         if (prog.total > 0) {
221:           desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
222:         }
223:       },
224:     }),
225:     limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
226:     'Listing folder',
227:     options.signal,
228:   );
229: 
230:   let discovered = 0;
231:   for (const entry of entries) {
232:     alive(options.signal);
233:     const relPath = normalizeRelativePath(entry.relativePath);
234:     if (ignored(relPath) || !imageFile({ name: relPath, type: '' })) continue;
235:     assertPathLimits(relPath);
236:     assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
237:     assertAtMost(source.files.size + 1, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
238: 
239:     source.files.set(
240:       relPath,
241:       new DesktopHandle(
242:         relPath.split('/').at(-1) ?? relPath,
243:         entry.absolutePath,
244:         entry.size,
245:         entry.modified,
246:         entry.width,
247:         entry.height,
248:       ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
249:     );
250:   }
251: 
252:   return source;
253: }

270: export async function splitSource(source: Source): Promise<Source[]> {
271:   if (source.discoveredTitles && source.discoveredTitles.length > 1) {
272:     const splitTitles: Source[] = [];
273:     for (const dt of source.discoveredTitles) {
274:       const s: Source = {
275:         title: dt.title,
276:         localPath: dt.rootPath,
277:         files: new Map(),
278:         issues: [],
279:       };
280:       for (const ch of dt.chapters) {
281:         for (const p of ch.pages) {
282:           s.files.set(
283:             p.relativePath,
284:             new DesktopHandle(
285:               p.name,
286:               p.absolutePath,
287:               p.size,
288:               p.modified,
289:               p.width,
290:               p.height,
291:             ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
292:           );
293:         }
294:       }
295:       splitTitles.push(s);
296:       assertAtMost(splitTitles.length, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
297:     }
298:     return splitTitles.length > 0 ? splitTitles : [source];
299:   }
300: 
301:   const result = new Map<string, Source>();
302:   const sep = source.localPath?.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
303:   for (const [path, file] of source.files) {
304:     const parts = path.split('/');
305:     const title = parts.length > 1 ? parts.shift()! : `${source.title} — root pages`;
306:     let s = result.get(title);
307:     if (!s) {
308:       const childLocalPath =
309:         source.localPath && title !== `${source.title} — root pages`
310:           ? `${source.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '')}${sep}${title}`
311:           : source.localPath;
312:       s = {
313:         title,
314:         localPath: childLocalPath,
315:         files: new Map(),
316:         issues: [],
317:       };
318:       result.set(title, s);
319:       assertAtMost(result.size, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
320:     }
321:     s.files.set(parts.join('/'), file);
322:   }
323:   return result.size > 0 ? [...result.values()] : [source];
324: }

326: export function chapterGroups(source: Source): Map<string, number> {
327:   const groups = new Map<string, number>();
328:   for (const path of source.files.keys()) {
329:     const parts = path.split('/');
330:     const name = parts.length === 1 ? 'Root pages' : parts.slice(0, -1).join(' / ');
331:     if (parts.length === 1 && /^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path) && source.files.size > 1) continue;
332:     groups.set(name, (groups.get(name) || 0) + 1);
333:   }
334:   return groups;
335: }

337: export function chapterPreviews(source: Source): ChapterPreview[] {
338:   const groups = chapterGroups(source);
339:   return Array.from(groups.entries(), ([name, pageCount]) => ({ name, pageCount }));
340: }

342: export async function pooled<T>(
343:   items: T[],
344:   limitOrFn: number | ((item: T) => Promise<void>),
345:   fnOrSignal?: ((item: T) => Promise<void>) | AbortSignal,
346:   signal?: AbortSignal,
347: ): Promise<void> {
348:   const limit = typeof limitOrFn === 'number' ? limitOrFn : 4;
349:   const fn =
350:     typeof limitOrFn === 'function' ? limitOrFn : (fnOrSignal as (item: T) => Promise<void>);
351:   const sig = fnOrSignal instanceof AbortSignal ? fnOrSignal : signal;
352: 
353:   if (items.length === 0) return;
354:   const poolLimit = Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, items.length));
355:   let next = 0;
356: 
357:   const workers = Array.from({ length: poolLimit }, async () => {
358:     while (next < items.length) {
359:       if (sig?.aborted) throw new DOMException('Operation cancelled', 'AbortError');
360:       const idx = next++;
361:       await fn(items[idx]);
362:     }
363:   });
364: 
365:   await Promise.all(workers);
366: }

368: export async function indexSource(
369:   source: Source,
370:   options: ScanOptions = {},
371:   previous?: Manga,
372:   sort: Manga['sort'] = 'natural',
373: ) {
374:   if (previous) stopBackground(previous.id);
375: 
376:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.indexTitle && source.localPath) {
377:     alive(options.signal);
378:     const confirmedTitle = source.title?.trim() || undefined;
379:     const jobId = `index-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
380: 
381:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
382:     if (options.signal) {
383:       abortHandler = () => {
384:         void desktopAPI.cancelScan?.(jobId);
385:       };
386:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
387:     }
388: 
389:     try {
390:       const dto = await desktopAPI.indexTitle(
391:         source.localPath,
392:         {
393:           sort,
394:           previous,
395:           targetId: previous?.id,
396:           title: confirmedTitle,
397:           jobId,
398:           signal: options.signal,
399:         },
400:         (progress) => {
401:           alive(options.signal);
402:           options.update?.({
403:             phase: progress.phase as 'discover' | 'index',
404:             done: progress.done,
405:             total: progress.total,
406:             title: confirmedTitle || progress.title,
407:           });
408:           if (progress.total > 0) {
409:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress.done / progress.total)));
410:           }
411:         },
412:       );
413:       alive(options.signal);
414: 
415:       const m: Manga = {
416:         id: dto.id,
417:         title: confirmedTitle || dto.title,
418:         slug: dto.slug,
419:         localPath: dto.localPath,
420:         lastChapter: dto.lastChapter,
421:         addedAt: dto.addedAt,
422:         updatedAt: dto.updatedAt,
423:         revision: dto.revision,
424:         sort: dto.sort as 'natural' | 'name' | 'reverse',
425:         chapters: (dto.chapters || []).map((c: any) => ({
426:           ...(c.id ? { id: c.id } : {}),
427:           name: c.name,
428:           read: c.read,
429:           position: c.position,
430:           bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
431:           pages: (c.pages || []).map((p: any) => ({
432:             name: p.name,
433:             path: p.path,
434:             width: p.width,
435:             height: p.height,
436:             size: p.size,
437:             modified: p.modified,
438:             dimensionsPending: p.dimensionsPending,
439:           })),
440:         })),
441:       };
442: 
443:       alive(options.signal);
444:       // Natively committed directly to SQLite in Rust index_title; invalidate JS caches
445:       invalidateSummaryCache();
446:       invalidateBookmarkCache();
447:       broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id: m.id });
448: 
449:       return {
450:         m,
451:         issues: [...source.issues, ...dto.issues],
452:         reused: dto.reused,
453:       };
454:     } finally {
455:       if (abortHandler && options.signal) {
456:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
457:       }
458:     }
459:   }
460: 
461:   throw new Error('Desktop indexing requires native indexTitle and localPath');
462: }
463: 
464: export async function prepareChapter(
465:   manga: Manga,
466:   chapterIndex: number,
467:   options: PrepareChapterOptions = {},
468: ): Promise<Issue[]> {
469:   alive(options.signal);
470:   const ch = manga.chapters[chapterIndex];
471:   if (!ch) return [];
472: 
473:   const unmeasured = ch.pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending || !p.width || !p.height);
474:   if (!unmeasured.length) return [];
475: 
476:   const issues: Issue[] = [];
477: 
478:   if (desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders) {
479:     const paths = unmeasured.map((p) => p.path).filter((p): p is string => Boolean(p));
480:     if (paths.length > 0) {
481:       try {
482:         alive(options.signal);
483:         const headers = await desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders(paths, manga.id);
484:         alive(options.signal);
485:         const headerMap = new Map<string, PageHeader>();
486:         for (const h of headers) {
487:           headerMap.set(h.path, h);
488:         }
489:         let done = 0;
490:         let updated = false;
491:         for (const p of unmeasured) {
492:           const found = p.path ? headerMap.get(p.path) : undefined;
493:           if (found && found.width > 0 && found.height > 0) {
494:             assertImageDimensions(found.width, found.height);
495:             p.width = found.width;
496:             p.height = found.height;
497:             p.dimensionsPending = false;
498:             updated = true;
499:           } else {
500:             issues.push({
501:               path: p.path || p.name,
502:               reason: 'Could not resolve image dimensions',
503:             });
504:           }
505:           done++;
506:           options.update?.({
507:             phase: 'index',
508:             done,
509:             total: unmeasured.length,
510:             title: manga.title,
511:           });
512:         }
513:         if (updated) {
514:           await saveManga(manga);
515:         }
516:         return issues;
517:       } catch (err) {
518:         if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === 'AbortError') throw err;
519:         console.warn('Native prepareChapterHeaders failed:', err);
520:         return unmeasured.map((p) => ({
521:           path: p.path || p.name,
522:           reason: `Failed to prepare chapter headers: ${errorMessage(err)}`,
523:         }));
524:       }
525:     }
526:   }
527: 
528:   return unmeasured.map((p) => ({
529:     path: p.path || p.name,
530:     reason: 'Header preparation unavailable or paths missing',
531:   }));
532: }

534: interface BackgroundJob {
535:   id: string;
536:   revision?: number;
537:   updatedAt?: number;
538:   controller: AbortController;
539: }
540: 
541: const backgroundJobs = new Map<string, BackgroundJob>();
542: let backgroundRunning = false;
543: 
544: export function stopBackground(id: string): void {
545:   const job = backgroundJobs.get(id);
546:   if (job) {
547:     job.controller.abort();
548:     backgroundJobs.delete(id);
549:   }
550: }
551: 
552: export function stopAllBackground(): void {
553:   for (const job of backgroundJobs.values()) {
554:     job.controller.abort();
555:   }
556:   backgroundJobs.clear();
557: }
558: 
559: onMangaDelete((id) => {
560:   if (id) stopBackground(id);
561:   else stopAllBackground();
562: });
563: 
564: export function scheduleBackground(manga: Manga): void {
565:   if (
566:     backgroundJobs.has(manga.id) ||
567:     !manga.chapters.some((c) => c.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending))
568:   ) {
569:     return;
570:   }
571:   if (backgroundJobs.size >= limits.queuedJobs) return;
572:   backgroundJobs.set(manga.id, {
573:     id: manga.id,
574:     revision: manga.revision,
575:     updatedAt: manga.updatedAt,
576:     controller: new AbortController(),
577:   });
578:   void drainBackground();
579: }
580: 
581: async function runDrainPass(): Promise<void> {
582:   let remaining = backgroundJobs.size;
583:   while (backgroundJobs.size && remaining-- > 0) {
584:     const entry = backgroundJobs.entries().next().value;
585:     if (!entry) break;
586:     const [id, job] = entry;
587:     try {
588:       const initial = await getManga(id);
589:       if (
590:         !initial ||
591:         initial.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
592:         (job.revision !== undefined && initial.revision !== job.revision)
593:       ) {
594:         continue;
595:       }
596:       for (let i = 0; i < initial.chapters.length; i++) {
597:         alive(job.controller.signal);
598:         if (!initial.chapters[i].pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) continue;
599:         await new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
600:         alive(job.controller.signal);
601:         if (!(await access(initial))) break;
602:         const stored = await getManga(id);
603:         if (
604:           !stored ||
605:           stored.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
606:           (job.revision !== undefined && stored.revision !== job.revision)
607:         ) {
608:           break;
609:         }
610:         const issues = await prepareChapter(stored, i, { signal: job.controller.signal });
611:         job.updatedAt = stored.updatedAt;
612:         job.revision = stored.revision;
613:         if (issues.length > 0 && stored.chapters[i]?.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) {
614:           break;
615:         }
616:       }
617:     } catch {
618:       /* Interrupted jobs remain pending in saved metadata and can resume later */
619:     } finally {
620:       if (backgroundJobs.get(id) === job) {
621:         backgroundJobs.delete(id);
622:       }
623:     }
624:   }
625: }
626: 
627: async function drainBackground(): Promise<void> {
628:   if (backgroundRunning) return;
629:   backgroundRunning = true;
630:   try {
631:     await runDrainPass();
632:   } finally {
633:     backgroundRunning = false;
634:     if (backgroundJobs.size > 0) {
635:       setTimeout(() => void drainBackground(), 100);
636:     }
637:   }
638: }
639: 
640: export async function resumeBackground(): Promise<void> {
641:   try {
642:     const list = await listLibrary();
643:     for (const item of list) {
644:       const m = await getManga(item.id);
645:       if (m && m.chapters.some((c) => c.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending))) {
646:         scheduleBackground(m);
647:       }
648:     }
649:   } catch (err) {
650:     console.warn('Failed to resume background jobs:', err);
651:   }
652: }
653: 
654: export function scanImage(_entry: SourceFile, _signal?: AbortSignal) {
655:   return Promise.resolve({ width: 0, height: 0 });
656: }
657: export function discardScan(_sources: Source[]): void { }
658: export async function warmSources(
659:   sources: Source[],
660:   options: ScanOptions = {},
661:   _sort: Manga['sort'] = 'natural',
662: ): Promise<void> {
663:   for (const source of sources) {
664:     alive(options.signal);
665:     options.update?.({
666:       phase: 'discover',
667:       done: source.files.size,
668:       total: 0,
669:       title: source.title,
670:     });
671:   }
672: }

166: export async function fileFor(m: Manga, p: Page): Promise<File> {
167:   if (!p.path) throw new Error('Desktop page is missing path');
168:   const resolvedPath = desktopAPI.resolvePath
169:     ? await desktopAPI.resolvePath(m.id, p.path, m.localPath)
170:     : p.path;
171:   const buffer = await desktopAPI.readFile(resolvedPath);
172:   if (!buffer) throw new Error(`Could not read desktop file at: ${resolvedPath}`);
173:   return new File([buffer], p.name || 'image.webp');
174: }

176: export async function access(
177:   manga: Pick<Manga, 'id' | 'localPath'>,
178:   request = false,
179: ): Promise<boolean> {
180:   if (!manga) return false;
181: 
182:   const now = Date.now();
183:   if (!request) {
184:     const cached = accessCache.get(manga.id);
185:     if (cached && now < cached.expiresAt) {
186:       return cached.granted;
187:     }
188:   }
189: 
190:   try {
191:     const granted = desktopAPI.checkAccess
192:       ? await desktopAPI.checkAccess(manga.id, manga.localPath)
193:       : Boolean(manga.localPath);
194: 
195:     accessCache.set(manga.id, {
196:       expiresAt: now + ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
197:       granted,
198:     });
199:     return granted;
200:   } catch {
201:     accessCache.set(manga.id, {
202:       expiresAt: now + ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
203:       granted: false,
204:     });
205:     return false;
206:   }
207: }

7: export class ValidationError extends Error {
8:   constructor(message: string) {
9:     super(message);
10:     this.name = 'ValidationError';

14: export function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
15:   return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
16: }

18: const RESERVED_DOS_NAMES = new Set([
19:   'con',
20:   'prn',
21:   'aux',
22:   'nul',
23:   'com1',
24:   'com2',
25:   'com3',
26:   'com4',
27:   'com5',
28:   'com6',
29:   'com7',

43: export function validateRelativePath(path: unknown): string {
44:   if (typeof path !== 'string' || !path.trim()) {
45:     throw new ValidationError('Path must be a non-empty string');
46:   }
47:   if (path.startsWith(' ') || path.endsWith(' ')) {
48:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with whitespace: "${path}"`);
49:   }
50:   const trimmed = path.trim();
51:   if (trimmed.length > limits.pathChars) {
52:     throw new ValidationError(
53:       `Relative path length (characters) exceeds the limit of ${limits.pathChars}. Use a smaller selection.`,
54:     );
55:   }
56:   // Reject colons (Windows drive letters e.g. C:, alternate data streams e.g. :stream)
57:   if (trimmed.includes(':')) {
58:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot contain drive prefixes or colons: ${path}`);
59:   }
60:   const normalized = trimmed.replace(/\\/g, '/');
61:   // Reject rooted paths, leading double-slashes (UNC), and null bytes
62:   if (
63:     normalized.startsWith('/') ||
64:     normalized.startsWith('//') ||
65:     normalized.endsWith('/') ||
66:     normalized.includes('\0')
67:   ) {
68:     throw new ValidationError(`Invalid path traversal or structure: ${path}`);
69:   }
70: 
71:   // Validate individual path components, allowing legitimate double dots in filenames like chapter..jpg
72:   const segments = normalized.split('/');
73:   if (segments.length - 1 > limits.directoryDepth) {
74:     throw new ValidationError(
75:       `Folder depth exceeds the limit of ${limits.directoryDepth}. Use a smaller selection.`,
76:     );
77:   }
78:   for (const seg of segments) {
79:     if (!seg || seg === '.' || seg === '..') {
80:       throw new ValidationError(`Invalid path traversal segment: ${path}`);
81:     }
82:     if (seg.endsWith(' ') || seg.endsWith('.')) {
83:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment cannot end with a space or dot: ${seg}`);
84:     }
85:     const stem = seg.split('.')[0].toLowerCase();
86:     if (RESERVED_DOS_NAMES.has(stem)) {
87:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment uses reserved device name: ${seg}`);
88:     }
89:   }
90:   return normalized;
91: }
92: 
93: export function matchingPosition(
94:   saved?: Position,
95:   pages?: Page[],
96:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
97: ): Position | undefined {
98:   if (!saved || !pages || !pages.length) return undefined;
99:   const path = saved.path || oldPages[saved.page]?.path;
100:   let page = path ? pages.findIndex((p) => p.path === path) : -1;
101:   if (page < 0 && path) {
102:     const name = path.split('/').at(-1);
103:     const matches = pages.map((x, i) => (x.name === name ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
104:     if (matches.length === 1) page = matches[0];
105:   }
106:   if (page < 0) page = Math.min(saved.page, pages.length - 1);
107:   const validIndex = Math.max(0, page);
108:   return {
109:     page: validIndex,
110:     path: pages[validIndex].path,
111:     fraction: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number.isFinite(saved.fraction) ? saved.fraction : 0)),
112:     ...(saved.mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
113:     ...(typeof saved.timestamp === 'number' && Number.isFinite(saved.timestamp)
114:       ? { timestamp: saved.timestamp }
115:       : {}),
116:   };
117: }
118: 
119: export function parsePosition(
120:   value: unknown,
121:   pages?: Page[],
122:   fallback?: Position,
123:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
124: ): Position | undefined {
125:   if (value === undefined || value === null) return fallback;
126:   if (!isRecord(value)) throw new ValidationError('Position must be an object.');
127: 
128:   const page = value.page;
129:   if (typeof page !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(page) || page < 0) {
130:     throw new ValidationError('Position page must be a non-negative integer.');
131:   }
132: 
133:   const fraction = value.fraction;
134:   if (typeof fraction !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(fraction)) {
135:     throw new ValidationError('Position fraction must be a finite number.');
136:   }
137:   const clampedFraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fraction));
138: 
139:   const path = typeof value.path === 'string' ? validateRelativePath(value.path) : undefined;
140: 
141:   let mangaBookmark: boolean | undefined;
142:   if ('mangaBookmark' in value && value.mangaBookmark !== undefined) {
143:     if (typeof value.mangaBookmark !== 'boolean') {
144:       throw new ValidationError('Position mangaBookmark must be a boolean.');
145:     }
146:     if (value.mangaBookmark) {
147:       mangaBookmark = true;
148:     }
149:   }
150: 
151:   let timestamp: number | undefined;
152:   if ('timestamp' in value && value.timestamp !== undefined) {
153:     if (typeof value.timestamp !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value.timestamp)) {
154:       throw new ValidationError('Position timestamp must be a finite number.');
155:     }
156:     timestamp = value.timestamp;
157:   }
158: 
159:   const rawPos: Position = {
160:     page,
161:     fraction: clampedFraction,
162:     ...(path ? { path } : {}),
163:     ...(mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
164:     ...(timestamp !== undefined ? { timestamp } : {}),
165:   };
166: 
167:   if (pages && pages.length > 0) {
168:     return matchingPosition(rawPos, pages, oldPages);
169:   }
170:   return rawPos;
171: }
172: 
173: export function parsePage(value: unknown, seenPaths: Set<string>): Page {
174:   if (!isRecord(value)) throw new ValidationError('Page record must be an object.');
175: 
176:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
177:     throw new ValidationError('Page name must be a non-empty string.');
178:   }
179: 
180:   const path = validateRelativePath(value.path);
181:   if (seenPaths.has(path)) {
182:     throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate page path within chapter: "${path}"`);
183:   }
184:   seenPaths.add(path);
185: 
186:   const isPending = value.dimensionsPending === true;
187:   const isWidthPending = isPending && value.width === 0;
188:   const isHeightPending = isPending && value.height === 0;
189: 
190:   if (
191:     typeof value.width !== 'number' ||
192:     !Number.isInteger(value.width) ||
193:     typeof value.height !== 'number' ||
194:     !Number.isInteger(value.height) ||
195:     (value.width <= 0 && !isWidthPending) ||
196:     (value.height <= 0 && !isHeightPending)
197:   ) {
198:     throw new ValidationError('Page dimensions must be positive integer pixels.');
199:   }
200: 
201:   if (value.width > 0 && value.height > 0) {
202:     assertImageDimensions(value.width, value.height);
203:   }
204: 
205:   let size: number | undefined;
206:   if (value.size !== undefined) {
207:     if (typeof value.size !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(value.size) || value.size < 0) {
208:       throw new ValidationError('Page size must be a non-negative integer bytes count.');
209:     }
210:     size = value.size;
211:   }
212: 
213:   let modified: number | undefined;
214:   if (value.modified !== undefined) {
215:     if (
216:       typeof value.modified !== 'number' ||
217:       !Number.isFinite(value.modified) ||
218:       value.modified < 0
219:     ) {
220:       throw new ValidationError('Page modified timestamp must be a non-negative number.');
221:     }
222:     modified = value.modified;
223:   }
224: 
225:   const dimensionsPending = isPending ? true : undefined;
226: 
227:   return {
228:     name: value.name.trim(),
229:     path,
230:     width: value.width,
231:     height: value.height,
232:     ...(size !== undefined ? { size } : {}),
233:     ...(modified !== undefined ? { modified } : {}),
234:     ...(dimensionsPending !== undefined ? { dimensionsPending } : {}),
235:   };
236: }
237: 
238: export function parseChapter(value: unknown, chapterIndex: number, oldChapter?: Chapter): Chapter {
239:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
240:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} must be an object.`);
241:   }
242: 
243:   const name =
244:     typeof value.name === 'string' && value.name.trim()
245:       ? value.name.trim()
246:       : `Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1}`;
247: 
248:   if (!Array.isArray(value.pages) || !value.pages.length) {
249:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${name}" must contain at least one page.`);
250:   }
251: 
252:   const seenPaths = new Set<string>();
253:   const pages: Page[] = value.pages.map((p, idx) => {
254:     try {
255:       return parsePage(p, seenPaths);
256:     } catch (e) {
257:       throw new ValidationError(
258:         `Chapter "${name}" page ${idx + 1} is invalid: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`,
259:       );
260:     }
261:   });
262: 
263:   const position = value.position
264:     ? parsePosition(value.position, pages, undefined, oldChapter?.pages)
265:     : undefined;
266: 
267:   let bookmarks: Position[] | undefined;
268:   if (value.bookmarks !== undefined) {
269:     if (!Array.isArray(value.bookmarks)) {
270:       throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${name}" bookmarks must be an array.`);
271:     }
272:     assertAtMost(value.bookmarks.length, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
273:     const seenBookmarks = new Set<string>();
274:     bookmarks = [];
275:     for (const b of value.bookmarks) {
276:       const parsed = parsePosition(b, pages, undefined, oldChapter?.pages);
277:       if (parsed) {
278:         const key = `${parsed.page}:${parsed.path ?? ''}:${Boolean(parsed.mangaBookmark)}`;
279:         if (!seenBookmarks.has(key)) {
280:           seenBookmarks.add(key);
281:           bookmarks.push(parsed);
282:         }
283:       }
284:     }
285:   }
286: 
287:   const id = typeof value.id === 'string' && value.id.trim() ? value.id.trim() : undefined;
288: 
289:   return {
290:     ...(id ? { id } : {}),
291:     name,
292:     read: value.read === true,
293:     pages,
294:     position,
295:     bookmarks,
296:   };
297: }

299: export function parseManga(
300:   value: unknown,
301:   oldManga?: Manga,
302:   options: { preserveHardwarePointers?: boolean } = { preserveHardwarePointers: true },
303: ): Manga {
304:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
305:     throw new ValidationError('Manga record must be an object.');
306:   }
307: 
308:   if (typeof value.id !== 'string' || !value.id.trim()) {
309:     throw new ValidationError('Manga ID must be a non-empty string.');
310:   }
311: 
312:   if (typeof value.title !== 'string' || !value.title.trim()) {
313:     throw new ValidationError('Manga title must be a non-empty string.');
314:   }
315: 
316:   if (!Array.isArray(value.chapters) || !value.chapters.length) {
317:     throw new ValidationError(`Manga "${value.title}" must contain at least one chapter.`);
318:   }
319: 
320:   assertAtMost(value.chapters.length, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
321: 
322:   let titlePages = 0;
323:   const chapters: Chapter[] = value.chapters.map((c, idx) => {
324:     const oldChapter = oldManga?.chapters[idx];
325:     const parsed = parseChapter(c, idx, oldChapter);
326:     titlePages += parsed.pages.length;
327:     assertAtMost(titlePages, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Pages per title');
328:     return parsed;
329:   });
330: 
331:   const lastChapter =
332:     typeof value.lastChapter === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value.lastChapter)
333:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(chapters.length - 1, value.lastChapter))
334:       : 0;
335: 
336:   const now = Date.now();
337:   const addedAt =
338:     typeof value.addedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.addedAt) && value.addedAt > 0
339:       ? value.addedAt
340:       : now;
341: 
342:   const updatedAt =
343:     typeof value.updatedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.updatedAt) && value.updatedAt > 0
344:       ? value.updatedAt
345:       : now;
346: 
347:   const sort =
348:     value.sort === 'name' || value.sort === 'reverse' || value.sort === 'natural'
349:       ? value.sort
350:       : undefined;
351: 
352:   const revision =
353:     typeof value.revision === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0
354:       ? value.revision
355:       : 1;
356: 
357:   const title = value.title.trim();
358:   const slug =
359:     typeof value.slug === 'string' && value.slug.trim() ? value.slug.trim() : folderSlug(title);
360: 
361:   const localPath =
362:     options.preserveHardwarePointers &&
363:       typeof value.localPath === 'string' &&
364:       value.localPath.trim()
365:       ? value.localPath.trim()
366:       : undefined;
367: 
368:   const handle =
369:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.handle && typeof value.handle === 'object'
370:       ? (value.handle as Manga['handle'])
371:       : undefined;
372: 
373:   const cover =
374:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.cover instanceof Blob ? value.cover : undefined;
375: 
376:   return {
377:     id: value.id.trim(),
378:     title,
379:     slug,
380:     chapters,
381:     lastChapter,
382:     addedAt,
383:     updatedAt,
384:     revision,
385:     ...(sort ? { sort } : {}),
386:     ...(localPath ? { localPath } : {}),
387:     ...(handle ? { handle } : {}),
388:     ...(cover ? { cover } : {}),
389:   };

392: export function parseSummary(
393:   value: unknown,
394:   options: { preserveHardwarePointers?: boolean } = {},
395: ): Summary | undefined {
396:   if (!isRecord(value)) return undefined;
397:   if (typeof value.id !== 'string' || !value.id.trim()) return undefined;
398:   if (typeof value.title !== 'string' || !value.title.trim()) return undefined;
399: 
400:   const id = value.id.trim();
401:   const title = value.title.trim();
402:   const slug =
403:     typeof value.slug === 'string' && value.slug.trim() ? value.slug.trim() : folderSlug(title);
404: 
405:   const chapterCount =
406:     typeof value.chapterCount === 'number' &&
407:       Number.isInteger(value.chapterCount) &&
408:       value.chapterCount >= 0
409:       ? value.chapterCount
410:       : 0;
411: 
412:   const chaptersRead =
413:     typeof value.chaptersRead === 'number' &&
414:       Number.isInteger(value.chaptersRead) &&
415:       value.chaptersRead >= 0
416:       ? Math.min(chapterCount, value.chaptersRead)
417:       : 0;
418: 
419:   const lastChapter =
420:     typeof value.lastChapter === 'number' &&
421:       Number.isInteger(value.lastChapter) &&
422:       value.lastChapter >= 0
423:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.max(0, chapterCount - 1), value.lastChapter))
424:       : 0;
425: 
426:   const lastChapterName = typeof value.lastChapterName === 'string' ? value.lastChapterName : '';
427:   const hasProgress = typeof value.hasProgress === 'boolean' ? value.hasProgress : false;
428: 
429:   const now = Date.now();
430:   const indexedAt =
431:     typeof value.indexedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.indexedAt) && value.indexedAt > 0
432:       ? value.indexedAt
433:       : now;
434: 
435:   const addedAt =
436:     typeof value.addedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.addedAt) && value.addedAt > 0
437:       ? value.addedAt
438:       : now;
439: 
440:   const updatedAt =
441:     typeof value.updatedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.updatedAt) && value.updatedAt > 0
442:       ? value.updatedAt
443:       : now;
444: 
445:   const revision =
446:     typeof value.revision === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0
447:       ? value.revision
448:       : undefined;
449: 
450:   const localPath =
451:     typeof value.localPath === 'string' && value.localPath.trim()
452:       ? value.localPath.trim()
453:       : undefined;
454: 
455:   const handle =
456:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.handle && typeof value.handle === 'object'
457:       ? (value.handle as Summary['handle'])
458:       : undefined;
459: 
460:   const sort =
461:     value.sort === 'name' || value.sort === 'reverse' || value.sort === 'natural'
462:       ? value.sort
463:       : undefined;
464: 
465:   return {
466:     id,
467:     title,
468:     slug,
469:     chapterCount,
470:     chaptersRead,
471:     lastChapter,
472:     lastChapterName,
473:     hasProgress,
474:     indexedAt,
475:     addedAt,
476:     updatedAt,
477:     ...(revision !== undefined ? { revision } : {}),
478:     ...(localPath ? { localPath } : {}),
479:     ...(handle ? { handle } : {}),
480:     ...(sort ? { sort } : {}),
481:   };
482: }

602: 
603: export function validateBackup(value: unknown): { manga: Manga[]; settings: Settings } {
604:   if (typeof value === 'string') {
605:     try {
606:       value = JSON.parse(value);
607:     } catch {
608:       throw new ValidationError('Backup must be valid JSON');
609:     }
610:   }
611:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
612:     throw new ValidationError('Backup must be an object.');
613:   }
614: 
615:   if (value.version !== 1 && value.version !== 2) {
616:     throw new ValidationError('Invalid backup version. Supported versions: 1, 2.');
617:   }
618: 
619:   if (!Array.isArray(value.manga)) {
620:     throw new ValidationError('Backup manga list must be an array.');
621:   }
622: 
623:   assertAtMost(value.manga.length, limits.titles, 'Backup titles');
624: 
625:   const seenIds = new Set<string>();
626:   let totalBackupPages = 0;
627: 
628:   const manga: Manga[] = value.manga.map((rawManga, index) => {
629:     if (!isRecord(rawManga) || typeof rawManga.id !== 'string') {
630:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
631:     }
632:     const id = rawManga.id.trim();
633:     if (!id) {
634:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
635:     }
636:     if (seenIds.has(id)) {
637:       throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate manga ID found in backup: "${id}"`);
638:     }
639:     seenIds.add(id);
640: 
641:     const parsed = parseManga(rawManga, undefined, { preserveHardwarePointers: false });
642:     for (const ch of parsed.chapters) {
643:       totalBackupPages += ch.pages.length;
644:       assertAtMost(totalBackupPages, limits.backupPages, 'Backup pages');
645:     }
646:     return parsed;
647:   });
648: 
649:   const settings = parseSettings(value.settings);
650: 
651:   return {
652:     manga,
653:     settings,
654:   };
655: }

30: /**
31:  * Generate a UUID v4. Prefers crypto.randomUUID() (secure contexts only),
32:  * and falls back to crypto.getRandomValues() which works everywhere.
33:  */
34: function generateUUID(): string {
35:   if (typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') return crypto.randomUUID();
36:   // RFC 4122 §4.4 UUID v4 via getRandomValues (works in non-secure contexts)
37:   const b = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
38:   b[6] = (b[6]! & 0x0f) | 0x40; // version 4
39:   b[8] = (b[8]! & 0x3f) | 0x80; // variant 10xx
40:   const h = Array.from(b, (x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
41:   return `${h.slice(0, 8)}-${h.slice(8, 12)}-${h.slice(12, 16)}-${h.slice(16, 20)}-${h.slice(20)}`;
42: }
43: 
44: const natural = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
45: const lexical = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { sensitivity: 'base' });
46: 
47: export function naturalOrder(a: string, b: string): number {
48:   const tokens = (s: string) =>
49:     s
50:       .normalize('NFC')
51:       .toLowerCase()
52:       .replace(/\b(chapter|ch)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'ch ')
53:       .replace(/\b(volume|vol)\.?\s*(?=\d)/g, 'vol ')
54:       .match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?|[^\d]+/g) || [];
55:   const category = (s: string) =>
56:     /^(prologue|opening)\b/i.test(s) ? -1 : /^(extra|bonus|special|epilogue)\b/i.test(s) ? 1 : 0;
57:   const categoryDiff = category(a) - category(b);
58:   if (categoryDiff) return categoryDiff;
59:   const aa = tokens(a),
60:     bb = tokens(b);
61:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(aa.length, bb.length); i++) {
62:     const x = aa[i],
63:       y = bb[i],
64:       nx = Number(x),
65:       ny = Number(y);
66:     const both = x.trim() !== '' && y.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(nx) && Number.isFinite(ny);
67:     const result = both ? nx - ny : natural.compare(x, y);
68:     if (result) return result;
69:   }
70:   return aa.length - bb.length || natural.compare(a, b);
71: }
72: 
73: /**
74:  * Natural sort a list of paths or names using native Rust command on desktop,
75:  * falling back to JavaScript naturalOrder collation on web.
76:  */
77: export async function naturalSortPaths(paths: string[]): Promise<string[]> {
78:   if (paths.length <= 1) return paths;
79:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths) {
80:     return await desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths(paths);
81:   }
82:   return [...paths].sort(naturalOrder);
83: }

96: const tick = () => {
97:   const sched = (
98:     typeof globalThis !== 'undefined'
99:       ? (globalThis as Record<string, unknown>).scheduler
100:       : undefined
101:   ) as { yield?: () => Promise<void> } | undefined;
102:   return typeof sched?.yield === 'function'
103:     ? sched.yield()
104:     : new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
105: };

107: function getDecodeConcurrency(): number {
108:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && typeof navigator !== 'undefined') {
109:     const cores = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4;
110:     const mem = (navigator as unknown as { deviceMemory?: number }).deviceMemory;
111:     if (cores >= 8 && (!mem || mem >= 8)) {
112:       return 6;
113:     }
114:     if (cores >= 4 && (!mem || mem >= 4)) {
115:       return 4;
116:     }
117:   }
118:   return limits.decodeConcurrency;
119: }

211: export function scanImage(entry: SourceFile, signal?: AbortSignal) {
212:   let task = scanned.get(entry);
213:   if (!task) {
214:     task = (async () => {
215:       alive(signal);
216:       const file = await readFile(entry);
217:       if (!file.size) throw new Error('Empty file.');
218:       const size = await dimensions(file, signal);
219:       alive(signal);
220:       return { file, ...size };
221:     })();
222:     scanned.set(entry, task);
223:     void task.catch(() => {
224:       // Failed reads must not poison later user-initiated retries.
225:       if (scanned.get(entry) === task) scanned.delete(entry);
226:     });
227:   }
228:   return task;
229: }

232:   for (const source of sources) for (const entry of source.files.values()) scanned.delete(entry);

235: export async function warmSources(
236:   sources: Source[],
237:   options: ScanOptions = {},
238:   sort: Manga['sort'] = 'natural',
239: ) {
240:   assertSourcesLimits(sources);
241:   for (const source of sources) {
242:     options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: source.files.size, total: 0, title: source.title });
243:     const compare =
244:       sort === 'name'
245:         ? lexical.compare
246:         : sort === 'reverse'
247:           ? (a: string, b: string) => naturalOrder(b, a)
248:           : naturalOrder;
249:     let chapterKeys = [...chapterGroups(source).keys()];
250:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths && sort !== 'name') {
251:       chapterKeys = await desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths(chapterKeys);
252:       if (sort === 'reverse') chapterKeys.reverse();
253:     } else {
254:       chapterKeys.sort(compare);
255:     }
256:     const chapters = new Set(chapterKeys.slice(0, limits.warmChapters));
257:     const entries = [...source.files].filter(([path, entry]) => {
258:       if (entry instanceof DesktopHandle && entry.width && entry.height) {
259:         return false;
260:       }
261:       return chapters.has(
262:         path.includes('/') ? path.split('/').slice(0, -1).join(' / ') : 'Root pages',
263:       );
264:     });
265:     await pooled(
266:       entries,
267:       async ([, entry]) => {
268:         try {
269:           await scanImage(entry, options.signal);
270:         } catch (e) {
271:           alive(options.signal);
272:           if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') {
273:             scanned.delete(entry);
274:             try {
275:               await scanImage(entry, options.signal);
276:             } catch {
277:               alive(options.signal);
278:             }
279:           }
280:         }
281:       },
282:       options.signal,
283:     );
284:   }
285: }

303: async function* directoryEntries(dir: Directory, signal?: AbortSignal) {
304:   const iterator = dir.values();
305:   try {
306:     for (let count = 0; count <= limits.discoveryEntries; count++) {
307:       alive(signal);
308:       const result = await withinTime(
309:         iterator.next(),
310:         limits.ioTimeoutMs,
311:         'Reading folder',
312:         signal,
313:       );
314:       if (result.done) return;
315:       assertAtMost(count + 1, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Folder entries');
316:       yield result.value;
317:     }
318:   } finally {
319:     if (iterator.return)
320:       await withinTime(iterator.return(), limits.ioTimeoutMs, 'Closing folder iterator');
321:   }
322: }

324: export async function fromHandle(handle: Directory, options: ScanOptions = {}): Promise<Source> {
325:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
326:     throw new Error('fromHandle is not supported in desktop builds');
327:   }
328:   const source: Source = { title: handle.name, handle, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
329:   let count = 0;
330:   const chapterNames = new Set<string>();
331:   const queue: SourceFile[] = [];
332:   const tasks = new Set<Promise<void>>();
333:   let discovered = 0;
334:   let active = 0;
335:   const pump = () => {
336:     while (active < getDecodeConcurrency() && queue.length && !options.signal?.aborted) {
337:       const entry = queue.shift()!;
338:       active++;
339:       const task = scanImage(entry, options.signal)
340:         .then(
341:           () => { },
342:           () => { },
343:         )
344:         .finally(() => {
345:           active--;
346:           tasks.delete(task);
347:           pump();
348:         });
349:       tasks.add(task);
350:     }
351:   };
352:   const add = async (path: string, entry: SourceFile) => {
353:     assertPathLimits(path);
354:     assertAtMost(
355:       source.files.size + (source.files.has(path) ? 0 : 1),
356:       limits.pagesPerTitle,
357:       'Image candidates per title',
358:     );
359:     source.files.set(path, entry);
360:     const chapter = path.includes('/') ? path.split('/').slice(0, -1).join(' / ') : 'Root pages';
361:     // A root cover is not an extra chapter when other pages exist.
362:     if (path.includes('/') || !/^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path)) chapterNames.add(chapter);
363:     assertAtMost(chapterNames.size, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
364:     if ([...chapterNames].sort(naturalOrder).slice(0, limits.warmChapters).includes(chapter)) {
365:       // Wait for one worker before retaining another queued source handle.
366:       while (queue.length >= limits.queuedJobs) {
367:         alive(options.signal);
368:         await Promise.race(tasks);
369:       }
370:       alive(options.signal);
371:       queue.push(entry);
372:       pump();
373:     }
374:   };
375: 
376:   interface StackFrame {
377:     dir: Directory;
378:     prefix: string;
379:     depth: number;
380:   }
381:   const stack: StackFrame[] = [{ dir: handle, prefix: '', depth: 0 }];
382:   let lastYield = performance.now();
383: 
384:   try {
385:     while (stack.length > 0) {
386:       alive(options.signal);
387:       const { dir, prefix, depth } = stack.pop()!;
388:       if (depth > limits.directoryDepth) {
389:         source.issues.push({
390:           path: prefix,
391:           reason: `Folder nesting exceeds ${limits.directoryDepth} levels.`,
392:         });
393:         continue;
394:       }
395:       try {
396:         const entries: FileSystemHandle[] = [];
397:         for await (const entry of directoryEntries(dir, options.signal)) {
398:           alive(options.signal);
399:           assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
400:           assertAtMost(
401:             (prefix + entry.name).length,
402:             limits.pathChars,
403:             'Relative path length (characters)',
404:           );
405:           if (!ignored(prefix + entry.name)) entries.push(entry);
406:         }
407:         entries.sort((a, b) => naturalOrder(a.name, b.name));
408: 
409:         // Push subdirectories in reverse order so pop() processes alphabetically
410:         for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
411:           const entry = entries[i];
412:           const path = prefix + entry.name;
413:           if (entry.kind === 'directory') {
414:             stack.push({ dir: entry as Directory, prefix: path + '/', depth: depth + 1 });
415:           }
416:         }
417: 
418:         // Process files
419:         for (const entry of entries) {
420:           if (entry.kind === 'directory') continue;
421:           alive(options.signal);
422:           const path = prefix + entry.name;
423:           try {
424:             if (imageFile({ name: entry.name, type: '' }))
425:               await add(path, entry as FileSystemFileHandle);
426:             else {
427:               const file = await readFile(entry as FileSystemFileHandle);
428:               if (imageFile(file)) await add(path, entry as FileSystemFileHandle);
429:             }
430:           } catch (e) {
431:             alive(options.signal);
432:             if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
433:             source.issues.push({ path, reason: errorMessage(e) });
434:           }
435:           count++;
436:           const now = performance.now();
437:           if (count % 16 === 0 || now - lastYield >= 10) {
438:             lastYield = now;
439:             options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: count, total: 0, title: handle.name });
440:             await tick();
441:           }
442:         }
443:       } catch (e) {
444:         alive(options.signal);
445:         if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
446:         source.issues.push({ path: prefix || handle.name, reason: errorMessage(e) });
447:       }
448:     }
449: 
450:     while (active || queue.length) {
451:       alive(options.signal);
452:       await Promise.all(tasks);
453:     }
454:     await warmSources([source], options);
455:     alive(options.signal);
456:     options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: count, total: count, title: handle.name });
457:     return source;
458:   } catch (e) {
459:     queue.length = 0;
460:     await Promise.all(tasks);
461:     discardScan([source]);
462:     throw e;
463:   }
464: }
465: 
466: export function fromFiles(list: File[]): Source[] {
467:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
468:     return [];
469:   }
470:   assertAtMost(list.length, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Selected files');
471:   const groups = new Map<string, Source>();
472:   for (const f of list) {
473:     const parts = (f.webkitRelativePath || f.name).split('/');
474:     const title = parts.length > 1 ? parts.shift()! : 'Selected images';
475:     const path = parts.join('/');
476:     if (ignored(path) || !imageFile(f)) continue;
477:     assertPathLimits(path);
478:     const group = groups.get(title) || { title, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
479:     group.files.set(path, f);
480:     groups.set(title, group);
481:     assertAtMost(group.files.size, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
482:     assertAtMost(groups.size, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
483:   }
484:   const sources = [...groups.values()];
485:   sources.forEach(assertSourceLimits);
486:   return sources;
487: }
488: 
489: export async function fromDesktopFolder(
490:   folderPath: string,
491:   options: ScanOptions = {},
492: ): Promise<Source> {
493:   if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) throw new Error('Desktop API not available.');
494:   void desktopAPI.recordOpenedRoot?.(folderPath);
495:   const folderName = folderPath.split(/[\\/]/).at(-1) ?? folderPath;
496:   const source: Source = { title: folderName, localPath: folderPath, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
497: 
498:   // 1. Prefer native Rust library discovery when available
499:   if (desktopAPI.discoverLibrary) {
500:     try {
501:       const discovery = await withinTime(
502:         desktopAPI.discoverLibrary(folderPath, {
503:           signal: options.signal,
504:           onProgress: (prog) => {
505:             if (prog.total > 0) {
506:               desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
507:             }
508:           },
509:         }),
510:         limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
511:         'Discovering library',
512:         options.signal,
513:       );
514: 
515:       if (discovery && discovery.titles.length > 0) {
516:         source.discoveredTitles = discovery.titles;
517:         let discovered = 0;
518:         for (const title of discovery.titles) {
519:           for (const ch of title.chapters) {
520:             for (const p of ch.pages) {
521:               alive(options.signal);
522:               const relPath =
523:                 discovery.titles.length > 1 ? `${title.title}/${p.relativePath}` : p.relativePath;
524:               assertPathLimits(relPath);
525:               assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
526:               assertAtMost(
527:                 source.files.size + 1,
528:                 limits.pagesPerTitle,
529:                 'Image candidates per title',
530:               );
531:               source.files.set(
532:                 relPath,
533:                 new DesktopHandle(
534:                   p.name,
535:                   p.absolutePath,
536:                   p.size,
537:                   p.modified,
538:                   p.width,
539:                   p.height,
540:                 ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
541:               );
542:             }
543:           }
544:         }
545:         return source;
546:       }
547:     } catch (e) {
548:       if (e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError') {
549:         throw e;
550:       }
551:       if (e instanceof LimitError) {
552:         throw e;
553:       }
554:     }
555:   }
556: 
557:   // 2. Fallback to listFolder
558:   let entries: FolderEntry[];
559:   try {
560:     entries = await withinTime(
561:       desktopAPI.listFolder(folderPath, {
562:         signal: options.signal,
563:         onProgress: (prog) => {
564:           if (prog.total > 0) {
565:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(prog.done / prog.total);
566:           }
567:         },
568:       }),
569:       limits.ioTimeoutMs * 5,
570:       'Listing folder',
571:       options.signal,
572:     );
573:   } catch (e) {
574:     throw new Error(errorMessage(e));
575:   }
576:   let discovered = 0;
577:   for (const entry of entries) {
578:     alive(options.signal);
579:     const relPath = normalizeRelativePath(entry.relativePath);
580:     if (ignored(relPath) || !imageFile({ name: relPath, type: '' })) continue;
581:     try {
582:       assertPathLimits(relPath);
583:       assertAtMost(++discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
584:       assertAtMost(source.files.size + 1, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Image candidates per title');
585:       const name = relPath.split('/').at(-1) ?? relPath;
586:       source.files.set(
587:         relPath,
588:         new DesktopHandle(
589:           name,
590:           entry.absolutePath,
591:           entry.size,
592:           entry.modified,
593:           entry.width,
594:           entry.height,
595:         ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
596:       );
597:     } catch (e) {
598:       if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
599:       source.issues.push({ path: relPath, reason: errorMessage(e) });
600:     }
601:     if (discovered % 256 === 0)
602:       options.update?.({ phase: 'discover', done: source.files.size, total: 0, title: folderName });
603:   }
604:   options.update?.({
605:     phase: 'discover',
606:     done: source.files.size,
607:     total: source.files.size,
608:     title: folderName,
609:   });
610:   await warmSources([source], options);
611:   return source;
612: }
613: 
614: interface Entry {
615:   isFile: boolean;
616:   isDirectory: boolean;
617:   name: string;
618:   file(cb: (f: File) => void, err: (e: Error) => void): void;
619:   createReader(): { readEntries(cb: (e: Entry[]) => void, err: (e: Error) => void): void };
620: }
621: 
622: export function droppedSources(
623:   items: DataTransferItem[],
624:   options: ScanOptions,
625: ): () => Promise<Source[]> {
626:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
627:     return async () => [];
628:   }
629:   // Reject asynchronously: App.drop owns cleanup in the returned promise chain.
630:   if (items.length > limits.discoveryEntries)
631:     return async () => {
632:       throw new LimitError(`Dropped items exceed the limit of ${limits.discoveryEntries}.`);
633:     };
634:   // Snapshot entries and start handle requests while the drop event is active.
635:   const entries = items
636:     .filter((i) => i.kind === 'file')
637:     .map((item) => {
638:       const typed = item as DataTransferItem & {
639:         getAsFileSystemHandle?: () => Promise<FileSystemHandle | null>;
640:       };
641:       return {
642:         entry: item.webkitGetAsEntry?.() as unknown as Entry | null,
643:         file: item.getAsFile(),
644:         handle: typed.getAsFileSystemHandle
645:           ? withinTime(
646:             typed.getAsFileSystemHandle(),
647:             limits.ioTimeoutMs,
648:             'Reading dropped handle',
649:             options.signal,
650:           ).catch(() => null)
651:           : undefined,
652:       };
653:     });
654:   return async () => {
655:     let discovered = 0;
656:     const sources: Source[] = [],
657:       loose: File[] = [];
658:     for (const item of entries) {
659:       alive(options.signal);
660:       assertAtMost(sources.length + 1, limits.titles, 'Dropped titles');
661:       const handle = await item.handle;
662:       if (handle?.kind === 'directory') {
663:         sources.push(await fromHandle(handle as Directory, options));
664:         assertSourcesLimits(sources);
665:         continue;
666:       }
667:       if (item.entry?.isDirectory) {
668:         const files = new Map<string, SourceFile>(),
669:           issues: Issue[] = [];
670:         async function walk(e: Entry, path: string, depth: number) {
671:           alive(options.signal);
672:           if (ignored(path)) return;
673:           // Count directories, not the leaf file (which is one recursion level deeper).
674:           if (!e.isFile && depth > limits.directoryDepth) {
675:             issues.push({
676:               path,
677:               reason: `Folder nesting exceeds ${limits.directoryDepth} levels.`,
678:             });
679:             return;
680:           }
681:           try {
682:             assertAtMost(path.length, limits.pathChars, 'Relative path length (characters)');
683:             if (e.isFile) {
684:               if (imageFile({ name: e.name, type: '' })) {
685:                 assertPathLimits(path);
686:                 assertAtMost(
687:                   files.size + (files.has(path) ? 0 : 1),
688:                   limits.pagesPerTitle,
689:                   'Image candidates per title',
690:                 );
691:                 files.set(
692:                   path,
693:                   await withinTime(
694:                     new Promise<File>((r, j) => e.file(r, j)),
695:                     limits.ioTimeoutMs,
696:                     'Reading dropped file',
697:                     options.signal,
698:                   ),
699:                 );
700:               }
701:               return;
702:             }
703:             const reader = e.createReader();
704:             while (true) {
705:               alive(options.signal);
706:               const list = await withinTime(
707:                 new Promise<Entry[]>((r, j) => reader.readEntries(r, j)),
708:                 limits.ioTimeoutMs,
709:                 'Reading dropped folder',
710:                 options.signal,
711:               );
712:               if (!list.length) break;
713:               discovered += list.length;
714:               assertAtMost(discovered, limits.discoveryEntries, 'Discovered entries');
715:               for (const child of list.sort((a, b) => naturalOrder(a.name, b.name)))
716:                 await walk(child, path ? path + '/' + child.name : child.name, depth + 1);
717:               await warmSources([{ title: item.entry?.name || 'Manga', files, issues }], options);
718:               options.update?.({
719:                 phase: 'discover',
720:                 done: files.size,
721:                 total: 0,
722:                 title: item.entry?.name,
723:               });
724:               await tick();
725:             }
726:           } catch (e) {
727:             alive(options.signal);
728:             if (e instanceof LimitError) throw e;
729:             issues.push({ path, reason: errorMessage(e) });
730:           }
731:         }
732:         await walk(item.entry, '', 0);
733:         sources.push({ title: item.entry.name, files, issues });
734:         assertSourcesLimits(sources);
735:       } else if (item.file) loose.push(item.file);
736:     }
737:     sources.push(...fromFiles(loose));
738:     assertSourcesLimits(sources);
739:     return sources;
740:   };
741: }
742: 
743: export async function splitSource(source: Source): Promise<Source[]> {
744:   assertSourceLimits(source);
745:   if (source.discoveredTitles && source.discoveredTitles.length > 1) {
746:     const splitTitles: Source[] = [];
747:     for (const dt of source.discoveredTitles) {
748:       const s: Source = {
749:         title: dt.title,
750:         localPath: dt.rootPath,
751:         files: new Map(),
752:         issues: [],
753:       };
754:       for (const ch of dt.chapters) {
755:         for (const p of ch.pages) {
756:           s.files.set(
757:             p.relativePath,
758:             new DesktopHandle(
759:               p.name,
760:               p.absolutePath,
761:               p.size,
762:               p.modified,
763:               p.width,
764:               p.height,
765:             ) as unknown as FileSystemFileHandle,
766:           );
767:         }
768:       }
769:       splitTitles.push(s);
770:       assertAtMost(splitTitles.length, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
771:     }
772:     return splitTitles.length > 0 ? splitTitles : [source];
773:   }
774: 
775:   const result = new Map<string, Source>();
776:   const sep = source.localPath?.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
777:   for (const [path, file] of source.files) {
778:     const parts = path.split('/');
779:     const title = parts.length > 1 ? parts.shift()! : source.title + ' — root pages';
780:     let s = result.get(title);
781:     if (!s) {
782:       const childLocalPath =
783:         source.localPath && title !== source.title + ' — root pages'
784:           ? `${source.localPath.replace(/[\\/]+$/, '')}${sep}${title}`
785:           : source.localPath;
786:       s = { title, localPath: childLocalPath, files: new Map(), issues: [] };
787:       result.set(title, s);
788:       assertAtMost(result.size, limits.titles, 'Selected titles');
789:     }
790:     s.files.set(parts.join('/'), file);
791:   }
792:   for (const s of result.values())
793:     if (source.handle && s.title !== source.title + ' — root pages') {
794:       try {
795:         s.handle = (await withinTime(
796:           source.handle.getDirectoryHandle(s.title),
797:           limits.ioTimeoutMs,
798:           'Opening subfolder',
799:         )) as Directory;
800:       } catch (e) {
801:         s.issues.push({ path: s.title, reason: errorMessage(e) });
802:       }
803:     }
804:   return [...result.values()];
805: }
806: 
807: export function chapterGroups(source: Source) {
808:   const groups = new Map<string, number>();
809:   for (const path of source.files.keys()) {
810:     const parts = path.split('/');
811:     const name = parts.length === 1 ? 'Root pages' : parts.slice(0, -1).join(' / ');
812:     if (parts.length === 1 && /^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path) && source.files.size > 1) continue;
813:     groups.set(name, (groups.get(name) || 0) + 1);
814:   }
815:   return groups;
816: }
817: 
818: export function chapterPreviews(source: Source): ChapterPreview[] {
819:   const groups = chapterGroups(source);
820:   return Array.from(groups.entries(), ([name, pageCount]) => ({ name, pageCount }));
821: }
822: 
823: function chapterMatch(name: string, pages: Page[], old: Manga | undefined) {
824:   const exact = old?.chapters.find((c) => c.name === name);
825:   if (exact) return exact;
826:   const signature = (p: Page) => `${p.name}\0${p.size ?? ''}\0${p.modified ?? ''}`;
827:   const values = new Set(pages.map(signature));
828:   const matches = old?.chapters.filter(
829:     (c) => c.pages.length === pages.length && c.pages.every((p) => values.has(signature(p))),
830:   );
831:   return matches?.length === 1 ? matches[0] : undefined;
832: }
833: 
834: export async function pooled<T>(
835:   items: T[],
836:   work: (item: T) => Promise<void>,
837:   signal?: AbortSignal,
838: ) {
839:   let next = 0;
840:   const results = await Promise.allSettled(
841:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(getDecodeConcurrency(), items.length) }, async () => {
842:       while (next < items.length) {
843:         alive(signal);
844:         await work(items[next++]);
845:       }
846:     }),
847:   );
848:   const failure = results.find((r) => r.status === 'rejected');
849:   if (failure?.status === 'rejected') throw failure.reason;
850: }
851: 
852: export async function indexSource(
853:   source: Source,
854:   options: ScanOptions = {},
855:   previous?: Manga,
856:   sort: Manga['sort'] = 'natural',
857: ) {
858:   assertSourceLimits(source);
859:   if (previous) stopBackground(previous.id);
860: 
861:   // High-performance native Rust indexing on desktop
862:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.indexTitle && source.localPath) {
863:     alive(options.signal);
864:     const confirmedTitle = source.title?.trim() || undefined;
865:     const jobId = `index-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
866: 
867:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
868:     if (options.signal) {
869:       abortHandler = () => {
870:         void desktopAPI.cancelScan?.(jobId);
871:       };
872:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
873:     }
874: 
875:     try {
876:       const dto = await desktopAPI.indexTitle(
877:         source.localPath,
878:         {
879:           sort,
880:           previous,
881:           targetId: previous?.id,
882:           title: confirmedTitle,
883:           jobId,
884:           signal: options.signal,
885:         },
886:         (progress) => {
887:           alive(options.signal);
888:           options.update?.({
889:             phase: progress.phase as 'discover' | 'index',
890:             done: progress.done,
891:             total: progress.total,
892:             title: confirmedTitle || progress.title,
893:           });
894:           if (progress.total > 0) {
895:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress.done / progress.total)));
896:           }
897:         },
898:       );
899:       alive(options.signal);
900: 
901:       const m: Manga = {
902:         id: dto.id,
903:         title: confirmedTitle || dto.title,
904:         slug: dto.slug,
905:         localPath: dto.localPath,
906:         lastChapter: dto.lastChapter,
907:         addedAt: dto.addedAt,
908:         updatedAt: dto.updatedAt,
909:         revision: dto.revision,
910:         sort: dto.sort as 'natural' | 'name' | 'reverse',
911:         chapters: (dto.chapters || []).map((c: any) => ({
912:           ...(c.id ? { id: c.id } : {}),
913:           name: c.name,
914:           read: c.read,
915:           position: c.position,
916:           bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
917:           pages: (c.pages || []).map((p: any) => ({
918:             name: p.name,
919:             path: p.path,
920:             width: p.width,
921:             height: p.height,
922:             size: p.size,
923:             modified: p.modified,
924:             dimensionsPending: p.dimensionsPending,
925:           })),
926:         })),
927:       };
928: 
929:       alive(options.signal);
930:       invalidateSummaryCache();
931:       invalidateBookmarkCache();
932:       broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id: m.id });
933: 
934:       return {
935:         m,
936:         issues: [...source.issues, ...dto.issues],
937:         reused: dto.reused,
938:       };
939:     } finally {
940:       if (abortHandler && options.signal) {
941:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
942:       }
943:     }
944:   }
945: 
946:   const issues = [...source.issues];
947:   const grouped = new Map<string, Page[]>();
948:   const oldPages = new Map(
949:     previous?.chapters.flatMap((c) => c.pages.map((p) => [p.path, p] as const)) || [],
950:   );
951:   let reused = 0;
952:   const compare =
953:     sort === 'name'
954:       ? lexical.compare
955:       : sort === 'reverse'
956:         ? (a: string, b: string) => naturalOrder(b, a)
957:         : naturalOrder;
958:   for (const [path, entry] of source.files) {
959:     alive(options.signal);
960:     if (!path.includes('/') && /^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(path) && source.files.size > 1)
961:       continue;
962:     const parts = path.split('/');
963:     const name = parts.length === 1 ? 'Root pages' : parts.slice(0, -1).join(' / ');
964:     const cached = oldPages.get(path);
965:     const desktop = entry instanceof DesktopHandle ? entry : undefined;
966:     const known = entry instanceof File ? entry : desktop;
967:     const knownModified = entry instanceof File ? entry.lastModified : desktop?.lastModified;
968:     const hasDesktopDim = Boolean(desktop?.width && desktop?.height);
969:     const unchanged = Boolean(
970:       known &&
971:       cached &&
972:       !cached.dimensionsPending &&
973:       cached.size === known.size &&
974:       cached.modified === knownModified,
975:     );
976:     const pages = grouped.get(name) || [];
977:     pages.push({
978:       name: parts.at(-1)!,
979:       path,
980:       width: desktop?.width ?? cached?.width ?? 800,
981:       height: desktop?.height ?? cached?.height ?? 1200,
982:       size: known?.size ?? cached?.size,
983:       modified: knownModified ?? cached?.modified,
984:       dimensionsPending: !unchanged && !hasDesktopDim,
985:     });
986:     grouped.set(name, pages);
987:   }
988:   const naturalSort = desktopAPI.isDesktop ? desktopAPI.naturalSortPaths : undefined;
989:   let chapterNames = [...grouped.keys()];
990:   if (naturalSort && sort !== 'name') {
991:     chapterNames = await naturalSort(chapterNames);
992:     if (sort === 'reverse') chapterNames.reverse();
993:   } else {
994:     chapterNames.sort(compare);
995:   }
996:   const first = chapterNames.slice(0, limits.warmChapters);
997:   const initial = first.flatMap((name) => grouped.get(name)!);
998:   let done = 0;
999:   let lastYield = performance.now();
1000:   options.update?.({
1001:     phase: 'index',
1002:     done,
1003:     total: initial.length,
1004:     title: source.title + ' · first 5 chapters',
1005:   });
1006:   const failed = new Set<string>();
1007:   await pooled(
1008:     initial,
1009:     async (page) => {
1010:       try {
1011:         const entry = source.files.get(page.path)!;
1012:         const desktop = entry instanceof DesktopHandle ? entry : undefined;
1013:         if (desktop?.width && desktop?.height && !page.dimensionsPending) {
1014:           assertImageDimensions(desktop.width, desktop.height);
1015:           done++;

1017:         }
1018:         if (desktop && desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.getImageDimensions) {
1019:           const dim = await desktopAPI.getImageDimensions(desktop.absPath);

1030:             return;

1045:           cached.modified === f.lastModified

1047:           size = cached;

1053:           height: size.height,

1055:           modified: f.lastModified,

1062:       }

1068:           phase: 'index',
1069:           done,
1070:           total: initial.length,
1071:           title: source.title + ' · first 5 chapters',
1072:         });

1080:     if (kept.length) grouped.set(name, kept);
1081:     else grouped.delete(name);

1087:         return sort === 'reverse' ? names.reverse() : names;

1092:     sortedNames.map(async (name) => {

1097:         const sortedPageNames = await naturalSort(pageNames);
1098:         if (sort === 'reverse') sortedPageNames.reverse();

1100:         pages = sortedPageNames.map((n) => pMap.get(n)!).filter(Boolean);

1125:     .find(([p]) => !p.includes('/') && /^(thumbnail|cover)\./i.test(p))?.[1];

1127:   const mangaId = previous?.id || generateUUID();

1129:   // Desktop optimization (C2): Generate thumbnail in Rust via image crate + fast_image_resize directly from disk

1141:       // Thumbnail generated and saved atomically on disk under cache/thumbnails/{mangaId}.webp
1142:       // Cover Blob is left undefined to prevent memory retention and JS-to-Rust IPC upload
1143:     } catch {
1144:       // Fallback to JS canvas thumbnail path
1145:     }

1148:   // Web fallback: ImageBitmap and canvas path only for web builds

1154:     } catch {

1176:     handle: source.handle,
1177:     localPath: source.localPath, // persisted so native desktop can re-access files after restart
1178:     cover,

1180:     addedAt: previous?.addedAt || Date.now(),
