1194: export async function prepareChapter(m: Manga, index: number, options: PrepareChapterOptions = {}) {
1195:   const chapter = m.chapters[index];
1196:   if (!chapter) return [];
1197:   const database = typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined' ? await db : undefined;
1198:   const cached = database ? ((await database.get('manga', m.id)) as Manga | undefined) : undefined;
1199:   alive(options.signal);
1200:   const savedChapter =
1201:     cached?.updatedAt === m.updatedAt || cached?.chapters[index]?.name === chapter.name
1202:       ? cached.chapters[index]
1203:       : undefined;
1204:   const savedMap = new Map(savedChapter?.pages.map((p) => [p.path, p]));
1205:   const pages = chapter.pages.map((p) => {
1206:     const hit = savedMap.get(p.path);
1207:     if (hit && !hit.dimensionsPending) {
1208:       sessionDimensions.set(`${m.id}:${p.path}`, { width: hit.width, height: hit.height });
1209:       return { ...hit };
1210:     }
1211:     const sessionHit = sessionDimensions.get(`${m.id}:${p.path}`);
1212:     if (sessionHit) {
1213:       return { ...p, ...sessionHit, dimensionsPending: false };
1214:     }
1215:     return { ...p };
1216:   });
1217:   const pending = pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending);
1218:   if (!pending.length) {
1219:     m.chapters[index] = { ...chapter, pages };
1220:     return [];
1221:   }
1222: 
1223:   // Batch dimension extraction via native Rust prepareChapterHeaders on desktop
1224:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders && m.localPath) {
1225:     const sep = m.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
1226:     const pathMap = new Map<string, Page>();
1227:     const absPaths: string[] = [];
1228:     for (const page of pending) {
1229:       const absPath = m.localPath + sep + page.path.split('/').join(sep);
1230:       pathMap.set(absPath, page);
1231:       absPaths.push(absPath);
1232:     }
1233:     try {
1234:       const headers = await desktopAPI.prepareChapterHeaders(absPaths, m.id);
1235:       for (const h of headers) {
1236:         const page = pathMap.get(h.path);
1237:         if (page && h.width > 0 && h.height > 0) {
1238:           assertImageDimensions(h.width, h.height);
1239:           page.width = h.width;
1240:           page.height = h.height;
1241:           page.size = h.size;
1242:           page.modified = h.modified;
1243:           page.dimensionsPending = false;
1244:           sessionDimensions.set(`${m.id}:${page.path}`, { width: h.width, height: h.height });
1245:         }
1246:       }
1247:     } catch {
1248:       // Fall through to per-page resolution
1249:     }
1250:   }
1251: 
1252:   const remainingPending = pages.filter((p) => p.dimensionsPending);
1253:   if (!remainingPending.length) {
1254:     alive(options.signal);
1255:     m.chapters[index] = { ...chapter, pages };
1256:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1257:       await saveManga(m);
1258:     } else if (database) {
1259:       const tx = database.transaction('manga', 'readwrite');
1260:       const stored = (await tx.store.get(m.id)) as unknown as Manga | undefined;
1261:       if (
1262:         stored &&
1263:         stored.updatedAt === m.updatedAt &&
1264:         (m.revision === undefined || stored.revision === m.revision) &&
1265:         stored.chapters[index]?.name === chapter.name
1266:       ) {
1267:         stored.chapters[index].pages = pages;
1268:         await tx.store.put(stored as unknown as Omit<Manga, 'cover'>);
1269:       }
1270:       await tx.done;
1271:     }
1272:     return [];
1273:   }
1274: 
1275:   const issues: Issue[] = [];
1276:   let done = 0;
1277:   let lastYield = performance.now();
1278:   await pooled(
1279:     remainingPending,
1280:     async (page) => {
1281:       try {
1282:         if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.getImageDimensions && m.localPath) {
1283:           const sep = m.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
1284:           const absPath = m.localPath + sep + page.path.split('/').join(sep);
1285:           const dim = await desktopAPI.getImageDimensions(absPath);
1286:           if (dim && dim.width > 0 && dim.height > 0) {
1287:             assertImageDimensions(dim.width, dim.height);
1288:             Object.assign(page, dim, { dimensionsPending: false });
1289:             sessionDimensions.set(`${m.id}:${page.path}`, {
1290:               width: dim.width,
1291:               height: dim.height,
1292:             });
1293:             done++;
1294:             const now = performance.now();
1295:             if (done % 8 === 0 || done === pending.length || now - lastYield >= 10) {
1296:               lastYield = now;
1297:               options.update?.({
1298:                 phase: 'index',
1299:                 done,
1300:                 total: pending.length,
1301:                 title: chapter.name,
1302:               });
1303:               await tick();
1304:             }
1305:             return;
1306:           }
1307:         }
1308:         const file = await fileFor(m, page);
1309:         alive(options.signal);
1310:         if (!file.size) throw new Error('Empty file.');
1311:         const size = await dimensions(file, options.signal);
1312:         Object.assign(page, size, {
1313:           size: file.size,
1314:           modified: file.lastModified,
1315:           dimensionsPending: false,
1316:         });

1318:       } catch (e) {
1319:         alive(options.signal);
1320:         issues.push({ path: page.path, reason: errorMessage(e) });
1321:       }
1322:       done++;
1323:       const now = performance.now();
1324:       if (done % 8 === 0 || done === pending.length || now - lastYield >= 10) {
1325:         lastYield = now;
1326:         options.update?.({ phase: 'index', done, total: pending.length, title: chapter.name });
1327:         await tick();
1328:       }
1329:     },
1330:     options.signal,
1331:   );
1332:   alive(options.signal);
1333:   m.chapters[index] = { ...chapter, pages };
1334:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1335:     await saveManga(m);
1336:   } else if (database) {
1337:     const tx = database.transaction('manga', 'readwrite');
1338:     const stored = (await tx.store.get(m.id)) as unknown as Manga | undefined;
1339:     if (
1340:       stored &&
1341:       stored.updatedAt === m.updatedAt &&
1342:       (m.revision === undefined || stored.revision === m.revision) &&
1343:       stored.chapters[index]?.name === chapter.name
1344:     ) {
1345:       stored.chapters[index].pages = pages;
1346:       await tx.store.put(stored as unknown as Omit<Manga, 'cover'>);
1347:     }
1348:     await tx.done;
1349:   }
1350:   return issues;
1351: }

1353: interface BackgroundJob {
1354:   id: string;
1355:   revision?: number;
1356:   updatedAt: number;
1357:   controller: AbortController;
1358: }
1359: 
1360: const backgroundJobs = new Map<string, BackgroundJob>();
1361: let backgroundRunning = false;
1362: 
1363: export function stopBackground(id: string) {
1364:   backgroundJobs.get(id)?.controller.abort();
1365:   backgroundJobs.delete(id);
1366: }
1367: 
1368: export function stopAllBackground() {
1369:   for (const job of backgroundJobs.values()) {
1370:     job.controller.abort();
1371:   }
1372:   backgroundJobs.clear();
1373: }
1374: 
1375: // Subscribe to manga deletions so jobs are cleanly halted
1376: onMangaDelete((id) => {
1377:   if (id) stopBackground(id);
1378:   else stopAllBackground();
1379: });

1381: export function scheduleBackground(manga: Manga) {
1382:   if (
1383:     backgroundJobs.has(manga.id) ||
1384:     !manga.chapters.some((c) => c.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending))
1385:   )
1386:     return;
1387:   // Overflow stays pending in IndexedDB; opening that chapter prepares it on demand.
1388:   if (backgroundJobs.size >= limits.queuedJobs) return;
1389:   backgroundJobs.set(manga.id, {
1390:     id: manga.id,
1391:     revision: manga.revision,
1392:     updatedAt: manga.updatedAt,
1393:     controller: new AbortController(),
1394:   });
1395:   void drainBackground();
1396: }
1397: 
1398: async function runDrainPass() {
1399:   let remaining = backgroundJobs.size; // New arrivals run in a later bounded pass.
1400:   while (backgroundJobs.size && remaining-- > 0) {
1401:     const [id, job] = backgroundJobs.entries().next().value!;
1402:     try {
1403:       const initial = await getManga(id);
1404:       if (
1405:         !initial ||
1406:         initial.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
1407:         (job.revision !== undefined && initial.revision !== job.revision)
1408:       ) {
1409:         continue;
1410:       }
1411:       for (let i = 0; i < initial.chapters.length; i++) {
1412:         alive(job.controller.signal);
1413:         if (!initial.chapters[i].pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) continue;
1414:         await new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
1415:         alive(job.controller.signal);
1416:         if (!(await access(initial))) break;
1417:         const stored = await getManga(id);
1418:         if (
1419:           !stored ||
1420:           stored.updatedAt !== job.updatedAt ||
1421:           (job.revision !== undefined && stored.revision !== job.revision)
1422:         ) {
1423:           break;
1424:         }
1425:         const issues = await prepareChapter(stored, i, { signal: job.controller.signal });
1426:         job.updatedAt = stored.updatedAt;
1427:         job.revision = stored.revision;
1428:         if (issues.length > 0 && stored.chapters[i]?.pages.some((p) => p.dimensionsPending)) {
1429:           break;
1430:         }
1431:       }
1432:     } catch {
1433:       /* Interrupted jobs remain pending in saved metadata and can resume later. */
1434:     } finally {
1435:       if (backgroundJobs.get(id) === job) backgroundJobs.delete(id);
1436:     }
1437:   }
1438: }
1439: 
1440: async function drainBackground() {
1441:   if (backgroundRunning) return;
1442:   backgroundRunning = true;
1443:   try {
1444:     if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'locks' in navigator && navigator.locks?.request) {
1445:       await navigator.locks.request('manga-reader:bg-prep', { ifAvailable: true }, async (lock) => {
1446:         if (!lock) return;
1447:         await runDrainPass();
1448:       });
1449:     } else {
1450:       await runDrainPass();
1451:     }
1452:   } finally {
1453:     backgroundRunning = false;
1454:     if (backgroundJobs.size) setTimeout(() => void drainBackground(), 0);
1455:   }
1456: }
1457: 
1458: export async function resumeBackground() {
1459:   for (const item of await listLibrary()) {
1460:     const m = await getManga(item.id);
1461:     if (m && (await access(m))) scheduleBackground(m);
1462:   }
1463: }

86: export const syncChannel =
87:   typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined' ? new BroadcastChannel('manga-reader:sync') : null;
88: 
89: const localSyncListeners = new Set<(msg: StorageSyncMessage) => void>();
90: 
91: export function onStorageSync(listener: (msg: StorageSyncMessage) => void): () => void {
92:   localSyncListeners.add(listener);
93:   return () => {
94:     localSyncListeners.delete(listener);
95:   };
96: }
97: 
98: export function broadcastSync(msg: StorageSyncMessage): void {
99:   // 1. Dispatch locally to all registered store subscribers in the current window
100:   for (const listener of localSyncListeners) {
101:     try {
102:       listener(msg);
103:     } catch (e) {
104:       console.warn('Error in local storage sync listener:', e);
105:     }
106:   }
107: 
108:   // 2. Dispatch synthetic MessageEvent on local syncChannel so local event listeners receive it
109:   if (syncChannel && typeof MessageEvent !== 'undefined') {
110:     try {
111:       syncChannel.dispatchEvent(new MessageEvent('message', { data: msg }));
112:     } catch {
113:       // Ignore dispatch failure if environment does not support synthetic MessageEvent
114:     }
115:   }
116: 
117:   // 3. Dispatch across contexts / other windows via native BroadcastChannel
118:   try {
119:     syncChannel?.postMessage(msg);
120:   } catch {
121:     // Ignore postMessage failure in disconnected or restricted frames
122:   }
123: }

145: syncChannel?.addEventListener('message', () => {
146:   cachedSummaries = null;
147:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
148:   clearDirHandleCache();
149: });
150: 
151: export const summary = (m: Manga): Summary => {
152:   const { chapters, cover: _cover, ...rest } = m;
153: 
154:   return {
155:     ...rest,
156:     chapterCount: chapters.length,
157:     chaptersRead: chapters.filter((c) => c.read).length,
158:     lastChapterName: chapters[m.lastChapter]?.name || '',
159:     hasProgress: chapters.some((c) => Boolean(c.position)),
160:     indexedAt: m.updatedAt,
161:     revision: m.revision ?? 1,
162:   };
163: };
164: 
165: export async function migrateV1ToV2(tx: any, _d?: IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>): Promise<void> {
166:   let cursor = await tx.objectStore('manga').openCursor();
167:   while (cursor) {
168:     const m = cursor.value as unknown as Manga;
169:     await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(summary(m));
170:     if (m.cover) await tx.objectStore('covers').put(m.cover, m.id);
171:     for (const c of m.chapters) {
172:       await tx.objectStore('progress').put({
173:         mangaId: m.id,
174:         chapter: c.name,
175:         read: c.read,
176:         position: c.position,
177:         bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
178:       });
179:     }
180:     const { cover: _cover, ...metadata } = m;
181:     await cursor.update({
182:       ...metadata,
183:       chapters: m.chapters.map(({ position: _p, bookmarks: _b, ...c }) => ({
184:         ...c,
185:         read: false,
186:       })),
187:     });
188:     cursor = await cursor.continue();
189:   }
190: }
191: 
192: let dbInstance: Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>> | null = null;
193: 
194: function getWebDb(): Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>> {
195:   if (!dbInstance) {
196:     dbInstance =
197:       typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined'
198:         ? openDB<MangaDB>('manga-reader', 2, {
199:           async upgrade(d, old, _new, tx) {
200:             if (old < 1) {
201:               d.createObjectStore('manga', { keyPath: 'id' });
202:               d.createObjectStore('settings');
203:             }
204:             if (old < 2) {
205:               d.createObjectStore('summaries', { keyPath: 'id' });
206:               d.createObjectStore('covers');
207:               const p = d.createObjectStore('progress', { keyPath: ['mangaId', 'chapter'] });
208:               p.createIndex('mangaId', 'mangaId');
209:               try {
210:                 await migrateV1ToV2(tx, d);
211:               } catch (err) {
212:                 console.error('[db upgrade] Migration v1->v2 failed, aborting upgrade:', err);
213:                 tx.abort();
214:                 throw err;
215:               }
216:             }
217:           },
218:           blocking() {
219:             void dbInstance?.then((d) => d.close());
220:           },
221:           blocked() {
222:             if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
223:               window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('reader-storage-blocked'));
224:             }
225:           },
226:         })
227:         : (new Promise(() => { }) as unknown as Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>>);
228:   }
229:   return dbInstance;
230: }
231: 
232: export const db: Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>> = desktopAPI.isDesktop
233:   ? ({
234:     then: (onfulfilled?: any, onrejected?: any) =>
235:       getWebDb().then(onfulfilled, onrejected),
236:   } as Promise<IDBPDatabase<MangaDB>>)
237:   : getWebDb();

239: export const listLibraryIdb = async (): Promise<Summary[]> => {
240:   if (cachedSummaries !== null) {
241:     return cachedSummaries.slice();
242:   }
243:   if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
244:     return [];
245:   }
246:   const database = await db;
247:   const rawEntries = (await database.getAll('summaries')) as unknown[];
248:   const entries: Summary[] = [];
249:   let needsSlugs = false;
250:   for (const raw of rawEntries) {
251:     const s = parseSummary(raw, { preserveHardwarePointers: true });
252:     if (s) {
253:       if (!s.slug) needsSlugs = true;
254:       entries.push(s);
255:     }
256:   }
257:   if (needsSlugs) {
258:     const tx = database.transaction(['manga', 'summaries'], 'readwrite');
259:     const used = new Set(entries.map((m) => m.slug).filter((s): s is string => Boolean(s)));
260:     for (const m of entries.sort((a, b) => a.addedAt - b.addedAt || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))) {
261:       if (!m.slug) {
262:         m.slug = uniqueSlug(m.title, used);
263:         used.add(m.slug);
264:         await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(m);
265:         const stored = await tx.objectStore('manga').get(m.id);
266:         if (stored && isRecord(stored)) {
267:           await tx.objectStore('manga').put({ ...stored, slug: m.slug });
268:         }
269:       }
270:     }
271:     await tx.done;
272:     cachedSummaries = null;
273:     invalidateBookmarkCache();
274:   }
275:   entries.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
276:   cachedSummaries = entries;
277:   return entries.slice();
278: };

280: export async function getCoverIdb(id: string): Promise<Blob | undefined> {
281:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.getThumbnail) {
282:     try {
283:       const buf = await desktopAPI.getThumbnail(id);
284:       if (buf && buf.byteLength > 0) {
285:         return new Blob([buf], { type: 'image/webp' });
286:       }
287:     } catch (err) {
288:       console.warn(`[getCover] Failed to read native thumbnail for "${id}":`, err);
289:     }
290:   }
291: 
292:   // Fallback to IndexedDB
293:   if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
294:     return undefined;
295:   }
296: 
297:   try {
298:     const d = await db;
299:     const blob = await d.get('covers', id);
300:     if (blob instanceof Blob) {
301:       if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveThumbnail) {
302:         void blob
303:           .arrayBuffer()
304:           .then((ab) => desktopAPI.saveThumbnail?.(id, ab))
305:           .catch(() => { });
306:       }
307:       return blob;
308:     }
309:   } catch (err) {
310:     console.warn(`[getCover] Failed to read IndexedDB cover for "${id}":`, err);
311:   }
312:   return undefined;
313: }

315: export async function getMangaIdb(id: string): Promise<Manga | undefined> {
316:   const d = await db;
317:   const raw = await d.get('manga', id);
318:   if (!raw) return undefined;
319:   let m: Manga;
320:   try {
321:     m = parseManga(raw);
322:   } catch (err) {
323:     console.warn(`[getManga] Malformed metadata for title "${id}":`, err);
324:     return undefined;
325:   }
326:   const [entries, s, cover] = await Promise.all([
327:     d.getAllFromIndex('progress', 'mangaId', id),
328:     d.get('summaries', id),
329:     getCoverIdb(id),
330:   ]);
331:   const byName = new Map(
332:     (entries as Progress[])
333:       .filter((p) => p && typeof p.chapter === 'string')
334:       .map((p) => [p.chapter, p]),
335:   );
336:   return {
337:     ...m,
338:     cover: cover instanceof Blob ? cover : m.cover,
339:     lastChapter: s && Number.isInteger(s.lastChapter) ? s.lastChapter : m.lastChapter,
340:     updatedAt: m.updatedAt,
341:     chapters: m.chapters.map((c) => {
342:       const p = byName.get(c.name);
343:       return {
344:         ...c,
345:         read: typeof p?.read === 'boolean' ? p.read : c.read,
346:         position: p?.position ? parsePosition(p.position, c.pages) : undefined,
347:         bookmarks: Array.isArray(p?.bookmarks)
348:           ? p.bookmarks
349:             .map((bm) => parsePosition(bm, c.pages))
350:             .filter((bm): bm is Position => Boolean(bm))
351:           : c.bookmarks || [],
352:       };
353:     }),
354:   };
355: }
356: 
357: export async function allMangaIdb(): Promise<Manga[]> {
358:   return (await Promise.all((await listLibraryIdb()).map((m) => getMangaIdb(m.id)))).filter(
359:     (m): m is Manga => Boolean(m),
360:   );
361: }

363: export async function saveMangaIdb(m: Manga): Promise<void> {
364:   assertMangaLimits(m);
365:   const updatedAt = Date.now();
366:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['manga', 'summaries', 'progress', 'covers'], 'readwrite');
367:   const existing = (await tx.objectStore('summaries').get(m.id)) as Summary | undefined;
368:   if (!existing && (await tx.objectStore('summaries').count()) >= limits.titles) {
369:     tx.abort();
370:     await tx.done.catch(() => { }); // Expected rejection from our explicit abort.
371:     throw new LimitError(
372:       `Library title limit is ${limits.titles}. Remove a title before importing.`,
373:     );
374:   }
375:   const revision = (existing?.revision ?? 0) + 1;
376:   const entries = (await tx.objectStore('summaries').getAll()) as Summary[];
377:   const slug =
378:     existing?.slug ||
379:     uniqueSlug(m.title, new Set(entries.filter((x) => x.id !== m.id).map((x) => x.slug || '')));
380:   const { cover, ...metadata } = m;
381:   await tx.objectStore('manga').put({
382:     ...metadata,
383:     updatedAt,
384:     revision,
385:     slug,
386:     chapters: m.chapters.map(({ position: _p, bookmarks: _b, ...c }) => ({ ...c, read: false })),
387:   });
388:   await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(summary({ ...m, updatedAt, revision, slug }));
389:   if (cover) await tx.objectStore('covers').put(cover, m.id);
390:   const progressStore = tx.objectStore('progress');
391:   const keys = await progressStore.index('mangaId').getAllKeys(m.id);
392:   await Promise.all(keys.map((k) => progressStore.delete(k)));
393:   await Promise.all(
394:     m.chapters.map((c) =>
395:       progressStore.put({
396:         mangaId: m.id,
397:         chapter: c.name,
398:         read: c.read,
399:         position: c.position,
400:         bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
401:       }),
402:     ),
403:   );
404:   await tx.done;
405:   m.updatedAt = updatedAt;
406:   m.revision = revision;
407:   m.slug = slug;
408:   cachedSummaries = null;
409:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
410:   clearDirHandleCache(m.id);
411: 
412:   if (cover && desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveThumbnail) {
413:     try {
414:       const buffer = await cover.arrayBuffer();
415:       await desktopAPI.saveThumbnail(m.id, buffer);
416:     } catch (err) {
417:       console.warn(`[saveManga] Failed to cache thumbnail to disk for "${m.id}":`, err);
418:     }
419:   }
420: 
421:   broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id: m.id });
422: }
423: 
424: export type SaveProgressResult = 'saved' | 'stale' | 'missing';
425: 
426: export async function saveProgressIdb(
427:   m: Manga,
428:   index: number,
429:   position: Position,
430:   read: boolean,
431:   bookmarks?: Position[],
432:   updateLast = true,
433: ): Promise<SaveProgressResult> {
434:   const chapter = m.chapters[index];
435:   if (!chapter) return 'missing';
436:   if (bookmarks)
437:     assertAtMost(bookmarks.length, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
438:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['progress', 'summaries'], 'readwrite');
439:   const s = (await tx.objectStore('summaries').get(m.id)) as Summary | undefined;
440:   if (!s) {
441:     await tx.done;
442:     return 'missing';
443:   }
444:   if (s.indexedAt !== m.updatedAt || (m.revision && s.revision && s.revision !== m.revision)) {
445:     await tx.done;
446:     return 'stale';
447:   }
448:   const key: [string, string] = [m.id, chapter.name];
449:   const old = (await tx.objectStore('progress').get(key)) as Progress | undefined;
450:   const isRead = Boolean(old?.read || read);
451:   await tx.objectStore('progress').put({
452:     mangaId: m.id,
453:     chapter: chapter.name,
454:     read: isRead,
455:     position,
456:     bookmarks: bookmarks ?? old?.bookmarks ?? [],
457:   });
458:   await tx.objectStore('summaries').put({
459:     ...s,
460:     ...(updateLast
461:       ? {
462:         lastChapter: index,
463:         lastChapterName: chapter.name,
464:         hasProgress: true,
465:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
466:       }
467:       : {}),
468:     chaptersRead: s.chaptersRead + (!old?.read && isRead ? 1 : 0),
469:   });
470:   await tx.done;
471:   if (cachedSummaries) {
472:     const idx = cachedSummaries.findIndex((x) => x.id === m.id);
473:     if (idx >= 0) {
474:       const cur = cachedSummaries[idx];
475:       cachedSummaries[idx] = {
476:         ...cur,
477:         ...(updateLast
478:           ? {
479:             lastChapter: index,
480:             lastChapterName: chapter.name,
481:             hasProgress: true,
482:             updatedAt: Date.now(),
483:           }
484:           : {}),
485:         chaptersRead: cur.chaptersRead + (!old?.read && isRead ? 1 : 0),
486:       };
487:       cachedSummaries.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
488:     } else {
489:       cachedSummaries = null;
490:     }
491:   }
492:   if (bookmarks) {
493:     invalidateBookmarkCache();
494:   }
495:   broadcastSync({ type: 'progress', id: m.id });
496:   return 'saved';
497: }
498: 
499: async function ensureBookmarkCaches(): Promise<void> {
500:   if (cachedBookmarkedMangaIds && cachedMangaBookmarkedIds) {
501:     return;
502:   }
503:   if (bookmarkCachePromise) {
504:     return bookmarkCachePromise;
505:   }
506:   const currentGen = ++bookmarkGeneration;
507:   bookmarkCachePromise = (async () => {
508:     try {
509:       if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
510:         if (bookmarkGeneration === currentGen) {
511:           cachedBookmarkedMangaIds = new Set();
512:           cachedMangaBookmarkedIds = new Set();
513:         }
514:         return;
515:       }
516:       const d = await db;
517:       const progress = await d.getAll('progress');
518:       const allBookmarked = new Set<string>();
519:       const mangaBookmarked = new Set<string>();
520: 
521:       for (const p of progress) {
522:         if (!p.bookmarks || p.bookmarks.length === 0) continue;
523:         allBookmarked.add(p.mangaId);
524:         for (const b of p.bookmarks) {
525:           if (b.mangaBookmark) {
526:             mangaBookmarked.add(p.mangaId);
527:             break;
528:           }
529:         }
530:       }
531: 
532:       if (bookmarkGeneration === currentGen) {
533:         cachedBookmarkedMangaIds = allBookmarked;
534:         cachedMangaBookmarkedIds = mangaBookmarked;
535:       }
536:     } finally {
537:       bookmarkCachePromise = null;
538:     }
539:   })();
540:   return bookmarkCachePromise;
541: }
542: 
543: export async function getBookmarkedMangaIdsIdb(): Promise<Set<string>> {
544:   await ensureBookmarkCaches();
545:   return new Set(cachedBookmarkedMangaIds ?? []);
546: }
547: 
548: export async function getMangaBookmarkedIdsIdb(): Promise<Set<string>> {
549:   await ensureBookmarkCaches();
550:   return new Set(cachedMangaBookmarkedIds ?? []);
551: }
552: 
553: export async function addBookmarkIdb(
554:   mangaId: string,
555:   chapterName: string,
556:   position: Position,
557: ): Promise<Position[]> {
558:   const database = await db;
559:   const tx = database.transaction('progress', 'readwrite');
560:   const key: [string, string] = [mangaId, chapterName];
561:   const old = (await tx.store.get(key)) as Progress | undefined;
562:   const marks = Array.isArray(old?.bookmarks) ? [...old.bookmarks] : [];
563: 
564:   const exists = marks.some((p) => {
565:     if (position.mangaBookmark) {
566:       return Boolean(p.mangaBookmark);
567:     }
568:     return position.path ? p.path === position.path : p.page === position.page;
569:   });
570: 
571:   if (!exists) {
572:     assertAtMost(marks.length + 1, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
573:     marks.push(position);
574:     await tx.store.put({
575:       mangaId,
576:       chapter: chapterName,
577:       read: Boolean(old?.read),
578:       position: old?.position,
579:       bookmarks: marks,
580:     });
581:   }
582: 
583:   await tx.done;
584:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
585:   broadcastSync({ type: 'progress', id: mangaId });
586:   return marks;
587: }
588: 
589: export async function removeBookmarkIdb(
590:   mangaId: string,
591:   chapterName: string,
592:   position: Position,
593: ): Promise<Position[]> {
594:   const database = await db;
595:   const tx = database.transaction('progress', 'readwrite');
596:   const key: [string, string] = [mangaId, chapterName];
597:   const old = (await tx.store.get(key)) as Progress | undefined;
598:   const marks = (old?.bookmarks || []).filter((p: Position) => {
599:     if (position.mangaBookmark) {
600:       return !p.mangaBookmark;
601:     }
602:     if (p.mangaBookmark) {
603:       return true;
604:     }
605:     return position.path ? p.path !== position.path : p.page !== position.page;
606:   });
607: 
608:   if (old) {
609:     await tx.store.put({
610:       ...old,
611:       bookmarks: marks,
612:     });
613:   }
614: 
615:   await tx.done;
616:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
617:   broadcastSync({ type: 'progress', id: mangaId });
618:   return marks;
619: }

621: export async function deleteMangaIdb(id: string): Promise<void> {
622:   notifyDelete(id);
623:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress'], 'readwrite');
624:   const progressStore = tx.objectStore('progress');
625:   const keys = await progressStore.index('mangaId').getAllKeys(id);
626:   await Promise.all([
627:     tx.objectStore('manga').delete(id),
628:     tx.objectStore('summaries').delete(id),
629:     tx.objectStore('covers').delete(id),
630:     ...keys.map((k) => progressStore.delete(k)),
631:   ]);
632:   await tx.done;
633:   cachedSummaries = null;
634:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
635:   clearDirHandleCache(id);
636:   sessionFiles.delete(id);
637:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.deleteThumbnail) {
638:     desktopAPI.deleteThumbnail(id).catch((err) => {
639:       console.warn(`[deleteManga] Failed to delete thumbnail from disk for "${id}":`, err);
640:     });
641:   }
642:   broadcastSync({ type: 'library', id });
643: }
644: 
645: export async function clearLibraryIdb(): Promise<void> {
646:   notifyDelete(undefined);
647:   const tx = (await db).transaction(['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress'], 'readwrite');
648:   await Promise.all(
649:     (['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress'] as const).map((name) =>
650:       tx.objectStore(name).clear(),
651:     ),
652:   );
653:   await tx.done;
654:   cachedSummaries = [];
655:   invalidateBookmarkCache();
656:   clearDirHandleCache();
657:   sessionFiles.clear();
658:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.clearThumbnails) {
659:     desktopAPI.clearThumbnails().catch((err) => {
660:       console.warn(`[clearLibrary] Failed to clear disk thumbnails:`, err);
661:     });
662:   }
663:   broadcastSync({ type: 'library' });
664: }
665: 
666: let isRestoring = false;
667: 
668: export async function restoreBackupIdb(backup: {
669:   manga: Manga[];
670:   settings: Settings;
671: }): Promise<Settings> {
672:   if (isRestoring) {
673:     throw new Error('Another restore operation is already in progress.');
674:   }
675:   isRestoring = true;
676:   try {
677:     assertAtMost(backup.manga.length, limits.titles, 'Library titles in backup');
678:     const validatedSettings = parseSettings(backup.settings);
679: 
680:     for (const m of backup.manga) {
681:       assertMangaLimits(m);
682:     }
683: 
684:     const database = await db;
685: 
686:     // Read existing handles, local paths, and covers to preserve them
687:     const existingSummaries = await listLibraryIdb();
688:     const backupIds = new Set(backup.manga.map((m) => m.id));
689:     const usedSlugs = new Set<string>(
690:       existingSummaries
691:         .filter((s) => !backupIds.has(s.id) && Boolean(s.slug))
692:         .map((s) => s.slug!),
693:     );
694:     const existingManga = new Map<string, Manga>();
695:     for (const m of backup.manga) {
696:       const existing = await getMangaIdb(m.id);
697:       if (existing) {
698:         existingManga.set(m.id, existing);
699:       }
700:     }
701: 
702:     const now = Date.now();
703:     const preparedList: {
704:       manga: Omit<Manga, 'cover'>;
705:       summary: Summary;
706:       coverBlob?: Blob;
707:       progress: {
708:         mangaId: string;
709:         chapter: string;
710:         read: boolean;
711:         position?: Position;
712:         bookmarks?: Position[];
713:       }[];
714:     }[] = [];
715: 
716:     for (const raw of backup.manga) {
717:       const old = existingManga.get(raw.id);
718:       const handle = old?.handle;
719:       const localPath = old?.localPath;
720:       const coverBlob = old?.cover;
721: 
722:       const slug =
723:         old?.slug && !usedSlugs.has(old.slug) ? old.slug : uniqueSlug(raw.title, usedSlugs);
724:       usedSlugs.add(slug);
725: 
726:       const m: Manga = {
727:         ...raw,
728:         handle,
729:         localPath,
730:         slug,
731:         updatedAt: now,
732:       };
733: 
734:       const { cover: _c, ...metadata } = m;
735:       const mangaRecord = {
736:         ...metadata,
737:         updatedAt: now,
738:         slug,
739:         chapters: m.chapters.map(({ position: _p, bookmarks: _b, ...c }) => ({
740:           ...c,
741:           read: false,
742:         })),
743:       };
744: 
745:       const summaryRecord = summary(m);
746: 
747:       const progressRecords = m.chapters.map((c) => ({
748:         mangaId: m.id,
749:         chapter: c.name,
750:         read: Boolean(c.read),
751:         position: c.position,
752:         bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
753:       }));
754: 
755:       preparedList.push({
756:         manga: mangaRecord,
757:         summary: summaryRecord,
758:         coverBlob,
759:         progress: progressRecords,
760:       });
761:     }
762: 
763:     // Atomic transaction across all 5 stores: manga, summaries, covers, progress, settings
764:     const tx = database.transaction(
765:       ['manga', 'summaries', 'covers', 'progress', 'settings'],
766:       'readwrite',
767:     );
768: 
769:     try {
770:       for (const item of preparedList) {
771:         await tx.objectStore('manga').put(item.manga);
772:         await tx.objectStore('summaries').put(item.summary);
773:         if (item.coverBlob) {
774:           await tx.objectStore('covers').put(item.coverBlob, item.manga.id);
775:         }
776:         const progressStore = tx.objectStore('progress');
777:         const oldKeys = await progressStore.index('mangaId').getAllKeys(item.manga.id);
778:         await Promise.all(oldKeys.map((k) => progressStore.delete(k)));
779:         await Promise.all(item.progress.map((p) => progressStore.put(p)));
780:       }
781: 
782:       await tx.objectStore('settings').put(validatedSettings, 'global');
783:       await tx.done;
784:       cachedSummaries = null;
785:       invalidateBookmarkCache();
786:       clearDirHandleCache();
787: 
788:       if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveThumbnail) {
789:         for (const item of preparedList) {
790:           if (item.coverBlob) {
791:             try {
792:               const buffer = await item.coverBlob.arrayBuffer();
793:               await desktopAPI.saveThumbnail(item.manga.id, buffer);
794:             } catch (err) {
795:               console.warn(
796:                 `[restoreBackup] Failed to cache thumbnail to disk for "${item.manga.id}":`,
797:                 err,
798:               );
799:             }
800:           }
801:         }
802:       }
803:     } catch (err) {
804:       try {
805:         tx.abort();
806:       } catch { }
807:       throw err;
808:     }
809: 
810:     return validatedSettings;
811:   } finally {
812:     isRestoring = false;
813:   }
814: }

816: export async function resetProgressIdb(): Promise<void> {
817:   const database = await db;
818:   const tx = database.transaction(['progress', 'summaries'], 'readwrite');
819:   try {
820:     let cursor = await tx.objectStore('progress').openCursor();
821:     while (cursor) {
822:       const p = cursor.value;
823:       if (p.read || p.position) {
824:         await cursor.update({
825:           ...p,
826:           read: false,
827:           position: undefined,
828:         });
829:       }
830:       cursor = await cursor.continue();
831:     }
832: 
833:     let sCursor = await tx.objectStore('summaries').openCursor();
834:     while (sCursor) {
835:       const s = sCursor.value;
836:       await sCursor.update({
837:         ...s,
838:         chaptersRead: 0,
839:         lastChapter: 0,
840:         hasProgress: false,
841:       });
842:       sCursor = await sCursor.continue();
843:     }
844: 
845:     await tx.done;
846:     cachedSummaries = null;
847:     invalidateBookmarkCache();
848:   } catch (err) {
849:     try {
850:       tx.abort();
851:     } catch { }
852:     throw err;
853:   }
854: }

1022: export function errorMessage(e: unknown): string {
1023:   const err = e as { name?: string; message?: string };
1024:   if (err?.name === 'QuotaExceededError')
1025:     return 'Browser storage is full. Export a backup and remove unused library entries before retrying.';
1026:   if (err?.name === 'NotAllowedError' || err?.name === 'SecurityError')
1027:     return 'Folder permission was denied. Re-link the folder or use standard file selection.';
1028:   if (err?.name === 'NotFoundError')
1029:     return 'A file or folder moved or disappeared. Re-link the folder to update its index.';
1030:   if (err?.name === 'InvalidStateError')
1031:     return 'Storage is unavailable or another tab is upgrading it. Close other Reader tabs and reopen.';
1032:   return err?.message || 'The operation could not complete. Please try again.';
1033: }

1080: export async function fileFor(m: Manga, p: Page): Promise<File> {
1081:   assertPathLimits(p.path);
1082:   // Desktop: read directly from the filesystem via IPC — works across restarts.
1083:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && m.localPath) {
1084:     const sep = m.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
1085:     const absPath = m.localPath + sep + p.path.split('/').join(sep);
1086:     const buf = await withinTime(desktopAPI.readFile(absPath), limits.ioTimeoutMs, 'Reading file');
1087:     if (!buf) throw new Error('File not found on disk. The folder may have moved.');
1088:     return new File([buf], p.path.split('/').at(-1) ?? p.path);
1089:   }
1090:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
1091:     throw new Error('Desktop manga is missing localPath');
1092:   }
1093:   if (m.handle) {
1094:     const parts = p.path.split('/');
1095:     const dirParts = parts.slice(0, -1);
1096:     const dirPath = dirParts.join('/');
1097:     const cacheKey = `${m.id}:${m.updatedAt}:${dirPath}`;
1098: 
1099:     let h: FileSystemDirectoryHandle | undefined =
1100:       dirParts.length === 0 ? m.handle : dirHandleCache.get(cacheKey);
1101: 
1102:     try {
1103:       if (!h) {
1104:         h = m.handle;
1105:         let subPath = '';
1106:         for (const part of dirParts) {
1107:           subPath = subPath ? `${subPath}/${part}` : part;
1108:           const subKey = `${m.id}:${m.updatedAt}:${subPath}`;
1109:           let subHandle = dirHandleCache.get(subKey);
1110:           if (!subHandle) {
1111:             subHandle = await withinTime(
1112:               h.getDirectoryHandle(part),
1113:               limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1114:               'Opening folder',
1115:             );
1116:             if (dirHandleCache.size >= MAX_CACHED_DIR_HANDLES) {
1117:               const oldest = dirHandleCache.keys().next().value;
1118:               if (oldest) dirHandleCache.delete(oldest);
1119:             }
1120:             dirHandleCache.set(subKey, subHandle);
1121:           }
1122:           h = subHandle;
1123:         }
1124:         if (dirHandleCache.size >= MAX_CACHED_DIR_HANDLES) {
1125:           const oldest = dirHandleCache.keys().next().value;
1126:           if (oldest) dirHandleCache.delete(oldest);
1127:         }
1128:         dirHandleCache.set(cacheKey, h);
1129:       }
1130:       const handle = await withinTime(
1131:         h.getFileHandle(parts.at(-1)!),
1132:         limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1133:         'Opening file',
1134:       );
1135:       return await readFile(handle);
1136:     } catch (err) {
1137:       clearDirHandleCache(m.id);
1138:       throw err;
1139:     }
1140:   }
1141:   const f = sessionFiles.get(m.id)?.get(p.path);
1142:   if (!f) throw new Error('Folder access is needed. Re-link your folder to continue.');
1143:   return readFile(f);
1144: }
1145: 
1146: export async function access(
1147:   m: Pick<Manga, 'id' | 'handle' | 'localPath'>,
1148:   request = false,
1149: ): Promise<boolean> {
1150:   // Desktop: local files are always accessible via IPC; no permission model needed.
1151:   if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
1152:     if (m.localPath) return true;
1153:     if (sessionFiles.has(m.id)) return true;
1154:     const full = await getManga(m.id);
1155:     return Boolean(full?.localPath || sessionFiles.has(m.id));
1156:   }
1157: 
1158:   if (typeof __DESKTOP_BUILD__ !== 'undefined' && __DESKTOP_BUILD__) {
1159:     return false;
1160:   }
1161: 
1162:   // In-memory session files are always accessible without querying handle permissions.
1163:   if (sessionFiles.has(m.id)) {
1164:     return true;
1165:   }
1166: 
1167:   // Passive access checks (request=false) reuse cached permission status within TTL
1168:   // to avoid bursts of File System Access API queries when alt-tabbing or window refocusing.
1169:   const now = Date.now();
1170:   if (!request) {
1171:     const cached = accessCache.get(m.id);
1172:     if (cached && now - cached.timestamp < ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
1173:       return cached.granted;
1174:     }
1175:     const inFlight = accessInFlight.get(m.id);
1176:     if (inFlight) {
1177:       return inFlight;
1178:     }
1179:   }
1180: 
1181:   const queryOrRequest = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
1182:     let handle = m.handle;
1183:     if (!handle) {
1184:       if (sessionFiles.has(m.id)) return true;
1185:       const full = await getManga(m.id);
1186:       handle = full?.handle;
1187:     }
1188:     if (!handle) return sessionFiles.has(m.id);
1189:     try {
1190:       const res = await withinTime(
1191:         request
1192:           ? handle.requestPermission({ mode: 'read' })
1193:           : handle.queryPermission({ mode: 'read' }),
1194:         limits.ioTimeoutMs,
1195:         'Checking folder access',
1196:       );
1197:       return res === 'granted';
1198:     } catch {
1199:       return false;
1200:     }
1201:   };
1202: 
1203:   if (request) {
1204:     accessInFlight.delete(m.id);
1205:     const granted = await queryOrRequest();
1206:     accessCache.set(m.id, { granted, timestamp: Date.now() });
1207:     return granted;
1208:   }
1209: 
1210:   const task = queryOrRequest();
1211:   accessInFlight.set(m.id, task);
1212:   try {
1213:     const granted = await task;
1214:     accessCache.set(m.id, { granted, timestamp: Date.now() });
1215:     return granted;
1216:   } finally {
1217:     accessInFlight.delete(m.id);
1218:   }
1219: }

20: export function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
21:   return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);

24: /**
25:  * Validates a relative file path:
26:  * - Must be a non-empty string within pathChars and directoryDepth limits.
27:  * - Cannot start or end with a slash.
28:  * - Cannot contain backslashes, '.', or '..' segments.
29:  * - Cannot contain null bytes or control characters.
30:  */
31: const RESERVED_DOS_NAMES = new Set([
32:   'con',
33:   'prn',
34:   'aux',
35:   'nul',
36:   'com1',
37:   'com2',
38:   'com3',
39:   'com4',
40:   'com5',
41:   'com6',
42:   'com7',
43:   'com8',
44:   'com9',
45:   'lpt1',
46:   'lpt2',
47:   'lpt3',
48:   'lpt4',
49:   'lpt5',
50:   'lpt6',
51:   'lpt7',
52:   'lpt8',
53:   'lpt9',
54: ]);

56: export function validateRelativePath(path: unknown): string {
57:   if (typeof path !== 'string' || !path.trim()) {
58:     throw new ValidationError('Path must be a non-empty string.');
59:   }
60:   if (path.startsWith(' ') || path.endsWith(' ')) {
61:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with whitespace: "${path}"`);
62:   }
63:   const trimmed = path.trim();
64:   // Reject colons (Windows drive letters e.g. C:, alternate data streams e.g. :stream)
65:   if (trimmed.includes(':')) {
66:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot contain drive prefixes or colons: "${trimmed}"`);
67:   }
68:   if (trimmed.startsWith('/') || trimmed.endsWith('/')) {
69:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start or end with a slash: "${trimmed}"`);
70:   }
71:   if (trimmed.startsWith('//')) {
72:     throw new ValidationError(`Path cannot start with UNC prefix: "${trimmed}"`);
73:   }
74:   if (trimmed.includes('\\')) {
75:     throw new ValidationError(`Path must use forward slashes only: "${trimmed}"`);
76:   }
77:   if (trimmed.includes('\0')) {
78:     throw new ValidationError('Path contains invalid null byte.');
79:   }
80:   assertPathLimits(trimmed);
81:   const segments = trimmed.split('/');
82:   for (const seg of segments) {
83:     if (!seg || seg === '.' || seg === '..') {
84:       throw new ValidationError(`Path contains invalid traversal segment: "${trimmed}"`);
85:     }
86:     if (seg.endsWith(' ') || seg.endsWith('.')) {
87:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment cannot end with a space or dot: "${seg}"`);
88:     }
89:     const stem = seg.split('.')[0].toLowerCase();
90:     if (RESERVED_DOS_NAMES.has(stem)) {
91:       throw new ValidationError(`Path segment uses reserved device name: "${seg}"`);
92:     }
93:   }
94:   return trimmed;
95: }
96: 
97: /**
98:  * Resolves a Position against a chapter's pages list.
99:  */
100: export function matchingPosition(
101:   p: Position | undefined,
102:   pages: Page[],
103:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
104: ): Position | undefined {
105:   if (!p || !pages.length) return;
106:   const path = p.path || oldPages[p.page]?.path;
107:   let page = path ? pages.findIndex((x) => x.path === path) : -1;
108:   if (page < 0 && path) {
109:     const name = path.split('/').at(-1);
110:     const matches = pages.map((x, i) => (x.name === name ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
111:     if (matches.length === 1) page = matches[0];
112:   }
113:   if (page < 0) page = Math.min(p.page, pages.length - 1);
114:   const validIndex = Math.max(0, page);
115:   return {
116:     page: validIndex,
117:     path: pages[validIndex].path,
118:     fraction: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number.isFinite(p.fraction) ? p.fraction : 0)),
119:     ...(p.mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
120:     ...(typeof p.timestamp === 'number' && Number.isFinite(p.timestamp)
121:       ? { timestamp: p.timestamp }
122:       : {}),
123:   };
124: }
125: 
126: /**
127:  * Parses and strictly validates a Position object from unknown input.
128:  * Rejects non-integers, NaN, negative indexes, or out-of-range fractions.
129:  */
130: export function parsePosition(
131:   value: unknown,
132:   pages: Page[],
133:   oldPages: Page[] = [],
134: ): Position | undefined {
135:   if (!value) return undefined;
136:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
137:     throw new ValidationError('Position must be an object.');
138:   }
139: 
140:   if (typeof value.page !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(value.page) || value.page < 0) {
141:     throw new ValidationError('Position page must be a non-negative integer.');
142:   }
143: 
144:   if (typeof value.fraction !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value.fraction)) {
145:     throw new ValidationError('Position fraction must be a finite number.');
146:   }
147: 
148:   const fraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value.fraction));
149:   let validatedPath: string | undefined;
150:   if (value.path !== undefined) {
151:     validatedPath = validateRelativePath(value.path);
152:   }
153: 
154:   let mangaBookmark: boolean | undefined;
155:   if ('mangaBookmark' in value && value.mangaBookmark !== undefined) {
156:     if (typeof value.mangaBookmark !== 'boolean') {
157:       throw new ValidationError('Position mangaBookmark must be a boolean.');
158:     }
159:     if (value.mangaBookmark) {
160:       mangaBookmark = true;
161:     }
162:   }
163: 
164:   let timestamp: number | undefined;
165:   if ('timestamp' in value && value.timestamp !== undefined) {
166:     if (typeof value.timestamp !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value.timestamp)) {
167:       throw new ValidationError('Position timestamp must be a finite number.');
168:     }
169:     timestamp = value.timestamp;
170:   }
171: 
172:   return matchingPosition(
173:     {
174:       page: value.page,
175:       path: validatedPath,
176:       fraction,
177:       ...(mangaBookmark ? { mangaBookmark: true } : {}),
178:       ...(timestamp !== undefined ? { timestamp } : {}),
179:     },
180:     pages,
181:     oldPages,
182:   );
183: }
184: 
185: /**
186:  * Parses and validates an individual Page object.
187:  */
188: export function parsePage(value: unknown, seenPaths: Set<string>): Page {
189:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
190:     throw new ValidationError('Page record must be an object.');
191:   }
192: 
193:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
194:     throw new ValidationError('Page name must be a non-empty string.');
195:   }
196: 
197:   const path = validateRelativePath(value.path);
198:   if (seenPaths.has(path)) {
199:     throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate page path within chapter: "${path}"`);
200:   }
201:   seenPaths.add(path);
202: 
203:   const isPending = value.dimensionsPending === true;
204:   const isWidthPending = isPending && value.width === 0;
205:   const isHeightPending = isPending && value.height === 0;
206: 
207:   if (
208:     typeof value.width !== 'number' ||
209:     !Number.isInteger(value.width) ||
210:     typeof value.height !== 'number' ||
211:     !Number.isInteger(value.height) ||
212:     (value.width <= 0 && !isWidthPending) ||
213:     (value.height <= 0 && !isHeightPending)
214:   ) {
215:     throw new ValidationError('Page dimensions must be positive integer pixels.');
216:   }
217:   if (value.width > 0 && value.height > 0) {
218:     assertImageDimensions(value.width, value.height);
219:   }
220: 
221:   let size: number | undefined;
222:   if (value.size !== undefined) {
223:     if (typeof value.size !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(value.size) || value.size < 0) {
224:       throw new ValidationError('Page size must be a non-negative integer bytes count.');
225:     }
226:     size = value.size;
227:   }
228: 
229:   let modified: number | undefined;
230:   if (value.modified !== undefined) {
231:     if (
232:       typeof value.modified !== 'number' ||
233:       !Number.isFinite(value.modified) ||
234:       value.modified < 0
235:     ) {
236:       throw new ValidationError('Page modified timestamp must be a non-negative number.');
237:     }
238:     modified = value.modified;
239:   }
240: 
241:   const dimensionsPending =
242:     typeof value.dimensionsPending === 'boolean' ? value.dimensionsPending : undefined;
243: 
244:   return {
245:     name: value.name.trim(),
246:     path,
247:     width: value.width,
248:     height: value.height,
249:     ...(size !== undefined ? { size } : {}),
250:     ...(modified !== undefined ? { modified } : {}),
251:     ...(dimensionsPending !== undefined ? { dimensionsPending } : {}),
252:   };
253: }
254: 
255: /**
256:  * Parses and validates a Chapter object.
257:  */
258: export function parseChapter(value: unknown, chapterIndex: number, oldChapter?: Chapter): Chapter {
259:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
260:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} must be an object.`);
261:   }
262: 
263:   if (typeof value.name !== 'string' || !value.name.trim()) {
264:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter ${chapterIndex + 1} name must be a non-empty string.`);
265:   }
266: 
267:   if (!Array.isArray(value.pages) || !value.pages.length) {
268:     throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${value.name}" must contain at least one page.`);
269:   }
270: 
271:   const seenPaths = new Set<string>();
272:   const pages: Page[] = value.pages.map((p, idx) => {
273:     try {
274:       return parsePage(p, seenPaths);
275:     } catch (e) {
276:       throw new ValidationError(
277:         `Chapter "${value.name}" page ${idx + 1} is invalid: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`,
278:       );
279:     }
280:   });
281: 
282:   const position = value.position
283:     ? parsePosition(value.position, pages, oldChapter?.pages)
284:     : undefined;
285: 
286:   let bookmarks: Position[] | undefined;
287:   if (value.bookmarks !== undefined) {
288:     if (!Array.isArray(value.bookmarks)) {
289:       throw new ValidationError(`Chapter "${value.name}" bookmarks must be an array.`);
290:     }
291:     assertAtMost(value.bookmarks.length, limits.bookmarksPerChapter, 'Bookmarks per chapter');
292:     const seenBookmarks = new Set<string>();
293:     bookmarks = [];
294:     for (const b of value.bookmarks) {
295:       const parsed = parsePosition(b, pages, oldChapter?.pages);
296:       if (parsed) {
297:         const key = `${parsed.page}:${parsed.path ?? ''}:${Boolean(parsed.mangaBookmark)}`;
298:         if (!seenBookmarks.has(key)) {
299:           seenBookmarks.add(key);
300:           bookmarks.push(parsed);
301:         }
302:       }
303:     }
304:   }
305: 
306:   const id = typeof value.id === 'string' && value.id.trim() ? value.id.trim() : undefined;
307: 
308:   return {
309:     ...(id ? { id } : {}),
310:     name: value.name.trim(),
311:     read: value.read === true,
312:     pages,
313:     position,
314:     bookmarks,
315:   };
316: }
317: 
318: /**
319:  * Parses and validates a Manga object.
320:  */
321: export function parseManga(
322:   value: unknown,
323:   oldManga?: Manga,
324:   options: { preserveHardwarePointers?: boolean } = { preserveHardwarePointers: true },
325: ): Manga {
326:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
327:     throw new ValidationError('Manga record must be an object.');
328:   }
329: 
330:   if (typeof value.id !== 'string' || !value.id.trim()) {
331:     throw new ValidationError('Manga ID must be a non-empty string.');
332:   }
333: 
334:   if (typeof value.title !== 'string' || !value.title.trim()) {
335:     throw new ValidationError('Manga title must be a non-empty string.');
336:   }
337: 
338:   if (!Array.isArray(value.chapters) || !value.chapters.length) {
339:     throw new ValidationError(`Manga "${value.title}" must contain at least one chapter.`);
340:   }
341: 
342:   assertAtMost(value.chapters.length, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
343: 
344:   let titlePages = 0;
345:   const chapters: Chapter[] = value.chapters.map((c, idx) => {
346:     const oldChapter = oldManga?.chapters[idx];
347:     const parsed = parseChapter(c, idx, oldChapter);
348:     titlePages += parsed.pages.length;
349:     assertAtMost(titlePages, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Pages per title');
350:     return parsed;
351:   });
352: 
353:   const lastChapter =
354:     typeof value.lastChapter === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value.lastChapter)
355:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(chapters.length - 1, value.lastChapter))
356:       : 0;
357: 
358:   const now = Date.now();
359:   const addedAt =
360:     typeof value.addedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.addedAt) && value.addedAt > 0
361:       ? value.addedAt
362:       : now;
363: 
364:   const updatedAt =
365:     typeof value.updatedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.updatedAt) && value.updatedAt > 0
366:       ? value.updatedAt
367:       : now;
368: 
369:   const sort =
370:     value.sort === 'name' || value.sort === 'reverse' || value.sort === 'natural'
371:       ? value.sort
372:       : undefined;
373: 
374:   const revision =
375:     typeof value.revision === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0
376:       ? value.revision
377:       : 1;
378: 
379:   const title = value.title.trim();
380:   const slug =
381:     typeof value.slug === 'string' && value.slug.trim() ? value.slug.trim() : folderSlug(title);
382: 
383:   const localPath =
384:     options.preserveHardwarePointers &&
385:       typeof value.localPath === 'string' &&
386:       value.localPath.trim()
387:       ? value.localPath.trim()
388:       : undefined;
389: 
390:   const handle =
391:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.handle && typeof value.handle === 'object'
392:       ? (value.handle as Manga['handle'])
393:       : undefined;
394: 
395:   const cover =
396:     options.preserveHardwarePointers && value.cover instanceof Blob ? value.cover : undefined;
397: 
398:   return {
399:     id: value.id.trim(),
400:     title,
401:     slug,
402:     chapters,
403:     lastChapter,
404:     addedAt,
405:     updatedAt,
406:     revision,
407:     ...(sort ? { sort } : {}),
408:     handle,
409:     localPath,
410:     cover,
411:   };
412: }

459:       : now;

488:       ? value.sort

510: /**

530:     s.maxWidth === 920 || s.maxWidth === 1024 || s.maxWidth === 1280 || s.maxWidth === 0

535:     typeof s.seconds === 'number' && Number.isFinite(s.seconds)

542:   const customSeconds =
543:     typeof s.customSeconds === 'boolean' ? s.customSeconds : ![2, 3, 4, 5].includes(seconds);

546: 
547:   const resumeDelay =
548:     typeof s.resumeDelay === 'number' && Number.isFinite(s.resumeDelay)
549:       ? Math.max(0.5, Math.min(15, s.resumeDelay))
550:       : defaults.resumeDelay;

553:   const spread = typeof s.spread === 'boolean' ? s.spread : defaults.spread;

559:       : advance === false
560:         ? 'stop'
561:         : defaults.endAction;
562:

564:     typeof s.fontSizeTitle === 'number' && Number.isFinite(s.fontSizeTitle)

569:     typeof s.fontSizeUi === 'number' && Number.isFinite(s.fontSizeUi)

571:       : defaults.fontSizeUi;

574:     typeof s.fontSizeButton === 'number' && Number.isFinite(s.fontSizeButton)

577: 
578:   const confirmDelete =
579:     typeof s.confirmDelete === 'boolean' ? s.confirmDelete : (defaults.confirmDelete ?? false);
580: 
581:   const shelfSort: Settings['shelfSort'] =
582:     s.shelfSort === 'recent' ||
583:       s.shelfSort === 'title' ||
584:       s.shelfSort === 'added' ||
585:       s.shelfSort === 'progress'
586:       ? s.shelfSort
587:       : undefined;
588: 
589:   return {
590:     theme,
591:     mode,
592:     layout,

613: /**
614:  * Validates untrusted backup JSON records before any database writes.
615:  * Rejects corrupt versions, oversized payloads, duplicate IDs, and invalid structures.
616:  */
617: export function validateBackup(value: unknown): { manga: Manga[]; settings: Settings } {
618:   if (!isRecord(value)) {
619:     throw new ValidationError('Backup must be an object.');
620:   }
621: 
622:   if (value.version !== 1 && value.version !== 2) {
623:     throw new ValidationError('Invalid backup version. Supported versions: 1, 2.');
624:   }
625: 
626:   if (!Array.isArray(value.manga)) {
627:     throw new ValidationError('Backup manga list must be an array.');
628:   }
629: 
630:   assertAtMost(value.manga.length, limits.titles, 'Backup titles');
631: 
632:   const seenIds = new Set<string>();
633:   let totalBackupPages = 0;
634: 
635:   const manga: Manga[] = value.manga.map((rawManga, index) => {
636:     if (!isRecord(rawManga) || typeof rawManga.id !== 'string') {
637:       throw new ValidationError(`Manga item ${index + 1} has an invalid or missing ID.`);
638:     }
639:     const id = rawManga.id.trim();
640:     if (seenIds.has(id)) {
641:       throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate manga ID found in backup: "${id}"`);
642:     }
643:     seenIds.add(id);
644: 
645:     const parsed = parseManga(rawManga, undefined, { preserveHardwarePointers: false });
646:     for (const ch of parsed.chapters) {
647:       totalBackupPages += ch.pages.length;
648:       assertAtMost(totalBackupPages, limits.backupPages, 'Backup pages');
649:     }
650:     return parsed;
651:   });
652: 
653:   const settings = parseSettings(value.settings);
654: 
655:   return {
656:     manga,
657:     settings,
658:   };
659: }

154: type CoverVisibleCallback = () => void;

156: let sharedCoverObserver: IntersectionObserver | null = null;
157: 
158: function getSharedCoverObserver(): IntersectionObserver | null {
159:   if (typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') return null;
160:   if (!sharedCoverObserver) {
161:     sharedCoverObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
162:       (entries) => {
163:         for (const entry of entries) {
164:           if (entry.isIntersecting) {
165:             const cb = coverListeners.get(entry.target);
166:             if (cb) {
167:               coverListeners.delete(entry.target);
168:               sharedCoverObserver?.unobserve(entry.target);
169:               cb();
170:             }
171:           }
172:         }
173:       },
174:       { rootMargin: '100px 0px' },
175:     );
176:   }
177:   return sharedCoverObserver;
178: }
179: 
180: function observeCoverVisibility(el: HTMLElement, callback: CoverVisibleCallback): () => void {
181:   const observer = getSharedCoverObserver();
182:   if (!observer) {
183:     callback();
184:     return () => { };
185:   }
186:   coverListeners.set(el, callback);
187:   observer.observe(el);
188:   return () => {
189:     coverListeners.delete(el);
190:     observer.unobserve(el);
191:   };
192: }
193: 
194: export const Cover = memo(function Cover({
195:   m,
196: }: {
197:   m: Pick<Summary, 'id'> & { updatedAt?: number };
198: }) {
199:   const cacheKey = `${m.id}:${m.updatedAt ?? 0}`;
200:   const [url, setUrl] = useState<string>(() => coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey) || '');
201:   const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
202: 
203:   useEffect(() => {
204:     let live = true;
205:     const cached = coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey);
206:     if (cached) {
207:       setUrl(cached);
208:       return;
209:     }
210: 
211:     if (!host.current) return;
212: 
213:     const unobserve = observeCoverVisibility(host.current, () => {
214:       if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && (desktopAPI.getCoverImageUrl || desktopAPI.getThumbnailUrl)) {
215:         if (desktopAPI.getCoverImageUrl) {
216:           const coverUrl = desktopAPI.getCoverImageUrl(m.id, m.updatedAt);
217:           setUrl(coverUrl);
218:           return;
219:         }
220:         desktopAPI.getThumbnailUrl!(m.id)
221:           .then((thumbUrl) => {
222:             if (!live) return;
223:             if (thumbUrl) {
224:               setUrl(thumbUrl);
225:               return;
226:             }
227:             loadCoverBlob();
228:           })
229:           .catch(() => {
230:             if (live) loadCoverBlob();
231:           });
232:         return;
233:       }
234:       loadCoverBlob();
235: 
236:       function loadCoverBlob() {
237:         void getCover(m.id)
238:           .then((blob) => {
239:             if (!live || !blob) return;
240:             let objectUrl = coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey);
241:             if (!objectUrl) {
242:               if (coverUrlPool.size >= MAX_COVER_URLS) {
243:                 const oldestKey = coverUrlPool.keys().next().value;
244:                 if (oldestKey) clearCoverUrlPool(oldestKey);
245:               }
246:               objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
247:               coverUrlPool.set(cacheKey, objectUrl);
248:             }
249:             setUrl(objectUrl);
250:           })
251:           .catch(() => { });
252:       }
253:     });
254: 
255:     return () => {
256:       live = false;
257:       unobserve();
258:     };
259:   }, [cacheKey, m.id, m.updatedAt]);
260: 
261:   return (
262:     <div ref={host} className="cover">
263:       {url ? (
264:         <img
265:           src={url}
266:           alt=""
267:           onError={() => {
268:             if (url.includes('/cover/')) {
269:               void getCover(m.id)
270:                 .then((blob) => {
271:                   if (!blob) {
272:                     setUrl('');
273:                     return;
274:                   }
275:                   let objectUrl = coverUrlPool.get(cacheKey);
276:                   if (!objectUrl) {
277:                     if (coverUrlPool.size >= MAX_COVER_URLS) {
278:                       const oldestKey = coverUrlPool.keys().next().value;
279:                       if (oldestKey) clearCoverUrlPool(oldestKey);
280:                     }
281:                     objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
282:                     coverUrlPool.set(cacheKey, objectUrl);
283:                   }
284:                   setUrl(objectUrl);
285:                 })
286:                 .catch(() => setUrl(''));
287:             } else {
288:               setUrl('');
289:             }
290:           }}
291:         />
292:       ) : (
293:         <BookOpen size={24} />
294:       )}
295:     </div>
296:   );
297: });
298: function positionCardMenu(details: HTMLDetailsElement) {
299:   const rect = details.getBoundingClientRect();
300:   const popover = details.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.menu-popover');
301:   const popoverHeight = popover ? popover.offsetHeight || 195 : 195;
302:   const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
303:   const spaceAbove = rect.top;
304:   if (spaceBelow < popoverHeight && spaceAbove > spaceBelow) {
305:     details.classList.add('popover-top');
306:   } else {
307:     details.classList.remove('popover-top');
308:   }
309: }
310: 
311: function formatAddedDate(timestamp?: number): string {
312:   if (!timestamp) return '';
313:   const d = new Date(timestamp);
314:   if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
315:   const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
316:   const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
317:   const year = d.getFullYear();
318:   return `${day}/${month}/${year}`;
319: }
320: 
321: function formatWholeDate(timestamp?: number): string {
322:   if (!timestamp) return '';
323:   const d = new Date(timestamp);
324:   if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
325:   return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
326:     weekday: 'long',
327:     year: 'numeric',
328:     month: 'long',
329:     day: 'numeric',
330:   });
331: }

340:       strokeLinecap="round"
341:       strokeLinejoin="round"
342:       strokeWidth="2"
