381: 
382: function DateTooltip({ timestamp, children }: { timestamp?: number; children: ReactNode }) {
383:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
384:   const [placement, setPlacement] = useState<'top' | 'bottom'>('top');
385:   const [align, setAlign] = useState<'left' | 'right'>('left');
386:   const badgeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
387: 
388:   const showTooltip = () => {
389:     if (badgeRef.current) {
390:       const rect = badgeRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
391:       if (rect.top < 44) {
392:         setPlacement('bottom');
393:       } else {
394:         setPlacement('top');
395:       }
396:       if (rect.left + 260 > window.innerWidth) {
397:         setAlign('right');
398:       } else {
399:         setAlign('left');
400:       }
401:     }
402:     setOpen(true);
403:   };
404: 
405:   const hideTooltip = () => {
406:     setOpen(false);
407:   };
408: 
409:   const wholeDate = formatWholeDate(timestamp);
410: 
411:   return (
412:     <div
413:       ref={badgeRef}
414:       className="manga-card-date-badge"
415:       onPointerEnter={showTooltip}
416:       onPointerLeave={hideTooltip}
417:       onFocus={showTooltip}
418:       onBlur={hideTooltip}
419:       onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
420:       tabIndex={0}
421:       role="note"
422:       aria-label={wholeDate ? `Manga was added on ${wholeDate}` : undefined}
423:     >
424:       {children}
425:       {open && wholeDate && (
426:         <div className={`space-tooltip tooltip-${placement} tooltip-align-${align}`} role="tooltip">
427:           Manga was added on {wholeDate}
428:         </div>
429:       )}
430:     </div>
431:   );
432: }
433: 
434: export const shelfCollator = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
435: 
436: export function sortLibraryEntries(
437:   library: readonly Summary[],
438:   shelfSort: Settings['shelfSort'] = 'recent',
439: ): Summary[] {
440:   return [...library].sort((a, b) => {
441:     const byTitle = shelfCollator.compare(a.title, b.title);
442:     return (
443:       (shelfSort === 'title'
444:         ? byTitle
445:         : shelfSort === 'added'
446:           ? b.addedAt - a.addedAt
447:           : shelfSort === 'progress'
448:             ? b.chaptersRead / Math.max(1, b.chapterCount) -
449:             a.chaptersRead / Math.max(1, a.chapterCount)
450:             : b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt) ||
451:       byTitle ||
452:       shelfCollator.compare(a.id, b.id)
453:     );
454:   });
455: }
456: 
457: function Library({
458:   busy,
459:   scan,
460:   drop,
461:   cancel,
462: }: {
463:   busy: boolean;
464:   scan: ScanState;
465:   drop: (items: DataTransferItem[]) => void;
466:   cancel: () => void;
467: }) {
468:   const { library, pick, notify, refresh, openSettings, settings, setSettings } = useApp();
469:   const shelfSort = settings.shelfSort || 'recent';
470:   const sortedLibrary = useMemo(() => sortLibraryEntries(library, shelfSort), [library, shelfSort]);
471:   const [drag, setDrag] = useState(false);
472:   const [needs, setNeeds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
473:   const [supported, setSupported] = useState(false);
474:   const [removeId, setRemoveId] = useState<string>();
475:   const [bookmarkedIds, setBookmarkedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
476:   const nav = useNavigate();
477: 
478:   useEffect(() => {
479:     let live = true;
480:     function updateBookmarkedIds() {
481:       void getMangaBookmarkedIds().then((ids) => {
482:         if (live) setBookmarkedIds(ids);
483:       });
484:     }
485:     updateBookmarkedIds();
486:     const onSync = (e: MessageEvent) => {
487:       if (e.data?.type === 'progress') updateBookmarkedIds();
488:     };
489:     syncChannel?.addEventListener('message', onSync);
490:     return () => {
491:       live = false;
492:       syncChannel?.removeEventListener('message', onSync);
493:     };
494:   }, [library]);
495: 
496:   async function toggleMangaBookmark(m: Summary, e: React.MouseEvent) {
497:     e.stopPropagation();
498:     e.preventDefault();
499:     const isBookmarked = bookmarkedIds.has(m.id);
500:     try {
501:       const manga = await getManga(m.id);
502:       if (!manga) return;
503:       if (isBookmarked) {
504:         // Strictly remove title bookmarks only; never delete ordinary chapter/page bookmarks
505:         for (const ch of manga.chapters) {
506:           for (const b of ch.bookmarks || []) {
507:             if (b.mangaBookmark) {
508:               await removeBookmark(m.id, ch.name, b);
509:             }
510:           }
511:         }
512:         setBookmarkedIds((prev) => {
513:           const next = new Set(prev);
514:           next.delete(m.id);
515:           return next;
516:         });
517:         await refresh();
518:         notify(`Bookmark removed.`);
519:       } else {
520:         const ch = manga.chapters[0];
521:         if (ch) {
522:           const pagePath = ch.pages[0]?.path;
523:           const pos: Position = {
524:             page: 0,
525:             fraction: 0,
526:             path: pagePath,
527:             mangaBookmark: true,
528:             timestamp: Date.now(),
529:           };
530:           await addBookmark(m.id, ch.name, pos);
531:           setBookmarkedIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(m.id));
532:           await refresh();
533:           notify(`Bookmarked ${m.title}.`);
534:         }
535:       }
536:     } catch (err) {
537:       notify(errorMessage(err));
538:     }
539:   }
540:   useEffect(() => {
541:     function dismiss(event: MouseEvent | KeyboardEvent) {
542:       if (event instanceof KeyboardEvent && event.key !== 'Escape') return;
543:       document.querySelectorAll<HTMLDetailsElement>('.card-menu[open]').forEach((menu) => {
544:         if (event instanceof KeyboardEvent) {
545:           if (menu.contains(document.activeElement)) menu.querySelector('summary')?.focus();
546:           menu.open = false;
547:         } else if (event.target instanceof Node && !menu.contains(event.target)) {
548:           menu.open = false;
549:         }
550:       });
551:     }
552:     function reposition() {
553:       document.querySelectorAll<HTMLDetailsElement>('.card-menu[open]').forEach(positionCardMenu);
554:     }
555:     document.addEventListener('pointerdown', dismiss, true);
556:     document.addEventListener('click', dismiss, true);
557:     document.addEventListener('keydown', dismiss);
558:     window.addEventListener('resize', reposition);
559:     window.addEventListener('scroll', reposition, true);
560:     return () => {
561:       document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', dismiss, true);
562:       document.removeEventListener('click', dismiss, true);
563:       document.removeEventListener('keydown', dismiss);
564:       window.removeEventListener('resize', reposition);
565:       window.removeEventListener('scroll', reposition, true);
566:     };
567:   }, []);
568:   useEffect(() => {
569:     let live = true;
570:     let revision = 0;
571:     function checkAccess() {
572:       const current = ++revision;
573:       void Promise.all(library.map(async (m) => (!(await access(m)) ? m.id : ''))).then(
574:         (ids) => live && current === revision && setNeeds(new Set(ids.filter(Boolean))),
575:       );
576:     }
577:     function onVisible() {
578:       if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') checkAccess();
579:     }
580:     checkAccess();
581:     window.addEventListener('focus', checkAccess);
582:     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
583:     return () => {
584:       live = false;
585:       window.removeEventListener('focus', checkAccess);
586:       document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
587:     };
588:   }, [library]);
589:   async function mark(id: string, read: boolean) {
590:     const m = await getManga(id);
591:     if (!m) return;
592:     await saveManga({
593:       ...m,
594:       chapters: m.chapters.map((c) => ({
595:         ...c,
596:         read,
597:         ...(read ? {} : { position: undefined }),
598:       })),
599:     });
600:     await refresh();
601:   }
602:   async function remove() {
603:     if (!removeId) return;
604:     await deleteManga(removeId);
605:     setRemoveId(undefined);
606:     await refresh();
607:   }
608:   return (
609:     <>
610:       <header className="library-header">
611:         <Link to="/" className="brand">
612:           <BookOpen size={23} strokeWidth={1.5} />
613:           <span>
614:             Reader<span className="brand-period">.</span>
615:           </span>
616:         </Link>
617:         <div className="header-actions">
618:           <Link className="icon-button" to="/bookmarks" aria-label="Bookmarks" title="Bookmarks">
619:             <BookMarked size={19} />
620:           </Link>
621:           <ThemeButton />
622:           <IconButton label="Settings" onClick={openSettings}>
623:             <Gear size={19} />
624:           </IconButton>
625:         </div>
626:       </header>
627:       <main className="library-main">
628:         <div className="page-heading">
629:           <div>
630:             <h1>Your library</h1>
631:             <DailyWelcome />
632:           </div>
633:         </div>
634:         <div
635:           aria-busy={busy}
636:           onClick={(e) => {
637:             if (!busy && !(e.target as HTMLElement).closest('button,a')) void pick();
638:           }}
639:           onDragOver={(e) => {
640:             e.preventDefault();
641:             if (!busy) setDrag(true);
642:           }}
643:           onDragLeave={() => setDrag(false)}
644:           onDrop={(e) => {
645:             e.preventDefault();
646:             setDrag(false);
647:             if (!busy && !desktopAPI.isDesktop) drop(Array.from(e.dataTransfer.items));
648:           }}
649:           className={
650:             'upload-zone group bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 min-h-[350px] p-8 rounded w-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative transition-colors duration-300 cursor-pointer shadow ' +
651:             (drag ? 'dragging' : '')
652:           }
653:         >
654:           {busy ? (
655:             <>
656:               <LoaderCircle className="spin" size={26} />
657:               <strong className="index-count" aria-live="polite">
658:                 {scan.phase === 'index' ? 'Saving…' : `${scan.done} found`}
659:               </strong>
660:               <span>
661:                 {scan.phase === 'index' ? 'Adding to your library…' : 'Preparing your manga…'}
662:               </span>
663:               <span className="subtle">{scan.title}</span>
664:               <button className="secondary" onClick={cancel}>
665:                 Cancel import
666:               </button>
667:             </>
668:           ) : (
669:             <>
670:               <span className="upload-icon">
671:                 <UploadIcon size={29} strokeWidth={1.5} />
672:               </span>
673:               <h2>{drag ? 'Drop your stories here' : 'Make room for a good story'}</h2>
674:               <p>Drop manga folders here, or choose your files.</p>
675:               <div className="upload-buttons">
676:                 <button className="browse-button" onClick={() => void pick()}>
677:                   Choose a folder <UploadIcon size={16} />
678:                 </button>
679:                 <button className="secondary support-button" onClick={() => setSupported(true)}>
680:                   <Info size={16} /> What’s supported
681:                 </button>
682:               </div>
683:             </>
684:           )}
685:         </div>
686: 
687:         {library.length > 0 && (
688:           <>
689:             <BookmarkShelf carousel />
690:             <div className="shelf-toolbar">
691:               <div className="collection-heading">
692:                 <h2>
693:                   {library.length === 1
694:                     ? '1 manga on your shelf.'
695:                     : `${library.length} manga on your shelf.`}
696:                 </h2>
697:                 <span>Saved on this device</span>
698:               </div>
699:               {library.length > 1 && (
700:                 <label className="shelf-sort">
701:                   <Dropdown
702:                     aria-label="Sort manga shelf"
703:                     value={shelfSort}
704:                     onChange={(e) =>
705:                       setSettings({ ...settings, shelfSort: e.target.value as Settings['shelfSort'] })
706:                     }
707:                   >
708:                     <option value="recent">Recent activity</option>
709:                     <option value="title">Title A–Z</option>
710:                     <option value="added">Newest added</option>
711:                     <option value="progress">Most completed</option>
712:                   </Dropdown>
713:                 </label>
714:               )}
715:             </div>
716:             <div className="manga-grid">
717:               {sortedLibrary.map((m) => (
718:                 <article className="manga-card" key={m.id}>
719:                   <div
720:                     className="manga-open"
721:                     role="button"
722:                     tabIndex={0}
723:                     onClick={() =>
724:                       void getManga(m.id)
725:                         .then((latest) => {
726:                           if (latest) nav(mangaPath(latest));
727:                         })
728:                         .catch((error) => notify(errorMessage(error)))
729:                     }
730:                     onKeyDown={(e) => {
731:                       if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) return;
732:                       if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
733:                         e.preventDefault();
734:                         void getManga(m.id)
735:                           .then((latest) => {
736:                             if (latest) nav(mangaPath(latest));
737:                           })
738:                           .catch((error) => notify(errorMessage(error)));
739:                       }
740:                     }}
741:                     aria-label={(m.hasProgress ? 'Continue reading ' : 'Start reading ') + m.title}
742:                   >
743:                     <div className="manga-card-left">
744:                       <Cover m={m} />
745:                     </div>
746:                     <div className="manga-card-right">
747:                       <div className="manga-card-right-top">
748:                         <div className="manga-card-title-row">
749:                           <h3 title={m.title}>{m.title}</h3>
750:                           <span className="manga-card-counter">
751:                             {m.chaptersRead} / {m.chapterCount}
752:                           </span>
753:                         </div>
754:                         <div className="reading-progress">
755:                           <span
756:                             style={{
757:                               width: (100 * m.chaptersRead) / Math.max(1, m.chapterCount) + '%',
758:                             }}
759:                           />
760:                         </div>
761:                       </div>
762:                       <div className="manga-card-right-bottom">
763:                         <DateTooltip timestamp={m.addedAt}>
764:                           <CalendarIcon className="manga-card-calendar-icon" />
765:                           <span className="manga-card-date">{formatAddedDate(m.addedAt)}</span>
766:                         </DateTooltip>
767:                         <button
768:                           type="button"
769:                           className={
770:                             'manga-bookmark-btn' + (bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? ' bookmarked' : '')
771:                           }
772:                           onClick={(e) => void toggleMangaBookmark(m, e)}
773:                           title={bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this title'}
774:                           aria-label={
775:                             bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this title'
776:                           }
777:                           aria-pressed={bookmarkedIds.has(m.id)}
778:                         >
779:                           <Bookmark
780:                             size={16}
781:                             fill={bookmarkedIds.has(m.id) ? 'currentColor' : 'none'}
782:                           />
783:                         </button>
784:                       </div>
785:                     </div>
786:                   </div>
787:                   <div className="manga-card-bottom">
788:                     {needs.has(m.id) ? (
789:                       <button
790:                         type="button"
791:                         className="relink-needed-btn"
792:                         onClick={async (e) => {
793:                           e.stopPropagation();
794:                           try {
795:                             const allowed = await access(m, true);
796:                             if (allowed) {
797:                               setNeeds((prev) => {
798:                                 const next = new Set(prev);

126:             /* A saved cover remains available without folder permission. */
127:           }
128:           if (live && blob) {
129:             currentUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
130:             setUrl(currentUrl);
131:           }
132:         })();
133:       },
134:       { rootMargin: '100px' },
135:     );
136:     if (host.current) observer.observe(host.current);
137:     return () => {
138:       live = false;
139:       observer.disconnect();
140:       if (currentUrl && currentUrl.startsWith('blob:')) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentUrl);
141:     };
142:   }, [mark]);
143:   return (
144:     <div className="bookmark-thumbnail" ref={host}>
145:       {url ? (
146:         <img
147:           src={url}
148:           alt=""
149:           loading="lazy"
150:           decoding="async"
151:           draggable={false}
152:           onError={() => setUrl('')}
153:         />
154:       ) : (
155:         <BookOpen size={30} />
156:       )}
157:     </div>
158:   );
159: }
160: export function BookmarkShelf({

167:   const { library, notify, settings } = useApp();
168:   const [marks, setMarks] = useState<Mark[]>([]);

175:   const track = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

178:   const suppress = useRef(false);

180: 
181:   useEffect(() => {
182:     onCountChangeRef.current = onCountChange;
183:   });
184: 
185:   useEffect(() => {
186:     // Desktop: use SQL JOIN query — no per-manga getManga() calls needed.
187:     // Always fetch tab='all' so badge counts on each tab button remain accurate;
188:     // SQL handles sort order; client-side filter drives the active-tab display.
189:     if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop || !desktopAPI.getBookmarkCards) return;
190:     let live = true;
191:     let loadGen = 0;
192:     function loadBookmarks() {
193:       const currentGen = ++loadGen;
194:       setLoading(true);

196:         .then((res) => {
197:           if (!live || currentGen !== loadGen) return;

199:           setMarks(result);
200:           onCountChangeRef.current?.(result.length);
201:           setError('');
202:         })
203:         .catch((e: unknown) => {
204:           if (live && currentGen === loadGen) {
205:             setError(errorMessage(e));
206:             onCountChangeRef.current?.(0);
207:           }
208:         })
209:         .finally(() => {
210:           if (live && currentGen === loadGen) setLoading(false);
211:         });
212:     }
213:     loadBookmarks();
214:     const onSync = (e: MessageEvent) => {
215:       if (e.data?.type === 'progress' || e.data?.type === 'library') loadBookmarks();
216:     };
217:     syncChannel?.addEventListener('message', onSync);
218:     return () => {
219:       live = false;
220:       syncChannel?.removeEventListener('message', onSync);
221:     };
222:   }, [carousel, sortBy, library]);
223: 
224:   useEffect(() => {
225:     // Web fallback: load full Manga objects from IndexedDB via getManga().
226:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop) return;
227:     let live = true;
228:     let loadGen = 0;
229:     function loadBookmarks() {
230:       const currentGen = ++loadGen;
231:       setLoading(true);
232:       void (async () => {
233:         const ids = await getBookmarkedMangaIds();
234:         const result: Mark[] = [];
235:         for (const item of library) {
236:           if (!live || currentGen !== loadGen) return;
237:           if (!ids.has(item.id)) continue;
238:           const manga = await getManga(item.id);
239:           if (!manga) continue;
240:           for (let i = 0; i < manga.chapters.length; i++)
241:             for (const position of manga.chapters[i].bookmarks || [])
242:               result.push({ manga, chapter: i, position });
243:         }
244:         if (live && currentGen === loadGen) {
245:           setMarks(result);
246:           onCountChangeRef.current?.(result.length);
247:           setError('');
248:         }
249:       })()
250:         .catch((e) => {
251:           if (live && currentGen === loadGen) {
252:             setError(errorMessage(e));
253:             onCountChangeRef.current?.(0);
254:           }
255:         })
256:         .finally(() => {
257:           if (live && currentGen === loadGen) setLoading(false);
258:         });
259:     }
260:     loadBookmarks();
261:     const onSync = (e: MessageEvent) => {
262:       if (e.data?.type === 'progress') loadBookmarks();
263:     };
264:     syncChannel?.addEventListener('message', onSync);
265:     return () => {
266:       live = false;
267:       syncChannel?.removeEventListener('message', onSync);
268:     };
269:   }, [library]);

271:   // On desktop, marks is always all-tabs (SQL fetches tab='all'); filter here for tab display and counts.
272:   const mangaMarks = marks.filter((m) => Boolean(m.position.mangaBookmark));
273:   const individualMarks = marks.filter((m) => !m.position.mangaBookmark);

275:   function getSortedMarks(items: Mark[], sort: BookmarkSort): Mark[] {
276:     const list = [...items];
277:     switch (sort) {
278:       case 'newest':
279:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
280:           const timeA = a.position.timestamp || a.manga.updatedAt || a.manga.addedAt || 0;
281:           const timeB = b.position.timestamp || b.manga.updatedAt || b.manga.addedAt || 0;
282:           return timeB - timeA;
283:         });
284:       case 'oldest':
285:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
286:           const timeA = a.position.timestamp || a.manga.addedAt || a.manga.updatedAt || 0;
287:           const timeB = b.position.timestamp || b.manga.addedAt || b.manga.updatedAt || 0;
288:           return timeA - timeB;
289:         });
290:       case 'title-asc':
291:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
292:           const cmp = bookmarkCollator.compare(a.manga.title, b.manga.title);
293:           if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
294:           return a.chapter - b.chapter || a.position.page - b.position.page;
295:         });
296:       case 'title-desc':
297:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
298:           const cmp = bookmarkCollator.compare(b.manga.title, a.manga.title);
299:           if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
300:           return a.chapter - b.chapter || a.position.page - b.position.page;
301:         });
302:       case 'chapter':
303:         return list.sort((a, b) => {
304:           const cmp = bookmarkCollator.compare(a.manga.title, b.manga.title);
305:           if (cmp !== 0) return cmp;
306:           return a.chapter - b.chapter || a.position.page - b.position.page;
307:         });
308:       default:
309:         return list;
310:     }
311:   }
312: 
313:   const activeMarks = tab === 'all' ? marks : tab === 'manga' ? mangaMarks : individualMarks;
314:   const displayMarks = carousel ? getSortedMarks(marks, 'newest') : getSortedMarks(activeMarks, sortBy);

322:         end: el.scrollLeft + el.clientWidth >= el.scrollWidth - 1,

27:     ((day % welcomeMessages.length) + welcomeMessages.length) % welcomeMessages.length
28:   ];

34:     const update = () => {
35:       clearTimeout(timer);
36:       const now = new Date();

=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src\diagnostics.ts ===
1: export type DiagnosticLevel = 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';
2: 
3: export interface DiagnosticRecord {
4:   id: string;
5:   timestamp: number;
6:   operation: 'import' | 'cover' | 'background' | 'storage' | 'update' | 'reader';
7:   level: DiagnosticLevel;
8:   message: string;
9:   code?: string;
10:   safeIdentifier?: string;
11:   retryable?: boolean;
12:   durationMs?: number;
13: }
14: 
15: const MAX_DIAGNOSTICS = 50;
16: const ringBuffer: DiagnosticRecord[] = [];
17: const subscribers = new Set<() => void>();
18: 
19: let nextId = 1;
20: 
21: /**
22:  * Strips potentially sensitive or overly verbose paths, keeping only leaf names.
23:  */
24: export function sanitizeIdentifier(raw?: string): string | undefined {
25:   if (!raw) return undefined;
26:   const normalized = raw.replace(/\\/g, '/');
27:   const parts = normalized.split('/').filter(Boolean);
28:   return parts.slice(-2).join('/');
29: }
30: 
31: export function recordDiagnostic(
32:   entry: Omit<DiagnosticRecord, 'id' | 'timestamp'>,
33: ): DiagnosticRecord | null {
34:   // Silent on expected user cancellation or aborts
35:   if (
36:     entry.code === 'AbortError' ||
37:     entry.message.includes('Import cancelled') ||
38:     entry.message.includes('aborted')
39:   ) {
40:     return null;
41:   }
42: 
43:   const now = Date.now();
44: 
45:   // Deduplicate rapid repeat diagnostics (within 2000ms) with identical op, level, and message
46:   const recent = ringBuffer[ringBuffer.length - 1];
47:   if (
48:     recent &&
49:     recent.operation === entry.operation &&
50:     recent.level === entry.level &&
51:     recent.message === entry.message &&
52:     now - recent.timestamp < 2000
53:   ) {
54:     return recent;
55:   }
56: 
57:   const record: DiagnosticRecord = {
58:     id: `diag-${nextId++}`,
59:     timestamp: now,
60:     ...entry,
61:     safeIdentifier: sanitizeIdentifier(entry.safeIdentifier),
62:   };
63: 
64:   ringBuffer.push(record);
65:   if (ringBuffer.length > MAX_DIAGNOSTICS) {
66:     ringBuffer.shift();
67:   }
68: 
69:   for (const listener of subscribers) {
70:     try {
71:       listener();
72:     } catch {
73:       // Ignore listener error
74:     }
75:   }
76: 
77:   return record;
78: }
79: 
80: export function getDiagnostics(): DiagnosticRecord[] {
81:   return [...ringBuffer];
82: }
83: 
84: export function clearDiagnostics(): void {
85:   ringBuffer.length = 0;
86:   for (const listener of subscribers) {
87:     try {
88:       listener();
89:     } catch {}
90:   }
91: }
92: 
93: export function subscribeDiagnostics(listener: () => void): () => void {
94:   subscribers.add(listener);
95:   return () => {
96:     subscribers.delete(listener);
97:   };
98: }

=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src\imageDimensions.ts ===
1: // Read small headers for common manga formats; other formats use the browser decoder.
2: import { limits, assertImageDimensions, withinTime } from './limits';
3: 
4: const dimensionCache = new Map<string, { width: number; height: number }>();
5: const MAX_DIMENSION_CACHE = 1000;
6: 
7: export function clearDimensionCache(): void {
8:   dimensionCache.clear();
9: }
10: 
11: export async function headerDimensions(
12:   file: File,
13: ): Promise<{ width: number; height: number } | undefined> {
14:   const cacheKey = `${file.name}:${file.size}:${file.lastModified}`;
15:   const cached = dimensionCache.get(cacheKey);
16:   if (cached) return cached;
17: 
18:   const b = new Uint8Array(
19:     await withinTime(
20:       file.slice(0, limits.headerBytes).arrayBuffer(),
21:       limits.ioTimeoutMs,
22:       'Reading image header',
23:     ),
24:   );
25:   const v = new DataView(b.buffer);
26:   let width = 0,
27:     height = 0;
28: 
29:   // 1. PNG: 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A
30:   if (b.length >= 24 && v.getUint32(0) === 0x89504e47 && v.getUint32(4) === 0x0d0a1a0a) {
31:     width = v.getUint32(16);
32:     height = v.getUint32(20);
33:   }
34:   // 2. GIF: GIF87a or GIF89a
35:   else if (b.length >= 10 && String.fromCharCode(...b.slice(0, 6)).match(/^GIF8[79]a$/)) {
36:     width = v.getUint16(6, true);
37:     height = v.getUint16(8, true);
38:   }
39:   // 3. WebP: RIFF .... WEBP
40:   else if (
41:     b.length >= 25 &&
42:     v.getUint32(0) === 0x52494646 && // 'RIFF'
43:     v.getUint32(8) === 0x57454250 // 'WEBP'
44:   ) {
45:     const chunkType = v.getUint32(12);
46:     // VP8  (lossy) - requires at least 30 bytes
47:     if (
48:       b.length >= 30 &&
49:       chunkType === 0x56503820 &&
50:       b[23] === 0x9d &&
51:       b[24] === 0x01 &&
52:       b[25] === 0x2a
53:     ) {
54:       width = v.getUint16(26, true) & 0x3fff;
55:       height = v.getUint16(28, true) & 0x3fff;
56:     }
57:     // VP8L (lossless) - requires at least 25 bytes
58:     else if (chunkType === 0x5650384c && b[20] === 0x2f) {
59:       const bits = v.getUint32(21, true);
60:       width = (bits & 0x3fff) + 1;
61:       height = ((bits >> 14) & 0x3fff) + 1;
62:     }
63:     // VP8X (extended) - requires at least 30 bytes
64:     else if (b.length >= 30 && chunkType === 0x56503858) {
65:       width = 1 + (b[24] | (b[25] << 8) | (b[26] << 16));
66:       height = 1 + (b[27] | (b[28] << 8) | (b[29] << 16));
67:     }
68:   }
69:   // 4. JPEG: FF D8 ...
70:   else if (b.length > 4 && b[0] === 255 && b[1] === 216) {
71:     let i = 2;
72:     let swapForExif = false;
73:     while (i + 3 < b.length) {
74:       if (b[i++] !== 255) break;
75:       while (i < b.length && b[i] === 255) i++;
76:       const marker = b[i++];
77:       if (marker === 0xda || marker === 0xd9) break; // SOS / EOI
78:       if (marker === 1 || (marker >= 0xd0 && marker <= 0xd7)) continue;
79:       if (i + 2 > b.length) break;
80:       const length = v.getUint16(i);
81:       if (length < 2 || i + length > b.length) break;
82: 
83:       // APP1 (EXIF): parse orientation tag to swap dimensions if image is rotated 90/270 deg
84:       if (
85:         marker === 0xe1 &&
86:         length >= 14 &&
87:         b[i + 2] === 0x45 &&
88:         b[i + 3] === 0x78 &&
89:         b[i + 4] === 0x69 &&
90:         b[i + 5] === 0x66 &&
91:         b[i + 6] === 0 &&
92:         b[i + 7] === 0
93:       ) {
94:         try {
95:           const tiff = i + 8;
96:           const le = v.getUint16(tiff) === 0x4949; // 'II' = little endian, 'MM' = big endian
97:           if (v.getUint16(tiff + 2, le) === 0x002a) {
98:             const ifdOffset = v.getUint32(tiff + 4, le);
99:             const ifd0 = tiff + ifdOffset;
100:             if (ifd0 + 2 <= b.length) {
101:               const entries = v.getUint16(ifd0, le);
102:               for (let k = 0; k < entries && ifd0 + 2 + (k + 1) * 12 <= b.length; k++) {
103:                 const entry = ifd0 + 2 + k * 12;
104:                 if (v.getUint16(entry, le) === 0x0112) {
105:                   // Orientation tag
106:                   const orientation = v.getUint16(entry + 8, le);
107:                   if (orientation >= 5 && orientation <= 8) {
108:                     swapForExif = true;
109:                   }
110:                   break;
111:                 }
112:               }
113:             }
114:           }
115:         } catch {
116:           // If EXIF parse fails, continue with unswapped dimensions
117:         }
118:       }
119: 
120:       // SOF markers
121:       if (
122:         [0xc0, 0xc1, 0xc2, 0xc3, 0xc5, 0xc6, 0xc7, 0xc9, 0xca, 0xcb, 0xcd, 0xce, 0xcf].includes(
123:           marker,
124:         ) &&
125:         length >= 7
126:       ) {
127:         height = v.getUint16(i + 3);
128:         width = v.getUint16(i + 5);
129:         if (swapForExif) {
130:           const tmp = width;
131:           width = height;
132:           height = tmp;
133:         }
134:         break;
135:       }
136:       i += length;
137:     }
138:   }
139: 
140:   if (width > 0 && height > 0) {
141:     assertImageDimensions(width, height);
142:     const result = { width, height };
143:     if (dimensionCache.size >= MAX_DIMENSION_CACHE) {
144:       const oldest = dimensionCache.keys().next().value;
145:       if (oldest) dimensionCache.delete(oldest);
146:     }
147:     dimensionCache.set(cacheKey, result);
148:     return result;
149:   }
150:   return undefined;
151: }

15:   onError?: (err: unknown) => void;
16: }

18: interface QueuedItem {
19:   key: string;
20:   priority: number;

27: let mobileQuery: MediaQueryList | null = null;
28: let cachedIsMobile: boolean | null = null;
29: 
30: function getIsMobile(): boolean {
31:   if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') {
32:     return false;
33:   }
34:   if (!mobileQuery) {
35:     try {
36:       mobileQuery = window.matchMedia('(max-width:700px)');
37:       cachedIsMobile = mobileQuery.matches;
38:       const onChange = (e: MediaQueryListEvent | MediaQueryList) => {
39:         cachedIsMobile = e.matches;
40:       };
41:       if (typeof mobileQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {
42:         mobileQuery.addEventListener('change', onChange);
43:       } else if (typeof (mobileQuery as any).addListener === 'function') {
44:         (mobileQuery as any).addListener(onChange);
45:       }
46:     } catch {
47:       return false;
48:     }
49:   }
50:   return cachedIsMobile ?? false;
51: }
52: 
53: let cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit: number | null = null;
54: 
55: function getHardwareConcurrencyLimit(): number {
56:   if (cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit !== null) {
57:     return cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit;
58:   }
59:   if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined') {
60:     const nav = navigator as unknown as {
61:       hardwareConcurrency?: number;
62:       deviceMemory?: number;
63:     };
64:     const cores = nav.hardwareConcurrency;
65:     const mem = nav.deviceMemory;
66: 
67:     // Constrained devices (<= 2 cores or <= 2 GB RAM) -> concurrency 1
68:     if ((cores && cores <= 2) || (mem && mem <= 2)) {
69:       cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit = 1;
70:       return 1;
71:     }
72:     // Modest devices (<= 4 cores or <= 4 GB RAM) -> concurrency 2
73:     if ((cores && cores <= 4) || (mem && mem <= 4)) {
74:       cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit = Math.min(2, limits.desktopPageConcurrency);
75:       return cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit;
76:     }
77:   }
78: 
79:   cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit = limits.desktopPageConcurrency;
80:   return cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit;
81: }
82: 
83: export function resetConcurrencyCacheForTesting(): void {
84:   mobileQuery = null;
85:   cachedIsMobile = null;
86:   cachedHardwareConcurrencyLimit = null;
87: }
88: 
89: export class ImageQueue {
90:   private queue: QueuedItem[] = [];
91:   private waiting = new Map<string, QueuedItem>();
92:   private activeCount = 0;
93:   private inFlight = new Map<string, QueuedItem>();
94:   private queueDirty = false;
95:   private nextSubscriberId = 0;
96: 
97:   get pendingCount(): number {
98:     return this.queue.length;
99:   }
100: 
101:   get runningCount(): number {
102:     return this.activeCount;
103:   }
104: 
105:   private getMaxConcurrency(): number {
106:     if (getIsMobile()) {
107:       return limits.mobilePageConcurrency;
108:     }
109:     return getHardwareConcurrencyLimit();
110:   }
111: 
112:   /**
113:    * Updates priority of an existing waiting task and lazily marks queue dirty.
114:    */
115:   reprioritize(key: string, priority: number): void {
116:     const item = this.waiting.get(key);
117:     if (item && item.priority !== priority) {
118:       item.priority = priority;
119:       this.queueDirty = true;
120:     }
121:   }
122: 
123:   /**
124:    * Enqueues an image load task.
125:    * If a task with the identical key is already queued or in-flight, registers the caller
126:    * as a subscriber so every component receives its own completion.
127:    * Returns a cancellation handle that unregisters only this subscriber.
128:    */
129:   enqueue(options: ImageTaskOptions): CancelHandle | null {
130:     const { key, priority = 0, run, onComplete, onError } = options;
131:     const subId = ++this.nextSubscriberId;
132:     const sub: Subscriber = { onComplete, onError };
133: 
134:     // 1. Task is waiting in queue: register subscriber and boost priority if needed
135:     const existingWaiting = this.waiting.get(key);
136:     if (existingWaiting) {
137:       existingWaiting.subscribers.set(subId, sub);
138:       if (priority > existingWaiting.priority) {
139:         existingWaiting.priority = priority;
140:         this.queueDirty = true;
141:       }
142:       return () => this.cancelSubscriber(key, subId);
143:     }
144: 
145:     // 2. Task is already in-flight: register subscriber so it executes with completion
146:     const existingInFlight = this.inFlight.get(key);
147:     if (existingInFlight && !existingInFlight.canceled) {
148:       existingInFlight.subscribers.set(subId, sub);
149:       return () => this.cancelSubscriber(key, subId);
150:     }
151: 
152:     // 3. Queue capacity limit
153:     if (this.queue.length >= limits.queuedJobs) {
154:       return null;
155:     }
156: 
157:     const item: QueuedItem = {
158:       key,
159:       priority,
160:       primaryRun: run,
161:       subscribers: new Map([[subId, sub]]),
162:       controller: new AbortController(),
163:       canceled: false,
164:     };
165: 
166:     this.queue.push(item);
167:     this.waiting.set(key, item);
168:     this.queueDirty = true;
169:     this.pump();
170: 
171:     return () => this.cancelSubscriber(key, subId);
172:   }
173: 
174:   /**
175:    * Cancels an individual subscriber's handle.
176:    * The underlying operation is only aborted when all subscribers for that key have unsubscribed.
177:    */
178:   private cancelSubscriber(key: string, subId: number): void {
179:     // 1. Check waiting queue
180:     const waitingItem = this.waiting.get(key);
181:     if (waitingItem) {
182:       waitingItem.subscribers.delete(subId);
183:       if (waitingItem.subscribers.size === 0) {
184:         this.waiting.delete(key);
185:         waitingItem.canceled = true;
186:         waitingItem.controller.abort();
187:         const index = this.queue.indexOf(waitingItem);
188:         if (index >= 0) {
189:           this.queue.splice(index, 1);
190:         }
191:       }
192:       return;
193:     }
194: 
195:     // 2. Check in-flight tasks
196:     const running = this.inFlight.get(key);
197:     if (running) {
198:       running.subscribers.delete(subId);
199:       if (running.subscribers.size === 0) {
200:         running.canceled = true;
201:         running.controller.abort();
202:       }
203:     }
204:   }
205: 
206:   /**
207:    * Cancels a queued or running task by key unconditionally (all subscribers).
208:    */
209:   cancel(key: string): void {
210:     const waitingItem = this.waiting.get(key);
211:     if (waitingItem) {
212:       this.waiting.delete(key);
213:       waitingItem.canceled = true;
214:       waitingItem.controller.abort();
215:       const index = this.queue.indexOf(waitingItem);
216:       if (index >= 0) {
217:         this.queue.splice(index, 1);
218:       }
219:       return;
220:     }
221: 
222:     const running = this.inFlight.get(key);
223:     if (running) {
224:       running.canceled = true;
225:       running.controller.abort();
226:     }
227:   }
228: 
229:   /**
230:    * Clears all waiting tasks and aborts in-flight tasks.
231:    * In-flight tasks settle and clean themselves up via identity check.
232:    */
233:   clear(): void {
234:     for (const item of this.queue) {
235:       item.canceled = true;
236:       item.controller.abort();
237:     }
238:     this.queue.length = 0;
239:     this.waiting.clear();
240:     this.queueDirty = false;
241: 
242:     for (const running of this.inFlight.values()) {
243:       running.canceled = true;
244:       running.controller.abort();
245:     }
246:   }
247: 
248:   private ensureSorted(): void {
249:     if (!this.queueDirty) return;
250:     // Insertion sort is faster than Array.sort for nearly-sorted priority queues of size <= 64
251:     for (let i = 1; i < this.queue.length; i++) {
252:       const item = this.queue[i];
253:       let j = i - 1;
254:       while (j >= 0 && this.queue[j].priority < item.priority) {
255:         this.queue[j + 1] = this.queue[j];
256:         j--;
257:       }
258:       this.queue[j + 1] = item;
259:     }
260:     this.queueDirty = false;
261:   }
262: 
263:   private pump(): void {
264:     const maxConcurrency = this.getMaxConcurrency();
265: 
266:     this.ensureSorted();
267: 
268:     while (this.activeCount < maxConcurrency && this.queue.length > 0) {
269:       const item = this.queue.shift();
270:       if (!item) break;
271: 
272:       this.waiting.delete(item.key);
273: 
274:       if (item.canceled || item.subscribers.size === 0) {
275:         continue;
276:       }
277: 
278:       this.activeCount++;
279:       this.inFlight.set(item.key, item);
280: 
281:       void (async () => {
282:         try {
283:           if (!item.canceled && !item.controller.signal.aborted) {
284:             await item.primaryRun(item.controller.signal);
285: 
286:             if (!item.canceled && !item.controller.signal.aborted) {
287:               for (const sub of Array.from(item.subscribers.values())) {
288:                 try {
289:                   sub.onComplete?.();
290:                 } catch (e) {
291:                   console.error(`[imageQueue] Task ${item.key} onComplete error:`, e);
292:                 }
293:               }
294:             }
295:           }
296:         } catch (err) {
297:           if (!item.controller.signal.aborted) {
298:             console.warn(`[imageQueue] Task ${item.key} failed:`, err);
299:             for (const sub of Array.from(item.subscribers.values())) {
300:               try {
301:                 sub.onError?.(err);
302:               } catch (e) {
303:                 console.error(`[imageQueue] Task ${item.key} onError error:`, e);
304:               }
305:             }
306:           }
307:         } finally {
308:           this.activeCount = Math.max(0, this.activeCount - 1);
309:           // Strict identity check prevents an old task from deleting a newer task with the same key
310:           if (this.inFlight.get(item.key) === item) {
311:             this.inFlight.delete(item.key);
312:           }
313:           this.pump();
314:         }
315:       })();
316:     }
317:   }
318: }
319:

8: export async function dimensions(
9:   f: File,
10:   signal?: AbortSignal,
11: ): Promise<{ width: number; height: number }> {
12:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
13:   const header = await headerDimensions(f);
14:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
15:   if (header) return header;
16: 
17:   const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
18:   try {
19:     return await new Promise<{ width: number; height: number }>((resolve, reject) => {
20:       const im = new Image();
21:       let settled = false;
22:       const finish = (error?: Error) => {
23:         if (settled) return;
24:         settled = true;
25:         clearTimeout(timer);
26:         signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort);
27:         im.onload = null;
28:         im.onerror = null;
29:         try {
30:           if (error) reject(error);
31:           else {
32:             assertImageDimensions(im.naturalWidth, im.naturalHeight);
33:             resolve({ width: im.naturalWidth, height: im.naturalHeight });
34:           }
35:         } catch (e) {
36:           reject(e);
37:         } finally {
38:           im.src = '';
39:         }
40:       };
41:       const abort = () => finish(new DOMException('Import cancelled', 'AbortError'));
42:       const timer = setTimeout(
43:         () => finish(new Error('Image took too long to read. Retry this file.')),
44:         limits.imageTimeoutMs,
45:       );
46:       signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
47:       im.onload = () => finish();
48:       im.onerror = () =>
49:         finish(new Error('Corrupt image or a format this browser cannot display.'));
50:       im.src = url;
51:       if (signal?.aborted) abort();
52:     });
53:   } finally {
54:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
55:   }

58: export async function thumbnail(
59:   file: File,
60:   signal?: AbortSignal,
61:   quality = 0.65,
62: ): Promise<Blob | undefined> {
63:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
64:   const size = await headerDimensions(file);
65:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
66:   // A cover is optional; avoid a second large decode just to make a thumbnail.
67:   if (
68:     size &&
69:     (size.width * size.height > limits.imagePixels ||
70:       Math.max(size.width, size.height) > limits.imageSide)
71:   ) {
72:     return undefined;
73:   }
74: 
75:   const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
76:   try {
77:     const im = await new Promise<HTMLImageElement>((resolve, reject) => {
78:       const img = new Image();
79:       let settled = false;
80:       const finish = (error?: Error, result?: HTMLImageElement) => {
81:         if (settled) return;
82:         settled = true;
83:         clearTimeout(t);
84:         signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
85:         img.onload = null;
86:         img.onerror = null;
87:         if (error) {
88:           img.src = '';
89:           reject(error);
90:         } else if (result) {
91:           resolve(result);
92:         }
93:       };
94:       const onAbort = () => finish(new DOMException('Cover decode aborted', 'AbortError'));
95:       const t = setTimeout(() => finish(new Error('Cover timed out')), limits.coverTimeoutMs);
96:       signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
97:       img.onload = () => finish(undefined, img);
98:       img.onerror = () => finish(new Error('Cover unavailable'));
99:       img.src = url;
100:       if (signal?.aborted) onAbort();
101:     });
102: 
103:     try {
104:       assertImageDimensions(im.naturalWidth, im.naturalHeight);
105:       const c = document.createElement('canvas');
106:       try {
107:         const scale = Math.min(160 / im.naturalWidth, 320 / im.naturalHeight);
108:         c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(im.naturalWidth * scale));
109:         c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(im.naturalHeight * scale));
110:         const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
111:         if (!ctx) return undefined;
112:         ctx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
113:         return await withinTime(
114:           new Promise<Blob | undefined>((r) =>
115:             c.toBlob((b) => r(b ?? undefined), 'image/webp', quality),
116:           ),
117:           limits.coverTimeoutMs,
118:           'Encoding cover',
119:         );
120:       } finally {
121:         c.width = 0;
122:         c.height = 0;
123:       }
124:     } finally {
125:       im.src = '';
126:     }
127:   } finally {
128:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
129:   }
130: }

132: /**
133:  * Safely downscales an image using createImageBitmap and an offscreen canvas.
134:  * Guarantees ImageBitmap closure and canvas memory release on all exit paths.
135:  */
136: export async function downscaleImage(
137:   blob: Blob,
138:   targetWidth: number,
139:   targetHeight: number,
140:   signal?: AbortSignal,
141: ): Promise<Blob> {
142:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
143:   if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('createImageBitmap' in window)) {
144:     return blob;
145:   }
146: 
147:   let expired = false;
148:   const isConstrained =
149:     typeof navigator !== 'undefined' &&
150:     (Boolean(navigator.hardwareConcurrency && navigator.hardwareConcurrency <= 4) ||
151:       Boolean(
152:         (navigator as unknown as { deviceMemory?: number }).deviceMemory &&
153:         (navigator as unknown as { deviceMemory?: number }).deviceMemory! <= 4,
154:       ));
155:   const resizeQuality: ResizeQuality = isConstrained ? 'low' : 'medium';
156: 
157:   const task = createImageBitmap(blob, {
158:     resizeWidth: targetWidth,
159:     resizeHeight: targetHeight,
160:     resizeQuality,
161:   });
162: 
163:   // A timed-out or aborted decoder may still resolve; release its late bitmap.
164:   void task.then(
165:     (bitmap) => {
166:       if (expired || signal?.aborted) {
167:         bitmap.close();
168:       }
169:     },
170:     () => {},
171:   );
172: 
173:   const onAbort = () => {
174:     expired = true;
175:   };
176:   signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
177: 
178:   let bitmap: ImageBitmap;
179:   try {
180:     bitmap = await withinTime(task, limits.imageTimeoutMs, 'Resizing image');
181:   } catch (error) {
182:     expired = true;
183:     throw error;
184:   } finally {
185:     signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
186:   }
187: 
188:   if (signal?.aborted) {
189:     bitmap.close();
190:     throw new DOMException('Image resize aborted', 'AbortError');
191:   }
192: 
193:   const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
194:   try {
195:     canvas.width = bitmap.width;
196:     canvas.height = bitmap.height;
197:     const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
198:     if (!context) {
199:       throw new Error('Image resizing canvas context is unavailable.');
200:     }
201:     context.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
202:   } finally {
203:     bitmap.close();
204:   }
205: 
206:   try {
207:     return await withinTime(
208:       new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
209:         if (signal?.aborted) {
210:           reject(new DOMException('Image encoding aborted', 'AbortError'));
211:           return;
212:         }
213:         canvas.toBlob(
214:           (result) => {
215:             if (signal?.aborted) {
216:               reject(new DOMException('Image encoding aborted', 'AbortError'));
217:             } else {
218:               resolve(result || blob);
219:             }
220:           },
221:           'image/webp',
222:           0.8,
223:         );
224:       }),
225:       limits.imageTimeoutMs,
226:       'Encoding image',
227:     );
228:   } finally {
229:     canvas.width = 0;
230:     canvas.height = 0;
231:   }
232: }

62:   onChange: (i: number) => void;

64: }) {

71:           role="tab"

73:           aria-selected={value === i}

75:           tabIndex={value === i ? 0 : -1}
76:           onClick={() => onChange(i)}
77:           onKeyDown={(e) => {
78:             const next =
79:               e.key === 'ArrowRight'
80:                 ? (i + 1) % names.length
81:                 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft'

86:                       ? names.length - 1

93:         >
94:           {n}
95:         </button>
96:       ))}
97:     </div>
98:   );

244: export function ImportDialog({
245:   sources,

247:   library,
248:   onClose,
249:   onImport,
250: }: {
251:   sources: Source[];
252:   target?: Manga;
253:   library: Summary[];
254:   onClose: () => void;
255:   onImport: (plans: ImportPlan[]) => void;
256: }) {
257:   const [modes, setModes] = useState<boolean[]>(sources.map(() => false));
258:   const [plans, setPlans] = useState<ImportPlan[]>([]);
259:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
260:   const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false);
261:   const [mismatch, setMismatch] = useState(false);
262:   const [error, setError] = useState('');
263:   const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);
264:   useEffect(() => {
265:     let live = true;
266:     setLoading(true);
267:     setError('');
268:     void (async () => {
269:       const list: ImportPlan[] = [];
270:       for (let i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {
271:         const children = modes[i] ? await splitSource(sources[i]) : [sources[i]];
272:         for (const source of children) {
273:           let duplicate: Summary | undefined;
274:           for (const candidate of library) {
275:             if (source.handle && candidate.handle) {
276:               try {
277:                 if (await source.handle.isSameEntry(candidate.handle)) {
278:                   duplicate = candidate;
279:                   break;
280:                 }
281:               } catch {
282:                 /* Compare metadata below when handle identity is unavailable. */
283:               }
284:             } else if (source.localPath && candidate.localPath) {
285:               try {
286:                 const same = desktopAPI.isSamePath
287:                   ? await desktopAPI.isSamePath(source.localPath, candidate.localPath)
288:                   : source.localPath.toLowerCase().replace(/[\\/]+$/, '') ===
289:                     candidate.localPath.toLowerCase().replace(/[\\/]+$/, '');
290:                 if (same) {
291:                   duplicate = candidate;
292:                   break;
293:                 }
294:               } catch {
295:                 /* Ignore and fall through */
296:               }
297:             }
298:           }
299:           list.push({
300:             source,
301:             targetId: target?.id || duplicate?.id,
302:             sort: target?.sort || 'natural',
303:           });
304:         }
305:       }
306:       if (live) {
307:         setPlans(list);
308:         setLoading(false);
309:       }
310:     })().catch(() => {

312:         setError('Could not prepare the import preview. Re-select the folders.');
313:         setLoading(false);
314:       }
315:     });
316:     return () => {
317:       live = false;
318:     };
319:   }, [sources, modes]);
320:   useEffect(() => {
321:     let live = true;
322:     const controller = new AbortController();
323:     setConfirmed(false);
324:     setMismatch(false);
325:     setChecking(true);
326:     void (async () => {
327:       for (const plan of plans) {
328:         await warmSources([plan.source], { signal: controller.signal }, plan.sort);
329:         if (!plan.targetId) continue;
330:         const previous = target?.id === plan.targetId ? target : await getManga(plan.targetId);
331:         if (!previous) continue;
332:         const oldPages = previous.chapters.flatMap((c) => c.pages);
333:         const sample = oldPages
334:           .filter((_, i) => i % Math.max(1, Math.floor(oldPages.length / 24)) === 0)
335:           .slice(0, 24);
336:         let matched = 0;
337:         for (const page of sample) {
338:           const candidate = plan.source.files.get(page.path);
339:           if (!candidate) continue;
340:           try {
341:             const cand = candidate as any;
342:             const candidateSize =
343:               typeof cand.size === 'number'
344:                 ? cand.size
345:                 : candidate instanceof File
346:                   ? candidate.size
347:                   : undefined;
348:             const candidateModified =
349:               typeof cand.modified === 'number'
350:                 ? cand.modified
351:                 : typeof cand.lastModified === 'number'
352:                   ? cand.lastModified
353:                   : candidate instanceof File
354:                     ? candidate.lastModified
355:                     : undefined;
356: 
357:             if (candidateSize !== undefined && candidateModified !== undefined) {
358:               if (
359:                 page.size !== undefined &&
360:                 page.size === candidateSize &&
361:                 page.modified === candidateModified
362:               ) {
363:                 matched++;
364:               }
365:             } else {
366:               const file = candidate instanceof File ? candidate : await candidate.getFile();
367:               if (
368:                 page.size !== undefined &&
369:                 page.size === file.size &&
370:                 page.modified === file.lastModified
371:               ) {
372:                 matched++;
373:               }
374:             }
375:           } catch {
376:             /* Unreadable candidates require confirmation and appear in the import report. */
377:           }
378:         }
379:         if (
380:           (previous.title !== plan.source.title ||
381:             matched < sample.length * 0.5 ||
382:             !sample.length) &&

384:         )
385:           setMismatch(true);
386:       }
387:     })()

390:       })
391:       .finally(() => {
392:         if (live) setChecking(false);
393:       });

395:       live = false;
396:       controller.abort();
397:     };
398:   }, [plans]);
399: 
400:   const duplicateTargets = plans.some(
401:     (p, i) => p.targetId && plans.some((q, j) => j < i && q.targetId === p.targetId),
402:   );
403:   return (

3: // Units, scope, rationale and loop audit: docs/limits.md.
4: // Policy ceilings, not a promise of peak memory usage on every browser/device.
5: export const limits = Object.freeze({
6:   backupBytes: 32 * 1024 * 1024,
7:   titles: 1_000,
8:   chaptersPerTitle: 1_000,
9:   pagesPerTitle: 100_000,

12:   pathChars: 4_096,
13:   directoryDepth: 64,
14:   discoveryEntries: 100_000,
15:   // Budgets for optional bitmap/canvas work, not source-image restrictions.
16:   imagePixels: 16_000_000,
17:   imageSide: 32_768,
18:   queuedJobs: 64,
19:   decodeConcurrency: 3,
20:   mobilePageConcurrency: 1,
21:   desktopPageConcurrency: 4,
22:   headerBytes: 256 * 1024,
23:   imageTimeoutMs: 20_000,
24:   coverTimeoutMs: 10_000,

26:   updateTimeoutMs: 10_000,

30: export class LimitError extends Error {
31:   constructor(message: string) {
32:     super(message);
33:     this.name = 'LimitError';
34:   }
35: }
36: 
37: export function assertAtMost(value: number, maximum: number, label: string): void {
38:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0 || value > maximum) {
39:     throw new LimitError(`${label} exceeds the limit of ${maximum}. Use a smaller selection.`);
40:   }
41: }
42: 
43: export function assertPathLimits(path: string): void {
44:   assertAtMost(path.length, limits.pathChars, 'Relative path length (characters)');
45:   assertAtMost(path.split('/').length - 1, limits.directoryDepth, 'Folder depth');
46: }
47: 
48: export function assertImageDimensions(width: number, height: number): void {
49:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(width) || !Number.isSafeInteger(height) || width < 1 || height < 1) {
50:     throw new LimitError('Image dimensions must be positive integer pixels.');
51:   }
52:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(width * height)) {
53:     throw new LimitError('Image area cannot be represented safely.');
54:   }
55: }
56: 
57: // Resource checks for typed data. Unknown-data parsing/allowlisting belongs to step 04.
58: export function assertMangaLimits(manga: Manga): number {
59:   assertAtMost(manga.chapters.length, limits.chaptersPerTitle, 'Chapters per title');
60:   let pages = 0;
61:   for (const chapter of manga.chapters) {
62:     pages += chapter.pages.length;
63:     assertAtMost(pages, limits.pagesPerTitle, 'Pages per title');
64:     assertAtMost(
65:       chapter.bookmarks?.length ?? 0,
66:       limits.bookmarksPerChapter,
67:       'Bookmarks per chapter',
68:     );
69:     for (const page of chapter.pages) {
70:       assertPathLimits(page.path);
71:       if (!(page.dimensionsPending && page.width === 0 && page.height === 0)) {
72:         assertImageDimensions(page.width, page.height);
73:       }
74:     }
75:   }
76:   return pages;
77: }
78: 
79: // Stops waiting; cannot cancel browser work that has no cancellation API.
80: // Attach handlers immediately so a late rejection is still consumed.
81: export function withinTime<T>(
82:   task: PromiseLike<T>,
83:   milliseconds: number,
84:   operation: string,
85:   signal?: AbortSignal,
86: ): Promise<T> {
87:   return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
88:     let settled = false;
89:     const finish = (success: boolean, value: unknown) => {
90:       if (settled) return;
91:       settled = true;
92:       clearTimeout(timer);
93:       signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort);
94:       if (success) resolve(value as T);
95:       else reject(value);
96:     };
97:     const abort = () =>
98:       finish(false, signal?.reason ?? new DOMException('Cancelled', 'AbortError'));
99:     const timer = setTimeout(
100:       () => finish(false, new Error(`${operation} timed out. Please retry.`)),
101:       milliseconds,
102:     );
103:     signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
104:     Promise.resolve(task).then(
105:       (value) => finish(true, value),
106:       (error) => finish(false, error),
107:     );
108:     if (signal?.aborted) abort();
109:   });
110: }

=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src\objectUrlPool.ts ===
1: /**
2:  * Bounded LRU Object URL Pool with Consumer Reference Counting / Pinning.
3:  *
4:  * Keeps a bounded cache of recent object URLs in memory so rapid scroll-back
5:  * avoids re-reading blobs across IPC or re-decoding pixels in the browser.
6:  * Evicts least-recently-used entries and revokes URLs only when both the cache
7:  * and mounted consumers have released the resource.
8:  */
9: 
10: interface PoolEntry {
11:   url: string;
12:   refCount: number;
13:   inCache: boolean;
14: }
15: 
16: export class ObjectUrlPool {
17:   private entries = new Map<string, PoolEntry>();
18:   private readonly maxEntries: number;
19: 
20:   constructor(maxEntries = 32) {
21:     this.maxEntries = Math.max(1, maxEntries);
22:   }
23: 
24:   get size(): number {
25:     let count = 0;
26:     for (const entry of this.entries.values()) {
27:       if (entry.inCache) count++;
28:     }
29:     return count;
30:   }
31: 
32:   get totalAllocated(): number {
33:     return this.entries.size;
34:   }
35: 
36:   /**
37:    * Retrieves a cached object URL, moving it to the most-recently-used position.
38:    */
39:   get(key: string): string | undefined {
40:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
41:     if (!entry || !entry.inCache) return undefined;
42: 
43:     // Refresh LRU order (delete and re-append in insertion-ordered Map)
44:     this.entries.delete(key);
45:     this.entries.set(key, entry);
46:     return entry.url;
47:   }
48: 
49:   /**
50:    * Pins/retains a resource so it is not revoked while mounted in a consumer.
51:    * Returns a release function that must be called when the consumer unmounts.
52:    */
53:   retain(key: string): () => void {
54:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
55:     if (!entry) return () => {};
56:     entry.refCount++;
57:     let done = false;
58:     return () => {
59:       if (done) return;
60:       done = true;
61:       entry.refCount = Math.max(0, entry.refCount - 1);
62:       if (!entry.inCache && entry.refCount <= 0) {
63:         if (this.entries.get(key) === entry) this.entries.delete(key);
64:         try {
65:           URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
66:         } catch {
67:           /* Ignore revocation errors */
68:         }
69:       }
70:     };
71:   }
72: 
73:   /**
74:    * Decrements consumer reference count for a key. If the resource was already evicted
75:    * from the cache and no consumers remain, revokes the URL immediately.
76:    */
77:   release(key: string): void {
78:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
79:     if (!entry) return;
80:     entry.refCount = Math.max(0, entry.refCount - 1);
81:     if (!entry.inCache && entry.refCount <= 0) {
82:       if (this.entries.get(key) === entry) this.entries.delete(key);
83:       try {
84:         URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
85:       } catch {
86:         /* Ignore revocation errors */
87:       }
88:     }
89:   }
90: 
91:   /**
92:    * Stores an object URL. If key already exists with a different URL, revokes the old URL.
93:    * If capacity is exceeded, evicts the oldest entry from the cache.
94:    */
95:   set(key: string, url: string): void {
96:     const existing = this.entries.get(key);
97:     if (existing) {
98:       this.entries.delete(key);
99:       if (existing.url !== url) {
100:         existing.inCache = false;
101:         if (existing.refCount <= 0) {
102:           try {
103:             URL.revokeObjectURL(existing.url);
104:           } catch {
105:             /* Ignore revocation errors */
106:           }
107:         }
108:       } else {
109:         // Re-insert existing entry at MRU position
110:         existing.inCache = true;
111:         this.entries.set(key, existing);
112:         return;
113:       }
114:     } else if (this.size >= this.maxEntries) {
115:       // Find oldest entry in cache
116:       for (const [oldKey, oldEntry] of this.entries.entries()) {
117:         if (oldEntry.inCache) {
118:           oldEntry.inCache = false;
119:           if (oldEntry.refCount <= 0) {
120:             this.entries.delete(oldKey);
121:             try {
122:               URL.revokeObjectURL(oldEntry.url);
123:             } catch {
124:               /* Ignore revocation errors */
125:             }
126:           }
127:           break;
128:         }
129:       }
130:     }
131: 
132:     this.entries.set(key, {
133:       url,
134:       refCount: 0,
135:       inCache: true,
136:     });
137:   }
138: 
139:   has(key: string): boolean {
140:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
141:     return Boolean(entry && entry.inCache);
142:   }
143: 
144:   /**
145:    * Explicitly evicts and revokes a single cached URL.
146:    */
147:   revoke(key: string): void {
148:     const entry = this.entries.get(key);
149:     if (entry) {
150:       entry.inCache = false;
151:       if (entry.refCount <= 0) {
152:         this.entries.delete(key);
153:         try {
154:           URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
155:         } catch {
156:           /* Ignore revocation errors */
157:         }
158:       }
159:     }
160:   }
161: 
162:   /**
163:    * Evicts all entries from cache.
164:    * Unpinned URLs are revoked immediately. Pinned URLs are revoked once released.
165:    */
166:   clear(): void {
167:     for (const [key, entry] of Array.from(this.entries.entries())) {
168:       entry.inCache = false;
169:       if (entry.refCount <= 0) {
170:         this.entries.delete(key);
171:         try {
172:           URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
173:         } catch {
174:           /* Ignore revocation errors */
175:         }
176:       }
177:     }
178:   }
179: }
180: 
181: export function getAdaptivePoolSize(): number {
182:   if (typeof navigator === 'undefined') return 32;
183:   const nav = navigator as Navigator & { deviceMemory?: number; hardwareConcurrency?: number };
184:   const mem = nav.deviceMemory ?? 4;
185:   const cores = nav.hardwareConcurrency ?? 4;
186:   if (mem <= 2 || cores <= 2) return 12;
187:   if (mem <= 4 || cores <= 4) return 24;
188:   if (mem >= 8 && cores >= 8) return 64;
189:   return 40;
190: }
191: 
192: export const defaultObjectUrlPool = new ObjectUrlPool(getAdaptivePoolSize());
193: 
194: export function getObjectUrl(key: string): string | undefined {
195:   return defaultObjectUrlPool.get(key);
196: }
197: 
198: export function storeObjectUrl(key: string, url: string): void {
199:   defaultObjectUrlPool.set(key, url);
200: }
201: 
202: export function retainObjectUrl(key: string): () => void {
203:   return defaultObjectUrlPool.retain(key);
204: }
205: 
206: export function releaseObjectUrl(key: string): void {
207:   defaultObjectUrlPool.release(key);
208: }
209: 
210: export function clearObjectUrlPool(): void {
211:   defaultObjectUrlPool.clear();
212: }

10: let observer: IntersectionObserver | undefined;
11: 
12: function observe(element: Element, callback: (visible: boolean) => void) {
13:   observer ??= new IntersectionObserver(
14:     (entries) => {
15:       for (const entry of entries) watched.get(entry.target)?.(entry.isIntersecting);
16:     },
17:     { rootMargin: '180% 0px' },
18:   );
19:   watched.set(element, callback);
20:   observer.observe(element);
21:   return () => {
22:     watched.delete(element);
23:     observer?.unobserve(element);
24:     if (!watched.size) {
25:       observer?.disconnect();
26:       observer = undefined;
27:     }
28:   };
29: }

164:     const load = () => {
165:       if (objectUrl || !wanted) return;
166:       const taskKey = `${mangaId}:${mangaUpdatedAt}:${pagePath}:${lowQuality ? 'low' : 'full'}`;
167: 
168:       // Check LRU Object URL Pool first for instant scroll-back
169:       const cached = getObjectUrl(taskKey);
170:       if (cached) {
171:         if (unpinTask) unpinTask();
172:         unpinTask = retainObjectUrl(taskKey);
173:         objectUrl = cached;
174:         setUrl(cached);
175:         setError('');
176:         return;
177:       }
178: 
179:       const cancel = enqueueImage({
180:         key: taskKey,
181:         priority,
182:         run: async (signal) => {
183:           try {
184:             if (!live || !wanted || signal.aborted) return;
185:             assertImageDimensions(pageWidth, pageHeight);
186: 
187:             const isLowQualityNeeded =
188:               lowQuality &&
189:               typeof matchMedia === 'function' &&
190:               matchMedia('(max-width:700px)').matches &&
191:               pageWidth > 1000 &&
192:               pageWidth * pageHeight <= limits.imagePixels &&
193:               Math.max(pageWidth, pageHeight) <= limits.imageSide;
194: 
195:             if (
196:               isLowQualityNeeded &&
197:               desktopAPI.isDesktop &&
198:               desktopAPI.generateImageVariant &&
199:               m.localPath
200:             ) {
201:               try {
202:                 const targetHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round((pageHeight * 1000) / pageWidth));
203:                 const sep = m.localPath.includes('\\') ? '\\' : '/';
204:                 const absPath =
205:                   m.localPath.replace(/[/\\]+$/, '') +
206:                   sep +
207:                   page.path.replace(/^[/\\]+/, '').split('/').join(sep);
208:                 const variantRes = await desktopAPI.generateImageVariant(
209:                   absPath,
210:                   1000,
211:                   targetHeight,
212:                 );
213:                 if (variantRes?.url) {
214:                   if (live && wanted && !signal.aborted) {
215:                     setUrl(variantRes.url);
216:                     setError('');
217:                   }
218:                   return;
219:                 }
220:               } catch {
221:                 /* Fallback to reading file and downscaling */
222:               }
223:             }
224: 
225:             let blob: Blob = await fileFor(m, page);
226:             if (!live || !wanted || signal.aborted) return;
227: 
228:             if (isLowQualityNeeded) {
229:               const targetHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round((pageHeight * 1000) / pageWidth));
230:               try {
231:                 blob = await downscaleImage(blob, 1000, targetHeight, signal);
232:               } catch {
233:                 /* Use original unmodified blob if downscaling is unavailable or fails */
234:               }
235:             }
236: 
237:             if (live && wanted && !signal.aborted) {
238:               objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
239:               storeObjectUrl(taskKey, objectUrl);
240:               if (unpinTask) unpinTask();
241:               unpinTask = retainObjectUrl(taskKey);
242:               setUrl(objectUrl);
243:               setError('');
244:             }
245:           } catch (e) {
246:             if (live && wanted && !signal.aborted) {
247:               const message = errorMessage(e);
248:               setError(message);
249:               reportRef.current(message);
250:             }
251:           }
252:         },
253:         onComplete: () => {
254:           if (!live || !wanted) return;
255:           const cached = getObjectUrl(taskKey);
256:           if (cached) {
257:             if (unpinTask) unpinTask();
258:             unpinTask = retainObjectUrl(taskKey);
259:             objectUrl = cached;
260:             setUrl(cached);
261:             setError('');
262:           }
263:         },
264:         onError: (err) => {
265:           if (!live || !wanted) return;
266:           const message = errorMessage(err);
267:           setError(message);
268:           reportRef.current(message);
269:         },
270:       });
271: 
272:       if (!cancel) {
273:         const message = `Image queue is full (${limits.queuedJobs} waiting). Retry this page shortly.`;
274:         setError(message);
275:         reportRef.current(message);
276:         return;
277:       }
278:       cancelTask = cancel;
279:     };
280: 
281:     const stop = host.current
282:       ? observe(host.current, (v) => {
283:         wanted = v;
284:         if (v) load();
285:         else release();
286:       })
287:       : () => { };
288: 
289:     return () => {
290:       live = false;
291:       stop();
292:       release();
293:     };
294:   }, [
295:     mangaId,
296:     mangaUpdatedAt,
297:     mangaHandle,
298:     mangaLocalPath,
299:     pagePath,
300:     pageWidth,
301:     pageHeight,
302:     page.modified,
303:     Boolean(dimensionsPending),
304:     lowQuality,
305:     retry,
306:     useBlobFallback,
307:     priority,
308:   ]);
309: 
310:   return (
311:     <div
312:       ref={host}
313:       data-page={index}
314:       className="reader-page"
315:       style={
316:         {
317:           aspectRatio: `${page.width}/${page.height}`,
318:           '--native-width': page.width + 'px',
319:           '--page-ratio': page.width / page.height,
320:         } as CSSProperties
321:       }
322:     >
323:       {page.dimensionsPending ? (
324:         <button className="page-retry" onClick={retryPreparation}>
325:           Page {index + 1}: Dimensions unavailable. Retry preparation.
326:         </button>
327:       ) : error ? (
328:         <button
329:           className="page-retry"
330:           onClick={() => {
331:             setError('');
332:             setUseBlobFallback(false);
333:             setRetry((x) => x + 1);
334:           }}
335:         >
336:           Page {index + 1}: {error} Tap to retry.
337:         </button>
338:       ) : url ? (
339:         <img
340:           src={url}
341:           alt={`Page ${index + 1}: ${page.name}`}
342:           draggable={false}
343:           decoding="async"
344:           onError={() => {
345:             if (!useBlobFallback && desktopAPI.isDesktop && mangaLocalPath) {
346:               // Gracefully fall back to the blob IPC path before showing an error
347:               setUseBlobFallback(true);
348:               return;
349:             }
350:             const message = `Page ${index + 1} (${page.name}): This browser could not decode the image. It may be damaged, unsupported, or too large for this device. Try another browser or a smaller copy of this image.`;
351:             setError(message);
352:             reportRef.current(message);
353:           }}
354:         />
355:       ) : (
356:         <span className="page-number-placeholder">{index + 1}</span>
357:       )}
358:     </div>
359:   );
360: }

362: function arePropsEqual(prev: PageImageProps, next: PageImageProps): boolean {
363:   const prevDist = Math.min(10, Math.abs(prev.index - (prev.activePage ?? prev.index)));
364:   const nextDist = Math.min(10, Math.abs(next.index - (next.activePage ?? next.index)));
365:

367:     prev.index === next.index &&
368:     prevDist === nextDist &&
369:     prev.lowQuality === next.lowQuality &&

371:     prev.m.updatedAt === next.m.updatedAt &&

377:     prev.page.name === next.page.name &&
378:     prev.page.modified === next.page.modified &&
379:     Boolean(prev.page.dimensionsPending) === Boolean(next.page.dimensionsPending)

1: /**
2:  * Normalizes absolute or system paths, preserving leading `//` for UNC network paths
3:  * while converting backslashes to forward slashes and stripping redundant slashes.
4:  */
5: export function normalizePath(path: string): string {
6:   if (!path) return '';
7:   const isUnc = path.startsWith('\\\\') || path.startsWith('//');
8:   const converted = path.replace(/\\/g, '/');
9:   if (isUnc) {
10:     const withoutUncPrefix = converted.replace(/^\/\/+/, '');
11:     const cleaned = withoutUncPrefix.replace(/\/+/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
12:     return '//' + cleaned;
13:   }
14:   return converted.replace(/\/+/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
15: }
16: 
17: /**
18:  * Normalizes a logical relative manga/page path.
19:  * Strips leading/trailing slashes and collapses internal redundant slashes.
20:  */
21: export function normalizeRelativePath(path: string): string {
22:   if (!path) return '';
23:   return path.replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^\/+/, '').replace(/\/+/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
24: }
25: 
26: /** Keep native-script letters while removing emoji, unsafe punctuation and Latin accents. */
27: export function folderSlug(name: string) {
28:   return (
29:     name
30:       .normalize('NFKD')
31:       .replace(/[0-9#*]\uFE0F?\u20E3/g, '')
32:       .replace(
33:         /[\p{Extended_Pictographic}\p{Regional_Indicator}\p{Emoji_Modifier}\uFE0F\u200D\u{E0020}-\u{E007F}]/gu,
34:         '',
35:       )
36:       .replace(/([A-Za-z])\p{M}+/gu, '$1')
37:       .toLowerCase()
38:       .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\p{M}]+/gu, '-')
39:       .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
40:       .slice(0, 100)
41:       .replace(/-+$/, '')
42:       .normalize('NFC') || 'manga'
43:   );
44: }
45: export function uniqueSlug(name: string, used: Set<string>) {
46:   const base = folderSlug(name);
47:   const reserved = ['read', 'assets', 'api', 'bookmarks', 'index.html'];
48:   // At most used.size + reserved.length candidates can collide.
49:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= used.size + reserved.length; attempt++) {
50:     const slug = attempt === 0 ? base : base + '-' + (attempt + 1);
51:     if (!used.has(slug) && !reserved.includes(slug)) return slug;
52:   }
53:   throw new Error('Could not allocate a unique title URL.');
54: }
55: export function mangaPath(
56:   m: { slug?: string; title: string; lastChapter: number },
57:   index = m.lastChapter,
58: ) {
59:   return '/' + encodeURIComponent(m.slug || folderSlug(m.title)) + '/ch' + (index + 1);
60: }
61: export function chapterIndex(segment: string | undefined, count: number, fallback: number) {
62:   const match = segment?.match(/^ch([1-9]\d*)$/);
63:   const n = match ? Number(match[1]) - 1 : fallback;
64:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.floor(n) : 0));
65: }
66: 
67: export function mangaDetailsPath(m: { slug?: string; title: string }) {
68:   return '/' + encodeURIComponent(m.slug || folderSlug(m.title)) + '/';
69: }

74: export function createPageStore(initialPage = 0): PageStore {
75:   let current = initialPage;
76:   const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
77:   return {
78:     getPage: () => current,
79:     setPage: (page: number) => {
80:       if (current === page) return;
81:       current = page;
82:       for (const listener of listeners) {
83:         listener();
84:       }
85:     },
86:     subscribe: (listener: () => void) => {
87:       listeners.add(listener);
88:       return () => listeners.delete(listener);
89:     },
90:   };
91: }
