352:     s,
353:     ci,
354:     ready: ready && prepared === ci,
355:     allowed,
356:     page: s.mode === 'single' ? singlePage : pageStore.getPage(),
357:     zoom,
358:   };
359:   const { groups, pageToGroup } = useMemo(() => {
360:     const result: number[][] = [];
361:     const p2g = new Int32Array(chapter.pages.length);
362:     for (let i = 0; i < chapter.pages.length;) {
363:       const groupIdx = result.length;
364:       if (
365:         !s.spread ||
366:         !wide ||
367:         (s.singleCover && i === 0) ||
368:         chapter.pages[i].width > chapter.pages[i].height ||
369:         i === chapter.pages.length - 1 ||
370:         chapter.pages[i + 1].width > chapter.pages[i + 1].height
371:       ) {
372:         p2g[i] = groupIdx;
373:         result.push([i++]);
374:       } else {
375:         p2g[i] = groupIdx;
376:         p2g[i + 1] = groupIdx;
377:         result.push([i, i + 1]);
378:         i += 2;
379:       }
380:     }
381:     return { groups: result, pageToGroup: p2g };
382:   }, [chapter, s.spread, s.singleCover, wide]);
383:   const pageToGroupRef = useRef(pageToGroup);
384:   pageToGroupRef.current = pageToGroup;
385:   const groupIndex =
386:     s.mode === 'single'
387:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(groups.length - 1, pageToGroup[singlePage] ?? 0))
388:       : 0;
389:   const visible = s.mode === 'single' ? groups[groupIndex] || [] : [];
390:   const groupsRef = useRef(groups);
391:   groupsRef.current = groups;
392:   useEffect(() => {
393:     let live = true;
394:     void access(manga).then((v) => live && setAllowed(v));
395:     return () => {
396:       live = false;
397:     };
398:   }, [manga]);
399:   const { dirty, lastSave, snapshotRevision, flush, capture } = useProgressSave({
400:     metadata,
401:     chapterRef,
402:     position,
403:     latest,
404:     offsets,
405:     pageToGroupRef,
406:     groupsRef,
407:     edgeProgress,
408:     restoring,
409:     singlePage,
410:     pageStoreRef,
411:     setReadRevision,
412:     notify,
413:   });
414:   function updateWindow(force = false, targetPage?: number) {
415:     if (
416:       latest.current.s.mode !== 'continuous' ||
417:       chapter.pages.length <= VIRTUALIZATION_THRESHOLD
418:     ) {
419:       setWindowRange((prev) => {
420:         if (prev.startIndex === 0 && prev.endIndex === Math.max(0, chapter.pages.length - 1)) {
421:           return prev;
422:         }
423:         return {
424:           startIndex: 0,
425:           endIndex: Math.max(0, chapter.pages.length - 1),
426:           topSpacer: 0,
427:           bottomSpacer: 0,
428:         };
429:       });
430:       return;
431:     }
432:     const y =
433:       targetPage !== undefined && offsets.current[targetPage]
434:         ? offsets.current[targetPage].top
435:         : window.scrollY;
436:     const h = window.innerHeight;
437:     if (!force && Math.abs(y - lastWindowScrollY.current) < h * 1.0) {
438:       return;
439:     }
440:     lastWindowScrollY.current = y;
441:     const next = getWindowRange(offsets.current, y, h, 3);
442:     setWindowRange((prev) => {
443:       if (
444:         prev.startIndex === next.startIndex &&
445:         prev.endIndex === next.endIndex &&
446:         Math.abs(prev.topSpacer - next.topSpacer) < 2 &&
447:         Math.abs(prev.bottomSpacer - next.bottomSpacer) < 2
448:       ) {
449:         return prev;
450:       }
451:       return next;
452:     });
453:   }
454:   function measure(recomputeOnly = false) {
455:     if (!recomputeOnly) {
456:       const nodes = content.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-page]');
457:       if (nodes) {
458:         for (const el of nodes) {
459:           const p = Number(el.dataset.page);
460:           const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
461:           if (rect.height > 0) {
462:             measuredHeights.current.set(p, rect.height);
463:           }
464:         }
465:       }
466:     }
467:     const containerWidth = content.current?.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
468:     const viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;
469:     offsets.current = computeOffsets(
470:       chapter.pages,
471:       {
472:         containerWidth,
473:         viewportHeight,
474:         layout: latest.current.s.layout,
475:         maxWidth: latest.current.s.maxWidth,
476:         zoom: latest.current.zoom,
477:       },
478:       measuredHeights.current,
479:     );
480:   }
481:   function restore(anchor: Position) {
482:     const index = Math.max(0, Math.min(offsets.current.length - 1, anchor.page));
483:     const o = offsets.current[index];
484:     if (o) window.scrollTo({ top: o.top + o.height * anchor.fraction, behavior: 'instant' });
485:     position.current = { ...anchor };
486:     playbackRevision.current++;
487:     restoring.current = false;
488:     updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
489:   }
490:   async function goChapter(index: number, reset = false) {
491:     if (index < 0 || index >= manga.chapters.length) return;
492:     capture();
493:     if (index === ci + 1) {
494:       metadata.current.chapters[ci].read = true;
495:       dirty.current = true;
496:       snapshotRevision.current++;
497:       setReadRevision((v) => v + 1);
498:     }
499:     await flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
500:     // Suspend playback synchronously until the destination has been measured/restored.
501:     restoring.current = true;
502:     playbackRevision.current++;
503:     if (reset)
504:       metadata.current.chapters[index].position = {
505:         page: 0,
506:         path: metadata.current.chapters[index].pages[0]?.path,
507:         fraction: 0,
508:       };
509:     clearImageQueue();
510:     clearObjectUrlPool();
511:     if (index === ci) {
512:       pendingRestore.current = metadata.current.chapters[index].position || {
513:         page: 0,
514:         fraction: 0,
515:       };
516:       setSinglePage(0);
517:       pageStore.setPage(0);
518:       setZoom(1);
519:       measuredHeights.current.clear();
520:       requestAnimationFrame(() => {
521:         measure();
522:         updateWindow(true, 0);
523:         restore(pendingRestore.current!);
524:         capture();
525:       });
526:     } else navigate(mangaPath(manga, index));
527:     endHandled.current = false;
528:     setEnded(false);
529:   }
530:   function goPage(n: number, user = true) {
531:     const count = chapter.pages.length;
532:     n = Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, Math.floor(n) || 0));
533:     if (user) pause();
534:     setEnded(false);
535:     endHandled.current = false;
536:     if (s.mode === 'single') {
537:       const gIndex = pageToGroupRef.current[n] ?? 0;
538:       const group = groupsRef.current[gIndex] || [n];
539:       n = group[0];
540:       position.current = { page: n, path: chapter.pages[n].path, fraction: 0 };
541:       metadata.current.chapters[ci].position = { ...position.current };
542:       pendingRestore.current = { ...position.current };
543:       setSinglePage(n);
544:       pageStore.setPage(n);
545:       setZoom(1);
546:       dirty.current = true;
547:       snapshotRevision.current++;
548:       if (edgeProgress.current) {
549:         const progress = (Math.max(...group) + 1) / chapter.pages.length;
550:         edgeProgress.current.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress))})`;
551:       }
552:       if (performance.now() - lastSave.current >= 1500) {
553:         void flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
554:       }
555:     } else {
556:       // A chapter's saved-position restore may still be queued when the user jumps.
557:       // Replace that anchor as well, so it cannot later undo the user's selection.
558:       const anchor = { page: n, path: chapter.pages[n].path, fraction: 0 };
559:       pendingRestore.current = anchor;
560:       pageStore.setPage(n);
561:       measure();
562:       updateWindow(true, n);
563:       restore(anchor);
564:       capture();
565:     }
566:   }
567:   function step(direction: number) {
568:     const list = groupsRef.current;
569:     const index = Math.max(
570:       0,
571:       Math.min(list.length - 1, pageToGroupRef.current[latest.current.page] ?? 0),
572:     );
573:     const next = list[index + direction];
574:     if (next) goPage(next[0]);
575:   }
576:   const actions = useRef({
577:     capture,
578:     flush,
579:     goChapter,
580:     goPage,
581:     step,
582:     pause: () => { },
583:     updateWindow,
584:     measure,
585:   });
586:   actions.current = {
587:     capture,
588:     flush,
589:     goChapter,
590:     goPage,
591:     step,
592:     pause: () => { },
593:     updateWindow,
594:     measure,
595:   };
596: 
597:   const {
598:     keysHeld,
599:     onKeyDown: key,
600:     onKeyUp: keyup,
601:   } = useReaderKeys({
602:     latest,
603:     actions,
604:     setPlaying: (v) => setPlaying(v),
605:     setBars,
606:     toggleBookmark: () => toggleBookmark(),
607:   });
608: 
609:   const { playing, setPlaying, ended, setEnded, endHandled, pauseUntil, playbackRevision, pause } =
610:     useAutoplay({
611:       allowed,
612:       ready,
613:       prepared,
614:       ci,
615:       manga,
616:       settings: s,
617:       offsets,
618:       groupsRef,
619:       pageToGroupRef,
620:       chapterRef,
621:       restoring,
622:       held,
623:       keysHeld,
624:       latest,
625:       actions,
626:       setBars,
627:       notify,
628:     });
629:   actions.current.pause = pause;
630:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
631:     if (!allowed || prepared !== ci) {
632:       setReady(false);
633:       return;
634:     }
635:     setReady(false);
636:     restoring.current = true;
637:     chapterRef.current = ci;
638:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[ci];
639:     const anchor = matchingPosition(c.position, c.pages) || {
640:       page: 0,
641:       path: c.pages[0].path,
642:       fraction: 0,
643:     };
644:     position.current = anchor;
645:     pendingRestore.current = anchor;
646:     setSinglePage(anchor.page);
647:     pageStore.setPage(anchor.page);
648:     setZoom(1);
649:     setEnded(false);
650:     endHandled.current = false;
651:     measuredHeights.current.clear();
652:     const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
653:       measure();
654:       updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
655:       restore(pendingRestore.current || anchor);
656:       setReady(true);
657:     });
658:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
659:   }, [ci, allowed, prepared]);
660:   const prevLayoutProps = useRef({
661:     mode: s.mode,
662:     layout: s.layout,
663:     maxWidth: s.maxWidth,
664:     spread: s.spread,
665:     singleCover: s.singleCover,
666:     direction: s.direction,
667:     zoom,
668:     wide,
669:   });
670:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
671:     if (!allowed || !ready) return;
672:     const prev = prevLayoutProps.current;
673:     const layoutUnchanged =
674:       prev.mode === s.mode &&
675:       prev.layout === s.layout &&
676:       prev.spread === s.spread &&
677:       prev.singleCover === s.singleCover &&
678:       prev.direction === s.direction &&
679:       prev.wide === wide;
680: 
681:     if (layoutUnchanged) {
682:       if (prev.zoom !== zoom || prev.maxWidth !== s.maxWidth) {
683:         if (prev.zoom !== zoom && prev.zoom > 0) {
684:           const scale = zoom / prev.zoom;
685:           for (const [k, v] of measuredHeights.current) {
686:             measuredHeights.current.set(k, Math.max(1, Math.round(v * scale)));
687:           }
688:         }
689:         // When only zoom or max-width changes, recompute offsets from existing measured heights
690:         measure(true);
691:         const anchor = pendingRestore.current || position.current;
692:         updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
693:         if (latest.current.s.mode === 'continuous') {
694:           restore(anchor);
695:         }
696:         pendingRestore.current = undefined;
697:         capture();
698:       }
699:       prevLayoutProps.current = {
700:         mode: s.mode,
701:         layout: s.layout,
702:         maxWidth: s.maxWidth,
703:         spread: s.spread,
704:         singleCover: s.singleCover,
705:         direction: s.direction,
706:         zoom,
707:         wide,
708:       };
709:       return;
710:     }
711: 
712:     prevLayoutProps.current = {
713:       mode: s.mode,
714:       layout: s.layout,
715:       maxWidth: s.maxWidth,
716:       spread: s.spread,
717:       singleCover: s.singleCover,
718:       direction: s.direction,
719:       zoom,
720:       wide,
721:     };
722: 
723:     restoring.current = true;
724:     measuredHeights.current.clear();
725:     const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
726:       const anchor = pendingRestore.current || position.current;
727:       measure();
728:       updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
729:       restore(anchor);
730:       pendingRestore.current = undefined;
731:       capture();
732:     });
733:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
734:   }, [
735:     s.mode,
736:     s.layout,
737:     s.maxWidth,
738:     s.spread,
739:     s.singleCover,
740:     s.direction,
741:     zoom,
742:     wide,
743:     s.mode === 'single' ? singlePage : 0,
744:     ready,
745:   ]);
746:   useEffect(() => {
747:     if (!ready || !content.current) return;
748:     let frame = 0;
749:     let width = innerWidth,
750:       height = innerHeight;
751:     const resize = () => {
752:       if (window.visualViewport && window.visualViewport.scale > 1.01) return;
753:       const changed = width !== innerWidth || height !== innerHeight;
754:       width = innerWidth;
755:       height = innerHeight;
756:       if (!changed) return;
757:       const anchor = { ...position.current };
758:       restoring.current = true;
759:       setWide(innerWidth >= 700);
760:       measuredHeights.current.clear();
761:       cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
762:       frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
763:         measure();
764:         updateWindow(true, anchor.page);
765:         restore(anchor);
766:         actions.current.capture();
767:       });
768:     };
769:     window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
770:     return () => {
771:       window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
772:       cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
773:     };
774:   }, [ready]);
775:   useEffect(() => {
776:     if (!ready) return;
777:     let frame = 0;
778:     const scroll = () => {
779:       if (restoring.current) return;
780:       if (!frame)
781:         frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
782:           frame = 0;
783:           if (latest.current.s.autoHideBars) {
784:             const y = pointer.current;
785:             setBars((old) => {
786:               const top = y >= 0 && y < 60,
787:                 bottom = y > innerHeight - 60;
788:               return old.top === top && old.bottom === bottom ? old : { top, bottom };
789:             });
790:           }
791:           actions.current.capture();
792:           actions.current.updateWindow();
793:         });
794:     };
795:     const move = (e: PointerEvent) => {
796:       pointer.current = e.clientY;
797:       if (held.current.size) actions.current.pause();
798:       if (e.clientY < 60 || e.clientY > innerHeight - 60)
799:         setBars({ top: e.clientY < 60, bottom: e.clientY > innerHeight - 60 });
800:     };
801:     const down = (e: PointerEvent) => {
802:       held.current.add(e.pointerId);
803:       actions.current.pause();
804:     };
805:     const up = (e: PointerEvent) => {
806:       held.current.delete(e.pointerId);
807:       actions.current.pause();
808:     };
809:     const wheel = () => actions.current.pause();
810:     const blur = () => {
811:       held.current.clear();
812:       keysHeld.current.clear();
813:       void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
814:     };
815:     const leave = () => {
816:       pointer.current = -1;
817:     };
818:     const save = () => {
819:       actions.current.capture();
820:       void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
821:     };
822:     const update = (e: Event) => {
823:       actions.current.capture();
824:       (e as CustomEvent<Promise<unknown>[]>).detail.push(actions.current.flush());
825:     };
826:     const interval = setInterval(() => {
827:       if (dirty.current) void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
828:     }, 1500);
829:     window.addEventListener('scroll', scroll, { passive: true });
830:     window.addEventListener('pointermove', move, { passive: true });
831:     window.addEventListener('pointerdown', down, { passive: true });
832:     window.addEventListener('pointerup', up, { passive: true });
833:     window.addEventListener('pointercancel', up, { passive: true });
834:     window.addEventListener('wheel', wheel, { passive: true });
835:     window.addEventListener('keydown', key);
836:     window.addEventListener('keyup', keyup);
837:     window.addEventListener('blur', blur);
838:     document.addEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
839:     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', save);
840:     window.addEventListener('pagehide', save);
841:     window.addEventListener('reader-before-update', update);
842:     requestAnimationFrame(() => actions.current.capture());
843:     return () => {
844:       clearInterval(interval);
845:       cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
846:       window.removeEventListener('scroll', scroll);
847:       window.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
848:       window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', down);
849:       window.removeEventListener('pointerup', up);
850:       window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', up);
851:       window.removeEventListener('wheel', wheel);
852:       window.removeEventListener('keydown', key);
853:       window.removeEventListener('keyup', keyup);
854:       window.removeEventListener('blur', blur);
855:       document.removeEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
856:       document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', save);
857:       window.removeEventListener('pagehide', save);
858:       window.removeEventListener('reader-before-update', update);
859:     };
860:   }, [ready]);
861:   useEffect(
862:     () => () => {
863:       void actions.current
864:         .flush()
865:         .then((res) => {
866:           if (res === 'saved') {
867:             refresh();
868:           }
869:         })
870:         .catch((e) => {
871:           console.warn('Failed to flush progress on unmount:', e);
872:         });
873:       clearImageQueue();
874:       clearObjectUrlPool();
875:     },
876:     [],
877:   );
878:   function markComplete() {
879:     capture();
880:     metadata.current.chapters[ci].read = true;
881:     dirty.current = true;
882:     snapshotRevision.current++;
883:     setReadRevision((v) => v + 1);
884:     void flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
885:   }
886:   function changeZoom(value: number) {
887:     capture();
888:     pendingRestore.current = { ...position.current };
889:     setZoom(Math.min(3, Math.max(0.5, value)));
890:   }
891:   useEffect(() => {
892:     const close = (e: PointerEvent) => {
893:       if ((e.target as HTMLElement).closest('[data-reader-dropdown]')) return;
894:       for (const panel of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLDetailsElement>(
895:         '.reader-bar details[open]',
896:       ))
897:         if (!panel.contains(e.target as Node)) panel.open = false;
898:     };
899:     const escape = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
900:       if (e.key === 'Escape')
901:         for (const panel of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLDetailsElement>(
902:           '.reader-bar details[open]',
903:         ))
904:           panel.open = false;
905:     };
906:     document.addEventListener('pointerdown', close);
907:     document.addEventListener('keydown', escape);
908:     return () => {
909:       document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', close);
910:       document.removeEventListener('keydown', escape);
911:     };
912:   }, []);
913:   function toggleBookmark() {
914:     actions.current.capture();
915:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[chapterRef.current];
916:     if (!c) return;
917:     const p = { ...position.current, timestamp: Date.now() };
918:     const marks = c.bookmarks || [];
919:     const exists = marks.findIndex(
920:       (b) => !b.mangaBookmark && (p.path ? b.path === p.path : b.page === p.page),
921:     );
922:     if (exists < 0 && marks.length >= limits.bookmarksPerChapter) {
923:       notify(
924:         `Bookmark limit is ${limits.bookmarksPerChapter} per chapter. Remove one before adding another.`,
925:       );
926:       return;
927:     }
928:     const op = exists >= 0 ? removeBookmark(manga.id, c.name, p) : addBookmark(manga.id, c.name, p);
929:     void op
930:       .then((updatedMarks) => {
931:         c.bookmarks = updatedMarks;
932:         setBookmarkRevision((v) => v + 1);
933:       })
934:       .catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
935:     notify(exists >= 0 ? 'Bookmark removed.' : 'Page bookmarked.');
936:   }
937: 
938:   const [, setDimRevision] = useState(0);
939:   const dimSaveTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
940:   useEffect(() => {
941:     return () => {
942:       if (dimSaveTimer.current) clearTimeout(dimSaveTimer.current);
943:     };
944:   }, []);
945:   const onDimensionsResolved = useCallback(
946:     (pageIndex: number, width: number, height: number) => {
947:       const c = metadata.current.chapters[ci];
948:       if (c && c.pages && c.pages[pageIndex]) {
949:         c.pages[pageIndex] = {
950:           ...c.pages[pageIndex],
951:           width,
952:           height,
953:           dimensionsPending: false,
954:         };
955:         setDimRevision((v) => v + 1);
956:         actions.current.measure(false);
957:         if (dimSaveTimer.current) clearTimeout(dimSaveTimer.current);
958:         dimSaveTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
959:           void saveManga(metadata.current).catch(() => { });
960:         }, 500);
961:       }
962:     },
963:     [ci],
964:   );
965: 
966:   if (allowed === null)
967:     return (
968:       <main className="reader-gate">
969:         <LoaderCircle className="spin" />
970:         <p>Checking folder access…</p>
971:       </main>
972:     );
973:   if (!allowed)
974:     return (
975:       <main className="reader-gate">
976:         <FolderSync size={32} />
977:         <h1>Your story is still here.</h1>
978:         <p>Re-link “{manga.title}” to continue from your saved page.</p>
979:         <p>
980:           {manga.handle
981:             ? 'Your browser needs permission to read this folder again. Choose “Allow on every visit” in the browser prompt, if offered, to remember access.'
982:             : 'This folder was added with temporary access. Choose the same folder again; your saved progress is kept. For remembered access, use Choose a folder in a browser that supports it.'}
983:         </p>
984:         <button
985:           className="primary"
986:           onClick={() =>
987:             void access(manga, true)
988:               .then((ok) => (ok ? setAllowed(true) : pick(manga)))
989:               .catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)))
990:           }
991:         >
992:           Re-link folder
993:         </button>
994:         <Link className="text-button" to="/">
995:           Back to library
996:         </Link>
997:       </main>
998:     );
999:   if (prepared !== ci)
1000:     return (
1001:       <main className="reader-gate">
1002:         <LoaderCircle className="spin" />

1: import type { Page, Settings } from './model';
2: 
3: export interface PageOffset {
4:   page: number;
5:   top: number;
6:   height: number;
7:   bottom: number;
8: }
9: 
10: export interface GeometryOptions {
11:   containerWidth: number;
12:   viewportHeight: number;
13:   layout: Settings['layout'];
14:   maxWidth: number;
15:   zoom: number;
16: }
17: 
18: export interface WindowRange {
19:   startIndex: number;
20:   endIndex: number;
21:   topSpacer: number;
22:   bottomSpacer: number;
23: }
24: 
25: /**
26:  * Calculates the rendered width and height of a page based on layout and container width.
27:  */
28: export function calculatePageDimensions(
29:   page: Pick<Page, 'width' | 'height'>,
30:   options: GeometryOptions,
31: ): { width: number; height: number } {
32:   const { containerWidth, viewportHeight, layout, maxWidth, zoom } = options;
33:   const safeZoom = Math.max(0.1, zoom || 1);
34:   const nativeWidth = Math.max(1, page.width) * safeZoom;
35:   const pageAspect = Math.max(1, page.width) / Math.max(1, page.height);
36: 
37:   let targetWidth: number;
38:   switch (layout) {
39:     case 'width':
40:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, nativeWidth);
41:       break;
42:     case 'height': {
43:       const byHeight = Math.max(1, viewportHeight) * pageAspect * safeZoom;
44:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, byHeight, nativeWidth);
45:       break;
46:     }
47:     case 'auto':
48:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, 920 * safeZoom, nativeWidth);
49:       break;
50:     case 'max':
51:     default: {
52:       const readingWidth = maxWidth > 0 ? maxWidth * safeZoom : containerWidth;
53:       targetWidth = Math.min(containerWidth, readingWidth, nativeWidth);
54:       break;
55:     }
56:   }
57: 
58:   targetWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(targetWidth));
59:   const targetHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(targetWidth / pageAspect));
60:   return { width: targetWidth, height: targetHeight };
61: }
62: 
63: /**
64:  * Computes prefix offsets for all pages in a chapter.
65:  * Uses actual measured heights where recorded; otherwise falls back to calculated height.
66:  */
67: export function computeOffsets(
68:   pages: Page[],
69:   options: GeometryOptions,
70:   measuredHeights?: Map<number, number>,
71: ): PageOffset[] {
72:   const offsets: PageOffset[] = [];
73:   let currentTop = 0;
74: 
75:   for (let i = 0; i < pages.length; i++) {
76:     const page = pages[i];
77:     let height = measuredHeights?.get(i);
78:     if (height === undefined || height <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(height)) {
79:       height = calculatePageDimensions(page, options).height;
80:     }
81:     const bottom = currentTop + height;
82:     offsets.push({
83:       page: i,
84:       top: currentTop,
85:       height,
86:       bottom,
87:     });
88:     currentTop = bottom;
89:   }
90: 
91:   return offsets;
92: }
93: 
94: export const VIRTUALIZATION_THRESHOLD = 8;
95: 
96: /**
97:  * Determines the range of page indexes [startIndex, endIndex] to render
98:  * in continuous scroll mode, given a scroll position and buffer size.
99:  */
100: export function getWindowRange(
101:   offsets: PageOffset[],
102:   scrollY: number,
103:   viewportHeight: number,
104:   bufferScreenCount: number = 3,
105:   threshold: number = VIRTUALIZATION_THRESHOLD,
106: ): WindowRange {
107:   const count = offsets.length;
108:   if (count <= threshold) {
109:     return {
110:       startIndex: 0,
111:       endIndex: Math.max(0, count - 1),
112:       topSpacer: 0,
113:       bottomSpacer: 0,
114:     };
115:   }
116: 
117:   const safeViewportHeight = Math.max(1, viewportHeight || 800);
118:   const buffer = safeViewportHeight * Math.max(1, bufferScreenCount);
119:   const minY = Math.max(0, scrollY - buffer);
120:   const maxY = scrollY + safeViewportHeight + buffer;
121: 
122:   // Binary search for first page where bottom >= minY
123:   let lo = 0;
124:   let hi = count - 1;
125:   while (lo < hi) {
126:     const mid = Math.floor((lo + hi) / 2);
127:     if (offsets[mid].bottom >= minY) {
128:       hi = mid;
129:     } else {
130:       lo = mid + 1;
131:     }
132:   }
133:   const startIndex = Math.max(0, lo);
134: 
135:   // Binary search for last page where top <= maxY
136:   lo = startIndex;
137:   hi = count - 1;
138:   while (lo < hi) {
139:     const mid = Math.ceil((lo + hi) / 2);
140:     if (offsets[mid].top <= maxY) {
141:       lo = mid;
142:     } else {
143:       hi = mid - 1;
144:     }
145:   }
146:   const endIndex = Math.min(count - 1, Math.max(startIndex, lo));
147: 
148:   const topSpacer = offsets[startIndex].top;
149:   const bottomSpacer = offsets[count - 1].bottom - offsets[endIndex].bottom;
150: 
151:   return {
152:     startIndex,
153:     endIndex,
154:     topSpacer: Math.max(0, topSpacer),
155:     bottomSpacer: Math.max(0, bottomSpacer),
156:   };
157: }

367: export type LegacyReadResult =
368:   | { status: 'unavailable' }
369:   | { status: 'empty' }
370:   | { status: 'failed'; error: Error }
371:   | { status: 'data'; titles: Manga[]; settings?: Settings };

373: export async function readLegacyIndexedDB(): Promise<LegacyReadResult> {
374:   if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.indexedDB === 'undefined') {
375:     return { status: 'unavailable' };
376:   }
377: 
378:   return new Promise<LegacyReadResult>((resolve) => {
379:     let existed = true;
380:     let req: IDBOpenDBRequest;
381:     try {
382:       req = window.indexedDB.open('manga-reader');
383:     } catch (err) {
384:       return resolve({
385:         status: 'failed',
386:         error: err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
387:       });
388:     }
389: 
390:     req.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
391:       if (event.oldVersion === 0) {
392:         existed = false;
393:         try {
394:           req.transaction?.abort();
395:         } catch {
396:           // ignore abort error
397:         }
398:       }
399:     };
400: 
401:     req.onerror = () => {
402:       if (!existed) {
403:         return resolve({ status: 'empty' });
404:       }
405:       return resolve({
406:         status: 'failed',
407:         error: req.error ?? new Error('IndexedDB open error'),
408:       });
409:     };
410: 
411:     req.onsuccess = () => {
412:       const db = req.result;
413:       try {
414:         if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('manga')) {
415:           db.close();
416:           return resolve({ status: 'empty' });
417:         }
418: 
419:         const storeNames = ['manga'];
420:         if (db.objectStoreNames.contains('settings')) {
421:           storeNames.push('settings');
422:         }
423:         if (db.objectStoreNames.contains('progress')) {
424:           storeNames.push('progress');
425:         }
426: 
427:         const tx = db.transaction(storeNames, 'readonly');
428:         const mangaReq = tx.objectStore('manga').getAll();
429:         const settingsReq = db.objectStoreNames.contains('settings')
430:           ? tx.objectStore('settings').get('global')
431:           : null;
432:         const progressReq = db.objectStoreNames.contains('progress')
433:           ? tx.objectStore('progress').getAll()
434:           : null;
435: 
436:         tx.oncomplete = () => {
437:           db.close();
438:           try {
439:             const rawManga = (mangaReq.result || []) as Manga[];
440:             let settings: Settings | undefined = undefined;
441:             if (settingsReq?.result) {
442:               try {
443:                 settings = parseSettings(settingsReq.result);
444:               } catch {
445:                 // ignore malformed settings in legacy DB
446:               }
447:             }
448: 
449:             const rawProgress = (progressReq?.result || []) as any[];
450:             const progressByManga = new Map<string, Map<string, any>>();
451:             for (const p of rawProgress) {
452:               if (p && p.mangaId && p.chapter) {
453:                 let m = progressByManga.get(p.mangaId);
454:                 if (!m) {
455:                   m = new Map();
456:                   progressByManga.set(p.mangaId, m);
457:                 }
458:                 m.set(p.chapter, p);
459:               }
460:             }
461: 
462:             const titles: Manga[] = [];
463:             for (const item of rawManga) {
464:               if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') continue;
465:               const mangaProgress = progressByManga.get(item.id);
466:               if (mangaProgress && Array.isArray(item.chapters)) {
467:                 for (const ch of item.chapters) {
468:                   const prog = mangaProgress.get(ch.name);
469:                   if (prog) {
470:                     if (prog.read !== undefined) ch.read = prog.read;
471:                     if (prog.position) ch.position = prog.position;
472:                     if (prog.bookmarks) ch.bookmarks = prog.bookmarks;
473:                   }
474:                 }
475:               }
476:               titles.push(item);
477:             }
478: 
479:             if (titles.length === 0 && !settings) {
480:               return resolve({ status: 'empty' });
481:             }
482:             return resolve({ status: 'data', titles, settings });
483:           } catch (processErr) {
484:             return resolve({
485:               status: 'failed',
486:               error: processErr instanceof Error ? processErr : new Error(String(processErr)),
487:             });
488:           }
489:         };
490: 
491:         tx.onerror = () => {
492:           db.close();
493:           return resolve({
494:             status: 'failed',
495:             error: tx.error ?? new Error('IndexedDB transaction error'),
496:           });
497:         };
498:       } catch (err) {
499:         try {
500:           db.close();
501:         } catch { }
502:         return resolve({
503:           status: 'failed',
504:           error: err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
505:         });
506:       }
507:     };
508:   });
509: }

511: export async function ensureLibraryMigrated(idbRepo?: LibraryRepo): Promise<boolean> {
512:   if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop || !desktopAPI.isLibraryMigrated) {
513:     return false;
514:   }
515:   const alreadyMigrated = await desktopAPI.isLibraryMigrated();
516:   if (alreadyMigrated) {
517:     return true;
518:   }
519: 
520:   let titles: Manga[] = [];
521:   let settings: Settings | undefined;
522: 
523:   if (idbRepo) {
524:     try {
525:       titles = await idbRepo.allManga();
526:       settings = await idbRepo.getStoredSettings();
527:     } catch (err) {
528:       throw new Error(
529:         `Failed to read from provided migration repo: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`,
530:       );
531:     }
532:   } else {
533:     const legacyResult = await readLegacyIndexedDB();
534:     if (legacyResult.status === 'failed') {
535:       throw new Error(`Legacy IndexedDB migration read failed: ${legacyResult.error.message}`);
536:     }
537:     if (legacyResult.status === 'unavailable') {
538:       console.warn(
539:         '[ensureLibraryMigrated] IndexedDB unavailable in environment; skipping legacy migration',
540:       );
541:       return false;
542:     }
543:     if (legacyResult.status === 'data') {
544:       titles = legacyResult.titles;
545:       settings = legacyResult.settings;
546:     }
547:   }
548: 
549:   if (titles.length === 0 && !settings) {
550:     if (!desktopAPI.importLegacyLibrary) {
551:       throw new Error('desktopAPI.importLegacyLibrary is required on desktop');
552:     }
553:     await desktopAPI.importLegacyLibrary({ manga: [], settings: {} });
554:     return true;
555:   }
556: 
557:   const validated = validateBackup({
558:     version: 2,
559:     manga: titles,
560:     settings: settings ?? {},
561:   });
562: 
563:   if (!desktopAPI.importLegacyLibrary) {
564:     throw new Error('desktopAPI.importLegacyLibrary is required on desktop');
565:   }
566:   const ok = await desktopAPI.importLegacyLibrary(validated);
567:   if (!ok) {
568:     throw new Error('SQLite legacy library import failed');
569:   }
570: 
571:   return true;
572: }

46:       } catch {
47:         /* Fallback to navigator.storage */
48:       }
49:     }
50:     try {
51:       const estimate = await navigator.storage?.estimate?.();
52:       setUsage(estimate || null);
53:     } catch {
54:       setStorageError('Could not retrieve storage details.');
55:     }
56:   }
57:   useEffect(() => {
58:     if (open) {
59:       setConfirmAction(null);
60:       void updateStorage();
61:     }
62:   }, [open, library]);
63:   const change = <K extends keyof Settings>(key: K, value: Settings[K]) =>
64:     setSettings({ ...s, [key]: value });
65:   async function exportData() {
66:     const jsonStr = await exportBackup();
67:     if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveFileDialog) {
68:       const saved = await desktopAPI.saveFileDialog('manga-reader-backup.json', jsonStr);
69:       if (saved) {
70:         notify('Backup saved successfully.');
71:       }
72:       return;
73:     }
74:     const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([jsonStr], { type: 'application/json' }));
75:     const a = document.createElement('a');
76:     a.href = url;
77:     a.download = 'manga-reader-backup.json';
78:     a.click();
79:     setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
80:   }
81:   async function importData(file?: File) {
82:     if (!file) return;
83:     try {
84:       assertAtMost(file.size, limits.backupBytes, 'Backup size (bytes)');
85:       const text = await withinTime(file.text(), limits.ioTimeoutMs, 'Reading backup');
86:       const parsed = desktopAPI.isDesktop ? text : validateBackup(JSON.parse(text));
87:       const appliedSettings = await restoreBackup(parsed);
88:       setSettings(appliedSettings);
89:       await refresh();
90:       notify('Backup imported. Re-link folders when requested.');
91:     } catch (e) {
92:       notify(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not import backup.');
93:     }
94:   }
95:   async function clear() {
96:     if (!confirmAction) return;
97:     setClearing(true);
98:     try {
99:       if (confirmAction === 'progress') {
100:         await resetProgress();
101:       } else {
102:         await clearLibrary();
103:       }
104:       await refresh();
105:       notify(confirmAction === 'progress' ? 'Reading progress cleared.' : 'Manga data cleared.');
106:       setConfirmAction(null);
107:     } catch {
108:       notify('Could not clear data. Please try again.');
109:     } finally {
110:       setClearing(false);
111:     }
112:   }
113:   return (
114:     <Dialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={(v) => !v && onClose()}>
115:       <Dialog.Portal>
116:         <Dialog.Overlay className="dialog-overlay" />
117:         <Dialog.Content className="settings-panel tabbed-modal">
118:           <div className="modal-heading">
119:             <div className="dialog-heading">
120:               <Dialog.Title>Settings</Dialog.Title>
121:               <Dialog.Close asChild>
122:                 <button className="close-btn" aria-label="Close settings">
123:                   <X size={20} />
124:                 </button>
125:               </Dialog.Close>
126:             </div>
127:             <Dialog.Description>
128:               Make yourself comfortable. Changes save automatically.
129:             </Dialog.Description>
130:           </div>
131:           <div className="settings-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Settings categories">
132:             {tabs.map((name, i) => (
133:               <button
134:                 key={name}
135:                 type="button"
136:                 role="tab"
137:                 id={'settings-tab-' + name}
138:                 aria-controls={'settings-panel-' + name}
139:                 aria-selected={tab === name}
140:                 tabIndex={tab === name ? 0 : -1}
141:                 onClick={() => setTab(name)}
142:                 onKeyDown={(e) => {
143:                   let next = i;
144:                   if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') next = (i + 1) % tabs.length;
145:                   else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') next = (i + tabs.length - 1) % tabs.length;
146:                   else if (e.key === 'Home') next = 0;
147:                   else if (e.key === 'End') next = tabs.length - 1;
148:                   else return;
149:                   e.preventDefault();
150:                   setTab(tabs[next]);
151:                   document.getElementById('settings-tab-' + tabs[next])?.focus();
152:                 }}
153:               >
154:                 {name}
155:               </button>
156:             ))}
157:           </div>
158:           <div
159:             className="settings-tab-body"
160:             role="tabpanel"
161:             id={'settings-panel-' + tab}
162:             aria-labelledby={'settings-tab-' + tab}
163:             tabIndex={0}
164:           >
165:             {tab === 'Reading' && (
166:               <>
167:                 <section>
168:                   <h3>Pages & layout</h3>
169:                   <label>
170:                     Reading mode
171:                     <Dropdown
172:                       aria-label="Reading mode"
173:                       value={s.mode}
174:                       onChange={(e) => change('mode', e.target.value as Settings['mode'])}
175:                     >
176:                       <option value="continuous">Continuous scroll</option>
177:                       <option value="single">Single page</option>
178:                     </Dropdown>
179:                   </label>
180:                   <label>
181:                     Page fitting
182:                     <Dropdown
183:                       aria-label="Page fitting"
184:                       value={s.layout}
185:                       onChange={(e) => change('layout', e.target.value as Settings['layout'])}
186:                     >
187:                       <option value="max">Max-width</option>
188:                       <option value="width">Respect width</option>
189:                       <option value="height">Respect height</option>
190:                       <option value="auto">Auto</option>
191:                     </Dropdown>
192:                   </label>
193:                   <label>
194:                     Maximum width
195:                     <Dropdown
196:                       aria-label="Maximum width"
197:                       value={s.maxWidth}
198:                       onChange={(e) => change('maxWidth', Number(e.target.value))}
199:                     >
200:                       {[920, 1024, 1280, 0].map((n) => (
201:                         <option key={n} value={n}>
202:                           {n ? n + ' px' : 'Full width'}
203:                         </option>
204:                       ))}
205:                     </Dropdown>
206:                   </label>
207:                   <label>
208:                     Page-turn direction
209:                     <Dropdown
210:                       aria-label="Page-turn direction"
211:                       value={s.direction}
212:                       onChange={(e) => change('direction', e.target.value as Settings['direction'])}
213:                     >
214:                       <option value="ltr">Left to right</option>
215:                       <option value="rtl">Right to left</option>
216:                     </Dropdown>
217:                   </label>

219:                     Two-page spreads on wide screens
220:                     <input
221:                       type="checkbox"
222:                       checked={s.spread}

232:                     />
233:                   </label>

235:                     Spreads apply in single-page mode at widths of 700 pixels or more. Landscape
236:                     pages stay separate. Pinch zoom keeps native touch panning; swipes do not turn
237:                     pages while zoomed.
238:                   </p>

241:                     original proportions.

244:                 <section>

248:                     <Dropdown
249:                       aria-label={
250:                         s.mode === 'continuous' ? 'Seconds per screen' : 'Seconds per page'
251:                       }
252:                       value={s.customSeconds ? 'custom' : s.seconds}
253:                       onChange={(e) =>
254:                         setSettings({
255:                           ...s,
256:                           customSeconds: e.target.value === 'custom',
257:                           ...(e.target.value !== 'custom'
258:                             ? { seconds: Number(e.target.value) }
259:                             : {}),
260:                         })
261:                       }
262:                     >
263:                       {[2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
264:                         <option value={n} key={n}>
265:                           {n} seconds
266:                         </option>
267:                       ))}
268:                       <option value="custom">Custom</option>
269:                     </Dropdown>
270:                   </label>
271:                   {s.customSeconds && (
272:                     <label>
273:                       Custom seconds

275:                         aria-label={

277:                             ? 'Custom seconds per screen'
278:                             : 'Custom seconds per page'
279:                         }
280:                         type="number"
281:                         min="0.5"
282:                         max="120"
283:                         step="0.5"
284:                         value={s.seconds}
285:                         onChange={(e) =>
286:                           change(
287:                             'seconds',
288:                             Math.min(120, Math.max(0.5, Number(e.target.value) || 3)),
289:                           )
290:                         }
291:                       />
292:                     </label>
293:                   )}
294:                   <p className="subtle">
295:                     {s.mode === 'continuous'
296:                       ? 'Scrolls one screen height in this time, regardless of page length. More seconds means slower scrolling.'
297:                       : 'Turns to the next page or spread after this time.'}
298:                   </p>
299:                   <label>
300:                     At the end of playback
301:                     <Dropdown
302:                       aria-label="At the end of playback"
303:                       value={s.endAction}
304:                       onChange={(e) =>
305:                         setSettings({
306:                           ...s,
307:                           endAction: e.target.value as Settings['endAction'],
308:                           advance: e.target.value === 'next',
309:                         })
310:                       }
311:                     >
312:                       <option value="next">Next chapter</option>
313:                       <option value="stop">Stop</option>
314:                       <option value="replay">Replay chapter</option>
315:                     </Dropdown>
316:                   </label>
317:                   <label>
318:                     Resume after interaction
319:                     <Dropdown
320:                       aria-label="Resume after interaction"
321:                       value={s.resumeDelay}
322:                       onChange={(e) => change('resumeDelay', Number(e.target.value))}
323:                     >
324:                       {[1, 1.8, 3, 5, 10].map((n) => (
325:                         <option key={n} value={n}>
326:                           {n} seconds
327:                         </option>
328:                       ))}
329:                     </Dropdown>
330:                   </label>

475:                 </section>
476:               </>
477:             )}
478:             {tab === 'Storage' && (
479:               <>
480:                 <section>

483:                     {nativeStats ? (

486:                           <dt>Total storage used</dt>

492:                         </div>

495:                           <dd>{bytes(nativeStats.cache_bytes)}</dd>
496:                         </div>
497:                       </>
498:                     ) : (

500:                         <div>

565:                     </button>

568:                       ref={input}
569:                       type="file"
570:                       accept="application/json,.json"
571:                       onChange={(e) => {
572:                         void importData(e.target.files?.[0]);
573:                         e.target.value = '';
574:                       }}
575:                     />
576:                   </div>

578:                     JSON backups include library metadata, progress, and preferences and
579:                     bookmarks—not manga images, covers, or folder permissions. Import adds missing

581:                     another device.
582:                   </p>

591:                           : desktopAPI.isDesktop

621:                       >

623:                       </button>

628:                             await desktopAPI.clearThumbnails();
629:                             await updateStorage();
630:                             notify('Thumbnail cache cleared.');
631:                           } else {
632:                             notify('Thumbnail cache is managed by your browser storage.');
633:                           }
634:                         }}

636:                         Clear thumbnail cache
637:                       </button>
638:                     </div>

641:                     These actions leave your settings and thumbnail cache in place.

643:                 </section>

649:                       onClick={async () => {
650:                         const logs = getDiagnostics();
651:                         if (!logs.length) {
652:                           notify('No diagnostic entries recorded.');
653:                           return;
654:                         }
655:                         const text = logs
656:                           .map(
657:                             (d) =>
658:                               `[${new Date(d.timestamp).toISOString()}] [${d.level.toUpperCase()}] [${d.operation}] ${d.code ? `(${d.code}) ` : ''}${d.safeIdentifier ? `[${d.safeIdentifier}] ` : ''}${d.message}${d.durationMs ? ` (${d.durationMs}ms)` : ''}`,
659:                           )
660:                           .join('\n');
661:                         if (desktopAPI.isDesktop && desktopAPI.saveFileDialog) {
662:                           const saved = await desktopAPI.saveFileDialog(
663:                             'manga-reader-diagnostics.txt',
664:                             text,
665:                           );
666:                           if (saved) {
667:                             notify('Diagnostic log saved.');
668:                           }
669:                           return;
670:                         }
671:                         const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: 'text/plain' }));
672:                         const a = document.createElement('a');
673:                         a.href = url;
674:                         a.download = 'manga-reader-diagnostics.txt';
675:                         a.click();
676:                         setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
677:                       }}
678:                     >
679:                       <Download size={16} /> Export diagnostic log
680:                     </button>
681:                   </div>
682:                   <p className="subtle">
683:                     Keeps a bounded log of the last 50 operation events for troubleshooting without
684:                     retaining private files.
685:                   </p>
686:                 </section>
687:               </>
688:             )}

698:                   </p>
699:                 </section>
700:                 <section>
701:                   <h3>Organize your folders</h3>
702:                   <p>
703:                     Choose a title folder, a parent collection, or several dropped folders. Review

705:                     chapter; nested folder paths become chapter labels.
706:                   </p>
707:                   <p>
708:                     Natural sorting handles numbered chapters and decimals. You can choose filename
709:                     or reverse order in the import preview. Hidden/system files and non-image files
710:                     are ignored; inaccessible folders and unreadable images encountered during
711:                     initial preparation are listed in the import report. Empty folders contain no
712:                     pages and are omitted.
713:                   </p>
714:                   <p>
715:                     Add a file named thumbnail.png, thumbnail.jpg, or another supported image format
716:                     in the root for a cover. Otherwise, Reader uses the first readable page.
717:                   </p>
718:                 </section>
719:                 <section>
720:                   <h3>Faster, staged indexing</h3>
721:                   <p>
722:                     Reader discovers folders and prepares image sizes together in one scan. The
723:                     first five chapters are prepared before you confirm import. These measurements
724:                     stay temporary until you add the title; cancelling discards them. Changing
725:                     grouping or sorting updates the preparation before the Import button becomes
726:                     available.
727:                   </p>
728:                   <p>
729:                     Page dimensions keep scrolling, page fitting, two-page spreads, and saved
730:                     reading positions stable. Common image sizes are read from small file headers
731:                     instead of decoding the entire image. Other formats use the browser decoder,
732:                     with at most three checks running together.
733:                   </p>
734:                   <p>
735:                     After a title is saved, remaining chapters continue preparing quietly in the
736:                     background. Opening an unfinished chapter gives it priority. Completed
737:                     dimensions are cached locally; pending work resumes on a later visit when folder
738:                     access is available. Removing a title stops its background work. Unreadable
739:                     pages remain available for retry and show an error when opened.
740:                   </p>
741:                 </section>
742:                 <section>
743:                   <h3>Folder access & reading</h3>
744:                   <p>
745:                     In the browser’s folder permission prompt, choose “Allow on every visit”, if
746:                     offered, to avoid granting access each session. “Allow this time” is temporary.
747:                     The browser controls these choices; Reader cannot force permanent access or make
748:                     all options appear again.
749:                   </p>
750:                   <p>
751:                     To change a previous choice, open the browser’s address-bar site controls and
752:                     review its file or folder permissions. Then return to Reader and open a title to
753:                     request access again. Available options depend on your browser and previous
754:                     choices. Keep this site’s stored data to preserve your library and progress.
755:                   </p>
756:                   <p>
757:                     Where supported, direct folder selection can retain access with your permission.
758:                     Embedded views and other browsers use standard folder selection and need
759:                     re-linking after a reload. Saved indexing and progress remain.
760:                   </p>
761:                   <p>
762:                     When folders move or permission expires, use Re-link / re-index on the title.
763:                     Reader reuses unchanged dimensions and restores progress by page path. Renamed
764:                     chapters with uniquely matching page metadata can keep progress. A mismatched
765:                     selection requires explicit confirmation in the import preview.
766:                   </p>
767:                 </section>
768:                 <section>
769:                   <h3>Reader controls</h3>
770:                   <p>
771:                     The information icon beside the theme button lists keyboard shortcuts. In
772:                     single-page mode, swipe left or right to turn a page. With auto-hide enabled,
773:                     tap near a screen edge to bring controls back.
774:                   </p>
775:                 </section>
776:               </>
777:             )}
778:           </div>
779:         </Dialog.Content>
780:       </Dialog.Portal>
781:     </Dialog.Root>
782:   );

163: export interface PageImageResourceOptions {
164:   mangaId: string;
165:   pagePath: string;
166:   version?: string | number;
167:   transform?: 'full' | 'raw' | 'low' | 'preview' | string;
168:   localPath?: string;

171: export function getPageImageUrl(options: PageImageResourceOptions): string {
172:   const { mangaId, pagePath, version = 1, transform = 'full', localPath } = options;
173:   const encodedMangaId = encodeURIComponent(mangaId);
174:   const encodedVersion = encodeURIComponent(String(version));
175:   const encodedTransform = encodeURIComponent(transform);
176:   const normalizedPath = pagePath.replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^\/+/, '');
177:   const segments = normalizedPath.split('/').filter(Boolean).map(encodeURIComponent);
178:   const encodedPagePath = segments.join('/');
179: 
180:   let url = `http://mangaimg.localhost/page/${encodedMangaId}/${encodedVersion}/${encodedTransform}/${encodedPagePath}`;
181:   if (localPath) {
182:     url += `?root=${encodeURIComponent(localPath)}`;
183:   }
184:   return url;
185: }
186: 
187: export function getCoverImageUrl(mangaId: string, version?: string | number): string {
188:   const encodedMangaId = encodeURIComponent(mangaId);
189:   const ver =
190:     version !== undefined && version !== null ? `/${encodeURIComponent(String(version))}` : '';
191:   return `http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/${encodedMangaId}${ver}`;
192: }

205: export interface DbErrorPayload {
206:   code:
207:   | 'Sqlite'
208:   | 'Migration'
209:   | 'Lock'
210:   | 'Validation'
211:   | 'NotFound'
212:   | 'Conflict'
213:   | 'Task'
214:   | 'Io'
215:   | 'Serialization';
216:   message?: string;
217: }
218: 
219: export type TauriErrorPayload =
220:   FsErrorPayload | DbErrorPayload | { code: 'Unknown'; message: string };
221: 
222: export function parseFsError(err: unknown): FsErrorPayload | null {
223:   if (err && typeof err === 'object') {
224:     if ('code' in err && typeof (err as any).code === 'string') {
225:       return err as FsErrorPayload;
226:     }
227:   }
228:   if (typeof err === 'string') {
229:     try {
230:       const parsed = JSON.parse(err);
231:       if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && 'code' in parsed) {
232:         return parsed as FsErrorPayload;
233:       }
234:     } catch {
235:       // not JSON
236:     }
237:   }
238:   return null;
239: }
240: 
241: export function parseTauriError(err: unknown): TauriErrorPayload {
242:   const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
243:   if (fsErr) return fsErr;
244:   if (err && typeof err === 'object' && 'code' in err && typeof (err as any).code === 'string') {
245:     return err as DbErrorPayload;
246:   }
247:   if (typeof err === 'string') {
248:     try {
249:       const parsed = JSON.parse(err);
250:       if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && 'code' in parsed) {
251:         return parsed as DbErrorPayload;
252:       }
253:     } catch {
254:       // not JSON
255:     }
256:     return { code: 'Unknown', message: err };
257:   }
258:   if (err instanceof Error) {
259:     return { code: 'Unknown', message: err.message };
260:   }
261:   return { code: 'Unknown', message: String(err) };
262: }

376: export async function invokeCommand<K extends keyof TauriCommands>(
377:   cmd: K,
378:   args?: TauriCommands[K]['args'],
379:   options?: any,
380: ): Promise<TauriCommands[K]['return']> {
381:   try {
382:     return await invoke<TauriCommands[K]['return']>(cmd, args as any, options);
383:   } catch (err) {
384:     const parsed = parseTauriError(err);
385:     console.error(`[invokeCommand] ${cmd} failed:`, parsed);
386:     throw parsed;
387:   }
388: }

500: export class TauriDesktopBridge implements DesktopAPI {
501:   readonly isDesktop = true;
502:   readonly isTauri = true;
503: 
504:   async selectFolder(): Promise<string | null> {
505:     try {
506:       const selected = await invokeCommand('select_folder');
507:       return selected ?? null;
508:     } catch (err) {
509:       console.error('Tauri selectFolder failed:', parseFsError(err) ?? err);
510:       return null;
511:     }
512:   }
513: 
514:   async listFolder(folderPath: string, options?: ListFolderOptions): Promise<FolderEntry[]> {
515:     let unlisten: (() => void) | undefined;
516:     const scanId =
517:       options?.scanId ??
518:       (options?.signal || options?.onProgress
519:         ? `scan-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`
520:         : undefined);
521: 
522:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
523:     if (options?.signal && scanId) {
524:       if (options.signal.aborted) {
525:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
526:       }
527:       abortHandler = () => {
528:         void this.cancelScan(scanId);
529:       };
530:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
531:     }
532: 
533:     try {
534:       if (options?.onProgress && isTauri) {
535:         try {
536:           unlisten = await listen<ScanProgress>('scan-progress', (event) => {
537:             if (!scanId || event.payload.scanId === scanId) {
538:               options.onProgress?.(event.payload);
539:             }
540:           });
541:         } catch (err) {
542:           console.warn('Tauri scan-progress listen failed:', err);
543:         }
544:       }
545: 
546:       return await invokeCommand('list_folder', { folderPath, scanId });
547:     } catch (err) {
548:       const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
549:       if (fsErr?.code === 'Cancelled') {
550:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
551:       }
552:       console.error('Tauri listFolder failed:', fsErr ?? err);
553:       return [];
554:     } finally {
555:       if (abortHandler && options?.signal) {
556:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
557:       }
558:       unlisten?.();
559:     }
560:   }
561: 
562:   async discoverLibrary(
563:     folderPath: string,
564:     options?: ListFolderOptions,
565:   ): Promise<LibraryDiscovery | null> {
566:     let unlisten: (() => void) | undefined;
567:     const scanId =
568:       options?.scanId ??
569:       (options?.signal || options?.onProgress
570:         ? `scan-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`
571:         : undefined);
572: 
573:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
574:     if (options?.signal && scanId) {
575:       if (options.signal.aborted) {
576:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
577:       }
578:       abortHandler = () => {
579:         void this.cancelScan(scanId);
580:       };
581:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
582:     }
583: 
584:     try {
585:       if (options?.onProgress && isTauri) {
586:         try {
587:           unlisten = await listen<ScanProgress>('scan-progress', (event) => {
588:             if (!scanId || event.payload.scanId === scanId) {
589:               options.onProgress?.(event.payload);
590:             }
591:           });
592:         } catch (err) {
593:           console.warn('Tauri scan-progress listen failed:', err);
594:         }
595:       }
596: 
597:       return await invokeCommand('discover_library', { folderPath, scanId });
598:     } catch (err) {
599:       const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
600:       if (fsErr?.code === 'Cancelled') {
601:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
602:       }
603:       console.error('Tauri discoverLibrary failed:', fsErr ?? err);
604:       return null;
605:     } finally {
606:       if (abortHandler && options?.signal) {
607:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
608:       }
609:       unlisten?.();
610:     }
611:   }

674:         absolutePath,
675:       });
676:       if (response instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
677:         return response;
678:       }
679:       if (response instanceof Uint8Array) {
680:         return response.buffer.slice(
681:           response.byteOffset,
682:           response.byteOffset + response.byteLength,
683:         ) as ArrayBuffer;
684:       }
685:       return null;
686:     } catch (err) {

1038:   async isSamePath(pathA: string, pathB: string): Promise<boolean> {

1040:       return await invokeCommand('is_same_path', { pathA, pathB });
1041:     } catch {
1042:       return (
1043:         pathA.trim().replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/\/+$/, '').toLowerCase() ===

1045:       );

1048: 
1049:   async indexTitle(
1050:     root: string,
1051:     options?: IndexTitleOptions,
1052:     onProgress?: (progress: IndexScanProgress) => void,
1053:   ): Promise<IndexTitleDto> {
1054:     const channel = new Channel<IndexScanProgress>();
1055:     const jobId =
1056:       options?.jobId ??
1057:       (options?.signal ? `index-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}` : undefined);
1058: 
1059:     let abortHandler: (() => void) | undefined;
1060:     if (options?.signal && jobId) {
1061:       if (options.signal.aborted) {
1062:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
1063:       }
1064:       abortHandler = () => {
1065:         void this.cancelScan?.(jobId);
1066:       };
1067:       options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
1068:     }
1069: 
1070:     if (onProgress) {
1071:       channel.onmessage = (progress) => {
1072:         if (options?.signal?.aborted) {
1073:           if (jobId) void this.cancelScan?.(jobId);
1074:           return;
1075:         }
1076:         onProgress(progress);
1077:       };
1078:     }
1079: 
1080:     try {
1081:       return await invokeCommand('index_title', {
1082:         root,
1083:         options: options
1084:           ? {
1085:             sort: options.sort,
1086:             warmChapters: options.warmChapters,
1087:             jobId,
1088:             title: options.title,
1089:             targetId: options.targetId ?? options.previous?.id,
1090:             previous: options.previous
1091:               ? {
1092:                 id: options.previous.id,
1093:                 title: options.previous.title,
1094:                 slug: options.previous.slug,
1095:                 lastChapter: options.previous.lastChapter,
1096:                 addedAt: options.previous.addedAt,
1097:                 revision: options.previous.revision,
1098:                 chapters: options.previous.chapters.map((c) => ({
1099:                   name: c.name,
1100:                   read: c.read,
1101:                   position: c.position,
1102:                   bookmarks: c.bookmarks,
1103:                   pages: c.pages.map((p) => ({
1104:                     name: p.name,
1105:                     path: p.path,
1106:                     width: p.width,
1107:                     height: p.height,
1108:                     size: p.size,
1109:                     modified: p.modified,
1110:                     dimensionsPending: p.dimensionsPending,
1111:                   })),
1112:                 })),
1113:               }
1114:               : undefined,
1115:           }
1116:           : undefined,
1117:         onProgress: channel,
1118:       } as any);
1119:     } catch (err) {
1120:       const fsErr = parseFsError(err);
1121:       if (
1122:         fsErr?.code === 'Cancelled' ||
1123:         (typeof err === 'string' && err.includes('Indexing cancelled')) ||
1124:         (err instanceof Error && err.message.includes('Indexing cancelled'))
1125:       ) {
1126:         throw new DOMException('The operation was aborted', 'AbortError');
1127:       }
1128:       throw err;
1129:     } finally {
1130:       if (abortHandler && options?.signal) {
1131:         options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abortHandler);
1132:       }
1133:     }
1134:   }
1135: 
1136:   convertFileSrc(filePath: string, protocol = 'mangaimg'): string {
1137:     if (typeof tauriConvertFileSrc === 'function' && isTauri) {
1138:       try {
1139:         return tauriConvertFileSrc(filePath, protocol);
1140:       } catch {
1141:         // fallback below
1142:       }
1143:     }
1144:     const normalized = filePath.replace(/\\/g, '/');
1145:     const isWindowsDrive = /^([a-zA-Z]):/.test(normalized);
1146:     const drivePrefix = isWindowsDrive ? normalized.slice(0, 1) + '%3A' : '';
1147:     const rest = isWindowsDrive
1148:       ? normalized.slice(2).replace(/^\/+/, '')
1149:       : normalized.replace(/^\/+/, '');
1150:     const segments = rest.split('/').filter(Boolean);
1151:     const encoded = segments.map((s) => encodeURIComponent(s)).join('/');
1152:     const cleanPath = drivePrefix ? drivePrefix + '/' + encoded : encoded;
1153:     return `http://${protocol}.localhost/${cleanPath}`;
1154:   }

1163: 
1164:   onDragDrop(callback: (paths: string[]) => void): () => void {
1165:     if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
1166:       return () => { };
1167:     }
1168:     let unlistenNative: (() => void) | undefined;
1169:     let unlistenTauri: (() => void) | undefined;
1170:     let active = true;
1171: 
1172:     if (typeof (window as any).__TAURI_EVENT_PLUGIN_INTERNALS__ !== 'object') {
1173:       (window as any).__TAURI_EVENT_PLUGIN_INTERNALS__ = {
1174:         unregisterListener: () => { },
1175:       };
1176:     }
1177: 
1178:     void listen<string[]>('native-drag-drop', (event) => {
1179:       if (!active) return;
1180:       const paths = event.payload;
1181:       if (Array.isArray(paths) && paths.length > 0) {
1182:         callback(paths);
1183:       }
1184:     })
1185:       .then((un) => {
1186:         if (!active) {
1187:           try {
1188:             void un();
1189:           } catch { }
1190:         } else {
1191:           unlistenNative = un;
1192:         }
1193:       })
1194:       .catch(() => { });
1195: 
1196:     void listen<{ paths?: string[] }>('tauri://drag-drop', (event) => {
1197:       if (!active) return;
1198:       const paths = event.payload?.paths;
1199:       if (Array.isArray(paths) && paths.length > 0) {
1200:         callback(paths);
1201:       }
1202:     })

34:   applying = true;

36:     const tasks: Promise<unknown>[] = [];

49: export function useAutoplay({
50:   allowed,
51:   ready,
52:   prepared,
53:   ci,
54:   manga,
55:   settings,
56:   offsets,
57:   groupsRef,
58:   pageToGroupRef,
59:   chapterRef,
60:   restoring,
61:   held,
62:   keysHeld,
63:   latest,
64:   actions,
65:   setBars,
66:   notify,
67: }: UseAutoplayOptions) {
68:   const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
69:   const [ended, setEnded] = useState(false);
70:   const endHandled = useRef(false);
71:   const pauseUntil = useRef(0);
72:   const playbackRevision = useRef(0);
73: 
74:   function pause() {
75:     playbackRevision.current++;
76:     pauseUntil.current = performance.now() + latest.current.s.resumeDelay * 1000;
77:   }
78: 
79:   useEffect(() => {
80:     if (!playing || !allowed || !ready || prepared !== ci) return;
81:     // Each start owns a fresh clock and uses the viewport, never a previous play target.
82:     actions.current.measure();
83:     actions.current.capture();
84:     let frame = 0,
85:       last = 0,
86:       elapsed = 0,
87:       scrollRemainder = 0,
88:       revision = playbackRevision.current;
89: 
90:     function end() {
91:       if (endHandled.current) return;
92:       endHandled.current = true;
93:       actions.current.capture();
94:       void actions.current.flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
95: 
96:       const handleTransition = (promise: Promise<void>) => {
97:         promise.catch((err) => {
98:           setPlaying(false);
99:           setEnded(true);
100:           setBars({ top: true, bottom: true });
101:           notify(errorMessage(err));
102:         });
103:       };
104: 
105:       const action = latest.current.s.endAction;
106:       if (action === 'next' && ci < manga.chapters.length - 1)
107:         handleTransition(actions.current.goChapter(ci + 1, true));
108:       else if (action === 'replay') handleTransition(actions.current.goChapter(ci, true));
109:       else {
110:         setPlaying(false);
111:         setEnded(true);
112:         setBars({ top: true, bottom: true });
113:       }
114:     }
115: 
116:     const animate = (now: number) => {
117:       const reset = revision !== playbackRevision.current;
118:       if (reset) {
119:         revision = playbackRevision.current;
120:         elapsed = 0;
121:         scrollRemainder = 0;
122:       }
123:       const dt = last && !reset ? Math.min(now - last, 80) / 1000 : 0;
124:       last = now;
125:       if (
126:         !document.hidden &&
127:         latest.current.ready &&
128:         chapterRef.current === ci &&
129:         !restoring.current &&
130:         !held.current.size &&
131:         !keysHeld.current.size &&
132:         now > pauseUntil.current &&
133:         !document.querySelector(
134:           '[role="dialog"],[data-reader-dropdown],.reader-bar details[open]',
135:         ) &&
136:         (window.visualViewport?.scale || 1) <= 1.01
137:       ) {
138:         const state = latest.current;
139:         if (state.s.mode === 'single') {
140:           elapsed += dt;
141:           if (elapsed >= state.s.seconds) {
142:             elapsed = 0;
143:             const list = groupsRef.current;
144:             const i = pageToGroupRef.current[state.page] ?? 0;
145:             if (i < list.length - 1) actions.current.goPage(list[i + 1][0], false);
146:             else end();
147:           }
148:         } else {
149:           // One visible screen per interval, independent of image height or zoom.
150:           // Read each frame so mobile browser chrome and rotation adapt immediately.
151:           const viewportHeight = window.visualViewport?.height || innerHeight;
152:           const speed = viewportHeight / state.s.seconds;
153:           const y = scrollY + speed * dt + scrollRemainder;
154:           window.scrollTo({ top: y, behavior: 'instant' });
155:           // Preserve subpixel movement at slow speeds / high refresh rates.
156:           // Discard clamped movement at document boundaries.
157:           scrollRemainder = Math.abs(y - scrollY) < 1 ? y - scrollY : 0;
158:           const final = offsets.current.at(-1);
159:           if (final && scrollY + innerHeight >= final.bottom - 1) end();
160:         }
161:       } else {
162:         elapsed = 0;
163:         scrollRemainder = 0;
164:       }
165:       frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
166:     };
167: 
168:     frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
169:     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
170:   }, [playing, allowed, ready, prepared, ci, settings.mode, settings.seconds]);
171: 
172:   return {
173:     playing,
174:     setPlaying,
175:     ended,
176:     setEnded,
177:     endHandled,
178:     pauseUntil,
179:     playbackRevision,
180:     pause,
181:   };
182: }

64:     activeOp.current++;
