66:       controller.current.abort();
67:     }
68:     finish();
69:     setState({ status: 'idle' });
70:   }, [finish]);
71: 
72:   // Clean up on component unmount
73:   useEffect(() => {
74:     return () => {
75:       if (controller.current) {
76:         controller.current.abort();
77:       }
78:       cleanupStaged();
79:       desktopAPI.setProgressBar(-1);
80:     };
81:   }, [cleanupStaged]);
82: 
83:   const startOptions = useCallback((opId: number, phase: 'discover' | 'index'): ScanOptions => {
84:     controller.current = new AbortController();
85:     let last = 0;
86:     desktopAPI.setProgressBar(2);
87: 
88:     return {
89:       signal: controller.current.signal,
90:       update: (s: ScanState) => {
91:         if (activeOp.current !== opId) return;
92:         const now = performance.now();
93:         if (now - last > 80 || s.done === s.total) {
94:           last = now;
95:           setState((prev) => {
96:             if (phase === 'discover' && prev.status === 'discovering') {
97:               return { status: 'discovering', scan: s };
98:             }
99:             if (phase === 'index' && prev.status === 'indexing') {
100:               return { status: 'indexing', scan: s };
101:             }
102:             return prev;
103:           });
104:           if (s.total > 0) {
105:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, s.done / s.total)));
106:           } else {
107:             desktopAPI.setProgressBar(2);
108:           }
109:         }
110:       },
111:     };
112:   }, []);
113: 
114:   const discover = useCallback(
115:     async (read: (options: ScanOptions) => Promise<Source[]>, target?: Summary | Manga) => {
116:       const opId = ++activeOp.current;
117:       cleanupStaged();
118:       setState({
119:         status: 'discovering',
120:         scan: { phase: 'discover', done: 0, total: 0, title: target?.title ?? '' },
121:       });
122: 
123:       const options = startOptions(opId, 'discover');
124:       let sources: Source[] = [];
125:       const startTime = performance.now();
126:       let outcome: 'preview' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'idle' = 'idle';
127: 
128:       try {
129:         sources = await read(options);
130:         if (activeOp.current !== opId) return;
131:         stagedSources.current = sources;
132:         await warmSources(sources, options);
133:         if (activeOp.current !== opId) return;
134:         options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
135: 
136:         if (!sources.length) {
137:           throw new Error(
138:             'No supported image candidates found. Select folders with images, or extract archives first.',
139:           );
140:         }
141: 
142:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
143:           const targetManga = target ? await getManga(target.id) : undefined;
144:           outcome = 'preview';
145:           setState({
146:             status: 'preview',
147:             preview: { sources, target: targetManga },
148:           });
149:         }
150:       } catch (e) {
151:         cleanupStaged();
152:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
153:           const isAbort = e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError';
154:           outcome = isAbort ? 'cancelled' : 'failed';
155:           const msg = isAbort ? 'Import cancelled. Existing data is unchanged.' : errorMessage(e);
156:           setState(isAbort ? { status: 'idle' } : { status: 'failed', message: msg });
157:           onNotify(msg);
158:           if (!isAbort) {
159:             recordDiagnostic({
160:               operation: 'import',
161:               level: 'warn',
162:               code: (e as any)?.name,
163:               message: msg,
164:               safeIdentifier: target?.title,
165:               durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startTime),
166:               retryable: true,
167:             });
168:           }
169:         }
170:       } finally {
171:         if (activeOp.current === opId && outcome !== 'preview') {
172:           desktopAPI.setProgressBar(-1);
173:         }
174:       }
175:     },
176:     [cleanupStaged, onNotify, startOptions],
177:   );
178: 
179:   const runImport = useCallback(
180:     async (plans: ImportPlan[]) => {
181:       const opId = ++activeOp.current;
182:       setState({
183:         status: 'indexing',
184:         scan: { phase: 'index', done: 0, total: 0, title: plans[0]?.source.title ?? '' },
185:       });
186: 
187:       const options = startOptions(opId, 'index');
188:       const issues: Issue[] = [];
189:       const retry: ImportPlan[] = [];
190:       const titles: Manga[] = [];
191:       let count = 0;
192:       let reused = 0;
193:       const startTime = performance.now();
194: 
195:       try {
196:         for (const plan of plans) {
197:           if (activeOp.current !== opId) break;
198:           options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
199:           try {
200:             const previous = plan.targetId ? await getManga(plan.targetId) : undefined;
201:             const result = await indexSource(plan.source, options, previous, plan.sort);
202:             if (activeOp.current !== opId || options.signal?.aborted) {
203:               break;
204:             }
205:             if (!desktopAPI.isDesktop) {
206:               await saveManga(result.m);
207:             }
208:             count++;
209:             titles.push(result.m);
210:             scheduleBackground(result.m);
211:             reused += result.reused;
212:             issues.push(
213:               ...result.issues.map((i) => ({ ...i, path: plan.source.title + '/' + i.path })),
214:             );
215:             if (result.issues.length) {
216:               retry.push({ ...plan, targetId: result.m.id });
217:             }
218:           } catch (e) {
219:             if (activeOp.current !== opId || options.signal?.aborted) {
220:               break;
221:             }
222:             options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
223:             const msg = errorMessage(e);
224:             issues.push({ path: plan.source.title, reason: msg });
225:             retry.push(plan);
226:             recordDiagnostic({
227:               operation: 'import',
228:               level: 'error',
229:               code: (e as any)?.name,
230:               message: msg,
231:               safeIdentifier: plan.source.title,
232:               retryable: true,
233:             });
234:           }
235:         }
236: 
237:         await onRefresh();
238:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
239:           setState({
240:             status: 'complete',
241:             report: { titles, count, reused, issues, retry },
242:           });
243:         }
244:       } catch (e) {
245:         await onRefresh();
246:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
247:           const isAbort = e instanceof DOMException && e.name === 'AbortError';
248:           const msg = isAbort
249:             ? `Import cancelled. ${count} completed titles saved; the unfinished title was not changed.`
250:             : errorMessage(e);
251:           setState(isAbort ? { status: 'idle' } : { status: 'failed', message: msg });
252:           onNotify(msg);
253:           if (!isAbort) {
254:             recordDiagnostic({
255:               operation: 'import',
256:               level: 'error',
257:               code: (e as any)?.name,
258:               message: msg,
259:               durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startTime),
260:               retryable: true,
261:             });
262:           }
263:         }
264:       } finally {
265:         discardScan(plans.map((p) => p.source));
266:         if (activeOp.current === opId) {
267:           finish();
268:         }
269:       }
270:     },
271:     [finish, onNotify, onRefresh, startOptions],
272:   );
273: 
274:   const dismiss = useCallback(() => {
275:     cleanupStaged();
276:     setState({ status: 'idle' });
277:   }, [cleanupStaged]);
278: 
279:   return {
280:     state,
281:     isBusy: state.status === 'discovering' || state.status === 'indexing',
282:     scanState:
283:       state.status === 'discovering' || state.status === 'indexing'
284:         ? state.scan
285:         : { phase: 'discover' as const, done: 0, total: 0, title: '' },
286:     discover,
287:     runImport,
288:     cancel,
289:     dismiss,
290:   };

1: import { useEffect } from 'react';
2: // One delegated listener; no timers, observers or animation loop.
3: export default function useInterfaceMotion(reduced: boolean) {
4:   useEffect(() => {
5:     if (reduced) return;
6:     const media = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
7:     const press = (event: MouseEvent) => {
8:       if (media.matches || !(event.target instanceof Element)) return;
9:       const control = event.target.closest<HTMLElement>(
10:         '.icon-button,.close-btn,.primary,.secondary,.browse-button',
11:       );
12:       if (!control || control.matches(':disabled') || !control.animate) return;
13:       for (const animation of control.getAnimations()) animation.cancel();
14:       control.animate([{ scale: '1' }, { scale: '.96' }, { scale: '1' }], {
15:         duration: 160,
16:         easing: 'ease-out',
17:       });
18:     };
19:     document.addEventListener('click', press);
20:     return () => document.removeEventListener('click', press);
21:   }, [reduced]);
22: }

13: export interface UseProgressSaveOptions {
14:   metadata: MutableRefObject<Manga>;
15:   chapterRef: MutableRefObject<number>;
16:   position: MutableRefObject<Position>;
17:   latest: MutableRefObject<{
18:     s: Settings;
19:     ci: number;
20:     ready: boolean;
21:     allowed: boolean | null;
22:     page: number;
23:     zoom: number;
24:   }>;
25:   offsets: MutableRefObject<PageOffset[]>;
26:   pageToGroupRef: MutableRefObject<Int32Array>;
27:   groupsRef: MutableRefObject<number[][]>;
28:   edgeProgress: RefObject<HTMLSpanElement | null>;
29:   restoring: MutableRefObject<boolean>;
30:   singlePage: number;
31:   pageStoreRef: MutableRefObject<PageStore | null>;
32:   setReadRevision: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
33:   notify: (msg: string) => void;

35: 
36: export function useProgressSave({
37:   metadata,
38:   chapterRef,
39:   position,
40:   latest,
41:   offsets,
42:   pageToGroupRef,
43:   groupsRef,
44:   edgeProgress,
45:   restoring,
46:   singlePage,
47:   pageStoreRef,
48:   setReadRevision,
49:   notify,
50: }: UseProgressSaveOptions) {
51:   const dirty = useRef(false);
52:   const lastSave = useRef(0);
53:   const inFlight = useRef<Promise<SaveProgressResult> | null>(null);
54:   const queuedFlush = useRef<Promise<SaveProgressResult> | null>(null);
55:   const snapshotRevision = useRef(0);
56:   const committedRevision = useRef(0);
57: 
58:   function flush(): Promise<SaveProgressResult> {
59:     if (!latest.current.ready || !latest.current.allowed) {
60:       return Promise.resolve('missing');
61:     }
62:     if (inFlight.current) {
63:       if (!queuedFlush.current) {
64:         queuedFlush.current = inFlight.current
65:           .catch(() => {
66:             // Absorb in-flight rejection so the queued retry attempt can proceed
67:           })
68:           .then(() => {
69:             queuedFlush.current = null;
70:             if (dirty.current) {
71:               return flush();
72:             }
73:             return 'saved';
74:           })
75:           .catch((err) => {
76:             queuedFlush.current = null;
77:             throw err;
78:           });
79:       }
80:       return queuedFlush.current;
81:     }
82:     if (!dirty.current) {
83:       return Promise.resolve('saved');
84:     }
85:     const index = chapterRef.current;
86:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[index];
87:     if (!c) {
88:       dirty.current = false;
89:       return Promise.resolve('missing');
90:     }
91:     const targetRevision = snapshotRevision.current;
92:     const p = { ...position.current };
93:     const isRead = Boolean(c.read);
94:     const bookmarks = c.bookmarks ? [...c.bookmarks] : undefined;
95: 
96:     dirty.current = false;
97:     lastSave.current = performance.now();
98: 
99:     const savePromise = (async (): Promise<SaveProgressResult> => {
100:       try {
101:         const result = await saveProgress(metadata.current, index, p, isRead, bookmarks);
102:         if (result === 'saved') {
103:           committedRevision.current = Math.max(committedRevision.current, targetRevision);
104:         } else {
105:           if (committedRevision.current < targetRevision) {
106:             dirty.current = true;
107:           }
108:         }
109:         return result;
110:       } catch (err) {
111:         if (committedRevision.current < targetRevision) {
112:           dirty.current = true;
113:         }
114:         throw err;
115:       }
116:     })();
117: 
118:     inFlight.current = savePromise;
119:     // Handled cleanup chain prevents unhandled rejection on the cleanup promise
120:     savePromise.then(
121:       () => {
122:         if (inFlight.current === savePromise) {
123:           inFlight.current = null;
124:         }
125:       },
126:       () => {
127:         if (inFlight.current === savePromise) {
128:           inFlight.current = null;
129:         }
130:       },
131:     );
132:     return savePromise;
133:   }
134: 
135:   function capture() {
136:     if (restoring.current || !latest.current.ready || !offsets.current.length) return;
137:     const y = window.scrollY;
138:     const list = offsets.current;
139:     let index = 0;
140:     if (latest.current.s.mode === 'continuous') {
141:       let lo = 0,
142:         hi = list.length - 1;
143:       while (lo < hi) {
144:         const mid = Math.ceil((lo + hi) / 2);
145:         if (list[mid].top <= y + 1) lo = mid;
146:         else hi = mid - 1;
147:       }
148:       index = lo;
149:     } else {
150:       index = Math.max(0, Math.min(list.length - 1, latest.current.page));
151:     }
152:     const o = list[index];
153:     position.current = {
154:       page: o.page,
155:       path: metadata.current.chapters[chapterRef.current].pages[o.page]?.path,
156:       fraction: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (y - o.top) / o.height)),
157:     };
158:     latest.current.page = o.page;
159:     if (latest.current.s.mode === 'continuous' && pageStoreRef.current) {
160:       pageStoreRef.current.setPage(o.page);
161:     }
162:     const c = metadata.current.chapters[chapterRef.current];
163:     const final = list[c.pages.length - 1] || list.at(-1);
164:     if (final && y + innerHeight >= final.bottom - 2 && !c.read) {
165:       c.read = true;
166:       setReadRevision((v) => v + 1);
167:     }
168:     if (edgeProgress.current) {
169:       const currentPage =
170:         latest.current.s.mode === 'single'
171:           ? singlePage
172:           : pageStoreRef.current
173:             ? pageStoreRef.current.getPage()
174:             : 0;
175:       const gIndex = pageToGroupRef.current[currentPage] ?? 0;
176:       const currentGroup = groupsRef.current[gIndex] || [currentPage];
177:       const progress =
178:         latest.current.s.mode === 'single'
179:           ? (Math.max(...currentGroup) + 1) / c.pages.length
180:           : final && y + innerHeight >= final.bottom - 2
181:             ? 1
182:             : (position.current.page + position.current.fraction) / c.pages.length;
183:       edgeProgress.current.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress))})`;
184:     }
185:     c.position = { ...position.current };
186:     dirty.current = true;
187:     snapshotRevision.current++;
188:     if (performance.now() - lastSave.current >= 1500) {
189:       void flush().catch((e) => notify(errorMessage(e)));
190:     }
191:   }
192: 
193:   return {
194:     dirty,
195:     lastSave,
196:     snapshotRevision,
197:     committedRevision,
198:     flush,
199:     capture,
200:   };
201: }

4: export interface UseReaderKeysOptions {

6:     s: Settings;
7:     ci: number;

10:     page: number;
11:     zoom: number;
12:   }>;

14:     pause: () => void;
15:     step: (direction: number) => void;
16:     goChapter: (index: number, reset?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
17:   }>;
18:   setPlaying: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
19:   setBars: Dispatch<SetStateAction<{ top: boolean; bottom: boolean }>>;
20:   toggleBookmark: () => void;
21: }
22: 
23: export function useReaderKeys({
24:   latest,
25:   actions,
26:   setPlaying,
27:   setBars,
28:   toggleBookmark,
29: }: UseReaderKeysOptions) {
30:   const keysHeld = useRef(new Set<string>());
31:   const navigatingRef = useRef(false);
32: 
33:   const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
34:     keysHeld.current.add(e.code);
35:     actions.current.pause();
36:     if (
37:       e.defaultPrevented ||
38:       document.querySelector('[role="dialog"],[data-reader-dropdown]') ||
39:       (e.target as HTMLElement).closest('input,select,button,a,[contenteditable="true"]')
40:     )
41:       return;
42:     if (e.code === 'Space') {
43:       e.preventDefault();
44:       if (!e.repeat) setPlaying((v) => !v);
45:     }
46:     if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
47:       e.preventDefault();
48:       const delta = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
49:       if (latest.current.s.mode === 'single') {
50:         actions.current.step(delta * (latest.current.s.direction === 'rtl' ? -1 : 1));
51:       } else {
52:         // Serialize navigation: ignore repeated keystrokes while chapter transition is active
53:         if (navigatingRef.current) return;
54:         navigatingRef.current = true;
55:         actions.current
56:           .goChapter(latest.current.ci + delta)
57:           .catch((err) => {
58:             console.warn('Chapter navigation failed:', err);
59:           })
60:           .finally(() => {
61:             navigatingRef.current = false;
62:           });
63:       }
64:     }
65:     if (e.key === 'Escape') {
66:       setPlaying(false);
67:       setBars({ top: true, bottom: true });
68:     }
69:     if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'b' && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
70:       e.preventDefault();
71:       toggleBookmark();
72:     }
73:   };
74: 
75:   const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
76:     keysHeld.current.delete(e.code);
77:     actions.current.pause();
78:   };
79: 
80:   return {
81:     keysHeld,
82:     onKeyDown,
83:     onKeyUp,
84:   };
85: }

6: const MAX_THUMBNAIL_BYTES: usize = 10 * 1024 * 1024; // 10 MiB safety cap
7: 
8: pub fn percent_decode(input: &str) -> String {
9:     let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(input.len());
10:     let mut chars = input.bytes();
11:     while let Some(b) = chars.next() {
12:         if b == b'%' {
13:             let h1 = chars.next();
14:             let h2 = chars.next();
15:             match (h1, h2) {
16:                 (Some(c1), Some(c2)) => {
17:                     let hex_str = [c1, c2];
18:                     if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(&hex_str) {
19:                         if let Ok(val) = u8::from_str_radix(s, 16) {
20:                             bytes.push(val);
21:                             continue;
22:                         }
23:                     }
24:                     bytes.push(b'%');
25:                     bytes.push(c1);
26:                     bytes.push(c2);
27:                 }
28:                 (Some(c1), None) => {
29:                     bytes.push(b'%');
30:                     bytes.push(c1);
31:                 }
32:                 _ => {
33:                     bytes.push(b'%');
34:                 }
35:             }
36:         } else {
37:             bytes.push(b);
38:         }
39:     }
40:     String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap_or_else(|_| input.to_string())
41: }
42: 
43: /// Sanitizes thumbnail ID to prevent path traversal attacks, namespace collisions,
44: /// and Windows reserved device name collisions (e.g. CON, PRN, AUX, NUL).
45: /// Always hashes with an explicit "thumb_v1:" domain separator and "t_" prefix.
46: pub fn sanitize_id(id: &str) -> String {
47:     if id.is_empty() {
48:         return String::new();
49:     }
50:     let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
51:     hasher.update(b"thumb_v1:");
52:     hasher.update(id.as_bytes());
53:     format!("t_{:x}", hasher.finalize())
54: }
55: 
56: /// Generates a collision-resistant SHA-256 hash for variant cache keys with an explicit namespace.
57: pub fn hash_key(key: &str) -> String {
58:     let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
59:     hasher.update(b"variant_v1:");
60:     hasher.update(key.as_bytes());
61:     format!("v_{:x}", hasher.finalize())
62: }
63: 
64: pub fn get_thumbnail_path(app: &AppHandle, id: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
65:     if id.is_empty() {
66:         return Err("Thumbnail ID cannot be empty".into());
67:     }
68: 
69:     let safe_id = sanitize_id(id);
70:     let cache_dir = app
71:         .path()
72:         .app_cache_dir()
73:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
74: 
75:     let thumb_dir = cache_dir.join("thumbnails");
76:     if !thumb_dir.exists() {
77:         let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&thumb_dir);
78:     }
79: 
80:     Ok(thumb_dir.join(format!("{}.webp", safe_id)))
81: }
82: 
83: pub fn get_variant_path(
84:     app: &AppHandle,
85:     key: &str,
86:     width: u32,
87:     height: u32,
88: ) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
89:     let hash = hash_key(key);
90:     let cache_dir = app
91:         .path()
92:         .app_cache_dir()
93:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
94: 
95:     let var_dir = cache_dir.join("variants");
96:     if !var_dir.exists() {
97:         let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&var_dir);
98:     }
99: 
100:     Ok(var_dir.join(format!("{}_{}x{}.webp", hash, width, height)))
101: }

103: #[tauri::command]
104: pub async fn save_thumbnail(
105:     app: AppHandle,
106:     request: tauri::ipc::Request<'_>,
107: ) -> Result<(), String> {
108:     let (id, bytes) = match request.body() {
109:         tauri::ipc::InvokeBody::Raw(raw_bytes) => {
110:             let id_header = request
111:                 .headers()
112:                 .get("x-id")
113:                 .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
114:                 .ok_or_else(|| "missing x-id header".to_string())?;
115:             (percent_decode(id_header), raw_bytes.to_vec())
116:         }
117:         tauri::ipc::InvokeBody::Json(json) => {
118:             let id = json
119:                 .get("id")
120:                 .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
121:                 .unwrap_or("")
122:                 .to_string();
123:             let bytes = if let Some(arr) = json.get("bytes").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
124:                 let mut byte_vec = Vec::with_capacity(arr.len());
125:                 for (idx, item) in arr.iter().enumerate() {
126:                     let val = item.as_u64().ok_or_else(|| {
127:                         format!("Invalid byte entry at index {idx}: expected unsigned integer")
128:                     })?;
129:                     if val > 255 {
130:                         return Err(format!(
131:                             "Byte value {val} at index {idx} exceeds 255 (valid byte range 0..=255)"
132:                         ));
133:                     }
134:                     byte_vec.push(val as u8);
135:                 }
136:                 byte_vec
137:             } else {
138:                 return Err("Expected raw body or JSON 'bytes' array".into());
139:             };
140:             (id, bytes)
141:         }
142:     };
143: 
144:     if id.is_empty() {
145:         return Err("Thumbnail ID cannot be empty".into());
146:     }
147: 
148:     if bytes.len() > MAX_THUMBNAIL_BYTES {
149:         return Err(format!(
150:             "Thumbnail exceeds maximum size of {} bytes",
151:             MAX_THUMBNAIL_BYTES
152:         ));
153:     }
154: 
155:     let path = get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
156:     tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || -> Result<(), String> {
157:         crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&path, &bytes).map_err(|e| format!("{e}"))?;
158:         if !path.exists() {
159:             return Err("Failed to verify thumbnail file existence on disk".to_string());
160:         }
161:         Ok(())
162:     })
163:     .await
164:     .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to spawn blocking write: {e}"))?
165:     .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write thumbnail to disk: {e}"))?;
166: 
167:     Ok(())
168: }
169: 
170: #[tauri::command]
171: pub async fn read_thumbnail(app: AppHandle, id: String) -> Result<tauri::ipc::Response, String> {
172:     let path = get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
173:     if !path.exists() || !path.is_file() {
174:         return Ok(tauri::ipc::Response::new(Vec::new()));
175:     }
176: 
177:     let bytes = tokio::fs::read(&path)
178:         .await
179:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to read thumbnail: {e}"))?;
180: 
181:     Ok(tauri::ipc::Response::new(bytes))
182: }

194: /// Prunes image variants cache directory if size exceeds 500 MiB or 1,000 files.
195: pub fn prune_cache(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
196:     let cache_dir = app
197:         .path()
198:         .app_cache_dir()
199:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
200:     let var_dir = cache_dir.join("variants");
201:     if !var_dir.exists() {
202:         return Ok(());
203:     }
204: 
205:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&var_dir) {
206:         let mut files: Vec<(PathBuf, std::time::SystemTime, u64)> = Vec::new();
207:         for entry in entries.flatten() {
208:             if let Ok(meta) = entry.metadata() {
209:                 if meta.is_file() {
210:                     let mtime = meta.modified().unwrap_or(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH);
211:                     files.push((entry.path(), mtime, meta.len()));
212:                 }
213:             }
214:         }
215: 
216:         let mut total_size: u64 = files.iter().map(|f| f.2).sum();
217:         let mut remaining = files.len();
218:         if total_size > 500 * 1024 * 1024 || remaining > 1000 {
219:             files.sort_by_key(|f| f.1); // oldest first
220:             for (p, _, len) in files {
221:                 if total_size <= 400 * 1024 * 1024 && remaining <= 800 {
222:                     break;
223:                 }
224:                 if std::fs::remove_file(p).is_ok() {
225:                     total_size = total_size.saturating_sub(len);
226:                     remaining = remaining.saturating_sub(1);
227:                 }
228:             }
229:         }
230:     }
231: 
232:     Ok(())
233: }
234: 
235: #[tauri::command]
236: pub async fn delete_thumbnail(app: AppHandle, id: String) -> Result<(), String> {
237:     let path = get_thumbnail_path(&app, &id)?;
238:     if path.exists() {
239:         let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(&path).await;
240:     }
241:     let app_clone = app.clone();
242:     let _ = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
243:         let _ = prune_cache(&app_clone);
244:     })
245:     .await;
246:     Ok(())
247: }

249: #[tauri::command]
250: pub async fn clear_thumbnails(app: AppHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
251:     let cache_dir = app
252:         .path()
253:         .app_cache_dir()
254:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve cache dir: {e}"))?;
255: 
256:     let thumb_dir = cache_dir.join("thumbnails");
257:     if thumb_dir.exists() {
258:         let _ = tokio::fs::remove_dir_all(&thumb_dir).await;
259:     }
260:     let var_dir = cache_dir.join("variants");
261:     if var_dir.exists() {
262:         let _ = tokio::fs::remove_dir_all(&var_dir).await;
263:     }
264:     Ok(())
265: }

267: #[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
268: pub struct StorageStats {
269:     pub db_bytes: u64,
270:     pub cache_bytes: u64,
271:     pub total_bytes: u64,
272: }
273: 
274: fn dir_size(path: &Path) -> u64 {
275:     let mut total = 0;
276:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(path) {
277:         for entry in entries.flatten() {
278:             if let Ok(metadata) = entry.metadata() {
279:                 if metadata.is_dir() {
280:                     total += dir_size(&entry.path());
281:                 } else {
282:                     total += metadata.len();
283:                 }
284:             }
285:         }
286:     }
287:     total
288: }

290: #[tauri::command]
291: pub async fn get_storage_stats(app: AppHandle) -> Result<StorageStats, String> {
292:     tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
293:         let mut db_bytes = 0u64;
294:         if let Ok(data_dir) = app.path().app_data_dir() {
295:             for file_name in &["library.db", "library.db-wal", "library.db-shm"] {
296:                 let p = data_dir.join(file_name);
297:                 if let Ok(meta) = std::fs::metadata(&p) {
298:                     db_bytes += meta.len();
299:                 }
300:             }
301:         }
302: 
303:         let mut cache_bytes = 0u64;
304:         if let Ok(cache_dir) = app.path().app_cache_dir() {
305:             cache_bytes += dir_size(&cache_dir.join("thumbnails"));
306:             cache_bytes += dir_size(&cache_dir.join("variants"));
307:         }
308: 
309:         let total_bytes = db_bytes + cache_bytes;
310:         Ok(StorageStats {
311:             db_bytes,
312:             cache_bytes,
313:             total_bytes,
314:         })
315:     })
316:     .await
317:     .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
318: }

320: #[tauri::command]
321: pub async fn save_file_dialog(
322:     app: AppHandle,
323:     default_name: String,
324:     content: String,
325: ) -> Result<bool, String> {
326:     let safe_default = Path::new(&default_name)
327:         .file_name()
328:         .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
329:         .unwrap_or("export.txt")
330:         .to_string();
331: 
332:     let app_clone = app.clone();
333:     let path_opt = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
334:         let mut builder = app_clone.dialog().file();
335:         if !safe_default.is_empty() {
336:             builder = builder.set_file_name(&safe_default);
337:         }
338:         builder.blocking_save_file()
339:     })
340:     .await
341:     .map_err(|e| format!("Dialog task join error: {e}"))?;
342: 
343:     if let Some(file_path) = path_opt {
344:         let pb = file_path
345:             .into_path()
346:             .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid selected file path: {e}"))?;
347:         let ext = pb
348:             .extension()
349:             .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
350:             .map(|e| e.to_ascii_lowercase())
351:             .unwrap_or_default();
352:         if ext != "json" && ext != "txt" {
353:             return Err("Only .json and .txt exports are permitted".to_string());
354:         }
355:         let bytes = content.into_bytes();
356:         tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || crate::commands::fs::write_atomic(&pb, &bytes))
357:             .await
358:             .map_err(|e| format!("Write task join error: {e}"))?
359:             .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write file: {e}"))?;
360:         Ok(true)
361:     } else {
362:         Ok(false)
363:     }
364: }

370:     #[test]
371:     fn test_percent_decode() {
372:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("hello"), "hello");
373:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("hello%20world"), "hello world");
374:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("%2Fpath%2Fto%2Ffile"), "/path/to/file");
375:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("special%3A%2B%3D"), "special:+=");
376:         assert_eq!(percent_decode("incomplete%2"), "incomplete%2");
377:     }
378: 
379:     #[test]
380:     fn test_sanitize_id() {
381:         let safe1 = sanitize_id("manga-123_abc");
382:         assert!(safe1.starts_with("t_"));
383:         assert_eq!(safe1.len(), 66);
384: 
385:         // Deterministic hashing
386:         assert_eq!(safe1, sanitize_id("manga-123_abc"));
387: 
388:         // Windows reserved device names cannot form raw filename
389:         let con_safe = sanitize_id("con");
390:         assert!(con_safe.starts_with("t_"));
391:         assert_ne!(con_safe, "con");
392:         assert_eq!(con_safe.len(), 66);
393: 
394:         let aux_safe = sanitize_id("aux");
395:         assert!(aux_safe.starts_with("t_"));
396:         assert_ne!(aux_safe, "aux");
397: 
398:         // Traversal attempts cannot escape or contain slashes
399:         let traversal = sanitize_id("../../etc/passwd");
400:         assert!(!traversal.contains(".."));
401:         assert!(!traversal.contains('/'));
402:         assert!(!traversal.contains('\\'));
403:         assert_eq!(traversal.len(), 66);
404: 
405:         // Different IDs produce different hashes
406:         assert_ne!(sanitize_id("manga-1"), sanitize_id("manga-2"));
407: 
408:         // Empty string returns empty
409:         assert_eq!(sanitize_id(""), "");
410:     }
411:

413:     fn test_hash_key() {
414:         let hash1 = hash_key("path/to/image.png");
415:         let hash2 = hash_key("path/to/image.png");
416:         assert_eq!(
417:             hash1, hash2,
418:             "Identical inputs must produce identical hashes"
419:         );
420:         assert!(hash1.starts_with("v_"));
421: 
422:         let hash3 = hash_key("path/to/image2.png");
423:         assert_ne!(
424:             hash1, hash3,
425:             "Different inputs must produce different hashes"
426:         );
427: 
428:         assert_eq!(
429:             hash1.len(),
430:             66,
431:             "Hash key must be 66-char prefixed SHA-256 string"
432:         );
433:         assert!(hash1[2..].chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()));
434:     }

18: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default, PartialEq)]
19: #[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
20: pub struct AppConfig {
21:     pub last_opened_roots: Vec<String>,
22:     pub window_preferences: WindowPreferences,
23:     pub custom_preferences: HashMap<String, serde_json::Value>,
24: }
25: 
26: pub struct AppConfigState(pub RwLock<AppConfig>);
27: 
28: impl AppConfigState {
29:     pub fn new(app: &AppHandle) -> Self {
30:         let config = load_persisted_config(app);
31:         Self(RwLock::new(config))
32:     }
33: 
34:     pub fn get(&self) -> AppConfig {
35:         self.0.read().unwrap().clone()
36:     }
37: 
38:     pub fn update<F, R>(&self, app: &AppHandle, f: F) -> Result<R, String>
39:     where
40:         F: FnOnce(&mut AppConfig) -> R,
41:     {
42:         let mut lock = self.0.write().unwrap();
43:         let mut cloned = lock.clone();
44:         let res = f(&mut cloned);
45:         save_persisted_config(app, &cloned)?;
46:         *lock = cloned;
47:         Ok(res)
48:     }
49: }
50: 
51: fn get_config_path(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
52:     let app_dir = app
53:         .path()
54:         .app_data_dir()
55:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to resolve app data dir: {e}"))?;
56:     let _ = fs::create_dir_all(&app_dir);
57:     Ok(app_dir.join("app_config.json"))
58: }
59: 
60: pub fn load_persisted_config(app: &AppHandle) -> AppConfig {
61:     if let Ok(path) = get_config_path(app) {
62:         if path.exists() {
63:             if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&path) {
64:                 if let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<AppConfig>(&content) {
65:                     return config;
66:                 }
67:             }
68:         }
69:         // Fallback recovery: check if a valid .tmp file exists from an interrupted rename
70:         let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp");
71:         if tmp.exists() {
72:             if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&tmp) {
73:                 if let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<AppConfig>(&content) {
74:                     let _ = fs::rename(&tmp, &path);
75:                     return config;
76:                 }
77:             }
78:         }
79:     }
80:     AppConfig::default()
81: }
82: 
83: pub fn save_persisted_config(app: &AppHandle, config: &AppConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
84:     let path = get_config_path(app)?;
85:     let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(config)
86:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to serialize config: {e}"))?;
87:     write_atomic(&path, json.as_bytes())
88:         .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to write config atomically: {e}"))?;
89:     Ok(())
90: }

99: pub fn save_app_config(app: AppHandle, config: AppConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
100:     let state = app.state::<AppConfigState>();
101:     state.update(&app, |cfg| {

103:         for (k, v) in config.custom_preferences {

142: #[tauri::command(async)]
143: pub fn record_opened_root(app: AppHandle, root_path: String) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
144:     let trimmed = root_path.trim().to_string();
145:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
146:         return Ok(Vec::new());
147:     }
148: 
149:     let raw_path = std::path::Path::new(&trimmed);
150:     let canonical = dunce::canonicalize(raw_path)
151:         .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot record unresolvable path: {e}"))?;
152: 
153:     let allowed = app.state::<super::fs::AllowedRoots>();
154:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&canonical) {
155:         return Err(format!(
156:             "Path '{}' is not under an authorized root",
157:             canonical.display()
158:         ));
159:     }
160: 
161:     let canonical_str = canonical.to_string_lossy().to_string();
162:     let state = app.state::<AppConfigState>();
163:     state.update(&app, |cfg| {
164:         // Remove duplicate if already present, push to front
165:         cfg.last_opened_roots
166:             .retain(|r| r != &canonical_str && r != &trimmed);
167:         cfg.last_opened_roots.insert(0, canonical_str);
168:         // Cap at 25 entries
169:         if cfg.last_opened_roots.len() > 25 {
170:             cfg.last_opened_roots.truncate(25);
171:         }
172:         cfg.last_opened_roots.clone()
173:     })
174: }
175:

177: pub fn get_last_opened_roots(app: AppHandle) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
178:     let state = app.state::<AppConfigState>();
179:     Ok(state.get().last_opened_roots)
180: }

258:     #[test]

264:         // Updating window preferences does not clobber last_opened_roots
265:         let new_prefs = WindowPreferences {
266:             close_to_tray: true,
267:             minimize_to_tray: true,
268:             theme: Some("light".into()),
269:         };
270:         config.window_preferences = new_prefs;
271:         assert_eq!(config.last_opened_roots, vec!["C:/RootA", "C:/RootB"]);
272:         assert!(config.window_preferences.close_to_tray);
273:         assert!(config.window_preferences.minimize_to_tray);
274:         assert_eq!(config.window_preferences.theme, Some("light".into()));
275:     }
276: 
277:     #[test]
278:     fn test_root_capping_and_deduplication() {
279:         let mut roots = Vec::new();
280:         for i in 0..30 {
281:             let root = format!("C:\\Manga\\Title_{}", i);
282:             roots.retain(|r| r != &root);
283:             roots.insert(0, root);
284:             if roots.len() > 25 {
285:                 roots.truncate(25);
286:             }
287:         }
288:

290:         assert_eq!(roots[0], "C:\\Manga\\Title_29");
291: 
292:         // Re-adding Title_10 should bring it to the front
293:         let title_10 = "C:\\Manga\\Title_10".to_string();
294:         roots.retain(|r| r != &title_10);
295:         roots.insert(0, title_10.clone());
296:         assert_eq!(roots[0], title_10);
297:         assert_eq!(roots.len(), 25);
298:     }

120: #[derive(Default)]
121: pub struct DiscoveryState {
122:     snapshots: std::sync::Mutex<std::collections::HashMap<PathBuf, DiscoverySnapshot>>,
123: }
124: 
125: impl DiscoveryState {
126:     pub fn store(&self, root_path: PathBuf, candidates: Vec<DiscoveredPage>) {
127:         if let Ok(mut map) = self.snapshots.lock() {
128:             let now = std::time::Instant::now();
129:             map.retain(|_, v| now.duration_since(v.created_at).as_secs() < 900);
130:             if map.len() >= 8 {
131:                 if let Some(oldest) = map
132:                     .iter()
133:                     .min_by_key(|(_, v)| v.created_at)
134:                     .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
135:                 {
136:                     map.remove(&oldest);
137:                 }
138:             }
139:             map.insert(
140:                 root_path.clone(),
141:                 DiscoverySnapshot {
142:                     root_path,
143:                     created_at: now,
144:                     candidates,
145:                 },
146:             );
147:         }
148:     }
149: 
150:     pub fn get_for_path(&self, path: &Path) -> Option<DiscoverySnapshot> {
151:         if let Ok(map) = self.snapshots.lock() {
152:             if let Some(snap) = map.get(path) {
153:                 return Some(snap.clone());
154:             }
155:             for (root, snap) in map.iter() {
156:                 if path.starts_with(root) {
157:                     return Some(snap.clone());
158:                 }
159:             }
160:         }
161:         None
162:     }
163: }

165: pub fn natural_order(a: &str, b: &str) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
166:     fn has_word_prefix(s: &str, prefix: &str) -> bool {
167:         if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix(prefix) {
168:             rest.is_empty() || !rest.chars().next().unwrap().is_alphanumeric()
169:         } else {
170:             false
171:         }
172:     }
173: 
174:     fn category(s: &str) -> i32 {
175:         let trimmed = s.trim_start();
176:         let lower = trimmed.to_lowercase();
177:         if has_word_prefix(&lower, "prologue") || has_word_prefix(&lower, "opening") {
178:             -1
179:         } else if has_word_prefix(&lower, "extra")
180:             || has_word_prefix(&lower, "bonus")
181:             || has_word_prefix(&lower, "special")
182:             || has_word_prefix(&lower, "epilogue")
183:         {
184:             1
185:         } else {
186:             0
187:         }
188:     }
189: 
190:     let cat_a = category(a);
191:     let cat_b = category(b);
192:     if cat_a != cat_b {
193:         return cat_a.cmp(&cat_b);
194:     }
195: 
196:     #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
197:     enum Token<'a> {
198:         Num(u128, usize),
199:         BigNum(&'a str),
200:         Str(&'a str),
201:     }
202: 
203:     fn extract_tokens(s: &str) -> Vec<Token<'_>> {
204:         let mut tokens = Vec::new();
205:         let mut start = 0;
206:         let mut in_num = false;
207:         let bytes = s.as_bytes();
208:         let len = bytes.len();
209: 
210:         let mut i = 0;
211:         while i < len {
212:             let c = bytes[i];
213:             let is_digit = c.is_ascii_digit();
214:             if is_digit != in_num {
215:                 if i > start {
216:                     let slice = &s[start..i];
217:                     if in_num {
218:                         if let Ok(n) = slice.parse::<u128>() {
219:                             tokens.push(Token::Num(n, slice.len()));
220:                         } else {
221:                             tokens.push(Token::BigNum(slice));
222:                         }
223:                     } else {
224:                         tokens.push(Token::Str(slice));
225:                     }
226:                 }
227:                 start = i;
228:                 in_num = is_digit;
229:             }
230:             i += 1;
231:         }
232: 
233:         if start < len {
234:             let slice = &s[start..len];
235:             if in_num {
236:                 if let Ok(n) = slice.parse::<u128>() {
237:                     tokens.push(Token::Num(n, slice.len()));
238:                 } else {
239:                     tokens.push(Token::BigNum(slice));
240:                 }
241:             } else {
242:                 tokens.push(Token::Str(slice));
243:             }
244:         }
245: 
246:         tokens
247:     }
248: 
249:     let lower_a = a.to_lowercase();
250:     let lower_b = b.to_lowercase();
251: 
252:     let tokens_a = extract_tokens(&lower_a);
253:     let tokens_b = extract_tokens(&lower_b);
254: 
255:     for (x, y) in tokens_a.iter().zip(tokens_b.iter()) {
256:         match (x, y) {
257:             (Token::Num(nx, lx), Token::Num(ny, ly)) => {
258:                 let ord = nx.cmp(ny);
259:                 if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
260:                     return ord;
261:                 }
262:                 if lx != ly {
263:                     return lx.cmp(ly);
264:                 }
265:             }
266:             (Token::BigNum(bx), Token::BigNum(by)) => {
267:                 let tx = bx.trim_start_matches('0');
268:                 let ty = by.trim_start_matches('0');
269:                 let ord = tx.len().cmp(&ty.len()).then_with(|| tx.cmp(ty));
270:                 if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
271:                     return ord;
272:                 }
273:                 if bx.len() != by.len() {
274:                     return bx.len().cmp(&by.len());
275:                 }
276:             }
277:             (Token::Num(nx, _), Token::BigNum(by)) => {
278:                 let ty = by.trim_start_matches('0');
279:                 if ty.len() > 38 {
280:                     return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
281:                 }
282:                 if let Ok(ny) = ty.parse::<u128>() {
283:                     let ord = nx.cmp(&ny);
284:                     if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
285:                         return ord;
286:                     }
287:                 }
288:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
289:             }
290:             (Token::BigNum(bx), Token::Num(ny, _)) => {
291:                 let tx = bx.trim_start_matches('0');
292:                 if tx.len() > 38 {
293:                     return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
294:                 }
295:                 if let Ok(nx) = tx.parse::<u128>() {
296:                     let ord = nx.cmp(ny);
297:                     if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
298:                         return ord;
299:                     }
300:                 }
301:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
302:             }
303:             (Token::Str(sx), Token::Str(sy)) => {
304:                 let ord = sx.chars().cmp(sy.chars());
305:                 if ord != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
306:                     return ord;
307:                 }
308:             }
309:             (Token::Num(_, _) | Token::BigNum(_), Token::Str(_)) => {
310:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
311:             }
312:             (Token::Str(_), Token::Num(_, _) | Token::BigNum(_)) => {
313:                 return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
314:             }
315:         }
316:     }
317: 
318:     tokens_a.len().cmp(&tokens_b.len()).then_with(|| a.cmp(b))
319: }
320: 
321: #[tauri::command(async)]
322: pub fn natural_sort_paths(mut paths: Vec<String>) -> Vec<String> {
323:     paths.sort_by(|a, b| natural_order(a, b));
324:     paths
325: }
326: 
327: pub fn resolve_relative_path(base_dir: &Path, rel_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, FsError> {
328:     let trimmed = rel_path.trim().replace('\\', "/");
329:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
330:         return Err(FsError::NotFound("Empty relative path".to_string()));
331:     }
332:     let rel = Path::new(&trimmed);
333: 
334:     // Prevent parent directory traversal
335:     if rel
336:         .components()
337:         .any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir))
338:     {
339:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
340:             "Path contains illegal parent directory traversal: '{rel_path}'"
341:         )));
342:     }
343: 
344:     // Prevent absolute paths from escaping base_dir
345:     #[cfg(windows)]
346:     if trimmed.starts_with('/') || (trimmed.len() >= 2 && trimmed.as_bytes()[1] == b':') {
347:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
348:             "Path must be relative to root: '{rel_path}'"
349:         )));
350:     }
351:     #[cfg(not(windows))]
352:     if rel.is_absolute() {
353:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
354:             "Path must be relative to root: '{rel_path}'"
355:         )));
356:     }
357: 
358:     let combined = base_dir.join(rel);
359:     let canonical = dunce::canonicalize(&combined).map_err(|e| {
360:         FsError::NotFound(format!(
361:             "Failed to resolve path '{}' under '{}': {e}",
362:             rel_path,
363:             base_dir.display()
364:         ))
365:     })?;
366: 
367:     let canonical_base = dunce::canonicalize(base_dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| base_dir.to_path_buf());
368:     if !canonical.starts_with(&canonical_base) {
369:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
370:             "Path '{}' escapes base directory '{}'",
371:             rel_path,
372:             base_dir.display()
373:         )));
374:     }
375: 
376:     Ok(canonical)
377: }

379: #[tauri::command]
380: pub async fn resolve_manga_path(
381:     app: AppHandle,
382:     manga_id: Option<String>,
383:     relative_path: String,
384:     local_path: Option<String>,
385: ) -> Result<String, FsError> {
386:     let base_str = if let Some(ref lp) = local_path {
387:         if !lp.trim().is_empty() {
388:             Some(lp.trim().to_string())
389:         } else {
390:             None
391:         }
392:     } else {
393:         None
394:     };
395: 
396:     let base_str = match base_str {
397:         Some(s) => s,
398:         None => {
399:             if let Some(ref id) = manga_id {
400:                 if let Some(db) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
401:                     db.get_local_path(id).await.ok_or_else(|| {
402:                         FsError::NotFound(format!("Manga '{id}' has no localPath recorded"))
403:                     })?
404:                 } else {
405:                     return Err(FsError::NotFound(
406:                         "Database state not initialized".to_string(),
407:                     ));
408:                 }
409:             } else {
410:                 return Err(FsError::NotFound(
411:                     "Neither manga_id nor local_path provided".to_string(),
412:                 ));
413:             }
414:         }
415:     };
416: 
417:     let base_dir = PathBuf::from(base_str);
418:     let resolved = resolve_relative_path(&base_dir, &relative_path)?;
419: 
420:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
421:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
422:         return Err(FsError::PathNotAllowed(format!(
423:             "Resolved path '{}' is not under an allowed root",
424:             resolved.display()
425:         )));
426:     }
427: 
428:     Ok(resolved.to_string_lossy().to_string())
429: }

431: #[tauri::command]
432: pub async fn check_access(
433:     app: AppHandle,
434:     manga_id: Option<String>,
435:     local_path: Option<String>,
436: ) -> Result<bool, FsError> {
437:     let target_path = if let Some(ref lp) = local_path {
438:         if !lp.trim().is_empty() {
439:             Some(lp.trim().to_string())
440:         } else {
441:             None
442:         }
443:     } else {
444:         None
445:     };
446: 
447:     let target_path = match target_path {
448:         Some(p) => p,
449:         None => {
450:             if let Some(ref id) = manga_id {
451:                 if let Some(db) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
452:                     match db.get_local_path(id).await {
453:                         Some(p) => p,
454:                         None => return Ok(false),
455:                     }
456:                 } else {
457:                     return Ok(false);
458:                 }
459:             } else {
460:                 return Ok(false);
461:             }
462:         }
463:     };
464: 
465:     let trimmed = target_path.trim();
466:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
467:         return Ok(false);
468:     }
469: 
470:     let pb = Path::new(trimmed);
471:     let canonical = match dunce::canonicalize(pb) {
472:         Ok(c) => c,
473:         Err(_) => return Ok(false),
474:     };
475: 
476:     if !canonical.is_dir() {
477:         return Ok(false);
478:     }
479: 
480:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
481:     Ok(allowed.is_allowed(&canonical))
482: }

484: #[tauri::command]
485: pub async fn prepare_chapter_headers(
486:     app: AppHandle,
487:     paths: Vec<String>,
488:     manga_id: Option<String>,
489: ) -> Result<Vec<PageHeader>, FsError> {
490:     let allowed_set = {
491:         let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
492:         allowed.get_active_roots()
493:     };
494: 
495:     let base_dir: Option<PathBuf> = if let Some(ref id) = manga_id {
496:         if let Some(db) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
497:             db.get_local_path(id).await.map(PathBuf::from)
498:         } else {
499:             None
500:         }
501:     } else {
502:         None
503:     };
504: 
505:     // Parallel extraction with Rayon off main thread
506:     let results: Vec<PageHeader> = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
507:         paths
508:             .into_par_iter()
509:             .filter_map(|p_str| {
510:                 let trimmed = p_str.trim();
511:                 let path = Path::new(trimmed);
512: 
513:                 let resolved = if path.is_relative() {
514:                     if let Some(ref base) = base_dir {
515:                         resolve_relative_path(base, trimmed).ok()?
516:                     } else {
517:                         dunce::canonicalize(path).ok()?
518:                     }
519:                 } else {
520:                     dunce::canonicalize(path).ok()?
521:                 };
522: 
523:                 if allowed_set.is_empty() || !allowed_set.iter().any(|r| resolved.starts_with(r)) {
524:                     return None;
525:                 }
526:                 let ext = resolved.extension()?.to_str()?.to_lowercase();
527:                 if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
528:                     return None;
529:                 }
530:                 let metadata = std::fs::metadata(&resolved).ok()?;
531:                 if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.len() > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
532:                     return None;
533:                 }
534:                 let (width, height) = match parse_image_dimensions(&resolved) {
535:                     (Some(w), Some(h)) => (w, h),
536:                     _ => return None,
537:                 };
538: 
539:                 let modified = metadata
540:                     .modified()
541:                     .ok()
542:                     .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
543:                     .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
544:                     .unwrap_or(0.0);
545: 
546:                 // Lightweight checksum: FNV-1a of size + modified + first 512 bytes
547:                 let mut hash: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325;
548:                 let size = metadata.len();
549:                 for b in size.to_le_bytes() {
550:                     hash = (hash ^ (b as u64)).wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
551:                 }
552:                 for b in (modified as u64).to_le_bytes() {
553:                     hash = (hash ^ (b as u64)).wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
554:                 }
555:                 if let Ok(mut f) = File::open(&resolved) {
556:                     let mut sample = [0u8; 512];
557:                     if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut sample) {
558:                         for &b in &sample[..n] {
559:                             hash = (hash ^ (b as u64)).wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
560:                         }
561:                     }
562:                 }
563:                 let checksum = Some(format!("{:016x}", hash));
564: 
565:                 Some(PageHeader {
566:                     path: p_str,
567:                     width,
568:                     height,
569:                     size,
570:                     modified,
571:                     checksum,
572:                 })
573:             })
574:             .collect()
575:     })
576:     .await
577:     .map_err(|e| FsError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
578: 
579:     Ok(results)
580: }

582: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
583: #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
584: pub struct ScanProgressPayload {
585:     pub scan_id: Option<String>,
586:     pub done: usize,
587:     pub total: usize,
