137: /// Generates a clean URL slug from a title string.
138: pub fn generate_slug(name: &str) -> String {
139:     let mut slug = String::new();
140:     let mut prev_dash = false;
141:     for c in name.chars() {
142:         if c.is_alphanumeric() {
143:             for lc in c.to_lowercase() {
144:                 slug.push(lc);
145:             }
146:             prev_dash = false;
147:         } else if !prev_dash && !slug.is_empty() {
148:             slug.push('-');
149:             prev_dash = true;
150:         }
151:         if slug.len() >= 100 {
152:             break;
153:         }
154:     }
155:     let trimmed = slug.trim_matches('-');
156:     if trimmed.is_empty() || matches!(trimmed, "read" | "assets" | "api" | "bookmarks" | "index-html") {
157:         "manga".to_string()
158:     } else {
159:         trimmed.to_string()
160:     }
161: }
162: 
163: /// Resolves a reading position against a chapter's newly indexed pages list.
164: pub fn matching_position(
165:     p: &PositionDto,
166:     pages: &[PageDto],
167:     old_pages: &[PreviousPageDto],
168: ) -> Option<PositionDto> {
169:     if pages.is_empty() {
170:         return None;
171:     }
172: 
173:     let target_path = p
174:         .path
175:         .clone()
176:         .or_else(|| old_pages.get(p.page).map(|pg| pg.path.clone()));
177: 
178:     let mut page_idx = target_path
179:         .as_ref()
180:         .and_then(|tp| pages.iter().position(|x| &x.path == tp));
181: 
182:     // If path not found, try matching by leaf file name
183:     if page_idx.is_none() {
184:         if let Some(tp) = target_path.as_ref() {
185:             let leaf = tp.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(tp);
186:             let matches: Vec<usize> = pages
187:                 .iter()
188:                 .enumerate()
189:                 .filter(|(_, x)| x.name == leaf)
190:                 .map(|(i, _)| i)
191:                 .collect();
192:             if matches.len() == 1 {
193:                 page_idx = Some(matches[0]);
194:             }
195:         }
196:     }
197: 
198:     // Fall back to clamping by index
199:     let valid_idx = page_idx.unwrap_or_else(|| p.page.min(pages.len().saturating_sub(1)));
200:     let fraction = p
201:         .fraction
202:         .map(|f| if f.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { f.clamp(0.0, 1.0) });
203: 
204:     Some(PositionDto {
205:         page: valid_idx,
206:         path: Some(pages[valid_idx].path.clone()),
207:         fraction,
208:         manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
209:         timestamp: p.timestamp,
210:     })
211: }
212: 
213: /// Matches a newly indexed chapter against an existing manga record.
214: pub fn chapter_match<'a>(
215:     name: &str,
216:     pages: &[PageDto],
217:     previous: Option<&'a PreviousMangaDto>,
218: ) -> Option<&'a PreviousChapterDto> {
219:     let prev = previous?;
220: 
221:     // 1. Exact name match
222:     if let Some(exact) = prev.chapters.iter().find(|c| c.name == name) {
223:         return Some(exact);
224:     }
225: 
226:     // 2. Renamed chapter matching: same page count and all page signatures match
227:     fn sig(name: &str, size: Option<u64>, modified: Option<f64>) -> String {
228:         format!(
229:             "{}\0{}\0{}",
230:             name,
231:             size.unwrap_or(0),
232:             modified.map(|m| m.round() as i64).unwrap_or(0)
233:         )
234:     }
235: 
236:     let current_signatures: HashSet<String> = pages
237:         .iter()
238:         .map(|p| sig(&p.name, p.size, p.modified))
239:         .collect();
240: 
241:     let candidate_matches: Vec<&'a PreviousChapterDto> = prev
242:         .chapters
243:         .iter()
244:         .filter(|c| {
245:             if c.pages.len() != pages.len() {
246:                 return false;
247:             }
248:             c.pages
249:                 .iter()
250:                 .all(|p| current_signatures.contains(&sig(&p.name, p.size, p.modified)))
251:         })
252:         .collect();
253: 
254:     if candidate_matches.len() == 1 {
255:         Some(candidate_matches[0])
256:     } else {
257:         None
258:     }
259: }
260: 
261: pub fn manga_record_to_previous_dto(rec: crate::db::MangaRecord) -> PreviousMangaDto {
262:     PreviousMangaDto {
263:         id: rec.id,
264:         title: rec.title,
265:         slug: rec.slug,
266:         last_chapter: Some(rec.last_chapter),
267:         added_at: Some(rec.added_at),
268:         revision: rec.revision,
269:         chapters: rec
270:             .chapters
271:             .into_iter()
272:             .map(|c| PreviousChapterDto {
273:                 name: c.name,
274:                 read: Some(c.read),
275:                 position: c.position.map(|p| PositionDto {
276:                     page: p.page as usize,
277:                     path: p.path,
278:                     fraction: Some(p.fraction),
279:                     manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
280:                     timestamp: p.timestamp,
281:                 }),
282:                 bookmarks: c.bookmarks.map(|bms| {
283:                     bms.into_iter()
284:                         .map(|p| PositionDto {
285:                             page: p.page as usize,
286:                             path: p.path,
287:                             fraction: Some(p.fraction),
288:                             manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
289:                             timestamp: p.timestamp,
290:                         })
291:                         .collect()
292:                 }),
293:                 pages: c
294:                     .pages
295:                     .into_iter()
296:                     .map(|p| PreviousPageDto {
297:                         name: p.name,
298:                         path: p.path,
299:                         width: Some(p.width),
300:                         height: Some(p.height),
301:                         size: p.size,
302:                         modified: p.modified,
303:                         dimensions_pending: p.dimensions_pending,
304:                     })
305:                     .collect(),
306:             })
307:             .collect(),
308:     }
309: }
310: 
311: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
312: struct ScannedFile {
313:     rel_path: String,
314:     abs_path: PathBuf,
315:     name: String,
316:     size: u64,
317:     modified: f64,
318:     cached_width: Option<u32>,
319:     cached_height: Option<u32>,
320: }
321: 
322: #[tauri::command]
323: pub async fn index_title(
324:     app: AppHandle,
325:     root: String,
326:     options: Option<IndexOptions>,
327:     on_progress: tauri::ipc::Channel<ScanProgress>,
328: ) -> Result<TitleDto, String> {
329:     let raw_path = Path::new(root.trim());
330:     if !raw_path.exists() || !raw_path.is_dir() {
331:         return Err(format!(
332:             "Target path '{}' is not an existing directory",
333:             root
334:         ));
335:     }
336: 
337:     let root_path = dunce::canonicalize(raw_path).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_path.to_path_buf());
338: 
339:     // Security check: must be under allowed library roots
340:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
341:     if !allowed.is_allowed(&root_path) {
342:         return Err(format!(
343:             "Path '{}' is not under an allowed library root",
344:             root_path.display()
345:         ));
346:     }
347: 
348:     let opts = options.unwrap_or_default();
349:     let sort_mode = opts.sort.unwrap_or_else(|| "natural".to_string());
350:     let warm_count = opts.warm_chapters.unwrap_or(5);
351: 
352:     let scan_mgr = app.state::<crate::commands::fs::ScanManager>();
353:     let handle = opts.job_id.as_deref().map(|id| scan_mgr.register(id));
354:     let cancel_flag = handle.as_ref().map(|h| std::sync::Arc::clone(&h.flag));
355: 
356:     struct JobGuard<'a> {
357:         mgr: &'a crate::commands::fs::ScanManager,
358:         id: Option<&'a str>,
359:     }
360:     impl<'a> Drop for JobGuard<'a> {
361:         fn drop(&mut self) {
362:             if let Some(id) = self.id {
363:                 self.mgr.unregister(id);
364:             }
365:         }
366:     }
367:     let _guard = JobGuard {
368:         mgr: &scan_mgr,
369:         id: opts.job_id.as_deref(),
370:     };
371: 
372:     let check_cancelled = |handle: Option<&crate::commands::fs::ScanHandle>| -> Result<(), String> {
373:         if let Some(h) = handle {
374:             if h.flag.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) || *h.receiver.borrow() {
375:                 return Err("Indexing cancelled".to_string());
376:             }
377:         }
378:         Ok(())
379:     };
380: 
381:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
382: 
383:     let folder_name = root_path
384:         .file_name()
385:         .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
386:         .unwrap_or_else(|| "Manga".to_string());
387: 
388:     let root_clone = root_path.clone();
389: 
390:     // 0. Load previous manga from SQLite if not provided in options
391:     let mut previous_manga = opts.previous;
392:     if previous_manga.is_none() {
393:         if let Some(db_state) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
394:             let target_id = opts.target_id.as_deref();
395:             let root_str = root_path.to_string_lossy();
396:             if let Some(prev_rec) =
397:                 db_state.get_previous_manga_for_indexing_sync(target_id, Some(&root_str))
398:             {
399:                 previous_manga = Some(manga_record_to_previous_dto(prev_rec));
400:             }
401:         }
402:     }
403: 
404:     // 1. Check for retained discovery snapshot to eliminate duplicate walkdir and dimension parsing
405:     let (mut scanned_files, mut traversal_issues) =
406:         if let Some(discovery_state) = app.try_state::<crate::commands::fs::DiscoveryState>() {
407:             if let Some(snapshot) = discovery_state.get_for_path(&root_path) {
408:                 let relevant: Vec<_> = snapshot
409:                     .candidates
410:                     .into_iter()
411:                     .filter(|c| {
412:                         let p = Path::new(&c.absolute_path);
413:                         p.starts_with(&root_path)
414:                     })
415:                     .collect();
416: 
417:                 if !relevant.is_empty() {
418:                     let root_clone2 = root_path.clone();
419:                     let cancel_clone = cancel_flag.clone();
420:                     tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
421:                         let mut files = Vec::new();
422:                         let mut issues = Vec::new();
423:                         for c in relevant {
424:                             if let Some(ref cf) = cancel_clone {
425:                                 if cf.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) {
426:                                     break;
427:                                 }
428:                             }
429:                             let abs = PathBuf::from(&c.absolute_path);
430:                             let meta = match std::fs::metadata(&abs) {
431:                                 Ok(m) => m,
432:                                 Err(e) => {
433:                                     issues.push(ScanIssue {
434:                                         path: c.absolute_path.clone(),
435:                                         reason: format!("File missing on commit: {e}"),
436:                                     });
437:                                     continue;
438:                                 }
439:                             };
440:                             if !meta.is_file() {
441:                                 continue;
442:                             }
443:                             let mtime = meta
444:                                 .modified()
445:                                 .ok()
446:                                 .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
447:                                 .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
448:                                 .unwrap_or(0.0);
449:                             let size = meta.len();
450: 
451:                             let rel = match abs.strip_prefix(&root_clone2) {
452:                                 Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
453:                                 Err(_) => c.relative_path.clone(),
454:                             };
455: 
456:                             let name = abs
457:                                 .file_name()
458:                                 .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
459:                                 .unwrap_or_else(|| rel.clone());
460: 
461:                             let (w, h) = if size == c.size
462:                                 && (mtime - c.modified).abs() < 1000.0
463:                                 && c.width.is_some()
464:                                 && c.height.is_some()
465:                             {
466:                                 (c.width, c.height)
467:                             } else {
468:                                 parse_image_dimensions(&abs)
469:                             };
470: 
471:                             files.push(ScannedFile {
472:                                 rel_path: rel,
473:                                 abs_path: abs,
474:                                 name,
475:                                 size,
476:                                 modified: mtime,
477:                                 cached_width: w,
478:                                 cached_height: h,
479:                             });
480:                         }
481:                         (files, issues)
482:                     })
483:                     .await
484:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| (Vec::new(), Vec::new()))
485:                 } else {
486:                     (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
487:                 }
488:             } else {
489:                 (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
490:             }
491:         } else {
492:             (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
493:         };
494: 
495:     // If no retained snapshot or snapshot had no valid files, run standard recursive walkdir scan
496:     if scanned_files.is_empty() {
497:         let (files, issues) = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
498:             let mut files = Vec::new();
499:             let mut issues = Vec::new();
500:             let walker = walkdir::WalkDir::new(&root_clone)
501:                 .max_depth(MAX_DEPTH)
502:                 .follow_links(false)
503:                 .into_iter()
504:                 .filter_entry(|entry| {
505:                     if entry.depth() == 0 {
506:                         return true;
507:                     }
508:                     let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_lowercase();
509:                     if entry.file_type().is_dir() {
510:                         if name.starts_with('.') || IGNORED_FOLDER_NAMES.contains(&name.as_str()) {
511:                             return false;
512:                         }
513:                     }
514:                     true
515:                 });
516: 
517:             for entry_res in walker {
518:                 if let Some(ref cf) = cancel_flag {
519:                     if cf.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) {
520:                         break;
521:                     }
522:                 }
523:                 if files.len() >= MAX_SCAN_FILES {
524:                     issues.push(ScanIssue {
525:                         path: root_clone.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
526:                         reason: format!(
527:                             "Reached maximum scan limit of {MAX_SCAN_FILES} files; scan results may be incomplete"
528:                         ),
529:                     });
530:                     break;
531:                 }
532:                 let entry = match entry_res {
533:                     Ok(e) => e,
534:                     Err(err) => {
535:                         let p = err
536:                             .path()
537:                             .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string())
538:                             .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string());
539:                         issues.push(ScanIssue {
540:                             path: p,
541:                             reason: err.to_string(),
542:                         });
543:                         continue;
544:                     }
545:                 };
546: 
547:                 if !entry.file_type().is_file() {
548:                     continue;
549:                 }
550: 
551:                 let file_path = entry.path();
552:                 let ext = match file_path.extension() {
553:                     Some(e) => e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase(),
554:                     None => continue,
555:                 };
556: 
557:                 if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
558:                     continue;
559:                 }
560: 
561:                 let metadata = match entry.metadata() {
562:                     Ok(m) => m,
563:                     Err(err) => {
564:                         issues.push(ScanIssue {
565:                             path: file_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
566:                             reason: format!("Failed to read metadata: {err}"),
567:                         });
568:                         continue;
569:                     }
570:                 };
571: 
572:                 let rel_path = match file_path.strip_prefix(&root_clone) {
573:                     Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
574:                     Err(_) => continue,
575:                 };
576: 
577:                 let file_name = file_path
578:                     .file_name()
579:                     .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
580:                     .unwrap_or_else(|| rel_path.clone());
581: 
582:                 let modified = metadata
583:                     .modified()
584:                     .ok()
585:                     .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
586:                     .map(|d| d.as_millis() as f64)
587:                     .unwrap_or(0.0);
588: 
589:                 files.push(ScannedFile {
590:                     rel_path,
591:                     abs_path: file_path.to_path_buf(),
592:                     name: file_name,
593:                     size: metadata.len(),
594:                     modified,
595:                     cached_width: None,
596:                     cached_height: None,
597:                 });
598:             }
599:             (files, issues)
600:         })
601:         .await
602:         .map_err(|e| format!("Scan error: {e}"))?;
603: 
604:         scanned_files = files;
605:         traversal_issues.extend(issues);
606:     }
607: 
608:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
609: 
610:     let total_scanned = scanned_files.len();
611:     if total_scanned == 0 {
612:         return Err("No image pages found in this selection.".to_string());
613:     }
614: 
615:     if on_progress
616:         .send(ScanProgress {
617:             phase: "discover".to_string(),
618:             done: total_scanned,
619:             total: total_scanned,
620:             title: folder_name.clone(),
621:         })
622:         .is_err()
623:     {
624:         if let Some(ref h) = handle {
625:             h.flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
626:         }
627:         return Err("Indexing cancelled: progress channel disconnected".to_string());
628:     }
629: 
630:     // 2. Build lookup for previous dimensions reuse
631:     let mut old_pages_map: HashMap<String, PreviousPageDto> = HashMap::new();
632:     if let Some(ref prev) = previous_manga {
633:         for ch in &prev.chapters {
634:             for pg in &ch.pages {
635:                 old_pages_map.insert(pg.path.clone(), pg.clone());
636:             }
637:         }
638:     }
639: 
640:     // 3. Chapter grouping and cover detection
641:     let mut cover_candidate: Option<PathBuf> = None;
642:     let mut grouped_pages: HashMap<String, Vec<PageDto>> = HashMap::new();
643:     let mut reused_count = 0;
644: 
645:     let has_multiple_files = total_scanned > 1;
646: 
647:     for file in &scanned_files {
648:         let parts: Vec<&str> = file.rel_path.split('/').collect();
649:         let is_root = parts.len() == 1;
650: 
651:         if is_root && has_multiple_files {
652:             let lower = file.name.to_lowercase();
653:             if lower.starts_with("cover.") || lower.starts_with("thumbnail.") {
654:                 if cover_candidate.is_none() {
655:                     cover_candidate = Some(file.abs_path.clone());
656:                 }
657:                 continue;
658:             }
659:         }
660: 
661:         let chapter_name = if is_root {
662:             "Root pages".to_string()
663:         } else {
664:             parts[..parts.len() - 1].join(" / ")
665:         };
666: 
667:         let mut width = 800;
668:         let mut height = 1200;
669:         let mut dimensions_pending = true;
670: 
671:         if let (Some(w), Some(h)) = (file.cached_width, file.cached_height) {
672:             if w > 0 && h > 0 {
673:                 width = w;
674:                 height = h;
675:                 dimensions_pending = false;
676:                 reused_count += 1;
677:             }
678:         } else if let Some(cached) = old_pages_map.get(&file.rel_path) {
679:             let not_pending = !cached.dimensions_pending.unwrap_or(false);
680:             let size_matches = cached.size == Some(file.size);
681:             let mtime_matches = cached
682:                 .modified
683:                 .map(|m| (m - file.modified).abs() < 1000.0)
684:                 .unwrap_or(false);
685: 
686:             if not_pending && size_matches && mtime_matches {
687:                 if let (Some(w), Some(h)) = (cached.width, cached.height) {
688:                     if w > 0 && h > 0 {
689:                         width = w;
690:                         height = h;
691:                         dimensions_pending = false;
692:                         reused_count += 1;
693:                     }
694:                 }
695:             }
696:         }
697: 
698:         grouped_pages
699:             .entry(chapter_name)
700:             .or_default()
701:             .push(PageDto {
702:                 name: file.name.clone(),
703:                 path: file.rel_path.clone(),
704:                 width,
705:                 height,
706:                 size: Some(file.size),
707:                 modified: Some(file.modified),
708:                 dimensions_pending,
709:             });
710:     }
711: 
712:     if grouped_pages.is_empty() {
713:         return Err("No valid chapters or readable pages found in selection.".to_string());
714:     }
715: 
716:     // 4. Sort chapter names according to sort option
717:     let sort_cmp = |a: &str, b: &str| -> std::cmp::Ordering {
718:         match sort_mode.as_str() {
719:             "name" => a.cmp(b),
720:             "reverse" => natural_order(b, a),
721:             _ => natural_order(a, b),
722:         }
723:     };
724: 
725:     let mut chapter_names: Vec<String> = grouped_pages.keys().cloned().collect();
726:     chapter_names.sort_by(|a, b| sort_cmp(a, b));
727: 
728:     // Sort pages within each chapter
729:     for pages in grouped_pages.values_mut() {
730:         pages.sort_by(|a, b| sort_cmp(&a.name, &b.name));
731:     }
732: 
733:     // 5. Dimension extraction for warm chapters (first N chapters, default 5)
734:     let warm_names: Vec<String> = chapter_names.iter().take(warm_count).cloned().collect();
735: 
736:     // Collect pending pages from warm chapters
737:     let mut pending_warm: Vec<(String, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();
738:     for name in &warm_names {
739:         if let Some(pages) = grouped_pages.get(name) {
740:             for p in pages {
741:                 if p.dimensions_pending {
742:                     let abs = root_path.join(p.path.replace('/', std::path::MAIN_SEPARATOR_STR));
743:                     pending_warm.push((p.path.clone(), abs));
744:                 }
745:             }
746:         }
747:     }
748: 
749:     let total_pending = pending_warm.len();
750:     if total_pending > 0 {
751:         if on_progress
752:             .send(ScanProgress {
753:                 phase: "index".to_string(),
754:                 done: 0,
755:                 total: total_pending,
756:                 title: format!("{folder_name} · first {warm_count} chapters"),
757:             })
758:             .is_err()
759:         {
760:             if let Some(ref h) = handle {
761:                 h.flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
762:             }
763:             return Err("Indexing cancelled: progress channel disconnected".to_string());
764:         }
765: 
766:         let mut done = 0;
767:         let mut parsed_results: HashMap<String, (u32, u32)> = HashMap::with_capacity(total_pending);
768: 
769:         for chunk in pending_warm.chunks(SCAN_BATCH_SIZE) {
770:             check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
771: 
772:             let chunk_vec = chunk.to_vec();
773:             let parsed_batch: Vec<(String, Option<u32>, Option<u32>)> =
774:                 tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
775:                     chunk_vec
776:                         .into_par_iter()
777:                         .map(|(rel, abs)| {
778:                             let (w, h) = parse_image_dimensions(&abs);
779:                             (rel, w, h)
780:                         })
781:                         .collect()
782:                 })
783:                 .await
784:                 .map_err(|e| format!("Parse join error: {e}"))?;
785: 
786:             done += parsed_batch.len();
787:             for (rel, w, h) in parsed_batch {
788:                 if let (Some(width), Some(height)) = (w, h) {
789:                     if width > 0 && height > 0 {
790:                         parsed_results.insert(rel, (width, height));
791:                     } else {
792:                         traversal_issues.push(ScanIssue {
793:                             path: rel,
794:                             reason: "Zero or invalid image dimensions in header".to_string(),
795:                         });
796:                     }
797:                 } else {
798:                     traversal_issues.push(ScanIssue {
799:                         path: rel,
800:                         reason: "Could not parse image dimensions from header".to_string(),
801:                     });
802:                 }
803:             }
804: 
805:             if on_progress
806:                 .send(ScanProgress {
807:                     phase: "index".to_string(),
808:                     done,
809:                     total: total_pending,
810:                     title: format!("{folder_name} · first {warm_count} chapters"),
811:                 })
812:                 .is_err()
813:             {
814:                 if let Some(ref h) = handle {
815:                     h.flag.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
816:                 }
817:                 return Err("Indexing cancelled: progress channel disconnected".to_string());
818:             }
819:             tokio::task::yield_now().await;
820:         }
821: 
822:         // Apply parsed dimensions back to warm chapters
823:         for name in &warm_names {
824:             if let Some(pages) = grouped_pages.get_mut(name) {
825:                 pages.retain_mut(|p| {
826:                     if !p.dimensions_pending {
827:                         return true;
828:                     }
829:                     if let Some(&(w, h)) = parsed_results.get(&p.path) {
830:                         p.width = w;
831:                         p.height = h;
832:                         p.dimensions_pending = false;
833:                         true
834:                     } else {
835:                         false
836:                     }
837:                 });
838:             }
839:         }
840:     }
841: 
842:     grouped_pages.retain(|_, pages| !pages.is_empty());
843:     chapter_names.retain(|name| grouped_pages.contains_key(name));
844: 
845:     if chapter_names.is_empty() {
846:         return Err(if let Some(first_issue) = traversal_issues.first() {
847:             format!("No readable pages. {}: {}", first_issue.path, first_issue.reason)
848:         } else {
849:             "No image pages found in this selection.".to_string()
850:         });
851:     }
852: 
853:     check_cancelled(handle.as_ref())?;
854: 
855:     // 6. Re-link matching and chapters construction
856:     let mut final_chapters = Vec::with_capacity(chapter_names.len());
857: 
858:     for name in chapter_names {
859:         let pages = grouped_pages.remove(&name).unwrap_or_default();
860:         let old_match = chapter_match(&name, &pages, previous_manga.as_ref());
861: 
862:         let (read, position, bookmarks) = if let Some(old) = old_match {
863:             let old_names: HashSet<&str> = old.pages.iter().map(|p| p.name.as_str()).collect();
864:             let read = old.read.unwrap_or(false)
865:                 && pages.iter().all(|p| old_names.contains(p.name.as_str()));
866: 
867:             let position = old
868:                 .position
869:                 .as_ref()
870:                 .and_then(|pos| matching_position(pos, &pages, &old.pages));
871: 
872:             let bookmarks = old
873:                 .bookmarks
874:                 .as_ref()
875:                 .map(|bms| {
876:                     bms.iter()
877:                         .filter_map(|bm| matching_position(bm, &pages, &old.pages))
878:                         .collect()
879:                 })
880:                 .unwrap_or_default();
881: 
882:             (read, position, bookmarks)
883:         } else {
884:             (false, None, Vec::new())
885:         };
886: 
887:         final_chapters.push(ChapterDto {
888:             name,
889:             pages,
890:             read,
891:             position,
892:             bookmarks,
893:         });
894:     }
895: 
896:     // 7. Cover identification and thumbnail generation
897:     let first_page_abs = final_chapters
898:         .first()
899:         .and_then(|c| c.pages.first())
900:         .map(|p| root_path.join(p.path.replace('/', std::path::MAIN_SEPARATOR_STR)));
901: 
902:     let cover_abs = cover_candidate.or(first_page_abs);
903: 
904:     let title_name = opts
905:         .title
906:         .as_ref()
907:         .map(|t| t.trim().to_string())
908:         .filter(|t| !t.is_empty())
909:         .or_else(|| previous_manga.as_ref().map(|p| p.title.clone()))
910:         .unwrap_or_else(|| folder_name.clone());
911: 
912:     let manga_id = previous_manga
913:         .as_ref()
914:         .map(|p| p.id.clone())
915:         .unwrap_or_else(|| uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string());
916: 
917:     let base_slug = generate_slug(&title_name);
918:     let slug = if let Some(ref prev) = previous_manga {
919:         if opts.title.as_deref().map(|t| t.trim()) == Some(prev.title.as_str()) || opts.title.is_none() {
920:             prev.slug.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {
921:                 app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>()
922:                     .map(|db| db.allocate_unique_slug_sync(&base_slug, &manga_id))
923:                     .unwrap_or_else(|| base_slug.clone())
924:             })
925:         } else if let Some(db_state) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
926:             db_state.allocate_unique_slug_sync(&base_slug, &manga_id)
927:         } else {
928:             base_slug
929:         }
930:     } else if let Some(db_state) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
931:         db_state.allocate_unique_slug_sync(&base_slug, &manga_id)
932:     } else {
933:         base_slug
934:     };
935: 
936:     let now = SystemTime::now()
937:         .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
938:         .unwrap_or_default()
939:         .as_millis() as f64;
940: 
941:     let added_at = previous_manga
942:         .as_ref()
943:         .and_then(|p| p.added_at)
944:         .unwrap_or(now);
945: 
946:     let revision = previous_manga
947:         .as_ref()
948:         .and_then(|p| p.revision)
949:         .map(|r| r + 1)
950:         .unwrap_or(1);
951: 
952:     let last_chapter = previous_manga
953:         .as_ref()
954:         .and_then(|p| p.last_chapter)
955:         .unwrap_or(0)
956:         .min(final_chapters.len().saturating_sub(1));
957: 
958:     let mut final_cover_path: Option<String> = None;
959:     if let Some(cover_file) = cover_abs {
960:         final_cover_path = Some(cover_file.to_string_lossy().to_string());
961:         // Generate native thumbnail directly to disk cache
962:         let _ = crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(&app, &cover_file, &manga_id);
963:     }
964: 
965:     let chapter_count = final_chapters.len();
966:     let page_count = final_chapters.iter().map(|c| c.pages.len()).sum();
967:     let has_pending = final_chapters
968:         .iter()
969:         .any(|c| c.pages.iter().any(|p| p.dimensions_pending));
970: 
971:     // Natively commit manga directly to SQLite, eliminating duplicate serialization to JS and back
972:     if let Some(db_state) = app.try_state::<crate::db::DbState>() {
973:         let rec = crate::db::MangaRecord {
974:             id: manga_id.clone(),
975:             slug: Some(slug.clone()),
976:             title: title_name.clone(),
977:             local_path: Some(root_path.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
978:             last_chapter,
979:             added_at,
980:             updated_at: now,
981:             revision: Some(revision),
982:             sort: Some(sort_mode.clone()),
983:             chapters: final_chapters
984:                 .iter()
985:                 .map(|c| crate::db::ChapterRecord {
986:                     id: None,
987:                     name: c.name.clone(),
988:                     pages: c
989:                         .pages
990:                         .iter()
991:                         .map(|p| crate::db::PageRecord {
992:                             name: p.name.clone(),
993:                             path: p.path.clone(),
994:                             width: p.width,
995:                             height: p.height,
996:                             size: p.size,
997:                             modified: p.modified,
998:                             dimensions_pending: Some(p.dimensions_pending),
999:                         })
1000:                         .collect(),
1001:                     read: c.read,
1002:                     position: c.position.as_ref().map(|p| crate::db::PositionRecord {
1003:                         page: p.page as u32,
1004:                         path: p.path.clone(),
1005:                         fraction: p.fraction.unwrap_or(0.0),
1006:                         manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
1007:                         timestamp: p.timestamp,
1008:                     }),
1009:                     bookmarks: Some(
1010:                         c.bookmarks
1011:                             .iter()
1012:                             .map(|b| crate::db::PositionRecord {
1013:                                 page: b.page as u32,
1014:                                 path: b.path.clone(),
1015:                                 fraction: b.fraction.unwrap_or(0.0),
1016:                                 manga_bookmark: b.manga_bookmark,
1017:                                 timestamp: b.timestamp,
1018:                             })
1019:                             .collect(),
1020:                     ),
1021:                 })
1022:                 .collect(),
1023:         };
1024:         let _ = db_state.save_manga_sync(&rec);
1025:     }
1026: 
1027:     Ok(TitleDto {
1028:         id: manga_id,
1029:         title: title_name,
1030:         slug,
1031:         local_path: root_path.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
1032:         last_chapter,
1033:         added_at,
1034:         updated_at: now,
1035:         revision,
1036:         sort: sort_mode,
1037:         chapter_count,
1038:         page_count,
1039:         has_pending_dimensions: has_pending,
1040:         chapters: final_chapters,
1041:         cover_path: final_cover_path,
1042:         issues: traversal_issues,
1043:         reused: reused_count,
1044:     })
1045: }

1205:     #[test]
1206:     fn test_indexing_grouping_logic() {
1207:         // Test determine_chapter helper logic
1208:         fn determine_chapter(rel_path: &str, is_cover: bool) -> Option<String> {
1209:             if is_cover {
1210:                 return None;
1211:             }
1212:             if let Some(idx) = rel_path.rfind('/') {
1213:                 let dir_part = &rel_path[..idx];
1214:                 let chapter_name = dir_part.replace('/', " / ");
1215:                 Some(chapter_name)
1216:             } else {
1217:                 Some("Root pages".to_string())
1218:             }
1219:         }
1220: 
1221:         // Subdirectory becomes chapter name with slash normalization
1222:         assert_eq!(
1223:             determine_chapter("Chapter 1/01.jpg", false),
1224:             Some("Chapter 1".into())
1225:         );
1226:         assert_eq!(
1227:             determine_chapter("Volume 1/Chapter 1/01.jpg", false),
1228:             Some("Volume 1 / Chapter 1".into())
1229:         );
1230: 
1231:         // Root file becomes "Root pages"
1232:         assert_eq!(
1233:             determine_chapter("01.jpg", false),
1234:             Some("Root pages".into())
1235:         );
1236: 
1237:         // Cover file excluded from regular chapters
1238:         assert_eq!(determine_chapter("cover.jpg", true), None);
1239:         assert_eq!(determine_chapter("thumbnail.png", true), None);
1240:     }

16: #[tauri::command(async)]
17: pub fn set_progress_bar(window: WebviewWindow, progress: f64) -> Result<(), String> {
18:     let state = if progress < 0.0 {
19:         ProgressBarState {
20:             progress: None,
21:             status: Some(ProgressBarStatus::None),
22:         }
23:     } else if progress > 1.0 {
24:         ProgressBarState {
25:             progress: None,
26:             status: Some(ProgressBarStatus::Indeterminate),
27:         }
28:     } else {
29:         let percent = (progress * 100.0).round().clamp(0.0, 100.0) as u64;
30:         ProgressBarState {
31:             progress: Some(percent),
32:             status: Some(ProgressBarStatus::Normal),
33:         }
34:     };
35: 
36:     let _ = window.set_progress_bar(state);
37:     Ok(())
38: }

1: use rusqlite::{Connection, OptionalExtension, Transaction, params};
2: use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
3: use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
4: use tauri::State;
5: 
6: #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
7: #[serde(tag = "code", content = "message")]
8: pub enum DbError {
9:     #[error("Database error: {0}")]
10:     Sqlite(String),
11: 
12:     #[error("Migration error: {0}")]
13:     Migration(String),
14: 
15:     #[error("Lock error: {0}")]
16:     Lock(String),
17: 
18:     #[error("Validation error: {0}")]
19:     Validation(String),
20: 
21:     #[error("Not found: {0}")]
22:     NotFound(String),
23: 
24:     #[error("Stale revision conflict: {0}")]
25:     Conflict(String),
26: 
27:     #[error("Task execution error: {0}")]
28:     Task(String),
29: 
30:     #[error("IO error: {0}")]
31:     Io(String),
32: 
33:     #[error("Serialization error: {0}")]
34:     Serialization(String),
35: }
36: 
37: impl From<rusqlite::Error> for DbError {
38:     fn from(err: rusqlite::Error) -> Self {
39:         DbError::Sqlite(err.to_string())
40:     }
41: }
42: 
43: impl From<serde_json::Error> for DbError {
44:     fn from(err: serde_json::Error) -> Self {
45:         DbError::Serialization(err.to_string())
46:     }
47: }
48: 
49: pub type DbResult<T> = std::result::Result<T, DbError>;
50: 
51: #[derive(Clone)]
52: pub struct DbState {
53:     pub reader: Arc<Mutex<Connection>>,
54:     pub writer: Arc<Mutex<Connection>>,
55: }
56: 
57: impl DbState {
58:     pub fn new(reader: Connection, writer: Connection) -> Self {
59:         Self {
60:             reader: Arc::new(Mutex::new(reader)),
61:             writer: Arc::new(Mutex::new(writer)),
62:         }
63:     }
64: 
65:     pub async fn read<F, R>(&self, f: F) -> DbResult<R>
66:     where
67:         F: FnOnce(&Connection) -> DbResult<R> + Send + 'static,
68:         R: Send + 'static,
69:     {
70:         let reader = Arc::clone(&self.reader);
71:         tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
72:             let conn = reader.lock().map_err(|e| DbError::Lock(e.to_string()))?;
73:             f(&conn)
74:         })
75:         .await
76:         .map_err(|e| DbError::Task(format!("Database read task failed: {e}")))?
77:     }
78: 
79:     pub async fn write<F, R>(&self, f: F) -> DbResult<R>
80:     where
81:         F: FnOnce(&mut Connection) -> DbResult<R> + Send + 'static,
82:         R: Send + 'static,
83:     {
84:         let writer = Arc::clone(&self.writer);
85:         tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
86:             let mut conn = writer.lock().map_err(|e| DbError::Lock(e.to_string()))?;
87:             f(&mut conn)
88:         })
89:         .await
90:         .map_err(|e| DbError::Task(format!("Database write task failed: {e}")))?
91:     }
92: 
93:     pub async fn get_local_path(&self, manga_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
94:         let id = manga_id.to_string();
95:         self.read(move |conn| {
96:             let mut stmt = conn
97:                 .prepare_cached("SELECT local_path FROM manga WHERE id = ?1")
98:                 .map_err(|e| DbError::Sqlite(e.to_string()))?;
99:             let res = stmt
100:                 .query_row([&id], |row| row.get::<_, Option<String>>(0))
101:                 .optional()
102:                 .map_err(|e| DbError::Sqlite(e.to_string()))?
103:                 .flatten();
104:             Ok(res)
105:         })
106:         .await
107:         .ok()
108:         .flatten()
109:     }
110: 
111:     pub fn get_local_path_sync(&self, manga_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
112:         let conn = self.reader.lock().ok()?;
113:         let mut stmt = conn
114:             .prepare_cached("SELECT local_path FROM manga WHERE id = ?1")
115:             .ok()?;
116:         let res: Option<String> = stmt
117:             .query_row([manga_id], |row| row.get(0))
118:             .optional()
119:             .ok()
120:             .flatten();
121:         res
122:     }
123: 
124:     pub fn get_previous_manga_for_indexing_sync(
125:         &self,
126:         target_id: Option<&str>,
127:         local_path: Option<&str>,
128:     ) -> Option<MangaRecord> {
129:         let conn = self.reader.lock().ok()?;
130:         let id_found: Option<String> = if let Some(tid) = target_id {
131:             let mut stmt = conn
132:                 .prepare_cached("SELECT id FROM manga WHERE id = ?1")
133:                 .ok()?;
134:             stmt.query_row([tid], |row| row.get(0))
135:                 .optional()
136:                 .ok()
137:                 .flatten()
138:         } else if let Some(lp) = local_path {
139:             let mut stmt = conn
140:                 .prepare_cached("SELECT id FROM manga WHERE local_path = ?1")
141:                 .ok()?;
142:             stmt.query_row([lp], |row| row.get(0))
143:                 .optional()
144:                 .ok()
145:                 .flatten()
146:         } else {
147:             None
148:         };
149: 
150:         if let Some(id) = id_found {
151:             get_manga(&conn, &id).ok().flatten()
152:         } else {
153:             None
154:         }
155:     }
156: 
157:     pub fn save_manga_sync(&self, manga: &MangaRecord) -> DbResult<()> {
158:         let mut conn = self
159:             .writer
160:             .lock()
161:             .map_err(|e| DbError::Lock(e.to_string()))?;
162:         save_manga(&mut conn, manga)
163:     }
164:     
165:     pub fn allocate_unique_slug_sync(&self, base_slug: &str, manga_id: &str) -> String {
166:         let reserved = ["read", "assets", "api"];
167:         let used: std::collections::HashSet<String> = self
168:             .reader
169:             .lock()
170:             .ok()
171:             .and_then(|conn| {
172:                 let mut stmt = conn
173:                     .prepare_cached("SELECT slug FROM manga WHERE id != ?1")
174:                     .ok()?;
175:                 let rows = stmt
176:                     .query_map([manga_id], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))
177:                     .ok()?;
178:                 Some(rows.flatten().collect())
179:             })
180:             .unwrap_or_default();
181: 
182:         for attempt in 0..=(used.len() + reserved.len()) {
183:             let candidate = if attempt == 0 {
184:                 base_slug.to_string()
185:             } else {
186:                 format!("{}-{}", base_slug, attempt + 1)
187:             };
188:             if !used.contains(&candidate) && !reserved.contains(&candidate.as_str()) {
189:                 return candidate;
190:             }
191:         }
192:         format!("{}-{}", base_slug, uuid::Uuid::new_v4())
193:     }
194: }
195:

199:     #[serde(default)]
200:     pub dimensions_pending: Option<bool>,
201:     pub name: String,
202:     pub path: String,
203:     pub width: u32,
204:     pub height: u32,
205:     #[serde(default)]
206:     pub size: Option<u64>,
207:     #[serde(default)]
208:     pub modified: Option<f64>,
209: }

213: pub struct PositionRecord {

215:     #[serde(default)]
216:     pub path: Option<String>,
217:     pub fraction: f64,
218:     #[serde(default)]
219:     pub manga_bookmark: Option<bool>,
220:     #[serde(default)]

224: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
225: #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
226: pub struct ChapterRecord {
227:     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
228:     pub id: Option<String>,
229:     pub name: String,
230:     pub pages: Vec<PageRecord>,
231:     pub read: bool,
232:     #[serde(default)]
233:     pub position: Option<PositionRecord>,
234:     #[serde(default)]
235:     pub bookmarks: Option<Vec<PositionRecord>>,
236: }
237: 
238: /// Generates a stable chapter ID resilient to renames and reordering.
239: pub fn derive_chapter_id(manga_id: &str, chapter: &ChapterRecord, chapter_idx: usize) -> String {
240:     if let Some(ref id) = chapter.id {
241:         let trimmed = id.trim();
242:         if !trimmed.is_empty() {
243:             return trimmed.to_string();
244:         }
245:     }
246:     if let Some(first_page) = chapter.pages.first() {
247:         if let Some(parent) = std::path::Path::new(&first_page.path).parent() {
248:             let parent_str = parent.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/");
249:             if !parent_str.is_empty() && parent_str != "." {
250:                 return format!("{}:{}", manga_id, parent_str);
251:             }
252:         }
253:         return format!("{}:p:{}", manga_id, first_page.path);
254:     }
255:     let slug: String = chapter
256:         .name
257:         .chars()
258:         .map(|c| {
259:             if c.is_alphanumeric() {
260:                 c.to_ascii_lowercase()
261:             } else {
262:                 '-'
263:             }
264:         })
265:         .collect();
266:     format!("{}:{}:{}", manga_id, chapter_idx, slug.trim_matches('-'))
267: }
268:

281:     pub updated_at: f64,
282:     #[serde(default)]
283:     pub revision: Option<u64>,
284:     #[serde(default)]
285:     pub sort: Option<String>,
286: }

325: impl From<crate::validation::ValidatedPage> for PageRecord {
326:     fn from(p: crate::validation::ValidatedPage) -> Self {
327:         Self {
328:             name: p.name,
329:             path: p.path,
330:             width: p.width,
331:             height: p.height,
332:             size: p.size,
333:             modified: p.modified,
334:             dimensions_pending: p.dimensions_pending,
335:         }
336:     }
337: }

339: impl From<crate::validation::ValidatedPosition> for PositionRecord {
340:     fn from(p: crate::validation::ValidatedPosition) -> Self {
341:         Self {
342:             page: p.page,
343:             path: p.path,
344:             fraction: p.fraction,
345:             manga_bookmark: p.manga_bookmark,
346:             timestamp: p.timestamp,
347:         }
348:     }

351: impl From<crate::validation::ValidatedChapter> for ChapterRecord {
352:     fn from(c: crate::validation::ValidatedChapter) -> Self {
353:         Self {
354:             id: c.id,
355:             name: c.name,
356:             pages: c.pages.into_iter().map(PageRecord::from).collect(),
357:             read: c.read.unwrap_or(false),
358:             position: c.position.map(PositionRecord::from),
359:             bookmarks: c
360:                 .bookmarks
361:                 .map(|bms| bms.into_iter().map(PositionRecord::from).collect()),
362:         }
363:     }
