=== FILE: C:\Users\VKPs\Documents\manga-reader\src-tauri\src\migration.rs ===
1: use std::fs;
2: use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
3: 
4: pub struct MigrationLock {
5:     path: PathBuf,
6: }
7: 
8: impl MigrationLock {
9:     pub fn try_acquire(lock_dir: &Path) -> Option<Self> {
10:         let _ = fs::create_dir_all(lock_dir);
11:         let path = lock_dir.join("migration.lock");
12:         match fs::OpenOptions::new()
13:             .write(true)
14:             .create_new(true)
15:             .open(&path)
16:         {
17:             Ok(mut file) => {
18:                 use std::io::Write;
19:                 let _ = writeln!(file, "pid={}", std::process::id());
20:                 Some(Self { path })
21:             }
22:             Err(_) => {
23:                 // Stale lock recovery: if lock file is older than 60 seconds, clear and retry
24:                 if let Ok(meta) = path.metadata() {
25:                     if let Some(elapsed) = meta.modified().ok().and_then(|m| m.elapsed().ok()) {
26:                         if elapsed.as_secs() > 60 {
27:                             let _ = fs::remove_file(&path);
28:                             if let Ok(mut file) = fs::OpenOptions::new()
29:                                 .write(true)
30:                                 .create_new(true)
31:                                 .open(&path)
32:                             {
33:                                 use std::io::Write;
34:                                 let _ = writeln!(file, "pid={}", std::process::id());
35:                                 return Some(Self { path });
36:                             }
37:                         }
38:                     }
39:                 }
40:                 None
41:             }
42:         }
43:     }
44: }
45: 
46: impl Drop for MigrationLock {
47:     fn drop(&mut self) {
48:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&self.path);
49:     }
50: }
51: 
52: /// Copies a directory tree to destination with staging files, file-size verification,
53: /// and resumable retries. Writes .migration_complete marker only upon full success.
54: pub fn copy_dir_resumable_and_verify(src: &Path, dst: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
55:     if !dst.exists() {
56:         fs::create_dir_all(dst)?;
57:     }
58:     let marker_file = dst.join(".migration_complete");
59:     if marker_file.exists() {
60:         return Ok(());
61:     }
62: 
63:     let mut stack = vec![(src.to_path_buf(), dst.to_path_buf())];
64:     let mut total_files = 0;
65: 
66:     while let Some((current_src, current_dst)) = stack.pop() {
67:         if !current_dst.exists() {
68:             fs::create_dir_all(&current_dst)?;
69:         }
70:         for entry in fs::read_dir(&current_src)? {
71:             let entry = entry?;
72:             let file_type = entry.file_type()?;
73:             let dest_path = current_dst.join(entry.file_name());
74: 
75:             if file_type.is_dir() {
76:                 stack.push((entry.path(), dest_path));
77:             } else if file_type.is_file() {
78:                 total_files += 1;
79:                 let src_meta = entry.metadata()?;
80:                 let should_copy = if dest_path.exists() {
81:                     match dest_path.metadata() {
82:                         Ok(dst_meta) => dst_meta.len() != src_meta.len(),
83:                         Err(_) => true,
84:                     }
85:                 } else {
86:                     true
87:                 };
88: 
89:                 if should_copy {
90:                     let parent = dest_path.parent().unwrap_or(dst);
91:                     let file_name = dest_path
92:                         .file_name()
93:                         .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
94:                         .unwrap_or("mig");
95:                     let stage_path = parent.join(format!(".{file_name}.stage"));
96:                     fs::copy(entry.path(), &stage_path)?;
97:                     fs::rename(&stage_path, &dest_path)?;
98:                 }
99:             }
100:         }
101:     }
102: 
103:     let marker_data = format!(
104:         "migrated_files={total_files}\ntimestamp={}\n",
105:         std::time::SystemTime::now()
106:             .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
107:             .unwrap_or_default()
108:             .as_secs()
109:     );
110:     fs::write(&marker_file, marker_data)?;
111: 
112:     Ok(())
113: }
114: 
115: /// Automatically migrates WebView2 profile (IndexedDB, LevelDB, Local Storage)
116: /// and Roaming AppData (app_config.json, allowed_roots.json, window state)
117: /// from legacy identifier `com.mangareader.desktop` to canonical `com.mangareader.app`.
118: ///
119: /// Uses an exclusive migration lock to ensure it never copies an actively written
120: /// profile, verifies files via atomic staging, and records completion in a marker file.
121: pub fn migrate_legacy_data_if_needed() {
122:     #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
123:     {
124:         // 1. Local AppData migration (WebView2 User Data Folder & IndexedDB)
125:         if let Ok(local_app_data) = std::env::var("LOCALAPPDATA") {
126:             let local_dir = PathBuf::from(local_app_data);
127:             let old_local = local_dir.join("com.mangareader.desktop");
128:             let new_local = local_dir.join("com.mangareader.app");
129: 
130:             if old_local.exists() {
131:                 let _lock = MigrationLock::try_acquire(&new_local);
132:                 if _lock.is_none() {
133:                     log::warn!(
134:                         "Migration lock held by another process; skipping concurrent local data migration"
135:                     );
136:                 } else {
137:                     let old_ebwebview = old_local.join("EBWebView");
138:                     let new_ebwebview = new_local.join("EBWebView");
139: 
140:                     if old_ebwebview.exists() {
141:                         let _ = copy_dir_resumable_and_verify(&old_ebwebview, &new_ebwebview);
142:                     }
143: 
144:                     let _ = copy_dir_resumable_and_verify(&old_local, &new_local);
145:                 }
146:             }
147:         }
148: 
149:         // 2. Roaming AppData migration (app_config.json, allowed_roots.json, .window-state.json)
150:         if let Ok(roaming_app_data) = std::env::var("APPDATA") {
151:             let roaming_dir = PathBuf::from(roaming_app_data);
152:             let old_roaming = roaming_dir.join("com.mangareader.desktop");
153:             let new_roaming = roaming_dir.join("com.mangareader.app");
154: 
155:             if old_roaming.exists() {
156:                 let _lock = MigrationLock::try_acquire(&new_roaming);
157:                 if _lock.is_none() {
158:                     log::warn!(
159:                         "Migration lock held by another process; skipping concurrent roaming data migration"
160:                     );
161:                 } else if let Err(e) = copy_dir_resumable_and_verify(&old_roaming, &new_roaming) {
162:                     log::warn!(
163:                         "Failed to migrate roaming data from {} to {}: {e}",
164:                         old_roaming.display(),
165:                         new_roaming.display()
166:                     );
167:                 }
168:             }
169:         }
170:     }
171: 
172:     #[cfg(not(target_os = "windows"))]
173:     {
174:         if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME") {
175:             let home_path = PathBuf::from(home);
176:             // macOS ~/Library/Application Support/
177:             let mac_old = home_path.join("Library/Application Support/com.mangareader.desktop");
178:             let mac_new = home_path.join("Library/Application Support/com.mangareader.app");
179:             if mac_old.exists() {
180:                 let _lock = MigrationLock::try_acquire(&mac_new);
181:                 if _lock.is_some() {
182:                     let _ = copy_dir_resumable_and_verify(&mac_old, &mac_new);
183:                 }
184:             }
185: 
186:             // Linux ~/.local/share/
187:             let linux_old = home_path.join(".local/share/com.mangareader.desktop");
188:             let linux_new = home_path.join(".local/share/com.mangareader.app");
189:             if linux_old.exists() {
190:                 let _lock = MigrationLock::try_acquire(&linux_new);
191:                 if _lock.is_some() {
192:                     let _ = copy_dir_resumable_and_verify(&linux_old, &linux_new);
193:                 }
194:             }
195:         }
196:     }

255:         let lock2 = MigrationLock::try_acquire(&temp_dir);
256:         assert!(lock2.is_none());
257: 
258:         drop(lock1);
259: 
260:         // Can acquire after drop
261:         let lock3 = MigrationLock::try_acquire(&temp_dir);
262:         assert!(lock3.is_some());
263:         drop(lock3);

20: /// Restricts CORS headers to trusted application origins instead of wildcard *.
21: pub fn get_cors_allow_origin(req: &Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Option<&'static str> {
22:     if let Some(origin_val) = req.headers().get("origin").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()) {
23:         let trimmed = origin_val.trim();
24:         for &trusted in TRUSTED_ORIGINS {
25:             if trimmed.eq_ignore_ascii_case(trusted) {
26:                 return Some(trusted);
27:             }
28:         }
29:     }
30:     None
31: }
32: 
33: fn apply_cors(
34:     builder: tauri::http::response::Builder,
35:     req: &Request<Vec<u8>>,
36: ) -> tauri::http::response::Builder {
37:     if let Some(origin) = get_cors_allow_origin(req) {
38:         builder
39:             .header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin)
40:             .header("Vary", "Origin")
41:     } else {
42:         builder
43:     }
44: }

46: pub fn matches_if_none_match(header_val: &str, current_etag: &str) -> bool {
47:     let clean_current = current_etag
48:         .trim()
49:         .trim_start_matches("W/")
50:         .trim()
51:         .trim_matches('"');
52:     for tag in header_val.split(',') {
53:         let tag = tag.trim();
54:         if tag == "*" {
55:             return true;
56:         }
57:         let clean_tag = tag.trim_start_matches("W/").trim().trim_matches('"');
58:         if clean_tag == clean_current {
59:             return true;
60:         }
61:     }
62:     false
63: }

65: pub fn decode_request_path(path_str: &str) -> String {
66:     let decoded = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(path_str);
67:     #[cfg(windows)]
68:     {
69:         let trimmed = decoded.trim_start_matches('/');
70:         if trimmed.len() >= 2
71:             && trimmed.as_bytes()[1] == b':'
72:             && trimmed.as_bytes()[0].is_ascii_alphabetic()
73:         {
74:             return trimmed.to_string();
75:         }
76:     }
77:     decoded
78: }

80: /// Encodes a single path segment per RFC 3986, preserving unreserved characters
81: /// (alphanumeric, -, _, ., ~) and percent-encoding all others including spaces, #, %, and UTF-8 bytes.
82: pub fn encode_path_segment(segment: &str) -> String {
83:     let mut encoded = String::with_capacity(segment.len() * 3);
84:     for b in segment.as_bytes() {
85:         match b {
86:             b'a'..=b'z' | b'A'..=b'Z' | b'0'..=b'9' | b'-' | b'_' | b'.' | b'~' => {
87:                 encoded.push(*b as char);
88:             }
89:             _ => {
90:                 use std::fmt::Write;
91:                 let _ = write!(encoded, "%{:02X}", b);
92:             }
93:         }
94:     }
95:     encoded
96: }
97: 
98: /// Parses HTTP Range header.
99: /// Rejects suffix ranges on empty files, extra delimiters, and invalid numbers.
100: pub fn parse_range(header_val: &str, total_size: u64) -> Option<(u64, u64)> {
101:     if total_size == 0 {
102:         return None;
103:     }
104:     let trimmed = header_val.trim();
105:     if !trimmed.starts_with("bytes=") {
106:         return None;
107:     }
108:     let range_str = trimmed[6..].trim();
109:     if range_str.contains(',') {
110:         // Multi-range or trailing comma delimiters are not supported
111:         return None;
112:     }
113: 
114:     let mut bounds = range_str.split('-');
115:     let start_str = bounds.next()?.trim();
116:     let end_str = bounds.next()?.trim();
117:     if bounds.next().is_some() {
118:         // Extra hyphens (e.g., 0-10-20)
119:         return None;
120:     }
121: 
122:     if start_str.is_empty() && end_str.is_empty() {
123:         return None;
124:     }
125: 
126:     if start_str.is_empty() {
127:         if !end_str.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
128:             return None;
129:         }
130:         let suffix_len: u64 = end_str.parse().ok()?;
131:         if suffix_len == 0 {
132:             return None;
133:         }
134:         let start = total_size.saturating_sub(suffix_len);
135:         let end = total_size - 1;
136:         Some((start, end))
137:     } else {
138:         if !start_str.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
139:             return None;
140:         }
141:         let start: u64 = start_str.parse().ok()?;
142:         if start >= total_size {
143:             return None;
144:         }
145:         let end = if end_str.is_empty() {
146:             total_size - 1
147:         } else {
148:             if !end_str.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
149:                 return None;
150:             }
151:             let parsed_end: u64 = end_str.parse().ok()?;
152:             parsed_end.min(total_size - 1)
153:         };
154:         if start <= end {
155:             Some((start, end))
156:         } else {
157:             None
158:         }
159:     }
160: }

162: pub fn get_mime_type(ext: &str) -> &'static str {
163:     match ext {
164:         "jpg" | "jpeg" => "image/jpeg",
165:         "png" => "image/png",
166:         "webp" => "image/webp",
167:         "avif" => "image/avif",
168:         "gif" => "image/gif",
169:         "bmp" => "image/bmp",
170:         "svg" => "image/svg+xml",
171:         "ico" => "image/x-icon",
172:         "heic" => "image/heic",
173:         "heif" => "image/heif",
174:         "tif" | "tiff" => "image/tiff",
175:         "jxl" => "image/jxl",
176:         _ => "application/octet-stream",
177:     }
178: }

180: fn error_response(status: StatusCode, message: &str, req: &Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Response<Vec<u8>> {
181:     let mut builder = Response::builder()
182:         .status(status)
183:         .header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
184:     builder = apply_cors(builder, req);
185:     builder
186:         .body(message.as_bytes().to_vec())
187:         .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()))
188: }

190: /// Verifies whether a path is an explicitly managed image artifact in the thumbnail or variant cache.
191: /// Prevents broad authorization of arbitrary files in the cache or data directory.
192: pub fn is_explicit_cache_artifact(app: &AppHandle, path: &Path) -> bool {
193:     let file_name = match path.file_name().and_then(|f| f.to_str()) {
194:         Some(f) => f,
195:         None => return false,
196:     };
197: 
198:     if !file_name.ends_with(".webp") {
199:         return false;
200:     }
201:     let is_thumb_or_variant = file_name.starts_with("t_") || file_name.starts_with("v_");
202:     if !is_thumb_or_variant {
203:         return false;
204:     }
205: 
206:     let parent = match path.parent() {
207:         Some(p) => p,
208:         None => return false,
209:     };
210:     let parent_name = match parent.file_name().and_then(|f| f.to_str()) {
211:         Some(p) => p,
212:         None => return false,
213:     };
214:     if parent_name != "thumbnails" && parent_name != "variants" {
215:         return false;
216:     }
217: 
218:     if let Ok(cache_dir) = app.path().app_cache_dir() {
219:         if let Ok(canonical_cache) = dunce::canonicalize(&cache_dir) {
220:             if parent.starts_with(&canonical_cache) || parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
221:                 return true;
222:             }
223:         } else if parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
224:             return true;
225:         }
226:     }
227:     if let Ok(app_dir) = app.path().app_data_dir() {
228:         let cache_dir = app_dir.join("cache");
229:         if let Ok(canonical_cache) = dunce::canonicalize(&cache_dir) {
230:             if parent.starts_with(&canonical_cache) || parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
231:                 return true;
232:             }
233:         } else if parent.starts_with(&cache_dir) {
234:             return true;
235:         }
236:     }
237:     false
238: }
239: 
240: pub fn is_in_thumbnail_cache(app: &AppHandle, path: &Path) -> bool {
241:     is_explicit_cache_artifact(app, path)
242: }

244: /// Constructs properly encoded mangaimg URLs where path segments are individually escaped,
245: /// ensuring Windows paths with spaces, Unicode characters, #, and % roundtrip safely without URI truncation.
246: pub fn path_to_mangaimg_url(path: &Path) -> String {
247:     let s = path.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/");
248:     let trimmed = s.trim_start_matches('/');
249:     if trimmed.len() >= 2
250:         && trimmed.as_bytes()[1] == b':'
251:         && trimmed.as_bytes()[0].is_ascii_alphabetic()
252:     {
253:         let drive = &trimmed[0..1];
254:         let rest = trimmed[2..].trim_start_matches('/');
255:         let segments: Vec<String> = rest
256:             .split('/')
257:             .filter(|seg| !seg.is_empty())
258:             .map(encode_path_segment)
259:             .collect();
260:         format!(
261:             "http://mangaimg.localhost/{drive}%3A/{}",
262:             segments.join("/")
263:         )
264:     } else {
265:         let segments: Vec<String> = trimmed
266:             .split('/')
267:             .filter(|seg| !seg.is_empty())
268:             .map(encode_path_segment)
269:             .collect();
270:         format!("http://mangaimg.localhost/{}", segments.join("/"))
271:     }
272: }

288: pub fn parse_page_resource_uri(uri_path: &str) -> Option<PageResourceParams> {
289:     let rest = uri_path.strip_prefix("/page/")?;
290:     let parts: Vec<&str> = rest.splitn(4, '/').collect();
291:     if parts.len() < 4 {
292:         return None;
293:     }
294:     let manga_id = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(parts[0]);
295:     let version = parts[1].to_string();
296:     let transform = parts[2].to_string();
297:     let rel_path = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(parts[3]);
298:     if manga_id.is_empty() || rel_path.is_empty() {
299:         return None;
300:     }
301:     Some(PageResourceParams {
302:         manga_id,
303:         version,
304:         transform,
305:         rel_path,
306:     })
307: }
308: 
309: pub fn parse_cover_resource_uri(uri_path: &str) -> Option<CoverResourceParams> {
310:     let rest = uri_path.strip_prefix("/cover/")?;
311:     let trimmed = rest.trim_matches('/');
312:     let parts: Vec<&str> = trimmed.split('/').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).collect();
313:     if parts.is_empty() {
314:         return None;
315:     }
316:     let manga_id = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(parts[0]);
317:     if manga_id.is_empty() {
318:         return None;
319:     }
320:     let version = parts.get(1).map(|v| v.to_string());
321:     Some(CoverResourceParams { manga_id, version })
322: }
323: 
324: pub fn build_page_resource_url(
325:     manga_id: &str,
326:     version: &str,
327:     transform: &str,
328:     rel_path: &str,
329:     root: Option<&str>,
330: ) -> String {
331:     let enc_manga_id = encode_path_segment(manga_id);
332:     let enc_version = encode_path_segment(version);
333:     let enc_transform = encode_path_segment(transform);
334:     let clean_rel = rel_path.replace('\\', "/");
335:     let segments: Vec<String> = clean_rel
336:         .split('/')
337:         .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
338:         .map(encode_path_segment)
339:         .collect();
340:     let enc_path = segments.join("/");
341:     let base_url = format!(
342:         "http://mangaimg.localhost/page/{}/{}/{}/{}",
343:         enc_manga_id, enc_version, enc_transform, enc_path
344:     );
345:     if let Some(r) = root {
346:         if !r.is_empty() {
347:             return format!("{}?root={}", base_url, encode_path_segment(r));
348:         }
349:     }
350:     base_url
351: }

353: pub fn build_cover_resource_url(manga_id: &str, version: Option<&str>) -> String {
354:     let enc_manga_id = encode_path_segment(manga_id);
355:     match version {
356:         Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => {
357:             format!(
358:                 "http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/{}/{}",
359:                 enc_manga_id,
360:                 encode_path_segment(v)
361:             )
362:         }
363:         _ => format!("http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/{}", enc_manga_id),
364:     }
365: }

367: pub fn parse_fit_dimensions(transform: &str) -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
368:     let rest = transform.strip_prefix("fit_")?;
369:     let mut parts = rest.split('x');
370:     let w_str = parts.next()?;
371:     let h_str = parts.next()?;
372:     let w: u32 = w_str.parse().ok()?;
373:     let h: u32 = h_str.parse().ok()?;
374:     if w == 0 || h == 0 {
375:         return None;
376:     }
377:     Some((w, h))
378: }

380: fn try_find_and_generate_cover(
381:     app: &AppHandle,
382:     base_dir: &Path,
383:     manga_id: &str,
384: ) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
385:     if !base_dir.is_dir() {
386:         return Err("Base directory does not exist".into());
387:     }
388: 
389:     // Common cover filenames
390:     for name in &[
391:         "cover.jpg",
392:         "cover.jpeg",
393:         "cover.png",
394:         "cover.webp",
395:         "thumbnail.jpg",
396:         "thumbnail.jpeg",
397:         "thumbnail.png",
398:         "thumbnail.webp",
399:         "folder.jpg",
400:     ] {
401:         let candidate = base_dir.join(name);
402:         if candidate.is_file() {
403:             return crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(app, &candidate, manga_id);
404:         }
405:     }
406: 
407:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(base_dir) {
408:         let mut files: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
409:             .flatten()
410:             .map(|e| e.path())
411:             .filter(|p| p.is_file())
412:             .collect();
413:         files.sort_by(|a, b| {
414:             crate::commands::fs::natural_order(&a.to_string_lossy(), &b.to_string_lossy())
415:         });
416:         for path in files {
417:             if let Some(ext) = path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) {
418:                 if ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.to_lowercase().as_str()) {
419:                     return crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(app, &path, manga_id);
420:                 }
421:             }
422:         }
423:     }
424: 
425:     if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(base_dir) {
426:         let mut subdirs: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
427:             .flatten()
428:             .map(|e| e.path())
429:             .filter(|p| p.is_dir() && !crate::commands::fs::is_ignored_directory(p))
430:             .collect();
431:         subdirs.sort_by(|a, b| {
432:             crate::commands::fs::natural_order(&a.to_string_lossy(), &b.to_string_lossy())
433:         });
434:         for subdir in subdirs {
435:             if let Ok(sub_entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&subdir) {
436:                 let mut files: Vec<PathBuf> = sub_entries
437:                     .flatten()
438:                     .map(|e| e.path())
439:                     .filter(|p| p.is_file())
440:                     .collect();
441:                 files.sort_by(|a, b| {
442:                     crate::commands::fs::natural_order(&a.to_string_lossy(), &b.to_string_lossy())
443:                 });
444:                 for f in files {
445:                     if let Some(ext) = f.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) {
446:                         if ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.to_lowercase().as_str()) {
447:                             return crate::commands::image::generate_thumbnail_direct(
448:                                 app, &f, manga_id,
449:                             );
450:                         }
451:                     }
452:                 }
453:             }
454:         }
455:     }
456: 
457:     Err("No image found to generate cover".into())
458: }

460: fn safe_name(s: &str) -> bool {
461:     !s.is_empty() && !s.contains(['/', '\\', ':']) && !s.contains("..")
462: }

464: pub fn handle_mangaimg_request(app: &AppHandle, req: Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Response<Vec<u8>> {
465:     // 0. Method restriction: only GET and HEAD are permitted
466:     if req.method() != "GET" && req.method() != "HEAD" {
467:         let mut builder = Response::builder()
468:             .status(StatusCode::METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED)
469:             .header("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
470:             .header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
471:         builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
472:         return builder
473:             .body(
474:                 "Method not allowed. Only GET and HEAD are supported."
475:                     .as_bytes()
476:                     .to_vec(),
477:             )
478:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
479:     }
480: 
481:     let uri_path = req.uri().path();
482: 
483:     // Support resource URLs (/cover/, /page/), opaque image ID endpoints, and canonical path requests
484:     let raw_target: PathBuf = if let Some(cover_params) = parse_cover_resource_uri(uri_path) {
485:         let safe_id = crate::commands::cache::sanitize_id(&cover_params.manga_id);
486:         let cache_dir = app
487:             .path()
488:             .app_cache_dir()
489:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("cache"));
490:         let thumb_path = cache_dir.join("thumbnails").join(format!("{safe_id}.webp"));
491: 
492:         if thumb_path.exists() {
493:             thumb_path
494:         } else {
495:             let local_path_opt = app
496:                 .try_state::<crate::db::DbState>()
497:                 .and_then(|db| db.get_local_path_sync(&cover_params.manga_id));
498:             if let Some(ref lp) = local_path_opt {
499:                 let base = PathBuf::from(lp);
500:                 match try_find_and_generate_cover(app, &base, &cover_params.manga_id) {
501:                     Ok(p) => p,
502:                     Err(_) => {
503:                         return error_response(
504:                             StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
505:                             "Cover image not found",
506:                             &req,
507:                         );
508:                     }
509:                 }
510:             } else {
511:                 return error_response(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "Cover thumbnail not found", &req);
512:             }
513:         }
514:     } else if let Some(page_params) = parse_page_resource_uri(uri_path) {
515:         if let Err(e) = crate::validation::validate_relative_path(&page_params.rel_path) {
516:             return error_response(
517:                 StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
518:                 &format!("Invalid relative path in page URL: {e}"),
519:                 &req,
520:             );
521:         }
522: 
523:         let query_root = req.uri().query().and_then(|q| {
524:             q.split('&').find_map(|pair| {
525:                 pair.strip_prefix("root=")
526:                     .map(crate::commands::cache::percent_decode)
527:             })
528:         });
529: 
530:         let base_dir_str = match query_root {
531:             Some(ref r) if !r.trim().is_empty() => Some(r.clone()),
532:             _ => app
533:                 .try_state::<crate::db::DbState>()
534:                 .and_then(|db| db.get_local_path_sync(&page_params.manga_id)),
535:         };
536: 
537:         let base_dir_str = match base_dir_str {
538:             Some(s) => s,
539:             None => {
540:                 return error_response(
541:                     StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
542:                     &format!(
543:                         "Manga '{}' not found or localPath unavailable",
544:                         page_params.manga_id
545:                     ),
546:                     &req,
547:                 );
548:             }
549:         };
550: 
551:         let base_dir = PathBuf::from(base_dir_str.trim());
552:         let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
553:         if !allowed.is_allowed(&base_dir) {
554:             return error_response(
555:                 StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
556:                 "Base root is not under an authorized library root",
557:                 &req,
558:             );
559:         }
560:         let resolved =
561:             match crate::commands::fs::resolve_relative_path(&base_dir, &page_params.rel_path) {
562:                 Ok(p) => p,
563:                 Err(e) => {
564:                     return error_response(
565:                         StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
566:                         &format!("Page file not found: {e}"),
567:                         &req,
568:                     );
569:                 }
570:             };
571: 
572:         if !allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) {
573:             return error_response(
574:                 StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
575:                 "Path is not under an authorized library root",
576:                 &req,
577:             );
578:         }
579: 
580:         match page_params.transform.as_str() {
581:             "raw" | "full" | "original" => resolved,
582:             "thumb" => {
583:                 match crate::commands::image::generate_image_variant_direct(
584:                     app, &resolved, 320, 420,
585:                 ) {
586:                     Ok(variant_path) => variant_path,
587:                     Err(_) => resolved,
588:                 }
589:             }
590:             "preview" | "low" => {
591:                 match crate::commands::image::generate_image_variant_direct(
592:                     app, &resolved, 1000, 1600,
593:                 ) {
594:                     Ok(variant_path) => variant_path,
595:                     Err(_) => resolved,
596:                 }
597:             }
598:             fit if fit.starts_with("fit_") => {
599:                 let (w, h) = parse_fit_dimensions(fit).unwrap_or((1000, 1600));
600:                 match crate::commands::image::generate_image_variant_direct(app, &resolved, w, h) {
601:                     Ok(variant_path) => variant_path,
602:                     Err(_) => resolved,
603:                 }
604:             }
605:             _ => resolved,
606:         }
607:     } else if let Some(id_part) = uri_path.strip_prefix("/thumb/") {
608:         let decoded = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(id_part);
609:         if !safe_name(&decoded) {
610:             return error_response(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "Bad name", &req);
611:         }
612:         let filename = if decoded.starts_with("t_") && decoded.ends_with(".webp") {
613:             decoded
614:         } else {
615:             let safe_id = crate::commands::cache::sanitize_id(&decoded);
616:             format!("{safe_id}.webp")
617:         };
618:         let cache_dir = app
619:             .path()
620:             .app_cache_dir()
621:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("cache"));
622:         cache_dir.join("thumbnails").join(filename)
623:     } else if let Some(hash_part) = uri_path.strip_prefix("/variant/") {
624:         let decoded = crate::commands::cache::percent_decode(hash_part);
625:         if !safe_name(&decoded) {
626:             return error_response(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "Bad name", &req);
627:         }
628:         let filename = if decoded.ends_with(".webp") {
629:             decoded
630:         } else {
631:             format!("{decoded}.webp")
632:         };
633:         let cache_dir = app
634:             .path()
635:             .app_cache_dir()
636:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("cache"));
637:         cache_dir.join("variants").join(filename)
638:     } else {
639:         let decoded_path = decode_request_path(uri_path);
640:         if decoded_path.is_empty() {
641:             return error_response(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "Missing file path", &req);
642:         }
643:         PathBuf::from(&decoded_path)
644:     };
645: 
646:     let resolved = match dunce::canonicalize(&raw_target) {
647:         Ok(p) => p,
648:         Err(_) => return error_response(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "Target file not found", &req),
649:     };
650: 
651:     // 1. Path allowlist checks (AllowedRoots or explicit cache artifact)
652:     let allowed = app.state::<AllowedRoots>();
653:     let is_allowed = allowed.is_allowed(&resolved) || is_explicit_cache_artifact(app, &resolved);
654:     if !is_allowed {
655:         return error_response(
656:             StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
657:             "Path is not under an allowed library root or managed cache",
658:             &req,
659:         );
660:     }
661: 
662:     // 2. Extension check
663:     let ext = resolved
664:         .extension()
665:         .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
666:         .unwrap_or("")
667:         .to_lowercase();
668:     if !ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.contains(&ext.as_str()) {
669:         return error_response(
670:             StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
671:             "File extension is not an allowed image format",
672:             &req,
673:         );
674:     }
675: 
676:     // 3. Regular file and size check (100 MiB cap)
677:     // Open the descriptor once to eliminate TOCTOU race conditions between inspection and read.
678:     let mut file = match File::open(&resolved) {
679:         Ok(f) => f,
680:         Err(e) => {
681:             return error_response(
682:                 StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
683:                 &format!("Failed to open file: {e}"),
684:                 &req,
685:             );
686:         }
687:     };
688: 
689:     let metadata = match file.metadata() {
690:         Ok(m) => m,
691:         Err(_) => {
692:             return error_response(
693:                 StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
694:                 "Failed to inspect file metadata",
695:                 &req,
696:             );
697:         }
698:     };
699: 
700:     if !metadata.is_file() {
701:         return error_response(
702:             StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
703:             "Target path is not a regular file",
704:             &req,
705:         );
706:     }
707: 
708:     let initial_size = metadata.len();
709:     if initial_size > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
710:         return error_response(
711:             StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE,
712:             "File exceeds 100 MiB safety limit",
713:             &req,
714:         );
715:     }
716: 
717:     let mtime_ms = metadata
718:         .modified()
719:         .ok()
720:         .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
721:         .map(|d| d.as_millis())
722:         .unwrap_or(0);
723: 
724:     // Format as weak validator per RFC 9110 Section 8.8.3
725:     let etag = format!("W/\"{:x}-{:x}\"", mtime_ms, initial_size);
726:     let mime = get_mime_type(&ext);
727: 
728:     // 4. Conditional GET via ETag (304 Not Modified)
729:     if let Some(if_none_match) = req.headers().get("if-none-match") {
730:         if let Ok(val) = if_none_match.to_str() {
731:             if matches_if_none_match(val, &etag) {
732:                 let mut builder = Response::builder()
733:                     .status(StatusCode::NOT_MODIFIED)
734:                     .header("ETag", &etag)
735:                     .header("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
736:                 builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
737:                 return builder
738:                     .body(Vec::new())
739:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
740:             }
741:         }
742:     }
743: 
744:     let is_head = req.method() == "HEAD";
745: 
746:     // 5. HTTP Range request handling (206 Partial Content) with 8 MiB chunk capping
747:     if let Some(range_header) = req.headers().get("range") {
748:         if let Ok(range_str) = range_header.to_str() {
749:             if let Some((start, end)) = parse_range(range_str, initial_size) {
750:                 let capped_end = end.min(start.saturating_add(MAX_RANGE_CHUNK_BYTES - 1));
751:                 let length = capped_end - start + 1;
752:                 let body = if is_head {
753:                     Vec::new()
754:                 } else {
755:                     if let Err(e) = file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(start)) {
756:                         return error_response(
757:                             StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
758:                             &format!("Seek error: {e}"),
759:                             &req,
760:                         );
761:                     }
762:                     let mut buffer = vec![0u8; length as usize];
763:                     if let Err(e) = file.read_exact(&mut buffer) {
764:                         return error_response(
765:                             StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
766:                             &format!("Read error: {e}"),
767:                             &req,
768:                         );
769:                     }
770:                     buffer
771:                 };
772: 
773:                 let mut builder = Response::builder()
774:                     .status(StatusCode::PARTIAL_CONTENT)
775:                     .header("Content-Type", mime)
776:                     .header("Content-Length", length.to_string())
777:                     .header(
778:                         "Content-Range",
779:                         format!("bytes {}-{}/{}", start, capped_end, initial_size),
780:                     )
781:                     .header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
782:                     .header("ETag", &etag)
783:                     .header("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
784:                 builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
785:                 return builder
786:                     .body(body)
787:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
788:             } else {
789:                 let mut builder = Response::builder()
790:                     .status(StatusCode::RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE)
791:                     .header("Content-Range", format!("bytes */{}", initial_size));
792:                 builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
793:                 return builder
794:                     .body(Vec::new())
795:                     .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()));
796:             }
797:         }
798:     }
799: 
800:     // 6. Full content response (200 OK)
801:     let body = if is_head {
802:         Vec::new()
803:     } else {
804:         // Enforce the 100 MiB safety limit against the opened descriptor using take()
805:         let mut buffer = Vec::with_capacity(initial_size.min(MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES) as usize);
806:         let mut reader = (&mut file).take(MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES + 1);
807:         if let Err(e) = reader.read_to_end(&mut buffer) {
808:             return error_response(
809:                 StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
810:                 &format!("Failed to read file: {e}"),
811:                 &req,
812:             );
813:         }
814:         if buffer.len() as u64 > MAX_FILE_READ_BYTES {
815:             return error_response(
816:                 StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE,
817:                 "File exceeds 100 MiB safety limit",
818:                 &req,
819:             );
820:         }
821:         buffer
822:     };
823: 
824:     let actual_size = if is_head {
825:         initial_size
826:     } else {
827:         body.len() as u64
828:     };
829:     let mut builder = Response::builder()
830:         .status(StatusCode::OK)
831:         .header("Content-Type", mime)
832:         .header("Content-Length", actual_size.to_string())
833:         .header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
834:         .header("ETag", &etag)
835:         .header("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
836:     builder = apply_cors(builder, &req);
837:     builder
838:         .body(body)
839:         .unwrap_or_else(|_| Response::new(Vec::new()))
840: }
841: 
842: #[cfg(test)]
843: mod tests {
844:     use super::*;
845: 
846:     #[test]
847:     fn test_decode_request_path() {
848:         #[cfg(windows)]
849:         {
850:             assert_eq!(
851:                 decode_request_path("/C%3A%2FManga%2Fchapter1%2F01.jpg"),
852:                 "C:/Manga/chapter1/01.jpg"
853:             );
854:             assert_eq!(
855:                 decode_request_path("/C:/Manga/chapter1/01.jpg"),
856:                 "C:/Manga/chapter1/01.jpg"
857:             );
858:             assert_eq!(
859:                 decode_request_path("C:/Manga/chapter1/01.jpg"),
860:                 "C:/Manga/chapter1/01.jpg"
861:             );
862:         }
863:         #[cfg(not(windows))]
864:         {
865:             assert_eq!(
866:                 decode_request_path("/home/user/manga/01.jpg"),
867:                 "/home/user/manga/01.jpg"
868:             );
869:         }
870:     }
871: 
872:     #[test]
873:     fn test_parse_range() {
874:         let total = 1000;
875:         // Standard range
876:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-499", total), Some((0, 499)));
877:         // Open ended
878:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=500-", total), Some((500, 999)));
879:         // Suffix range
880:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=-200", total), Some((800, 999)));
881:         // Clamping end
882:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-2000", total), Some((0, 999)));
883:         // Out of bounds
884:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=1000-", total), None);
885:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=1500-2000", total), None);
886:         // Invalid syntax
887:         assert_eq!(parse_range("items=0-10", total), None);
888:     }
889: 
890:     #[test]
891:     fn test_parse_range_empty_file_and_delimiters() {
892:         // Suffix range on empty file must return None
893:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=-100", 0), None);
894:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-0", 0), None);
895: 
896:         // Extra delimiters must return None
897:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-10-20", 100), None);
898:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0--10", 100), None);
899:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=--10", 100), None);
900:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-10,", 100), None);
901:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=,0-10", 100), None);
902:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=0-10, 20-30", 100), None);
903:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=-", 100), None);
904:         assert_eq!(parse_range("bytes=abc-def", 100), None);
905:     }
906: 
907:     #[test]
908:     fn test_get_mime_type() {
909:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("jpg"), "image/jpeg");
910:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("jpeg"), "image/jpeg");
911:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("png"), "image/png");
912:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("webp"), "image/webp");
913:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("avif"), "image/avif");
914:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("gif"), "image/gif");
915:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("svg"), "image/svg+xml");
916:         assert_eq!(get_mime_type("unknown"), "application/octet-stream");
917:     }

919:     #[test]
920:     fn test_path_to_mangaimg_url() {
921:         let path = PathBuf::from("C:\\Manga\\Title\\01.jpg");
922:         let url = path_to_mangaimg_url(&path);
923:         assert_eq!(url, "http://mangaimg.localhost/C%3A/Manga/Title/01.jpg");
924:     }

926:     #[test]
927:     fn test_path_to_mangaimg_url_special_characters() {
928:         let path = PathBuf::from("C:\\Manga\\Ch #1\\01% 20 [テスト].jpg");
929:         let url = path_to_mangaimg_url(&path);
930:         assert_eq!(
931:             url,
932:             "http://mangaimg.localhost/C%3A/Manga/Ch%20%231/01%25%2020%20%5B%E3%83%86%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88%5D.jpg"
933:         );
934:         let uri_path = url.trim_start_matches("http://mangaimg.localhost");
935:         let decoded = decode_request_path(uri_path);
936:         assert_eq!(decoded, "C:/Manga/Ch #1/01% 20 [テスト].jpg");
937:     }
938: 
939:     #[test]
940:     fn test_matches_if_none_match() {
941:         let current = "W/\"12345-67890\"";
942: 
943:         // Exact match
944:         assert!(matches_if_none_match("W/\"12345-67890\"", current));
945:         assert!(matches_if_none_match("\"12345-67890\"", current));
946: 
947:         // List containing matching tag
948:         assert!(matches_if_none_match(
949:             "\"old-tag\", \"12345-67890\", \"other\"",
950:             current
951:         ));
952:         assert!(matches_if_none_match(
953:             "\"old-tag\", W/\"12345-67890\"",
954:             current
955:         ));
956: 
957:         // Wildcard
958:         assert!(matches_if_none_match("*", current));
959: 
960:         // Mismatches
961:         assert!(!matches_if_none_match("\"different\"", current));
962:         assert!(!matches_if_none_match("W/\"different\"", current));
963:         assert!(!matches_if_none_match("\"12345-67890-extra\"", current));
964:     }
965: 
966:     #[test]
967:     fn test_weak_etag_formatting() {
968:         let mtime = 1234567890u128;
969:         let size = 4096u64;
970:         let etag = format!("W/\"{:x}-{:x}\"", mtime, size);
971:         assert!(etag.starts_with("W/\""));
972:         assert!(matches_if_none_match(&etag, &etag));
973:         assert!(matches_if_none_match("\"499602d2-1000\"", &etag));
974:     }

976:     #[test]
977:     fn test_cors_origin_restriction() {
978:         let req_trusted = Request::builder()
979:             .header("Origin", "http://localhost:5173")
980:             .body(Vec::new())
981:             .unwrap();
982:         assert_eq!(
983:             get_cors_allow_origin(&req_trusted),
984:             Some("http://localhost:5173")
985:         );
986: 
987:         let req_untrusted = Request::builder()
988:             .header("Origin", "https://malicious-website.com")
989:             .body(Vec::new())
990:             .unwrap();
991:         assert_eq!(get_cors_allow_origin(&req_untrusted), None);
992: 
993:         let req_no_origin = Request::builder().body(Vec::new()).unwrap();
994:         assert_eq!(get_cors_allow_origin(&req_no_origin), None);
995:     }

997:     #[test]
998:     fn test_parse_and_build_page_resource_url() {
999:         let manga_id = "berserk #1";
1000:         let version = "17000000";
1001:         let transform = "preview";
1002:         let rel_path = "Ch 1/01 [raw] %20.jpg";
1003: 
1004:         let url = build_page_resource_url(manga_id, version, transform, rel_path, None);
1005:         assert!(url.starts_with("http://mangaimg.localhost/page/"));
1006:         assert!(url.contains("berserk%20%231"));
1007:         assert!(url.contains("17000000"));
1008:         assert!(url.contains("preview"));
1009: 
1010:         let uri_path = url.trim_start_matches("http://mangaimg.localhost");
1011:         let parsed = parse_page_resource_uri(uri_path).expect("should parse page URI");
1012:         assert_eq!(parsed.manga_id, "berserk #1");
1013:         assert_eq!(parsed.version, "17000000");
1014:         assert_eq!(parsed.transform, "preview");
1015:         assert_eq!(parsed.rel_path, "Ch 1/01 [raw] %20.jpg");
1016:     }
1017: 
1018:     #[test]
1019:     fn test_parse_and_build_cover_resource_url() {
1020:         let manga_id = "one piece #100";
1021:         let version = "17100000";
1022: 
1023:         let url = build_cover_resource_url(manga_id, Some(version));
1024:         assert_eq!(
1025:             url,
1026:             "http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/one%20piece%20%23100/17100000"
1027:         );
1028: 
1029:         let uri_path = url.trim_start_matches("http://mangaimg.localhost");
1030:         let parsed = parse_cover_resource_uri(uri_path).expect("should parse cover URI");
1031:         assert_eq!(parsed.manga_id, "one piece #100");
1032:         assert_eq!(parsed.version, Some("17100000".to_string()));
1033: 
1034:         let url_no_ver = build_cover_resource_url(manga_id, None);
1035:         assert_eq!(
1036:             url_no_ver,
1037:             "http://mangaimg.localhost/cover/one%20piece%20%23100"
1038:         );
1039:         let uri_no_ver = url_no_ver.trim_start_matches("http://mangaimg.localhost");
1040:         let parsed_no_ver =
1041:             parse_cover_resource_uri(uri_no_ver).expect("should parse cover URI without version");

135: pub const RESERVED_DOS_NAMES: &[&str] = &[
136:     "con", "prn", "aux", "nul", "com1", "com2", "com3", "com4", "com5", "com6", "com7", "com8",
137:     "com9", "lpt1", "lpt2", "lpt3", "lpt4", "lpt5", "lpt6", "lpt7", "lpt8", "lpt9",
138: ];

140: pub fn validate_relative_path(path: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
141:     if path.starts_with(' ') || path.ends_with(' ') {
142:         return Err(format!(
143:             "Path cannot start or end with whitespace: \"{path}\""
144:         ));
145:     }
146:     let trimmed = path.trim();
147:     if trimmed.is_empty() {
148:         return Err("Path must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
149:     }
150:     if trimmed.len() > LIMIT_PATH_CHARS {
151:         return Err(format!(
152:             "Relative path length (characters) exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_PATH_CHARS}. Use a smaller selection."
153:         ));
154:     }
155:     // Reject drive prefixes, alternate data streams, and URI schemes
156:     if trimmed.contains(':') {
157:         return Err(format!(
158:             "Path contains invalid colon or drive/stream prefix: \"{trimmed}\""
159:         ));
160:     }
161:     // Reject rooted paths, backslashes, and UNC/device syntax
162:     if trimmed.starts_with('/') || trimmed.ends_with('/') {
163:         return Err(format!(
164:             "Path cannot start or end with a slash: \"{trimmed}\""
165:         ));
166:     }
167:     if trimmed.contains('\\') {
168:         return Err(format!("Path must use forward slashes only: \"{trimmed}\""));
169:     }
170:     if trimmed.starts_with("//") {
171:         return Err(format!("Path cannot start with UNC prefix: \"{trimmed}\""));
172:     }
173:     if trimmed.contains('\0') {
174:         return Err("Path contains invalid null byte.".to_string());
175:     }
176:     if trimmed.chars().any(|c| c.is_control()) {
177:         return Err("Path contains invalid control character.".to_string());
178:     }
179: 
180:     let segments: Vec<&str> = trimmed.split('/').collect();
181:     if segments.len().saturating_sub(1) > LIMIT_DIRECTORY_DEPTH {
182:         return Err(format!(
183:             "Folder depth exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_DIRECTORY_DEPTH}. Use a smaller selection."
184:         ));
185:     }
186:     for seg in &segments {
187:         if seg.is_empty() || *seg == "." || *seg == ".." {
188:             return Err(format!(
189:                 "Path contains invalid traversal segment: \"{trimmed}\""
190:             ));
191:         }
192:         if seg.ends_with(' ') || seg.ends_with('.') {
193:             return Err(format!(
194:                 "Path segment cannot end with a space or dot: \"{seg}\""
195:             ));
196:         }
197:         let stem = seg.split('.').next().unwrap_or("").to_lowercase();
198:         if RESERVED_DOS_NAMES.contains(&stem.as_str()) {
199:             return Err(format!("Path segment uses reserved device name: \"{seg}\""));
200:         }
201:     }
202:     Ok(trimmed.to_string())
203: }
204: 
205: pub fn assert_image_dimensions(width: u32, height: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
206:     if width == 0 || height == 0 {
207:         return Err("Image dimensions must be positive integer pixels.".to_string());
208:     }
209:     let area = (width as u64).checked_mul(height as u64);
210:     match area {
211:         Some(a) if a <= 9_007_199_254_740_991 => Ok(()),
212:         _ => Err("Image area cannot be represented safely.".to_string()),
213:     }
214: }
215: 
216: pub fn validate_page(
217:     page: &mut ValidatedPage,
218:     seen_paths: &mut HashSet<String>,
219: ) -> Result<(), String> {
220:     let trimmed_name = page.name.trim();
221:     if trimmed_name.is_empty() {
222:         return Err("Page name must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
223:     }
224:     page.name = trimmed_name.to_string();
225: 
226:     let valid_path = validate_relative_path(&page.path)?;
227:     if seen_paths.contains(&valid_path) {
228:         return Err(format!(
229:             "Duplicate page path within chapter: \"{valid_path}\""
230:         ));
231:     }
232:     seen_paths.insert(valid_path.clone());
233:     page.path = valid_path;
234: 
235:     let is_pending_zero =
236:         page.dimensions_pending == Some(true) && page.width == 0 && page.height == 0;
237:     if !is_pending_zero {
238:         assert_image_dimensions(page.width, page.height)?;
239:     }
240: 
241:     if let Some(m) = page.modified {
242:         if !m.is_finite() || m < 0.0 {
243:             return Err("Page modified timestamp must be a non-negative number.".to_string());
244:         }
245:     }
246: 
247:     Ok(())
248: }
249: 
250: pub fn validate_position(pos: &mut ValidatedPosition, pages_count: usize) -> Result<(), String> {
251:     if pages_count == 0 {
252:         return Err("Position cannot be resolved against an empty chapter.".to_string());
253:     }
254: 
255:     if !pos.fraction.is_finite() {
256:         return Err("Position fraction must be a finite number.".to_string());
257:     }
258:     pos.fraction = pos.fraction.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
259: 
260:     if let Some(p) = &pos.path {
261:         pos.path = Some(validate_relative_path(p)?);
262:     }
263: 
264:     if let Some(ts) = pos.timestamp {
265:         if !ts.is_finite() {
266:             return Err("Position timestamp must be a finite number.".to_string());
267:         }
268:     }
269: 
270:     if (pos.page as usize) >= pages_count {
271:         pos.page = (pages_count - 1) as u32;
272:     }
273: 
274:     Ok(())
275: }
276: 
277: pub fn validate_chapter(
278:     chapter: &mut ValidatedChapter,
279:     chapter_index: usize,
280: ) -> Result<usize, String> {
281:     let trimmed_name = chapter.name.trim();
282:     if trimmed_name.is_empty() {
283:         return Err(format!(
284:             "Chapter {} name must be a non-empty string.",
285:             chapter_index + 1
286:         ));
287:     }
288:     chapter.name = trimmed_name.to_string();
289: 
290:     if chapter.pages.is_empty() {
291:         return Err(format!(
292:             "Chapter \"{}\" must contain at least one page.",
293:             chapter.name
294:         ));
295:     }
296: 
297:     let mut seen_paths = HashSet::new();
298:     for (idx, page) in chapter.pages.iter_mut().enumerate() {
299:         validate_page(page, &mut seen_paths).map_err(|e| {
300:             format!(
301:                 "Chapter \"{}\" page {} is invalid: {e}",
302:                 chapter.name,
303:                 idx + 1
304:             )
305:         })?;
306:     }
307: 
308:     let page_count = chapter.pages.len();
309: 
310:     if let Some(pos) = &mut chapter.position {
311:         validate_position(pos, page_count)?;
312:     }
313: 
314:     if let Some(bms) = &mut chapter.bookmarks {
315:         if bms.len() > LIMIT_BOOKMARKS_PER_CHAPTER {
316:             return Err(format!(
317:                 "Bookmarks per chapter exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_BOOKMARKS_PER_CHAPTER}. Use a smaller selection."
318:             ));
319:         }
320: 
321:         let mut seen_bms = HashSet::new();
322:         let mut valid_bms = Vec::new();
323:         for bm in bms.iter_mut() {
324:             if validate_position(bm, page_count).is_ok() {
325:                 let key = format!("{}:{}", bm.page, bm.path.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
326:                 if seen_bms.insert(key) {
327:                     valid_bms.push(bm.clone());
328:                 }
329:             }
330:         }
331:         *bms = valid_bms;
332:     }
333: 
334:     Ok(page_count)
335: }
336: 
337: pub fn validate_manga(
338:     manga: &mut ValidatedManga,
339:     seen_ids: &mut HashSet<String>,
340: ) -> Result<usize, String> {
341:     let trimmed_id = manga.id.trim();
342:     if trimmed_id.is_empty() {
343:         return Err("Manga ID must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
344:     }
345:     if !seen_ids.insert(trimmed_id.to_string()) {
346:         return Err(format!(
347:             "Duplicate manga ID found in backup: \"{trimmed_id}\""
348:         ));
349:     }
350:     manga.id = trimmed_id.to_string();
351: 
352:     let trimmed_title = manga.title.trim();
353:     if trimmed_title.is_empty() {
354:         return Err("Manga title must be a non-empty string.".to_string());
355:     }
356:     manga.title = trimmed_title.to_string();
357: 
358:     if manga.chapters.is_empty() {
359:         return Err(format!(
360:             "Manga \"{}\" must contain at least one chapter.",
361:             manga.title
362:         ));
363:     }
364:     if manga.chapters.len() > LIMIT_CHAPTERS_PER_TITLE {
365:         return Err(format!(
366:             "Chapters per title exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_CHAPTERS_PER_TITLE}. Use a smaller selection."
367:         ));
368:     }
369: 
370:     let mut total_pages = 0;
371:     for (idx, ch) in manga.chapters.iter_mut().enumerate() {
372:         let ch_pages = validate_chapter(ch, idx)?;
373:         total_pages += ch_pages;
374:         if total_pages > LIMIT_PAGES_PER_TITLE {
375:             return Err(format!(
376:                 "Pages per title exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_PAGES_PER_TITLE}. Use a smaller selection."
377:             ));
378:         }
379:     }
380: 
381:     let chapter_count = manga.chapters.len();
382:     let last_chapter = manga.last_chapter.unwrap_or(0);
383:     manga.last_chapter = Some(last_chapter.min(chapter_count.saturating_sub(1)));
384: 
385:     let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
386:         .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
387:         .unwrap_or_default()
388:         .as_millis() as f64;
389: 
390:     manga.added_at = Some(
391:         manga
392:             .added_at
393:             .filter(|t| t.is_finite() && *t > 0.0)
394:             .unwrap_or(now),
395:     );
396:     manga.updated_at = Some(
397:         manga
398:             .updated_at
399:             .filter(|t| t.is_finite() && *t > 0.0)
400:             .unwrap_or(now),
401:     );
402: 
403:     if let Some(sort) = &manga.sort {
404:         if sort != "name" && sort != "reverse" && sort != "natural" {
405:             manga.sort = None;
406:         }
407:     }
408: 
409:     if manga.slug.as_ref().is_none_or(|s| s.trim().is_empty()) {
410:         manga.slug = Some(crate::commands::indexing::generate_slug(&manga.title));
411:     }
412: 
413:     Ok(total_pages)
414: }

416: pub fn normalize_settings(settings: &ValidatedSettings) -> serde_json::Value {
417:     let theme = match settings.theme.as_deref() {
418:         Some("light") => "light",
419:         Some("dark") => "dark",
420:         _ => "system",
421:     };
422:     let mode = match settings.mode.as_deref() {
423:         Some("single") => "single",
424:         _ => "continuous",
425:     };
426:     let layout = match settings.layout.as_deref() {
427:         Some("width") => "width",
428:         Some("height") => "height",
429:         Some("auto") => "auto",
430:         _ => "max",
431:     };
432:     let max_width = match settings.max_width {
433:         Some(920) | Some(1024) | Some(1280) | Some(0) => settings.max_width.unwrap(),
434:         _ => 920,
435:     };
436:     let seconds = settings
437:         .seconds
438:         .filter(|s| s.is_finite())
439:         .map(|s| s.clamp(0.5, 120.0))
440:         .unwrap_or(3.0);
441:     let advance = settings.advance.unwrap_or(true);
442:     let reduced = settings.reduced.unwrap_or(false);
443:     let low_quality = settings.low_quality.unwrap_or(false);
444:     let custom_seconds = settings
445:         .custom_seconds
446:         .unwrap_or_else(|| ![2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0].contains(&seconds));
447:     let auto_hide_bars = settings.auto_hide_bars.unwrap_or(true);
448:     let resume_delay = settings
449:         .resume_delay
450:         .filter(|r| r.is_finite())
451:         .map(|r| r.clamp(0.5, 15.0))
452:         .unwrap_or(1.8);
453:     let direction = match settings.direction.as_deref() {
454:         Some("rtl") => "rtl",
455:         _ => "ltr",
456:     };
457:     let spread = settings.spread.unwrap_or(false);
458:     let single_cover = settings.single_cover.unwrap_or(true);
459:     let end_action = match settings.end_action.as_deref() {
460:         Some("stop") => "stop",
461:         Some("replay") => "replay",
462:         Some("next") => "next",
463:         _ => {
464:             if !advance {
465:                 "stop"
466:             } else {
467:                 "next"
468:             }
469:         }
470:     };
471:     let font_size_title = settings
472:         .font_size_title
473:         .filter(|f| f.is_finite())
474:         .map(|f| f.round().clamp(10.0, 36.0) as u32)
475:         .unwrap_or(15);
476:     let font_size_ui = settings
477:         .font_size_ui
478:         .filter(|f| f.is_finite())
479:         .map(|f| f.round().clamp(10.0, 36.0) as u32)
480:         .unwrap_or(15);
481:     let font_size_button = settings
482:         .font_size_button
483:         .filter(|f| f.is_finite())
484:         .map(|f| f.round().clamp(10.0, 36.0) as u32)
485:         .unwrap_or(15);
486:     let confirm_delete = settings.confirm_delete.unwrap_or(false);
487: 
488:     let mut map = serde_json::Map::new();
489:     map.insert("theme".into(), theme.into());
490:     map.insert("mode".into(), mode.into());
491:     map.insert("layout".into(), layout.into());
492:     map.insert("maxWidth".into(), max_width.into());
493:     map.insert("seconds".into(), seconds.into());
494:     map.insert("advance".into(), advance.into());
495:     map.insert("reduced".into(), reduced.into());
496:     map.insert("lowQuality".into(), low_quality.into());
497:     map.insert("customSeconds".into(), custom_seconds.into());
498:     map.insert("autoHideBars".into(), auto_hide_bars.into());
499:     map.insert("resumeDelay".into(), resume_delay.into());
500:     map.insert("direction".into(), direction.into());
501:     map.insert("spread".into(), spread.into());
502:     map.insert("singleCover".into(), single_cover.into());
503:     map.insert("endAction".into(), end_action.into());
504:     map.insert("fontSizeTitle".into(), font_size_title.into());
505:     map.insert("fontSizeUi".into(), font_size_ui.into());
506:     map.insert("fontSizeButton".into(), font_size_button.into());
507:     map.insert("confirmDelete".into(), confirm_delete.into());
508: 
509:     if let Some(sort) = &settings.shelf_sort {
510:         if sort == "recent" || sort == "title" || sort == "added" || sort == "progress" {
511:             map.insert("shelfSort".into(), sort.as_str().into());
512:         }
513:     }
514: 
515:     serde_json::Value::Object(map)
516: }
517: 
518: pub fn validate_backup_json(json_str: &str) -> Result<BackupPayload, String> {
519:     if json_str.len() > LIMIT_BACKUP_BYTES {
520:         return Err(format!(
521:             "Backup size (bytes) exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_BACKUP_BYTES}. Use a smaller selection."
522:         ));
523:     }
524: 
525:     let mut payload: BackupPayload = serde_json::from_str(json_str)
526:         .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid backup JSON structure: {e}"))?;
527: 
528:     if payload.version != 1 && payload.version != 2 {
529:         return Err("Invalid backup version. Supported versions: 1, 2.".to_string());
530:     }
531: 
532:     if payload.manga.len() > LIMIT_TITLES {
533:         return Err(format!(
534:             "Backup titles exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_TITLES}. Use a smaller selection."
535:         ));
536:     }
537: 
538:     let mut seen_ids = HashSet::new();
539:     let mut total_backup_pages = 0;
540: 
541:     for manga in payload.manga.iter_mut() {
542:         let pages = validate_manga(manga, &mut seen_ids)?;
543:         total_backup_pages += pages;
544:         if total_backup_pages > LIMIT_BACKUP_PAGES {
545:             return Err(format!(
546:                 "Backup pages exceeds the limit of {LIMIT_BACKUP_PAGES}. Use a smaller selection."
547:             ));
548:         }
549:     }
550: 
551:     Ok(payload)
552: }

554: #[cfg(test)]
555: mod tests {
556:     use super::*;
557: 
558:     #[test]
559:     fn test_validate_relative_path_traversal() {
560:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg").is_ok());
561:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/chapter..jpg").is_ok());
562:         assert!(validate_relative_path("../ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
563:         assert!(validate_relative_path("/ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
564:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg/").is_err());
565:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1\\01.jpg").is_err());
566:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/./01.jpg").is_err());
567:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/../01.jpg").is_err());
568:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/\001.jpg").is_err());
569:         // Windows prefix and stream security
570:         assert!(validate_relative_path("C:/ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
571:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg:stream").is_err());
572:         assert!(validate_relative_path("//server/share/01.jpg").is_err());
573:         assert!(validate_relative_path("nul.jpg").is_err());
574:         assert!(validate_relative_path("con").is_err());
575:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1./01.jpg").is_err());
576:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg.").is_err());
577:         assert!(validate_relative_path("ch1/01.jpg ").is_err());
578:         assert!(validate_relative_path(" ch1/01.jpg").is_err());
579:     }
580: 
581:     #[test]
582:     fn test_deny_unknown_fields_on_backup() {
583:         let json = r#"{
584:             "version": 2,
585:             "manga": [],
586:             "settings": {},
587:             "injectedKey": "malicious"
588:         }"#;
589:         assert!(validate_backup_json(json).is_err());
590:     }
591: 
592:     #[test]
593:     fn test_valid_backup_json_roundtrip() {
594:         let json = r#"{
595:             "version": 2,
596:             "manga": [
597:                 {
598:                     "id": "title-1",
599:                     "title": "Title 1",
600:                     "chapters": [
601:                         {
602:                             "name": "Chapter 1",
603:                             "pages": [
604:                                 { "name": "01.jpg", "path": "ch1/01.jpg", "width": 800, "height": 1200 }
605:                             ]
606:                         }
607:                     ]
608:                 }
609:             ],
610:             "settings": {
611:                 "theme": "dark",
612:                 "mode": "continuous"
613:             }
614:         }"#;
615: 
616:         let res = validate_backup_json(json);
617:         assert!(res.is_ok());
618:         let payload = res.unwrap();
619:         assert_eq!(payload.manga.len(), 1);
620:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].id, "title-1");
621:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].chapters[0].pages[0].width, 800);
622:     }
623: 
624:     #[test]
625:     fn test_duplicate_manga_id_rejected() {
626:         let json = r#"{
627:             "version": 2,
628:             "manga": [
629:                 {
630:                     "id": "title-1",
631:                     "title": "Title 1",
632:                     "chapters": [{ "name": "C1", "pages": [{ "name": "1.jpg", "path": "1.jpg", "width": 100, "height": 100 }] }]
633:                 },
634:                 {
635:                     "id": "title-1",
636:                     "title": "Title 1 Duplicate",
637:                     "chapters": [{ "name": "C1", "pages": [{ "name": "1.jpg", "path": "1.jpg", "width": 100, "height": 100 }] }]
638:                 }
639:             ],
640:             "settings": {}
641:         }"#;
642: 
643:         let err = validate_backup_json(json).unwrap_err();
644:         assert!(err.contains("Duplicate manga ID"));
645:     }

664:             "settings": {}

683:     #[test]
684:     fn test_backup_preserves_all_supported_fields_and_pending_dimensions() {
685:         let json = r#"{
686:             "version": 2,
687:             "manga": [
688:                 {
689:                     "id": "title-fields",
690:                     "title": "Title Fields",
691:                     "sort": "natural",
692:                     "revision": 7,
693:                     "chapters": [
694:                         {
695:                             "id": "ch-id-1",
696:                             "name": "Chapter 1",
697:                             "pages": [
698:                                 { "name": "01.jpg", "path": "ch1/01.jpg", "width": 0, "height": 0, "dimensionsPending": true }
699:                             ],
700:                             "position": {
701:                                 "page": 0,
702:                                 "fraction": 0.5,
703:                                 "path": "ch1/01.jpg",
704:                                 "mangaBookmark": true,
705:                                 "timestamp": 1731234567890.0
706:                             },
707:                             "bookmarks": [
708:                                 {
709:                                     "page": 0,
710:                                     "fraction": 0.5,
711:                                     "path": "ch1/01.jpg",
712:                                     "mangaBookmark": true,
713:                                     "timestamp": 1731234567890.0
714:                                 }
715:                             ]
716:                         }
717:                     ]
718:                 }
719:             ],
720:             "settings": {
721:                 "theme": "dark",
722:                 "fontSizeTitle": 24,
723:                 "fontSizeUi": 18,
724:                 "fontSizeButton": 16,
725:                 "confirmDelete": true,
726:                 "shelfSort": "progress"
727:             }
728:         }"#;
729: 
730:         let payload = validate_backup_json(json).expect("Validation should pass");
731:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].sort.as_deref(), Some("natural"));
732:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].revision, Some(7));
733:         assert_eq!(payload.manga[0].chapters[0].id.as_deref(), Some("ch-id-1"));
734:         assert_eq!(
735:             payload.manga[0].chapters[0].pages[0].dimensions_pending,
736:             Some(true)
737:         );
738:         let pos = payload.manga[0].chapters[0].position.as_ref().unwrap();
739:         assert_eq!(pos.manga_bookmark, Some(true));
740:         assert_eq!(pos.timestamp, Some(1731234567890.0));
741: 
742:         let norm = normalize_settings(&payload.settings);
743:         assert_eq!(norm["fontSizeTitle"], 24);
744:         assert_eq!(norm["fontSizeUi"], 18);
745:         assert_eq!(norm["fontSizeButton"], 16);
746:         assert_eq!(norm["confirmDelete"], true);
747:         assert_eq!(norm["shelfSort"], "progress");
748:     }
749: }
